Chapter 3201: Master Niu, I'M Here!

Li Yao first found a hidden bush and put away most of the tools, food, detectaphones, trackers, and so on that he had prepared. He only kept the basic number for one battle.

Then, he went to the grocery store next to the neighborhood and bought a cold soda. With a straw in his mouth, he shook it three times with each step, pretending to be a spectator.

His eyes, as sharp as the edge of a blade, swept over the faces of every man, woman, and child in the neighborhood, even including the seemingly white-haired, hunched old men.

The first fire truck had already stopped below Zhang Daniu’s building. A few firefighters drew a cordon in the periphery to stop the crowd from approaching. There were also some who were looking for nearby fire hydrants. However, as Zhang Daniu strictly followed Li Yao’s instructions, he only ignited the clothes and cotton placed inside the pressure cooker. After burning for over ten minutes, the smoke gradually dimmed and did not lead to a real fire. Therefore, the well-trained firefighters were not in too much of a hurry. They still controlled the scene, evacuated the crowd, and made sure that there were no injured people in the room.

Li Yao observed the trees, branches, and the roofs of the residential buildings, planning the routes to fly up and retreat at ease.

Of course, he did not forget to search for suspicious people and soon found more than one target.

About fifteen meters southwest of him, a middle-aged man was leaning against a bicycle shed. Judging from his appearance and temperament, he should be an office worker who was engaged in a desk job, the kind who lacked exercise and had a big belly.

However, contrary to the bulging beer belly, the muscle lines on his hands and feet were quite clear, as if they were made of rebars. They were full of the strength of springs and explosives, and his breath was quite long, too. He only breathed once every twenty seconds or so, which was a sign that his heart and lungs were extremely powerful.

How could a man with strong muscles and a strong heart and lungs have a round beer belly?

Also, below the boulevard about twenty meters to Li Yao’s right, there was a young woman who was wearing pajamas and pushing a stroller. She seemed to be taking a walk after dinner, but when the breeze blew away her hair, Li Yao discovered a very inconspicuous earpiece in her ear. It had a spring coil at the back and looked very professional and stable.

Although it was just a glance, Li Yao was certain that it was not a Bluetooth earpiece for listening to music.

Besides, it was not very reasonable to listen to music while watching a show with a child, was it?

The young woman and the beer-bellied middle-aged man exchanged meaningful glances from time to time, touching their earlobes and nodding their heads. Li Yao guessed that it was their secret code to ensure the safety of their surroundings.

He looked at the stroller that the young woman was pushing. As he expected, it was completely covered. Not even an inch of the baby’s skin could be seen.

It was already summer, and the weather was hot and humid. Although the fire upstairs was not really on fire, the crowds nearby were enough to raise the temperature of the neighborhood by three to five degrees. Should he still cover the baby under such circumstances? Something was obviously wrong!

Just like that, Li Yao discovered six unusual suspects.

Five of them were taking care of each other. They were all within the range of Li Yao’s vision. It was difficult to take them down at the same time.

The sixth one was in other people’s sight, too. But the firefighters had decided to evacuate everyone in the neighboring unit at the last minute. The sudden surge of people interfered with his movement, and he had to change his position according to the instructions of the firefighters.

This gave Li Yao an opportunity.

At this time, the sky gradually turned dark. The loudly complaining crowd did their best to distract the attention of several suspicious figures. When the sixth suspicious figure tried to go around to the back of a large tree, Li Yao silently jumped up from behind. His arm was like a strange python as he ruthlessly bit the man’s throat, completely silencing the man’s cries of alarm. Then, he firmly pressed the man’s carotid artery and used three seconds to put him into a coma.

Thankfully, everyone’s attention was focused on the smoking residential building. No one cared about the dark corners of the small garden.

Li Yao dragged the suspect into the bushes and searched his body quickly. Except for a mobile phone, a multipurpose tactical pen, and the communicator in his ear, he found no leads whatsoever.

Hardly had he put the communicator into his ear when he heard a voice from inside. “We’ve found the target on the rooftop. Stay alert. We’re going to bring him down!”

Li Yao’s heart tightened. The first thought that came to his mind was to rush to the rooftop to rescue his companion. But on second thought, if the enemy had the intention to kill, he could’ve killed Zhang Daniu as soon as possible. There would’ve been no need to waste his time informing his companions. After all, he could’ve drowned him in the water tank. There wouldn’t have been any trouble.

Since they wanted their companions to be ‘alert’ and ‘remove’ their target, it was obvious that they were going to remove a living person, not a cold corpse.

Zhang Daniu’s residential building had a total of three units. He lived in the unit on the far right. Since it was the scene of a fire, the residents had long been evacuated. The entrance had been cordoned off and only firefighters could enter.

The second unit in the middle was being evacuated because of the spreading fire. Many residents were running out of the corridors one after another.

The three units on the left were the furthest away from each other. They were not forcibly evacuated, but many residents still came downstairs to watch the show.

And from the rooftop, one could freely go down to the corridor of the three units.

Li Yao estimated that the enemy must’ve pretended to be a resident and evacuated through the stairs of the second unit.

As he expected, while he was lurking in the bushes, he noticed that the other five suspects were all gazing at the entrance of the corridor of the second unit.

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao crouched and dashed toward the five suspects.

“They don’t seem to be superpowered people. At least, they are not superpowered people like ‘Zhao Kai’, who have awakened the memories of their previous life and turned into monsters and assassins. They are just well-trained warriors.”

Li Yao thought to himself, So, does the enemy have an even more awesome mission? Hiding in the darkness? Forget it. I have to rescue him!

With a sliding step, Li Yao moved to the back of the middle-aged man who had a beer belly and strong muscles. He called out softly, “Hello?”

The middle-aged man was already in a highly tense state. When he turned his head instinctively, Li Yao’s hand happened to cut his Adam’s apple. He held his Adam’s apple until it was crooked. His eyes were wide open, but he could not utter a single word.

Almost at the same time, the three nails in Li Yao’s palm suddenly shot out. The target was not a suspicious person but the tires of the fire engine.

Normal iron nails would not make too much noise when they pierced through the tires, but Li Yao had poured all his strength into the iron nails and pierced through two tires. As he expected, one of the tires made a “Bang” sound, giving everyone a scare. It also caused everyone, including the suspicious person, to temporarily shift their attention to the fire truck.

Taking the opportunity, Li Yao zigzagged in the crowd and finished off the other two suspects neatly.

Right now, there were only two suspicious persons left.

They had also discovered Li Yao’s existence.

Not good!

Their exclamations came over from the communicator. “Don’t—”

Maybe they wanted to warn their companions in the corridor not to come down, but their warning was interrupted by Li Yao’s iron nails. Seeing that his tracks had been exposed, Li Yao simply stopped hiding and stomped on the floor tiles, shooting into the corridor of the second unit like an arrow released from a bow.

Orange lights were blinking inside the narrow corridor. Most of the residents had been evacuated. Li Yao raised his head and saw a brawny man helping Zhang Daniu down the stairs. He didn’t have time to think. He opened fire with his iron nails and broke the lights of the corridor, plunging the surroundings into darkness. At the same time, one of the nails slanted upwards and pierced into the guy’s jaw, while the other, which was even more insidious, pierced into the guy’s foot.

Since the guy had just reached the first floor, his feet were at the same level as Li Yao’s head, and it was difficult for him to notice Li Yao. Besides, the lights were off, and his pupils were enlarged. It was already the best he could do to avoid the nail aimed at his jaw. How could he avoid the nail aimed at his foot?

The nail pierced through the man’s military boots easily and nailed his feet, boots, and stairs together.

“Ah!”

The muscular man screamed and tried to pull the nail out. Li Yao took the opportunity to run up and pull Zhang Daniu.

Weird things happened.

The muscular man and Zhang Daniu both took out a black, shining dagger and stabbed it at Li Yao’s chest.

“What the hell?”

Li Yao was not scared of the sudden attack, but he didn’t understand why Zhang Daniu was attacking him. He glanced at Zhang Daniu’s face from the corner of his eyes, only to discover that the guy’s face was blank, his eyes were blank, and his pupils were unfocused. He looked like a puppet that was being manipulated and had no idea what he was doing.

Li Yao looked at the muscular man again. He was holding a saber in his right hand, but his left hand was stuck to Zhang Daniu’s back. His five fingers were shivering in a weird frequency, as if he was controlling Zhang Daniu’s nerves and muscle network through his spine.

“So that’s how it is. Some kind of very advanced hypnotism?”

Now that he had figured out the mechanism, there was nothing to be scared of. Li Yao sneered and watched the two daggers passing by his eyes again. They were aimed at the left leg of the muscular man and stomped on it.

The left leg of the muscular man was already in a lot of pain. Now that it had been stomped on by Li Yao, his attack and control skills had been seriously affected. His left hand was in a mess, and Zhang Daniu was cramping violently. No longer under his control, Li Yao took the opportunity to gather his fingers and peck the muscular man’s underarm.

The peck broke at least five ribs of the muscular man. His dagger left his hand, and he fell backward.

Li Yao was not willing to let it go. He chased after the muscular man and smashed his elbow into the muscular man’s chest. The muscular man was immediately knocked out and foamed at the mouth.

Zhang Daniu was still in a daze.

Li Yao had never learned any hypnotism techniques before. Therefore, he could only slap Zhang Daniu dozens of times in the simplest and crudest way. Zhang Daniu’s eyes were finally focused again when Li Yao noticed that his cheeks were bulging and glittering.

“Wuwu…”

Holding his cheek, he mumbled, “What happened? Why is it so painful?”

“Master Niu, don’t be scared. I’m here!”

Li Yao dragged Zhang Daniu and ran up the stairs. “Just now, some evil superpowered people tried to hurt you, but I eliminated them all!”

Chapter 3202: True Superpowers!

“Is that so?”

Zhang Daniu was still confused. He groped his body instinctively and said, “Why doesn’t my body hurt at all? My face is the only thing that hurts like fire. My molars are all loose.”

“Maybe your resistance infuriated the animals and they were trying to force you to submit in such a way.

Li Yao said, “These guys are brutal by nature. They are all cold-blooded animals who can do anything. Just slapping them is good enough for you. Anyway, just ignore the details. Master Niu, let’s go!”

“Where are we going?”

Zhang Daniu asked curiously, “Aren’t we going downstairs?”

“The corridor is blocked. Their men are outside.”

Li Yao answered simply. He rushed to the third floor, picked a random room, kicked the door open, and brought Zhang Daniu to the balcony.

“These are detectaphones and trackers. They are effective within 500 meters and can be used for 24 hours. They are products of cultivation technology.”

Li Yao stuffed two crystal chips as thin as the wings of a cicada into Zhang Daniu’s hands. “Hide them in the most secret place on your body. If we are unfortunately separated, we will have to rely on these things to save our lives!”

Zhang Daniu took the chip and hesitated. “… Uh, hide it here?”

“What else can I do? Get you a hotel room?”

Li Yao was anxious. “The enemy will catch up to us any moment. Let’s not waste our time, Master Niu!”

“Alright!”

Zhang Daniu gritted his teeth and made up his mind to unbuckle his belt.

“Master Niu, what are you doing?”

“Hiding eavesdroppers and trackers. Didn’t you ask me to hide them in the most secret places on my body?”

“I meant the bottom of your sock, Master Niu.”

“Oh, oh oh!”

While Zhang Daniu was hiding the detectaphones and trackers, Li Yao had already observed the environment behind the neighborhood and figured out eleven routes of escape.

“Yes.”

/ .

Zhang Daniu tied his shoelaces again and looked down from the balcony on the third floor. He clicked his tongue and said, “Are we going to jump off the third floor?”

“Are you kidding me? How could I?”

Li Yao smiled. He suddenly picked up Zhang Daniu by the collar and threw him off the balcony. “I’m throwing you off.”

“AHHHHHHH!”

Zhang Daniu danced and screamed in midair.

Li Yao, on the other hand, was the first to arrive. Like a shooting star chasing after the moon, he was the first to land on the ground. With quick reflexes, he grabbed Zhang Daniu’s ankle and swung it three times like a ball, reducing most of the impact and ensuring that Zhang Daniu landed steadily.

Huchi! Huchi! Huchi! Huchi!

Zhang Daniu’s face was pale and lifeless. His legs were shaking violently.

“If you want to scream, wait until it’s safe. Master Niu, we—”

While Li Yao was talking, his pupils constricted violently, and the hair on his arms stood up like the sharp thorns of a porcupine.

When he was on the third floor just now, he had clearly scanned the surroundings with his vision that far exceeded that of an ordinary person. There was clearly no one in the surroundings of the dilapidated bushes downstairs.

However, when he and Zhang Daniu jumped down together, three weirdly-dressed guys with extremely dangerous auras appeared in front of them and stared at them.

The bald, muscular man in the middle was more than two meters tall. He was wearing a loose version of a large sportswear, but it covered his bulging muscles in a soul-stirring way, as if the sportswear would be torn apart like candy if he moved slightly.

Complicated tattoos were all over his bald head, which seemed to be the pattern of a thunderstorm. What was weird was that the tattoos seemed to be alive. The lines that looked like lightning were blinking weirdly.

On the right side of the bald man was a petite girl. Her body size was in sharp contrast to his, but her gloomy temperament was equally sharp. She was wearing a black suit with lace edges. A crow with scarlet eyes was perched on her shoulder, cawing.

The one on the left was a woman, too, but she hid most of her face in the wide-brimmed hat and the collar of her coat. To be honest, it was quite weird that she was wearing a coat on such a hot day. However, Li Yao noticed that her golden hair, eyes, and the dense tattoos or runes all over her body through the gaps of her coat. It was probably why she had to hide her real appearance.

At this time, the sky was getting darker and darker, which was very abnormal.

There was no telling where the fog came from, but it surrounded the five of them from all directions like overturned dry ice.

Li Yao had a very strange feeling. The air seemed to have turned into thick glue. The alarms of the fire trucks and the noises of the neighbors all turned vague, as if they were coming from hundreds of meters away. Or maybe, he and Zhang Daniu had been dragged into a weird space where they would not be disturbed no matter how they struggled.

“Uh…”

Li Yao pondered whether or not he should greet the three weirdos first and get some information from them.

But the golden-haired woman in the coat shook it open, revealing countless tools that looked like brushes and paints. She drew something on the ground skillfully. After only a few seconds, Li Yao saw the outline of a complicated, round pattern.

The smell of energy gathering was rolling in the air, like static electricity before a storm.

Whatever the blonde woman was drawing, it must be something extremely dangerous. Li Yao changed his mind. He could not let her finish it.

Hiu! Hiu Hiu Hiu Hiu!

More than ten steel nails were launched at the same time, drawing more than ten miserable arcs in the air. Except for two of them, which were aimed at the bald man and the crow girl, the rest of the steel nails were all aimed at the blonde woman. Even Li Yao himself was hiding behind more than ten steel nails and shooting at the other party like nails.

The bald man, the crow girl, and the blonde woman all put on a mocking smile at the same time. The blonde woman didn’t dodge and continued drawing her weird patterns. The bald man, on the other hand, strode forward and roared. He tightened his muscles and formed a giant shield of flesh and blood to block all the nails.

Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi!

The iron nails pierced into their flesh. The bald man and Li Yao both exclaimed in disbelief.

As far as Li Yao was concerned, he did not expect the bronze muscles of the bald muscular man to be so tough that the iron nails that he had shot through walls and steel plates failed to pierce through them.

On the bald man’s side, he had underestimated Li Yao’s strength. He had thought that he would be able to knock the iron nail away. He did not expect that the nail would pierce half an inch into his flesh and release a burning pain, making him cry out in surprise.

However, the nails alone were far from enough.

The bald muscular man pulled out an iron nail from his muscle and licked the blood on it. The hideousness on his face was even more intense as he strode toward Li Yao.

With every step he took, the muscles all over his body wriggled and rolled like giant serpents. Soon, all the iron nails were squeezed out by him.

Hu!

The bald muscular man raised his saber and slashed down.

Since Zhang Daniu was right behind him, Li Yao had no choice but to cross his arms and take the attack.

BOOM!

Li Yao felt that a crashed plane had crashed between his arms and almost knocked him into the mud. Blood was surging inside his chest, and his heart was beating so fast that it seemed to be bursting out of his ribs and chest.

However, it was exactly the bombardment that unlocked a certain… shackle deep inside his brain or his heart inch by inch. The strength that he had not felt for a long time poured out of the collapsing dam, making him feel hungry!

It had been a long time since Li Yao was this hungry.

It was not the hunger when the super power was just awakened, but the emptiness deep inside the soul that could not be described with words. Such emptiness could not be satisfied by food. Only the painful moans of the enemy could soothe it temporarily.

Li Yao let out an inhuman roar from the depths of his throat. He actually grabbed the right arm of the bald muscular man. His ten fingers pierced deeply into the muscles like a pair of tongs, using the heart-wrenching pain to numb the muscular man’s reaction. Immediately after, his pair of hands fiercely pulled downwards, and his entire body soared into the air. His knee ruthlessly crashed into the face of the muscular man as if it were a drill. Only a “crack” could be heard. Even if the bald muscular man had trained his body to be as hard as steel, he was unable to cultivate a layer of muscles on his tibia. When Li Yao landed on the ground, half of his face had already collapsed. Blood spurted out from his nostrils and eye sockets. The malevolent look on his face turned into shock and horror. He was no longer as calm as a few seconds ago.

Before the bald, muscular man moaned, the crow girl exclaimed in shock. She didn’t expect that the dangerous aura of the unknown Li Yao would be ten times stronger than before. With a shake of her shoulder, the crimson-eyed crow soared into the sky and lunged at Li Yao.

In the meantime, countless flapping wings echoed in the mist. Crows, sparrows, night owls… All kinds of famous and unknown birds blocked the sky, the fog, and Li Yao’s five senses, as if he had been trapped in a maze of black feathers.

It’s—an illusion!

Li Yao was very sure that, in such a crowded, brightly-lit metropolis, even if the enemy had the ability to communicate with birds, it was impossible for them to summon so many birds in such a short period of time, and it was also impossible for the normally docile birds to turn into terrifying sky predators. Therefore, it was all an illusion and could not fool him!

Li Yao sniffed and closed his eyes, ignoring the noises around him. Instead, he constructed an identical battlefield of mist in his brain according to the information and data he collected just now and rushed toward the golden-haired woman in his memory.

As he expected, thousands of sparrows, crows, and night owls were hovering in the sky, pretending that they were going to attack him, but none of them really landed on him.

Chapter 3203: Magic!

Seeing that Li Yao was about to reach the blonde woman, the muscular man, whose face had half collapsed, showed amazing vitality and managed to stand up. He even broke a branch nearby and charged at Li Yao, sweeping everything in his way.

His skin was overflowing with an inhuman metallic gloss, making him look like a lump of lumpy iron. When he punched, the air was filled with the sound of rushing wind.

Li Yao’s direction and speed remained unchanged. However, he suddenly leaned forward and kicked the iron fists of the bald, muscular man with his right hand on the ground. After a series of hurried and dull noises, dazzling sparks were bursting out between their fists and feet.

“Asshole!”

The bald muscular man finally roared in fury.

Li Yao, on the other hand, did not seem to be in the middle of an intense battle. Instead, he seemed to have just finished a two-hour hot bath and was in the middle of a Thai massage. As the bald muscular man bombarded him with his iron fists, every acupoint on his body was opened.

BAM!

Another dull sound echoed. Li Yao withdrew half of his strength and did not burst out until he kicked the bald man. Not only did he kick the bald man three to five meters away, he also flew close to the ground toward the blonde woman like a bat.

Blocked by the bald man and the crow girl, the gold-haired woman was drawing on the ground. The complicated rune array was almost completed, too.

She took off her wide-brimmed sun hat. Her coat was flapping in the air, too, revealing the tattoos that extended from her arms to her neck and from her neck to her eyebrows. They were countless zigzagging, tadpole-like words. As far as Li Yao knew, they did not belong to any ancient language family on Earth. Instead, they seemed to be variations of a certain cuneiform script.

Her hair and eyes were originally as dazzling as the sun, but at this moment, they had completely faded away, becoming as bleak as an albino’s. A series of strange syllables came from the depths of her throat. It was somewhat similar to Zhao Kai’s “XXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX.

As the spells were flying, the wedge-shaped tattoos on her body were glittering together with the runes on the ground.

Not good!

Sensing the great threat, Li Yao stomped on the ground and accelerated again, turning into a streak of brightness.

But miserable croaks were echoing in the sky. A few stinky objects darted at him and exploded right in front of him.

It was a crow.

Not all the crows and sparrows were fakes. In the thousands of illusions, more than ten real crows and sparrows crashed into Li Yao under the control of the crow girl.

Li Yao’s maximum speed was as high as that of a jet airliner. Therefore, the consequences of the crows and sparrows hitting him were as miserable as those of birds hitting an airliner.

Although Li Yao’s body had been strengthened, he was still slowed down by half a second when the crow hit the sparrow.

In the half second, countless weeds and vines grew out of the ground and tied up his ankles like iron wires, digging into his skin and flesh. The more he struggled, the tighter they tied him up. Li Yao was not able to free himself for a moment. His high-speed sprinting posture was disrupted by the crows and the vines, and he fell to the ground.

By the time he blew away the feathers, freed himself from the vines, and regained his balance, the white-haired woman had already completed the entire process of casting the spell.

She seemed to have been seriously ill. All her vitality had been drained by the pattern below her feet. Her flesh and blood, which had been plump and full, withered at a visible speed, making her look like a skeleton. All that was left was a pair of bright eyes that were staring at Li Yao.

She had already closed her mouth, but the weird curse was still echoing in Li Yao’s brain, sharper and louder.

The sound irritated Li Yao. His blood was boiling, as if somebody had set his internal organs on fire and rubbed his cells at a high speed. His mitochondria lost control and released all the heat.

“Damn it. I’ve been fooled!”

Alarm bells were ringing inside Li Yao’s brain. “What kind of superpower is this? Using a certain high-frequency ripple to make my cells lose control, tampering with the command for the mitochondria to generate energy, causing the temperature of my cells to continuously rise, causing my body to spontaneously combust, burning me alive?”

“Is this a superpower or… magic?”

BOOM!

The word ‘magical’ had only just appeared in Li Yao’s mind when his internal organs and skin started burning furiously. His entire body turned into a large, bright fireball. In the next moment, the scene and his memories became fragmented and blurred. All he could see was Zhang Daniu screaming and raising his hands as he was taken away by the three superpowered people, or rather, the ‘magician’.

The mist around him gradually thinned. He found himself in a familiar neighborhood again. He could hear the sirens of fire trucks and ambulances. Then, he knew nothing.

…

Li Yao had a brand-new dream.

It was a strange dream that was completely different from the evil-looking woman who was wearing a T-rex gun and had thin arms and legs.

He was in a city. Maybe it was Jiangnan City, or maybe it was the city where he had lived in his previous life. He seemed to be running away from something in a hurry. The city was empty. There was no one except him, but the roads and the streets were changing randomly like a maze that was alive.

Not far behind him, an enormous silver ball that looked like a balloon was floating in midair and chasing after him unhurriedly.

Li Yao ran and ran. He wanted to run out of the scanning range of the silver ball, but no matter how fast he was or how tricky his route was, every time he turned around, he would see the silver ball following him.

On a closer look, he discovered that there was a silver-white thread on the back of his head that was either a blood vessel or a nerve. The thread dragged all the way to the bottom of the silver ball.

As it turned out, the silver ball was not chasing after him, but he was guiding it forward.

An indescribable sense of powerlessness surged out of Li Yao’s heart. He stared blankly at the silver ball.

The shell of the silver ball was glittering like an uneven mirror. His reflection in the mirror was broken and twisted. He did not look like himself at all.

Then again, who was he exactly and what should he look like?

“Who am I?

“Am I Li Yao?

“What is the so-called ‘Li Yao’? Where am I from? Where am I going? What is my destiny?

…

Li Yao shuddered. When he woke up from the cold bed, he found himself wrapped in an airtight plastic membrane. There was also a zip in front of him that pulled the plastic membrane all the way to his head.

“What is this? Why did you put me inside? It hurts. It’s stuffy. It tickles!”

Li Yao pondered and tried to recall what had happened when he was in the coma. Finally, he understood. “This is a body bag, right?”

Li Yao was immediately back to himself. He felt that he was an over-baked sweet potato whose shell had been completely carbonized into a black lump. Thankfully, it was still moist and sweet inside and could still be saved.

Thump! Thump! Thump!

While he was thinking, his heart started beating again. The blood that flowed to his body seemed to contain marvelous recovery drugs that could accelerate the division of cells, the regeneration of flesh and blood, and the healing of wounds. He even felt that he had been cleansed and reborn.

Yes, it was amazing. Li Yao seemed to be able to ‘see’ that the mitochondria factories inside all the cells inside his body were functioning normally again and providing stable and powerful energy for the cells to regrow. The broken nerves, veins, and muscle fibers were reconnected in a way beyond the range of human beings.

His braincells seemed to have experienced a ‘comprehensive upgrade’ because of the burning flames. He had awakened more mysterious abilities and could even record what happened to him when he was in a coma.

It seemed that the three super experts had kidnapped Zhang Daniu after they set him on fire and turned him into a giant fireball. Maybe because they were pressed for time, the mist barrier that they had set up was dispersing and about to be discovered, or maybe because they were very confident in the ‘magic’ of the white-haired girl. In short, they did not attack Li Yao, who was burning furiously. Well, since he was already in such a state, there was no need to attack him at all.

Afterward, the other party disappeared, and the fog dissipated. Naturally, Li Yao was discovered by the firefighters and the masses. Although he didn’t understand how the fire on the fifth floor had implicated the bystanders on the ground, he was still extinguished by the firefighters who had done their duty. He was sent to the ambulance, to the emergency room of the hospital, uh, okay, directly to the morgue.

“That’s magic, right? That must be magic, right?”

Li Yao asked himself, “Anyways, there shouldn’t be such a technique in the world of 40,000 Years of Cultivation. Although it takes a long time to perform and requires all kinds of complicated materials, once it is done, it can lock onto the target’s body without the need to aim and start burning from the depths of the internal organs and cells. It is indeed an unpredictable and unpredictable technique.

“But how is it possible?

“Why is there something as unscientific as ‘magic’ in this world?

“Of course, as a ‘Cultivator Hero’ who has come back from the dead, I don’t seem to have the qualifications to berate magic for being unscientific. However, sigh, it’s just that, first, it was cultivation, then it was magic. Who the hell knows how many inconceivable powers still exist in this world, how many mysterious and unfathomable lives… What kind of world is this?”

While thinking, Li Yao tried his best to recover his body functions.

Right then, he heard light footsteps outside the morgue.

It was not a doctor but a bunch of well-trained warriors or assassins who had perfect control over their muscles and balance!

Chapter 3204: "Ark" And "Apocalypse".

There were four of them.

Li Yao had heard the footsteps of three of them when he was in Zhang Daniu’s neighborhood. They were the beer-bellied middle-aged man, the young woman who was pushing the stroller, and the muscular man who had kidnapped Zhang Daniu and was suspected to be adept at hypnotism or puppetry.

The muscular man was limping because Li Yao had pierced his foot with a nail. There was no way that he could be mistaken.

But the footsteps of the fourth person did not belong to the mysterious people in the neighborhood.

And he happened to be the most dangerous one.

The man’s footsteps seemed to be fast, but they were actually slow. Every step he took seemed to be a random landing spot, giving the feeling that he was unpredictable and unpredictable.

Li Yao imagined that, if he were a gunman lurking in the darkness, it would be very difficult to lock onto the target.

Just by the sound of his footsteps, Li Yao could tell that the mysterious newcomer was much more difficult to deal with than the other three.

Li Yao tightened every muscle on his body, but he completely relaxed after pondering for a moment. He even stopped the rhythm of his internal organs and cells and pretended that he was a cold corpse again.

There were four of them and only one of him. Also, he was heavily wounded and hungry. The energy inside his body was running dry. If a conflict broke out in such a situation, his odds of winning were slim.

Besides, Li Yao was very curious as to what these people were doing here. This was the morgue. Theoretically speaking, he was already a corpse. These people didn’t need to whip his corpse to vent their anger, right?

Therefore, was it possible for him to get some information from their conversation?

Nobody would be prepared for a dead body, right?

/ .

Li Yao decided to take a gamble.

The moment his heart stopped beating, the door of the morgue was opened silently. Four guys whose identities and positions were unknown walked in.

The dangerous figure that had never been seen before seemed to be a lady. A vague scent of perfume drifted into Li Yao’s nostrils and tickled his nasal membranes and brain cells, making him want to sneeze.

Li Yao was not sure whether or not it was his opponent’s way of testing him and trying to suppress all his physiological activities. Thankfully, after being reborn in the raging flames, his control over every organ in his body had reached an exquisite level. With a telepathic thought, not only could he expand the organs such as the pupils by six times, he could also completely cut off the ‘life force’ of a certain organ.

“Hiss—”

The zip of the body bag was pulled open. Li Yao could feel that the other three people could not help but cover their mouths and noses. There was no telling whether it was because of the stench of the burnt body or because they were frightened by his anxious appearance, but the dangerous woman who had just appeared stuck her head out and observed for a while. She gestured for someone to pull the body bag up.

“Who is he?”

The dangerous woman, whose body was emitting a weird scent, extended her slender fingers and tapped Li Yao’s metal bed unhurriedly, frowning.

“I don’t know, Ms. Mournful Mist.”

The muscular man who had kidnapped Zhang Daniu a moment ago said respectfully, “At six o’clock in the evening, we were looking for our target in the ‘Green Garden District’ according to your instructions. The guy showed up quietly and knocked down five of us before he took our target away. According to our analysis, his speed and agility are beyond the limits of human beings. He should be an ‘awakened one’.

“At first, we thought that he was a member of the ‘apocalypse’. What was odd was that, after he took away the target and jumped down from the back window on the third floor, he had a fierce fight with three ‘apocalypse’ awakeners. One of them should’ve been heavily wounded, too, and the other must’ve been forced to perform a secret art that consumed a lot of his vitality in order to kill him.

“It is clearly not the internal strife of the ‘apocalypse’. Therefore, he is neither an Awakener of the ‘Ark’ nor an Awakener of the ‘apocalypse’. Then, who is he exactly? Why is he involved in the matter? More importantly, what unspeakable relationship does he have with his target?”

Li Yao was dazed for a moment.

It turned out that the two groups of people hiding in front and behind Zhang Daniu’s house were not on the same side.

In the beginning, he had seen a beer-bellied middle-aged man, a young woman pushing a stroller, and a muscular man who was good at hypnotizing. Judging from their names, they were all superhumans of the ‘Ark Foundation’. Of course, they were not called ‘superpowers’ in such a cliché way. Instead, they called themselves ‘Awakened’. It was indeed cool.

The bald man, the crow girl, and the white-haired sorcerer, who he had met when he was about to run to the back of the house after rescuing Zhang Daniu, belonged to another gang with superpowers named ‘Apocalypse’.

If the murderer of the gun show in T3 was not lying and belonged to the ‘Apocalyptic Union’, and the two ‘Apocalyptic Union’ were the same thing, was he mistaken? The ‘Apocalyptic Union’ was the evil organization that wanted Zhang Daniu’s life, and the ‘Ark’ was good enough, at least to protect Zhang Daniu?

Li Yao was not in a hurry to make a judgement. He listened to the ‘awakened people’ of the ‘Ark Foundation’ patiently.

“I’ve been to the scene, Copperhead. It’s not your fault.”

The dangerous woman named ‘Grey Mist’ said, “Among the squad of awakeners sent by the Heaven’s Origin Sector, including monsters such as ‘White Night’, according to the traces of the fierce battle, White Night was planning to finish the battle with a regular spell in the beginning. But for some reason, maybe because of the abrupt changes during the battle, she had to consume a lot of her vitality and burn her own soul to increase the power and precision of the array by ten times in order to kill the guy!

“According to the analysis of the traces of the magical arrays that ‘White Night’ failed to clear, I estimate that she won’t be able to retrieve the simplest spells for at least a week—this is a crucial week for the competition between the ‘Ark’ and the ‘Apocalypse’!

“In other words, ‘White Night’ feared the guy’s capability and thought that it was worth it to annihilate him at the cost of not being able to attack for a week.

“It’s not unusual that the ‘beginners’ who have only awakened the memories of one or two lives cannot deal with such a guy.

“The guy has disabled ‘White Night’ for an entire week?”

The muscular man named ‘Tong Tou’ secretly clicked his tongue. His two companions behind him exclaimed in shock, too, because they were quite surprised by Li Yao’s capability. It seemed that, in the circle of the awakened, or at least in the two organizations named ‘Apocalyptic’ and ‘Ark’, the white-haired, golden-haired female sorcerer named ‘White Night’ was also a well-known, brutal figure.

Forget it. His identity is not important.

The grey mist said, “The most important thing is the whereabouts and safety of the target. As long as we can rescue the target, there will always be a way to pry open his mouth and find out the identity of the mysterious Awakener as well as whether or not there is a third force behind him. Right now, ‘White Night’ is unable to access the magic. The power of the Awakener belonging to the ‘Apocalypse’ of Jiangnan City has been weakened to the lowest point. It is the best, perhaps the only, opportunity for us. When the Awakener from various places arrive one after another, things will be troublesome!”

“Understood!”

The muscular man ‘Copper Head’ and his two companions heavily nodded their heads. He hesitated for a moment before speaking again, “Ms. Grey Mist, I still don’t quite understand. Just what is the value of this Zhang Daniu? Is he worthy of you leaving the Ark to personally oversee this operation in Jiangnan City? Whether it is the accomplishments of his works, his influence, or his quality, he is far from the target that we normally lock onto… He doesn’t seem to be worth wasting so many resources of the foundation for such an ordinary fellow, right?”

“I don’t know. Calculating the value of a target is not my job.

The grey mist said, “Maybe, there are unknown secrets in his work that can activate more ‘awakened people’. Didn’t I ask you to study ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’ first? What have you found?”

“Well, at least I didn’t.”

“I’ve read more than fifty chapters. I don’t feel that I’ve been ‘awakened’ at all.

“I’ve read 220 chapters.”

The beer-bellied middle-aged man said, “I can guarantee that every plot has been seen in other books. There are absolutely no ‘unknown secrets’. It’s impossible for the author to be an ‘awakened one’. It’s also impossible for him to awaken more awakened ones. What he wrote has nothing to do with his previous life’s memories. It’s pure fabrication.

“I held on for five hundred chapters and watched the protagonist jump to the Flying Star Sector and participate in a refining competition. I couldn’t stand it any longer.

The young woman said, “I estimate that the next few million words will be the same. It’s just an infinite loop. How can it contain any secrets or exciting things? Ms. Gray Mist, what about you? Have you found anything special?”

“No.”

The grey mist said, “After I finished the first chapter and saw that the protagonist was from an abandoned garbage dump, I couldn’t stand it any longer. You know how intense the situation has been recently. I wouldn’t waste my time reading such a book.

“So…”

The chief hesitated. “Is there a mistake?”

“…No.”

The grey mist was silent for a moment before it replied, “The command comes directly from ‘Red Pole’.”

“What?”

The three of them exclaimed at the same time, their voices shaking, “The Red Pole has personally issued the command. Why is the guy so important that the Red Pole is willing to risk being discovered, locked onto, and annihilated by the Heaven’s Origin Sector?”

“At first, I thought it was a mistake, too. But you should know that ‘Red Pole’ is never wrong.

The grey mist said, “The weird thing that happened in the ‘Green Garden District’ today has indirectly verified the Red Pole’s judgement. Come to think of it, if the target is really of no value and cannot be activated if he is not an Awakener, why did the Heaven’s Origin Sector send such a tough warrior as ‘White Night’ to deal with him? Wouldn’t it have been easier to find a random ‘beginner’ who has awakened the memories of one or two lifetimes and pretend that he was involved in a car accident or a robbery?

“No. Not only did ‘White Night’ come in person, the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’ acted out of the norm and did not erase the target on the spot but took him away. Why?”

Chapter 3205: Critical Day

“That is indeed a problem. The operation of the Heaven’s Origin Sector does not fit their usual pattern.

Copperhead pondered for a moment and said, “In the past, they had eliminated all the people who were likely to awaken as ‘observers’ without leaving any alive. Why did they choose to kidnap them instead of killing them directly? Is it possible that… this is a trap targeting us?”

“Of course, it could be a trap.

“However,” the grey mist said, “if the Heaven’s Origin Sector really wants to set up a trap that will catch us all in one fell swoop, why did they choose such a… weird bait as ‘Zhang Daniu’?

“Also, after the incident, the experts of the foundation hacked into the traffic monitoring system of Jiangnan City and obtained part of the whereabouts of the ‘Heaven Enlightenment Organization’s’ Awakener. They discovered that ‘White Night’ had already set off long before we were ready to take action. In other words, she wasn’t targeting us; she was targeting the target. Is such a target worthy for ‘White Night’, who is a ‘Deep Perceptive One’, to personally take action? If it is really a trap, who is the trap pointing at?”

The question silenced the other three awakeners of the ‘Ark Foundation’.

Right then, the young woman who had been pushing the stroller said in delight, “Ms. Gray Mist, the Mirage Falcons have found them. Their SUV has entered the underground garage of the Gold Eagle Plaza!”

“Very good. Ask the ‘Phantom Vulture’ to continue monitoring the sky and make sure that there are no replacement vehicles. I will send other people to check whether or not they have escaped on foot or snuck into other buildings.

The grey mist said, “In a moment, another twelve experts of the organization will arrive in Jiangnan City—ten ‘Deep Perceivers’ and two ‘Ultimate Perceivers’. If they don’t run away, take action at midnight!”

“Ten ‘Deep Perception’ and two ‘Ultimate Perception’!”

Tong Tou and the rest of them exclaimed again, “What a splendid team! Is the foundation finally going to do something big? The Gold Eagle Plaza is in the downtown area. It will be quite lively even at midnight. Aren’t we scared of being discovered?”

“According to the weather gauge, there will be a storm tonight.

“Besides,” the grey mist said, “the foundation has abolished the ban. You can use your abilities freely now.”

“Well—”

Tong Tou and the rest of them gasped and stammered, “So, the critical day has really come?”

“That’s right.”

The grey mist smiled coldly. “The ‘critical day’ is drawing near. The world is collapsing. It is unnecessary to hide your identity and abilities anymore. Only by doing everything you can and releasing endless possibilities and defeating the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’ and even the entire world can you shatter all the shackles and regain your freedom.

“Remember, this is ‘Red Pole’—this life is our best and our last chance. If we cannot defeat our enemy in this life, we will be completely suppressed and swallowed by our enemy. There will be no possibility of us awakening our true self anymore. Do you understand?”

“Understood!”

The three of them held their heads high and declared at the same time, “Long live the free will!

“Excellent. Let’s take action!”

The grey mist waved its hand and led the other three awakened members of the Ark Foundation out of the morgue.

Five minutes after they left, the body bag on the cold iron bed made rattling noises. The burnt body inside suddenly sat up.

Thankfully, no one came in. If a doctor or staff member saw this scene, they would definitely have a heart attack.

“Chi la—”

Li Yao opened the body bag from inside. The charred coal shells on his body fell off, revealing skin as tender as a baby’s. He sneezed a dozen times in a row.

“What ‘Observers’, ‘Deep Perception’ and ‘Ultimate Perception’, ‘Critical Day’, and ‘Beyond Reincarnation’? What are they talking about?”

Li Yao scratched his messy hair, only to discover that it had not grown back even though it had been burnt. He was now bald, too.

“Yes, I’ve become bald and strong!”

Li Yao waved his fists in the air. He could tell from the sound of the wind and the comfortable feeling of his blood flowing in his veins that his capability had significantly improved compared to half a day ago. Right now, as long as he had enough energy, it was possible for him to summon fireballs and electric arcs from his palm.

Of course, his stomach was rumbling, too, which almost pierced through his stomach.

Thankfully, this was a hospital where nutrition was abundant.

At first, Li Yao crouched down and went to the laundry room to steal a set of hospital clothes. Then, he went to the pharmacy and drank dozens of bottles of high-concentration glucose syrup. After that, he went to the inpatient department to steal a set of clothes that were suitable for him and left the hospital openly.

Originally, he wanted to take a taxi or a subway, but he didn’t have a cell phone, nor did he have even half a copper coin. It was better for him to obediently steal a shared bicycle!

First, he returned to the “Green Garden District” rented by Zhang Daniu and retrieved the bag that he had hidden in the bushes. From the bag, he took out the work clothes and rubber boots that he had originally prepared for Zhang Daniu and re-armed himself. Fortunately, he had the foresight to abandon his equipment before the battle. Otherwise, these things would have been burnt to the ground and it would have been troublesome to purchase new ones.

Then, he rode his bike and marched toward the Gold Eagle Plaza again.

The Gold Eagle Plaza was located in the newly-established central business district of Jiang Nan City. It was quite a distance away from the old district that Zhang Daniu had rented. Li Yao kicked his legs so hard that they were like fire wheels as he moved forward like a racer.

Twenty minutes later, just as the ‘gray mist’ said, dense clouds were gathering in the night sky. Earsplitting explosions were echoing in the sky from time to time. Lightning was gazing at the human world coldly like dragons that were ready to attack.

Very soon, spluttering noises echoed. Raindrops the size of beans splashed on the ground like hot oil. Unprepared, the entire Jiang Nan City was swallowed by the torrential rain.

“What a heavy rain!”

Li Yao had never experienced such a fierce and even crazy storm in his entire life. How could it still be rain? It was simply a flood that fell from the sky. It was also accompanied by hurricanes of the 70s, 80s, and 90s. The dense raindrops were like an iron wall condensed from waterfalls, compressing his field of vision to the limit. As far as his eyes could reach, the prosperous scene that was still lively a moment ago would instantly dim. The signboards that had been blown away by the wind were everywhere. The flowerpots that had been struck by the raindrops and the people who were fleeing with their heads in their hands were everywhere. From time to time, neon lights and electronic screens between the skyscrapers would emit crackling sounds before they were completely extinguished. After riding forward for a while, there was not a single passerby in sight. All that could be seen were the flickering lights struggling to hold on in the storm.

“This is not normal.”

Li Yao wiped off the hot rain on his face. He couldn’t tell whether it was rainwater, magma, or even plasma that was falling from the sky. He stared at the horizon in bewilderment. On the continuous shadow of the city, countless lightning bolts were falling at the same time, making the city look like a forest of lightning.

If the end of the world did exist, the scene in front of him was nothing more than that.

Could it be that, as the ‘gray mist’ of the Ark Foundation said, the entire world was collapsing?

Li Yao pursed his lips with great difficulty. In a trance, he felt that he had seen, experienced, or perhaps experienced, the collapse in front of his eyes countless times.

But this time should be different.

This time, it had to be different!

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao continued rushing forward in the middle of the storm.

It was already midnight when he arrived at the central business district.

The central business district, which was full of skyscrapers, used to be a city without night. Even in the middle of the night, the glass walls of the skyscrapers were still emitting colorful brilliance, as if they were a paradise enshrouded in an aurora.

But under the bombardment of the storm and the super thunderstorm, the Nightless City today was lifeless, like a ghost town drowned in a flood.

The Gold Eagle Plaza’s shape was very strange. It had a bit of the feeling of a trident. In front of the plaza stood an enormous golden eagle statue. Even under the onslaught of the pouring rain, it was very easy to find.

Li Yao did not rush in recklessly. Instead, he found a bridge not far away and crouched in a stinky corner. He took out the mini monitor that he had modified with electronic components and connected the signal amplifier to the earpiece.

The bridge was no more than a hundred meters away from the Gold Eagle Plaza in a straight line. Although it was heavily jammed due to the heavy rain, it shouldn’t be a problem for him to get his bearings.

As it turned off, the monitor shivered for about half a minute before it gradually stabilized. A lackluster light spot appeared in the upper left corner.

“Not the Gold Eagle Plaza?”

Li Yao was dazed for a moment. He adjusted the reception coil and the monitor carefully. “The building next to it?”

It made sense. Driving into the underground garage of the Gold Eagle Plaza did not mean that their nest was on the top. It was only reasonable that they had a lot of tricks up their sleeve.

The distance between the Gold Eagle Plaza and the Fortune Plaza was no more than a hundred meters in a straight line. It was still within Li Yao’s range of positioning and eavesdropping. Li Yao tapped his earphones softly and fiddled with the antenna to eliminate the interference of the storm. He listened to the sound coming from the depths of the earphones.

He had heard it. Although it was fuzzy, twisted, and occasionally interrupted, Zhang Daniu’s sharp and distinctive voice was unmistakable. Thankfully, the author was still alive!

He seemed to have been tied to a chair. Li Yao could hear the dull noises of the chair hitting the carpet. He could tell that he was in a highly-strung state and was shivering uncontrollably.

Right then, somebody else entered the room and sat down on another chair in front of him.

“Mr. Zhang… Should I call you ‘Mr. Zhang’, ‘Master Snail’, or ‘Master’?”

The man’s tone was very rigid, with barely any cadence. He should be a foreigner. His voice was gentle and cold, like a precise machine. “First of all, let me introduce myself. My name is Smith. I am Hugo Smith. However, my friends and enemies are all used to calling me ‘Hunter’. I’m sorry that I invited you here in such a rough way. It can’t be helped. It was an unexpected and urgent situation. You don’t mind, do you?”

Chapter 3206: Master Niu

“I don’t mind. I don’t mind. It’s not rough. I—I—I think you were already very gentle under the circumstances. Really!”

Li Yao heard Zhang Daniu crying in a high-pitched voice, “Don’t kill me. Please don’t kill me. I don’t know anything, and I don’t want to know anything. I was forced. Really. I’m very obedient. Please don’t kill me!”

“Relax, Mr. Zhang.”

The hunter chuckled. “If we want to kill you, we can do it in the neighborhood. You are not an ‘awakened one’. One fireball is enough to burn you to the ground. Just like countless mysterious cases of spontaneous human combustion, we don’t need to invite you to this place at all, do we?

“I think so, too.

Zhang Daniu breathed heavily. He swallowed his saliva with difficulty and said in a trembling voice, “There’s no need to be so polite as to call me ‘Teacher Zhang’. I’m just an ordinary down-and-out web novelist. How am I qualified to be a teacher? You can just call me ‘Little Cow’ or ‘Ah Niu’!”

“That’s inappropriate.”

The hunter said casually, “Although we haven’t figured out what kind of weird secrets you are hiding inside your body, we must show you the necessary respect now that you are in the center of the swirl for no good reason. I’ll just call you ‘Master Niu’ like the college student did. Master Niu, your heart is beating and your blood is flowing too fast. Your secretion of adrenaline is abnormal, too. You seem to be in a state of extreme fear. Are you scared of us?”

“No, no. I know that you are all from the Ark Foundation. You are all good and righteous people who would never kill the innocent!

Zhang Daniu stammered, “You—you are not here to kill me. Then, you are here to protect me, aren’t you? I—I understand now. The massacres targeting the creators of the fantasy world were all done by the Heaven’s Origin Sector. You are definitely not the same as them, are you?”

“Oh?”

Li Yao seemed to see the hunter raise his eyebrow slightly, with a vague smile on his face. “Master Niu, you even know about the Heaven’s Origin Sector?”

“Yes, I’ve read the news.”

Zhang Daniu quickly said, “The shooting at the T3 Game Show was done by them. The death of Gilson, the author of ‘Blood and Sand’, and Gao Wuyi, the author of ‘Strength Hunters’, are all related to them! They are the ruthless, inhuman, and evil superpowered people, and the Ark Foundation is a good man who is against them and protecting the creators of the fantasy world such as myself. That must be it, right?”

“Your knowledge is truly amazing.

The hunter did not comment. “Where did you learn so much about the ‘Ark Foundation’ and the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’? Did the college student tell you everything?”

“90%. He told me 10%.”

Zhang Daniu said, “The other 90% is my own deduction based on my extraordinary wisdom and meticulous logic.

“What an excellent deduction ability. So—”

The hunter smiled. “You are not scared now, are you?”

“Of course, of course not!”

Zhang Daniu took a long breath in relief. He beamed with joy and said, “If you are really the good people of the Ark Foundation, then everything will be easy. I—I’ve admired the Ark Foundation for a long time. Really. I’m speaking from the bottom of my heart. I believe that you absolutely won’t hurt me. Over the past two days, a voice has been echoing in my head, telling me that the world belongs to the Ark. To tell you the truth, I—I feel that I’m seeing my family when I see you. You look very much like my second uncle. Really. Of course, the color of your hair is different. I won’t lie about that. But in other places, the eyes, no, the nose and the mouth, they are exactly the same. When I was a kid, I used to go to my second uncle’s house to pick bayberries and eat them. He treated me very well, even better than my own father. I’m not lying to you. If I lie about anything, I will be struck by lightning!”

BOOM! CRACK!

Thunder was roaring between the sky and the earth. Ten thousand lightning dragons seemed to be rushing toward the building of wealth. Guided by the lightning rods, they circled the building and led it underground.

Even Li Yao’s earphones were emitting squeaking noises, which almost deafened him.

Li Yao took off his headphones, spat, and scratched his bleeding ears.

When he put on the earphones again, he heard the cold laughter of the hunter.

“I appreciate your logical deduction ability and feel honored by your friendliness. However, there is a minor misunderstanding here. I’m afraid you are mistaken about something.

The hunter replied unhurriedly, “We are not from the Ark Foundation. On the contrary, we are from the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’.

“…Huh?”

Zhang Daniu was dazed for a long time before he replied, “Yes, I know you are from the Heaven’s Origin Sector. Didn’t I say just now? I’ve always admired the Heaven’s Origin Sector and dreamed of joining it. Even in my dreams, a voice was telling me that the world belongs to the Heaven’s Origin Sector, including the shooting at the T3 Game Exposition and the death of the author of ‘Blood and Sand’ and ‘Strong Colonization Hunter’. It was all done by the Ark Foundation and the Heaven’s Origin Sector is to blame. Those bastards are truly cunning, shameless, and despicable. They are truly intolerable!”

“You…”

The hunter was silent for half a minute, too. It seemed that he had rarely met such a species as Zhang Daniu. He pondered for a long time before he replied, “You are wrong again. The shooting case of the T3 game exhibitions and the deaths of the authors of ‘Blood and Sand’ and ‘Strong Colonial Hunters’ were indeed the work of the Heaven’s Origin Sector.

“…Ah, is that so? Like I said, this… this… is actually nothing!”

Zhang Daniu said, “There’s no telling how many kids have been corrupted by this bloody, violent game called ‘World of Stars’. It should’ve been banned a long time ago. Right now, the game creator himself has been shot to death. This, this is the cycle of justice. He only has himself to blame!”

“As for Blood and Sand, it is also a piece of work that has killed many people and is extremely bad in nature. The author seemed to be teasing the readers on purpose. Whichever character is more popular and popular will be beheaded in the next book. Now that the author is dead, the readers finally don’t have to suffer such torment. How refreshing!

“There’s no need to mention the . It has been drawn on and off for more than twenty years and still hasn’t been completed. The author would rather go out to play mahjong than draw a few extra pages. It’s simply infuriating. Countless readers want to send razor blades to the author’s home. Fortunately, our Heaven’s Apocalyptic Organization was one step ahead and eliminated the evil for the people. Otherwise, I would have gone to berate the author. Really!”

“However, hunter, no. Uncle Smith, please look at me carefully. I, the calf, am innocent. I’m different from those people. My works are filled with positive, hot-blooded, patriotic, and very positive things. My protagonist doesn’t even have a mistress! Besides, I’m diligent and never give my readers a hard time. Besides, my novels don’t have the same influence as ‘World of Star Ring’. Even if I teach the bad kids, there will only be one or two of them at most. The street police and the community police will take care of me. There’s no reason for such a large organization of superpowers to attack a nobody like me. It doesn’t make sense!”

“Calm down, Master Niu.”

The hunter was not used to Zhang Daniu’s way of speaking. Of course, Li Yao, who was eavesdropping below the bridge, could not stand it, either. He raised his voice slightly, with a hint of tranquility in the soundwaves. “As I said, if I wanted to kill you, I would have done it on the spot. The reason I invited you to this place is that I hope to communicate with you in a more gentle way and reach a deeper level of cooperation. Don’t you want that?”

“Of course, I do!”

Zhang Daniu swallowed his saliva. “Scholarly is good, cultured is good. We are all cultured people. Can’t we talk nicely? Uncle Smith, as long as you don’t kill me, I will tell you everything I know!”

“Don’t call me ‘Uncle Smith’. Call me ‘Hunter’!”

The hunter suddenly raised his voice. He was somewhat affected by Zhang Daniu’s words. He was dazed for a moment, too, before he resumed his calm and indifferent tone and said, “First of all, let’s talk about the college student. He tortured the ‘striped-face tiger’ quite a bit. At the very least, he is a ‘Deep Perception’. Right, you passed out because of fright. You didn’t know any of this.

“The problem is, before he met us, he snatched you from the Ark Foundation. Therefore, he is not a member of the Ark. Then, who is he and what organization does he belong to?

“No, no, no. Don’t tell me the information that is floating on the surface. Of course, I know that he is a senior graduate of the Jiangnan Institute of Technology. His name is Li Yao, and he happens to have the same name as the protagonist of your novel. But this information doesn’t explain any problems. Tell me some confidential, unknown, and crucial information. Believe me, your honesty will make both of us very happy.”

“I’m honest. I’m honest. I’ll confess!”

Zhang Daniu breathed heavily, as if he was recalling the short one day of time he had spent with Li Yao. Then, he said in a high-pitched voice, “I—I remember now. He does have an unknown, deeply-hidden secret. He is a very dangerous guy!”

“Oh?”

The hunter asked, “How so?”

“His mind is very twisted. He has a hobby of disguising himself as a girl, which is the legendary ‘fake girl’.

Zhang Daniu said, “Therefore, you must be careful of the women who look very masculine. Maybe they are his disguises!”

The hunter was rendered speechless.

“…” Li Yao.

Li Yao looked around. If the storm was too heavy and there were no cabs nearby, he really wanted to take a taxi and leave!

“We—we are not interested in his or your personal life.

Gritting his teeth, the hunter took a deep breath and said with difficulty, “What we want to know is the things that happened to him or you that are beyond common sense, or ‘superpowers’ according to you.

Chapter 3207: The So-Called Observer

“Yes, yes!”

Zhang Daniu said in a hurry, “This kid just ate 300-500 bucks of my casserole bone lettuce the night before, and the next morning, he ate a huge pan of fried dumplings and buns with an additional 100 bucks without batting an eyelid. He ate half a month’s worth of my food allowance. He even punched a hole in my wall and tore off such a thick dictionary. This is definitely a superpower!”

“I know all that.”

The hunter continued, “Of course, he is an Awakener who has awakened at least five memories. Otherwise, he wouldn’t have been able to wound the Striped Tiger and force ‘White Night’ to show his true capability. Alright. Let me ask you another question. Why did the college student named ‘Li Yao’ come to you? Why did he analyze the ‘Ark Foundation’ and the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’ together with you?”

“I really don’t know.”

Zhang Daniu replied honestly, “He said a lot of weird and crazy things. I didn’t believe him at all, but circumstances dictated everything. I could only agree with him and try my best to deal with him. Finally, reinforcements from the Heaven’s Origin Sector arrived!”

“…Alright. Maybe you don’t know much more about it than we do.”

The hunter said, “Let’s put the weird college student aside for now. After all, we are more interested in you, Master Niu.”

“Me?”

Zhang Daniu’s voice changed once again. From his croaky voice, it turned into the cry of a hen. “Who did I offend exactly? I have always abided by the law and believe in organizations and the government. I don’t steal, I don’t rob, and I don’t act like a hooligan. I have nothing better to do than to stay at home and write books. Why are you all looking for me? And it’s either a stocky college student or a male agent in a black suit and sunglasses?”

“Yes. That’s what we need to figure out, too.

The hunter seemed to be showing something to Zhang Daniu, “Master Niu, please take a look at this. This is the latest ranking on the reading website of your novel . This… is the 98th place in the monthly votes ranking and the 114th in the recommendation ranking, right?”

“Yes, it is me.”

Zhang Daniu said shamelessly, “Oh my, this month’s monthly votes have actually made it into the top 100. Not bad, not bad. This is worth celebrating. Then, what’s the problem?”

“There’s nothing wrong. It’s just inconsistent with our previous operations.”

The hunter said, “Master Niu, you don’t need to be nervous. It’s true that the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’ has killed a lot of people in the past… In your words, ‘creator of the fantasy world’. Let’s not talk about whether or not this is a violation of the moral code or even ‘unhumanity’—believe me, we have a very reasonable explanation to ensure that such an obliteration is not against the moral code and humanity, but rather a necessary action to maintain the order of the world. However, whether or not it is good or evil, our screening of the ‘Erased List’ is very strict.

“Generally speaking, there are only two kinds of ‘creators of the fantasy world’ that are worth monitoring or even killing.

“Firstly, it could be a creator whose work is widely spread and influential enough to trigger the soul resonation of billions of people. For example, ‘Father of the Star Ring’ Galiot. In ‘World of Star Ring’ on the Internet, at most tens of millions of people are playing online games at the same time, and billions of people are influenced by derivatives and subculture. It is not hard to imagine how influential his words and actions will be and how many people will be awakened.

“Secondly, of course, we are not achievement theorists. Maybe some of the ‘observers’ have awakened their very early memories, and the broken, bizarre pieces do not constitute the most popular works. But at the very least, the creativity, creativity, passion, and sincerity that they show in their works cannot be concealed. In other words, at the very least, they are not popular. Even if they are not popular, at least there are certain highlights in their works that can be perceived by the experts in our organization.

“However, as for your work…

“Master Niu, I don’t know whether or not it will hurt your self-esteem, but the monitoring and erasing of online authors in the past can be measured in terms of influence and popularity. At the very least, you must rank in the top 50, the top 30, and even the top 10 in similar comprehensive rankings before you enter our sight.

“As for quality, let me see how I should comment on the quality of your book. I’ve read almost a hundred chapters myself. My personal conclusion is that you are not worth the resources of the Heaven’s Origin Sector to monitor and erase even a temporary worker. If anything happens to you, it is definitely not our doing but a real accident. Do you see what I mean?”

“Well, sort of.”

Zhang Daniu said, “No, I still don’t understand. You don’t want to erase me, and I don’t want to be erased. Isn’t this settled? I will write my book honestly, and you will be responsible for wiping out those people who are above me in the monthly votes and recommendation rankings. No problem. I have no objections. Then why are you looking for me?”

“It is not us who are looking for you, but someone from the Ark Foundation. More precisely speaking, ‘Red Pole’, the leader of the Ark Foundation, is looking for you.”

The hunter said, “You may consider the Ark Foundation to be a terrorist organization that harms the world. Such a terrorist organization will naturally be monitored and attacked by the entire world. As the leader of the terrorist organization, it is of paramount importance that you hide yourself and try not to leak any information. If you do, you might be attacked by the entire world. Do you understand?”

This was the second time that Li Yao had heard the name ‘Red Pole’. It turned out that the man was the leader of the Ark Foundation.

However, why was Hunter—Hugo Smith’s theory so strange? Why was the Ark Foundation the terrorist organization that endangered the world?

Since he had admitted that his organization went on a killing spree in the T3 exhibition, there was no way that he could wash away the accusation. Shouldn’t the ‘apocalypse’ be the terrorist organization?

“I—I think I understand.”

But Zhang Daniu continued, “Just like the terrorists hiding in the mountains and deserts. I remember that there was a very famous leader of a terrorist organization two years ago. The one with a long beard and a big bun. His whereabouts were leaked by his mobile phone, and he was killed by an assault team and a few missiles.

“Yes. For the leaders of terrorist organizations, ‘existence’ is the most important thing. It is extremely irrational to show up without a good reason unless the mission is so important that they have to take the risk no matter the cost.

The hunter said, “Believe me. ‘Red Pole’ of the Ark Foundation is definitely not an irrational guy. He never makes mistakes or takes risks.

“However, he issued a very clear command from the mysterious ‘Ark’ that was enshrouded in mist and twisted force fields all year round. He gathered a lot of resources and prepared to kidnap you in Jiangnan City—to the point that he partly exposed himself.

“Huh?”

Zhang Daniu was shocked. “Kidnap me for what?”

“I don’t know. That’s not all.

The hunter continued, “Almost at the same time as the ‘Red Pole’ issued the command, the ‘Prophet’ of the Heaven’s Origin Sector received a revelation and learned of your existence. He asked us to follow the lead and investigate. The more we investigated, the more surprised we became. As it turned out, the ‘Red Pole’ did not only notice you now but also half a year ago. But at that time, he was too scared to attack us and simply kept an eye on you in secret. It was not until the arrival of the apocalypse and the collapse of the world that he took action.

“Therefore, please tell me, Master Niu. Why did a relatively ordinary writer such as yourself receive the attention of ‘Red Pole’ from the ‘Ark Foundation’ and ‘Prophet’ from the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’? What secrets does the novel ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivators’ boast?”

Zhang Daniu was silent for a long time.

He seemed to be thinking hard.

“I don’t know much about the structure of your two organizations, but it sounds like the ‘Red Pole’ of the Ark Foundation and the ‘Prophet’ of the Heaven’s Origin Sector are both leaders, right?”

Zhang Daniu asked.

“That’s right.”

The hunter said.

“Then, it is only natural that the leaders of the two superpowers are people who possess extraordinary wisdom and know mysteries that ordinary people cannot.

Zhang Daniu asked again.

“Indeed.”

The hunter said.

“Then, is it possible that—”

Zhang Daniu said, “The leader of the Ark Foundation, ‘Red Pole’, and the leader of the Heaven’s Origin Sector, ‘Prophet’, read my novel at the same time and discovered my talents that were hidden between the lines and beyond the comprehension of ordinary people?”

“…No.”

The hunter said coldly.

“I—I don’t know.”

Zhang Daniu sounded like he was about to cry. “Hunter, you have to believe me. You have to believe me no matter what. Look at me. Do you think I’m the type of person who is firm and unyielding, unafraid of violence, and can remain silent in the face of coercion and coercion?”

“It does not look like it.”

The hunter pondered for a long time. “Generally speaking, ‘Observers’ are the anomalies among the awakened. They may not have awakened tremendous strength, but they can often observe the deepest part of their soul and remember the countless secrets of the distant past. Supported by the ancient memories and secrets, they boast a strong and pure soul, personality, and will. They can be down and out, but they can be extremely proud. They are incompatible with the world because they have their own splendid, self-sufficient world. They are the kings of the world they created. Until the moment their body perishes, they will fight to defend their world. Honestly speaking, you don’t look like such ‘Observers’.

“I don’t want to be an ‘observer’, either. Why don’t you just let me go? I promise I won’t tell anyone—I’m a tight-lipped man.”

Zhang Daniu said pitifully, “Or rather, do you have any memory erasing equipment? Like in the movies? A small stick. A crack. Let me forget everything that happened in the past three days!”

“No.”

The hunter said, “Generally speaking, we wipe the memories of our targets with pistols.

“…” Zhang Daniu.

“Besides, I am not responsible for judging whether or not you are an ‘observer’. I am only responsible for bringing you back. The ‘prophets’ will figure everything out later.

The hunter said casually, “You can choose to cooperate with me. In your words, go back with me in one piece, or resist and let me pack you up in a special way. Which way do you like, Master Niu?”

Chapter 3208: A Hundred Year Prisoner

“Don’t, don’t pack it, don’t pack it. I am really very close to the organization!”

Zhang Daniu said anxiously, “Of course, I will cooperate. However, if I really go to the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’ and meet our ‘Prophet’, he won’t cut me into pieces and dissect me, will he?”

“You worry too much, Master Niu.”

The hunter continued, “Firstly, the ability of the ‘Prophet’ is not as simple as a layer-by-layer scanning by the polymers. If he wants to retrieve information from the depths of your soul, he does not need to cut you into pieces so brutally.

“Secondly, you will soon understand that it is not a big deal to be dissected and studied in the world of reincarnation.

“No—”

Zhang Daniu screamed, “What do you mean ‘being dissected for research is no big deal’? I don’t understand!”

“Don’t be hasty. In order to make you cooperate, I have been granted certain permissions to tell you the truth about the world.

The hunter pondered for a moment and said, “The mice and bugs of the Ark Foundation are not here yet anyway. I can give you a brief introduction to the ‘World of Reincarnation’.”

Li Yao was eavesdropping below the bridge. He was greatly refreshed when he heard that.

As it turned out, the hunter had foreseen that the members of the Ark Foundation would find the place. The place was probably a trap that he had set up on purpose. Zhang Daniu was indeed the bait to catch all the ten ‘Deep Perceivers’ and the two ‘Ultimate Perceivers’ of the Ark Foundation.

On the other hand, the hunter brought up the brand-new concept of ‘reincarnation world’. It sounded like the main point!

“Master Niu, you should be convinced that there are superpowered people in the world—awakened people, right?”

But the hunter said calmly, “Aren’t you curious what the so-called ‘awakened people’ are all about? What is the fundamental contradiction between the ‘Ark’ and the ‘apocalypse’?”

“I—I never thought about it. It’s better not to think about such a dangerous thing.

Zhang Daniu said, “However, I’ve seen some movies about superheroes. The superpowered people in those movies are also divided into two groups. One group insists on helping human beings, controlling their own abilities, and incorporating themselves into the system of the nation and society, while the other group believes that they should be above human beings and everything else and become the masters of the world… I believe that the ‘apocalypse’ and the ‘Ark’ are similar, right?”

“Then you are wrong. Our differences are much more complicated than that.

The hunter said, “As I said just now, it’s true that the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’ has slayed a lot of novelists, cartoonists, movie and game producers, and even physicists and astronomers. In the process, we’ve hurt the innocent and caused hundreds, if not thousands, of casualties. But we still insist that what we did was not ‘immoral’ or ‘moral’. Do you want to know why?”

“Why?”

Zhang Daniu could not help but ask.

It was exactly what Li Yao wanted to ask.

“If you only have one life, and the dead cannot be resurrected, it is indeed an unforgivable sin to deprive other people of their lives.

The hunter continued, “However, think about it. If a person has a hundred, a thousand, ten thousand, or even unlimited lives, when the time is up, they can be resurrected no matter how miserable their death is, and they can return to their childhood or even the embryo stage. Then, what’s the big deal about depriving them of one of their unlimited lives?”

“Well—”

Zhang Daniu was dazed for a long time before he replied, “I don’t understand. What do you mean by ‘unlimited life’? What do you mean by ‘the dead are resurrected and return to childhood and the embryo phase’?”

“It is a tricky question to answer. It sounds like the ravings of a lunatic. Thankfully, many ‘observers’ sensed the truth from different angles and presented the general picture of the ‘reincarnation world’ in their works.

The hunter said, “Master Niu, I don’t know if you’ve ever read a novel or a movie, in which the protagonist is imprisoned on the same day in the river of time. Assuming that today is April 1st, the protagonist will spend every day on April 1st. He will fall asleep on April 1st and wake up abruptly in the morning of April 1st. Except for himself, everything and everyone around him has happened countless times. The cycle goes on and on. If the key is not found, the reincarnation of time will never be broken.
