He will live on April 1st forever.”

“Well, I’ve seen it before.”

Zhang Daniu said, “Such a ‘time loop’ is a relatively common theme in science fiction, isn’t it? I remember that there’s a famous science fiction writer in our country named ‘Teacher Liu Wenli’ who wrote a classic work of this type called ‘Prisoner of the Day’. Oh, that’s right. I’ve also seen a movie called ‘The Day of the Mole’. It has similar meanings. Oh, there are also some movies called ‘Borderline Tomorrow’, ‘Source Code’, ‘Horrible Cruise’, ‘Happy Death Day’, and so on. The details are somewhat different, but they are all about the time loop. The protagonist in the movie is like playing a super difficult game that is constantly reloading. If he’s not careful, he will fail. Thankfully, after every failure, he can ‘read the record’ and return to the beginning of time and start all over again.”

“Wait, what do you mean? Are you saying that the novels and movies are not made up but refracting the truth of our world to some extent?”

“It’s great that you understand that.

The hunter said unhurriedly, “Then, if I tell you that our world, the blue planet below our feet, and the ‘spirits of all living creatures’ living on the planet are like the protagonists in the works you mentioned just now, trapped in a certain part of the timeline and miserable prisoners in an endless prison of time, you will not be surprised, will you?”

“What?”

Zhang Daniu blurted out, “That’s impossible!”

Below the bridge, Li Yao was dumbfounded, too, and did not come back to himself for a long time.

However, the bouncing waves deep inside his brain unlocked more memory pieces and displayed the scenes of his previous life, the previous life, and even the previous life clearly in front of him.

The world was a maze of reincarnation and a cage of time.

They were all misfits in the labyrinth and prisoners on death row.

“I can understand your reaction, Master Niu. It is indeed a hundred times more difficult to accept that the lovely Earth below our feet is an infinite prison of time than to accept ‘ordinary’ superpowers such as fireballs and electric arcs. Believe it or not, I am merely carrying out the command of the ‘prophecy’ and telling you everything simply.”

The hunter said casually, “Of course, it is different from the time-reversal sci-fi novels and movies. The protagonists of the works are often trapped on the same day and can only go on a cycle for one to two months at best. But in our Earth, from the end of the twentieth century to the middle of the twenty-first century, between fifty to sixty years, sixty to seventy years, and no more than a hundred years, endless cycles are taking place. The specific duration depends on the performance of all the earthlings in the ‘reversal’.

“For the sake of ease of understanding, you can simply imagine that the ‘starting point of time’ in our world is 1980. All the initial conditions are inputted in 1980 and run according to certain logic and rules. After roughly fifty to a hundred years, a large number of logic traps, rule errors, and data overflow gradually. In the end, when the error of information exceeds the critical range, ‘bang’, the entire world will experience a ‘reboot’. Of course, it can also be called ‘reincarnation’. It will return to 1980 and repeat everything.”

Zhang Daniu was dazed.

Li Yao squatted below the bridge, too. He was so shocked that he couldn’t breathe for a long time.

“Although it sounds hard to believe, you should have noticed some traces, right? Did the college student named Li Yao not tell you about some strange dreams, or did he grasp some skill that is absolutely impossible to grasp in this life?”

The hunter continued, “Including the entire network, there are a lot of ‘awakened people’ who don’t know what is going on. They are talking about the weird things that have happened to them and the ‘bug’ of the world. Now that you and Li Yao are talking about the ‘Ark Foundation’, it’s impossible that you don’t know anything about the rumors, right?

“Why do people feel that they are extremely familiar when they come to a strange place? Why do people suddenly speak a foreign language that they have never learned before after a car accident? Why do people really remember that something has happened, but it has never happened in their ‘life’? Why do people have a sense of familiarity when they first learn a skill or course and become a ‘genius’ in a certain field?

“The answer to all of this is the cycle of time and endless reincarnation. Every one of us has been reincarnated a hundred times, a thousand times, ten thousand times in this endless prison. We have played various different roles in countless reincarnations. We have experienced a variety of colorful and varied lives. Of course, we have also learned different languages and skills. In most cases, when a ‘reboot’ or ‘reincarnation’ begins, we should forget everything that happened in our previous life. But there are always loopholes and mistakes. There will always be someone who will vaguely remember something from their previous life or even… something from a long, long time ago, something that was not very pleasant.”

“But… Huhu… I… I don’t know whether or not I should believe it. But let’s put the truth aside for now. Why?”

Zhang Daniu shouted, “Why is there such a thing as ‘infinite cycles of time’? What’s the point of repeating such nonsense and being trapped for decades or even centuries?

“In the ancient Greek legends, Sisyphus offended the gods. In order to punish him, the gods demanded that he push a giant rock to the top of the mountain. Since the rock was so heavy that it rolled down the mountain before it reached the top, all his previous endeavors were wasted. Therefore, he did the same thing over and over again.

The hunter asked back, “What’s the significance of Sisyphus’ giant rock?”

—

This chapter is dedicated to Master Liu Wenyang, a science fiction writer who died young. His work ‘One Day in Jail’ was one of the inspirations of the old bull, no, the young bull. I sincerely recommend it to everyone. His work and the ‘Science Fiction World’ of his era really brought endless fun and fantasy to the young bull in his youth. It’s a pity that the heavens are jealous of the talented!

How he wished that there was reincarnation in the world so that Master Liu could continue to show his talents and persist in his passion in a new, brilliant, and unbelievable world!

Chapter 3209: Jailers And Prison Breakers

“So, this is… a punishment?”

Zhang Daniu’s voice was shaking. “But what did we do wrong to deserve such punishment? We—all the earthlings didn’t do anything at all!”

“Just because we didn’t do anything wrong right now doesn’t mean we didn’t do anything wrong a long, long time ago.

The hunter continued coldly, “A long time ago, perhaps tens of thousands of times ago, before everything began, we—all the earthlings—made unforgivable mistakes. We were all unpardonable cold-blooded animals, bloody and brutal invaders, the captives of our own ambitions and greed. Driven by our endless desires, we caused irreparable damage to the entire universe and almost destroyed the world and ourselves.

“Therefore, like Sisyphus in the legends, we are locked in this cage of time forever. On this endlessly looping Earth, we push the giant stone in our heart over and over again to punish our sins in such a way, even though it is simply not enough to punish even a tiny fraction of our sins.

“Well…”

Zhang Daniu was clearly not convinced.

Li Yao’s lips twitched, too.

The argument that ‘we are born with original sins’ might be popular in the west, but for the Easterners who believed that ‘my life is up to me and not the heavens’, they were naturally against it.

“Besides, it is not just punishment.

The hunter continued, “Or redemption.”

“Redemption?”

Zhang Daniu said, “I don’t understand.”

“Think about the novels and movies you’ve read about the ‘time loop’. Do you find any similarities between their exposition of the theme and their depiction of the protagonist’s escape from trouble?”

The hunter continued, “Before we enter the time loop, the protagonist is often someone who has serious psychological defects and suffered setbacks and misfortunes. He may blame the universe, complain about his life, and feel frustrated about everything about himself. He may even hate everyone around him and cannot get away from the swirl and abyss.

“One day, he fell into the cycle of time and encountered the infinitely repetitive ‘April 1st’. Time and time again, his head was bleeding in front of the wall of time. No matter how much he cried and shouted to end everything, he could not.

“As a result, the protagonist slowly learns to examine himself in what seems to be a boring and repetitive life. He discovers the flaws in his personality, finds a way to cleanse his soul, learns to get along with others in harmony, and finally discovers the true meaning of life. Only in this way can the time cycle end, and only then can the protagonist jump out of ‘April 1st’.

“Master Niu, think about it carefully. Do most of the time-reversal works follow the same pattern?”

Zhang Daniu thought for a moment and said, “Sounds about right.”

“Of course it is, because it is the purpose of the reincarnation world. Not only is it a punishment for us, it is also the ultimate salvation for us.

The hunter continued, “In the past, our hands were stained with blood, and our souls were corrupted by darkness and evil. Our crimes were unforgivable. Regular methods were not enough to help us correct our mistakes and restore us to normal. That’s why we were thrown into this special ‘World of Reincarnation’ and Time Prison. After every cycle in this place, the sins on our bodies will be washed away. After ten or a hundred cycles, some people will be cleansed of their evilness and greed. After ten thousand cycles, even the most stubborn criminals will be cleansed of their evilness and march toward a brighter future.

“In the end, when all the earthlings wash away the sins of the past, forget the evilness of the past, and be completely redeemed, the endless cycle of the World of Reincarnation will come to an end. We will jump out of the ‘April 1st’ like the protagonists in the science fiction novels and welcome a brilliant new life!

“Wow, it sounds quite… incredible. I need time to think about it carefully. The shock is too great.”

Zhang Dapao said, “I’m sorry. I—I didn’t know anything about it last night. This morning, I learned that human beings can eat hundreds of buns in one meal. Then, you told me that I used to be an unpardonable invader who harmed the universe and was being modified in a time prison of infinite reincarnation? Wow. This is too thrilling. My head is a mess right now. I need to digest it and sort out my thoughts.”

The eavesdropper was silent for a long time. Li Yao could imagine the dumbfounded look on Zhang Daniu’s face.

Because that was what he looked like right now.

After waiting for three minutes, Zhang Daniu continued, “However, I have a question. How did the Heaven’s Origin Sector learn of such a confidential and mysterious thing? Let’s say, let’s say, that the Earth we live on is really an infinite prison. Who is the one who locked us here and controlled… the functioning of the reincarnation world?”

“This question is very complicated. It is difficult to explain in detail before you are awakened.

“To put it simply,” the hunter said, “we have been granted certain permissions to unlock part of our memories from countless reincarnations and even before. As a result, we have grasped tremendous superpowers and learned part of the truth.

“After you pass the test of the ‘prophecy’ and prove that there are no problems, you might be granted the corresponding permissions to unlock more memories and abilities. You will understand everything then.

“Wait—”

Zhang Daniu suddenly became smart. “You have been granted certain privileges? By whom? The master of the World of Reincarnation, the ‘Warden’ who locked us in this prison? What is that exactly?”

“A name is just a code name. The great, sacred, bright, and unique existence has countless faces, countless techniques, and countless names.

The hunter said, “You can call it ‘Will of Earth’ if you want, but it is not the ‘Warden’ of the infinite-cycle prison of time. More precisely speaking, it is the prison itself.”

“…”

Zhang Daniu and Li Yao, who were hundreds of meters away, both fell into a shocked silence.

“This—this topic is too big. ‘Will of the Earth’. I—I feel that my brain is not functioning well enough. I can’t breathe.”

Zhang Daniu said, “Let’s talk about something more specific and subtle. Even if I believe everything you said, why did you erase so many ‘observers—the creators of the fantasy world’?”

“Because they were not the real creators of the fantasy world. None of the worlds they created were groundless or imaginary.

The hunter said, “As I said just now, the observers are all talented guys. They can observe their appearance hundreds of times, thousands of times, or even thousands of times, before they are reincarnated. Their sinfulness, bloodthirst, and brutality are reflected in their works.

“Such a work is very likely to resonate with part of the earthlings, who are the ‘prisoners’. The evil that the prisoners suppressed and forgot with great difficulty will be revived, and the redemption of thousands of reincarnations will be wasted.

“Maybe, the observers are too muddle-headed to understand the immense and dangerous power contained in their works. It is unfair for them to simply erase their work. But in order to prevent the sins of the past from happening again, we cannot take any risks. After all, after they are erased in this life, they can still be resurrected in the next life. After three to five obliterations, maybe they will forget their past and stop being ‘observers’.

“It sounds like you are carrying out the command of the ‘will of the earth’ and helping it maintain the order of the ‘World of Reincarnation, Prison of Time’. If it is a ‘Warden’, you are probably the ‘prisoners’.

Zhang Daniu said, “Is my understanding correct?”

“You can think of it that way.

The hunter continued, “The ‘prophets’ will send us the command of the ‘will of the earth’, which is the sacred ‘apocalypse’. With the blessing of the ‘apocalypse’, we will be granted privileges beyond that of ordinary prisoners, and certain memories and abilities will be awakened. We can even bring the memories and abilities to the next reincarnation and become special ‘prisoners’.

“What about the ‘Ark Foundation’?”

Zhang Daniu said, “You are the ‘model prisoners’ and ‘prisoners’. What are they?”

“The most dangerous, brutal, and stubborn prisoners, of course.

The hunter sighed and said, “After countless reincarnations, most of the prisoners have forgotten the sins of the past and come to realize their mistakes. They will repent and correct their mistakes honestly. In your words, they will turn over a new leaf and believe that, as long as they persist, we will be redeemed in the end and be reaccepted by the ‘will of the earth’.

“However, there is always a small group of irredeemable fanatics in any prison. These lawless fellows have absolutely no remorse for their past sins, nor are they prepared to abide by all the rules and systems in the prison. They have never thought of accepting the modifications dutifully until the day they are ‘released from prison’. Instead, they are planning to use their own methods to break out of the prison, or even destroy the entire prison and the sacred Earth!”

Destroy the Earth!

Destroy the Earth!

Destroy the Earth!

When Li Yao heard this part, he suddenly felt that there was a “buzzing” noise in the depths of his brain. It was as if burning little bugs were crawling here and there, causing his head to have a splitting headache. He could not help but moan in the storm.

“Who am I?

“What am I doing here?

“Beyond reincarnation and destroy the earth! What—what does it mean exactly? Is this the truth of the Vulture Plan? Am I one of the prisoners, the most defiant, the most stubborn, and the craziest?”

Chapter 3210: Fierce Battle In The Rain!

Countless bloody streaks burst out of Li Yao’s eyes, each of which looked like a crack in the world in front of him. He could see the picture of his ‘previous life’ through the crack and even his real soul before countless reincarnations.

The ‘cultivation force’ hidden in the depths of his soul was burning and expanding, too.

Zhang Daniu’s screams echoed inside the bug. He seemed to be deeply shocked by the hunter’s words again.

“That’s all I can say for now.

The hunter asked, “Any questions?”

In fact, there were a lot of questions. The theories such as ‘model prisoners’, ‘prisoners’ and ‘dangerous prisoners’ could explain a lot of things, but they raised even more questions.

At the very least, Li Yao immediately thought of something. If the world he was living in was truly a prison of infinite reincarnation, and the so-called ‘will of the earth’ was the master behind the scenes, why couldn’t the Heaven’s Origin Sector, which was backed by the ‘will of the earth’, do anything to the tiny Ark Foundation?

It was quite odd, wasn’t it? Since the entire Earth was a prison that could do such an unbelievable thing as a ‘time loop’, why couldn’t it lock onto all the members of the Ark Foundation, including the mysterious leader ‘Red Pole’, and kill him directly?

Moreover, the existence of the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’ was quite weird, too. If the ‘will of the earth’ mentioned by the hunters was really so awesome, why did they establish such a shady organization to compete with the Ark Foundation and manipulate the governments of various countries directly? Wasn’t it much simpler and faster?

But right now, not only had the Ark Foundation not been destroyed, it had even established mental clinics, mental hospitals, and charity organizations all over the world. It seemed to be even more open and aboveboard than the Heaven’s Origin Sector.

Were there such ‘prisoners’ and ‘prison breakers’?

Therefore, there must be something wrong.

Or, what the hunter said was not entirely true. Even if the so-called ‘will of the earth’ did exist, it was not strong enough to control every corner of the planet, every person, and every piece of information. It had its own restraints and weaknesses and was not invincible.

Either the Ark Foundation was not a simple ‘prison breaker’, or the ‘reincarnation world’ and ‘time prison’ did not exist for the simple purpose of ‘punishment and redemption’. Other than the two obvious reasons, it had a deeper purpose.

“Then what is it exactly… I think… I think I have found the answer before… but I have lost it!”

Li Yao’s head started aching again.

It was as if a new life was being bred and nurtured between his brain cells.

Of course, Zhang Daniu might not have Li Yao’s computational ability to think of such questions instantly. Even if he did, he did not have the courage to ask the hunter.

“No. No. No problem at all!”

As he expected, Zhang Daniu said.

“Very good.”

The hunter said, “Then, if you have to choose between the ‘apocalypse’ or the ‘arky’, I assume that you are sincerely seeking the embrace of the ‘apocalypse’?”

“Of course. The ‘Apocalyptic Organization’ is backed by the entire Earth. Wow. It is even more glorious than the United Nations. Of course, I’m going to choose the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’. Please don’t worry. I’ve always been an honest man. I will obey the organization and transform myself into a ‘model prisoner’!”

Zhang Daniu swallowed and changed the topic. “But, but, just like that. Isn’t there any…”

“What?”

The hunter asked, “What’s with the finger-twisting gesture?”

“I mean, benefits!”

Zhang Daniu said, “It’s true that the ‘Apocalyptic Group’ is strong and promising, but the ‘Ark Foundation’ is a bunch of insane lunatics who are willing to do anything. It seems a little dangerous to be enemies with such lunatics. Is it not beneficial for me to be loyal to the organization?”

“What benefits do you want?”

The hunter asked.

“In fact, it’s nothing. I’ve dedicated myself to writing. My requirements for life are very simple. I just need two things—money and beautiful women.”

Zhang Daniu quickly replied, “Of course, if the organization is in trouble, I can just give you money. I will solve the problem of the beauty myself.”

“…”

“That’s not a problem,” the hunter replied. “Even if we don’t resort to methods beyond the law and ethics, the memories of our previous lives alone are enough for our organization to accumulate enough wealth to rival a nation—as long as you are alive to spend it.”

“Then there won’t be a problem!”

Zhang Daniu was so happy that his voice changed, “Then let’s hurry up and leave. The earlier we return to the headquarters of the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’, the safer we will be. It won’t be good if the crazy prison breakers of the ‘Ark Foundation’ catch up to us!”

“Don’t worry. Just wait here.”

The hunter sneered, “They are already here!”

Li Yao was greatly alarmed. He looked at the time and found that it was indeed midnight.

It was raining cats and dogs. There was nothing but darkness and chaos between the sky and the earth. The sound of the rain was like the assault of a waterfall. As far as one’s eyes could see, except for the few skyscrapers in the central business district that were illuminated by ghost lights, the cities further ahead were drowned in the black rainwater, making them look like abandoned, collapsed graveyards.

The dim lights below the bridge reflected a small world that was less than a hundred meters in diameter. It was like a deformed space that had been sealed. Whatever happened in this place would not be noticed by the outside world.

It was truly the most suitable place and day for the battle of the awakened.

Li Yao immediately extinguished all the brightness around him and hid himself in the cold rain, trying to suppress his breath and heartbeat.

But every hair on his body was standing up like antennas while he scanned the space around him vigilantly.

Hiu! Hiu Hiu Hiu Hiu!

In the pouring rain, amid the deafening explosions of the waterfall, a few vague sounds of air being torn apart echoed. Then, a few shadows as thin as gossamer pierced into the building at a speed that even Li Yao found hard to distinguish.

Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi!

Intense noises were echoing inside the detectaphone. The connection between Li Yao and Zhang Daniu was cut off instantly. It seemed that some kind of weird force had locked the building of wealth, preventing any signals or ripples inside from leaking out.

Then, there were three minutes of silence. Even the few shining windows of the building dimmed down one by one.

In the meantime, a certain weird, intense, and oppressive atmosphere was growing in the darkness, as if two opposing forces were competing with each other. They were twisting and tearing the entire skyscraper, making the building more than forty floors high shake violently and about to collapse.

Finally—

BOOM!

A dull explosion echoed inside the building. Somebody had made a move.

The explosion seemed to be the prelude to a fierce battle. In only one second, Li Yao sensed that the building of wealth had turned into the center of an energy swirl. A volcano and a tornado seemed to be exploding inside the building at the same time. The glass walls that surrounded the building at first all exploded. Then, a lot of fireballs and electric arcs darted out of the empty windows. The flames and the lightning were like living animals that were crawling up, licking, and swallowing the windows!

Li Yao even saw someone—he was not sure whether or not the other party was still a standard human being, because the guy had grown a pair of huge wings on his back. He jumped out of the window and flew in the air, but the torrential rain drenched his wings, making it difficult for him to move and turn. Soon, he was hit by a few bullets that were surging with runes. Then, something terrible happened. The moment the bullets hit him, a few bolts of lightning surged out of the dense cloud and hit him heavily, directly burning the winged birdman into charcoal!

The shocking scene made Li Yao secretly click his tongue.

However, he did not hesitate or retreat. He dashed into the building like an arrow released from a bow.

…Although Zhang Daniu was a guy who had no backbone or courage, had no sense of justice when it came to profit, and cherished his body because of lust, he was the only clue for Li Yao to improve his cultivation and find the answer. Li Yao was still counting on him to write more chapters of . It was better to rescue this guy first!

As for the competition between ‘Apocalyptic’ and ‘Ark’, to be honest, Li Yao could not tell which of them was good and which was bad right now. Maybe it was quite boring to judge the two organizations based on the concepts of ‘good and bad’.

Li Yao was naturally biased toward the ‘Ark Foundation’.

It was not because of anything else, but because he hated the phrase ‘innate sin, a mistake from long, long ago’.

Other than the vast starry sky above our heads and the moral code in our hearts, there is nothing that can judge us—that is what Li Yao believed.

Even if the ‘will of the earth’ did exist, wasn’t ‘conquering the earth’ what all creatures should do?

But such feelings were not enough for him to choose the ‘Ark Foundation’ immediately.

Because the mystery lingering over the head of the Ark Foundation had not been solved yet, how could the organization survive the suppression of the ‘will of the earth’ and still operate openly? Were they the real ‘prisoners’ or were there something else wrong?

Li Yao decided to observe for a while after he rescued Zhang Daniu.

While thinking, Li Yao crawled into the underground garage of the Fortune Plaza.

The underground garage used to have very efficient drainage facilities, but it seemed to have been sabotaged by the intruders. After only a few hours of heavy rain, the water here was already above the knee. The lighting system had malfunctioned, too, plunging the place into mysterious darkness.

Li Yao quickly scanned his surroundings and drew a 3D map of the battlefield with the blueprint of the Building of Wealth.

“It seems to be faster to take the escape stairs. After all, the ‘Apocalypse’ and the ‘Ark’ have been engaged in a fierce battle. It is unlikely that they have deployed heavy troops on the stairs.

Li Yao mumbled to himself, “But the ventilation tubes nearby look very tempting, too. I feel like crawling. What should I do?”

Chapter 3211: Elevator Battle

After pondering for a long time, he decided to restrain the impulse in his heart for the time being. The main reason was that climbing the ventilation tube from here was really a waste of time. According to the display of the tracker, Zhang Daniu should have been locked in a certain room between the 25th floor and the 27th floor. There was no need for him to climb more than 20 floors through the ventilation tube, right? How long would he have to climb for?

Therefore, Li Yao planned to take the stairs to the twentieth floor and see how it went.

He crouched and sprinted on the stairs like a weightless shadow.

BOOM! BOOM BOOM BOOM BOOM!

Inside the building, explosions were breaking out one after another, mixed with high-pitched screams that sounded like the song of a banshee. There were even sounds of bones being crushed. Li Yao felt that his blood was freezing, and a hot stream was flowing out of his heart.

He stuck his palm to the wall. He could clearly feel the shaking of the wall and the waves of energy spreading out along the rebar and concrete. They were crashing and bursting like countless destructive swirls not far away from him.

Li Yao had no time to interfere with the battle between the apocalypse and the ark. He could only lower his head and rush forward.

At the corner of the seventh floor to the eighth floor, he encountered his first enemy.

“Mooooo!”

A weird shriek that could make one’s teeth ache and legs go weak rang out again. A woman with grayish-white skin emerged from the shadows. She held a heavy and sharp fire axe in her hand as she ruthlessly slashed towards Li Yao.

She had been crouching in a corner, like a hunter who was lurking on purpose. Had Li Yao not heard her feeble breathing and heartbeat in advance, he might’ve been hit by her.

But right now, Li Yao was certainly not going to be fooled. Before his opponent’s fire axe reached the highest point, he had already kicked his opponent into a corner.

After making sure that the other party had already tossed the fire axe to the side, Li Yao finally had the time to carefully observe.

It was a girl who could be considered pretty. Judging from her clothes, she should be an office girl from the Fortune Plaza. She did not look like a professional bodyguard or an assassin. Naturally, she did not look like an Awakener.

Her skin seemed to be covered in a layer of gray scales, and a beast-like glow blossomed in the depths of her eyes. Her expression was vicious and malevolent, and her temperament was completely not that of a white-collar worker in the metropolis. She was somewhat similar to… Li Yao’s roommate, Zhao Kai. Of course, it was Zhao Kai when he went on a rampage.

Li Yao glanced at the girl’s ear and found a Bluetooth earpiece on her left ear. He sighed and shot a steel nail out of his fingers. Before the girl recovered from the shock of her internal organs, he blew the earpiece into pieces. The girl was dazed as if she had been electrocuted. She vomited a mouthful of black blood and collapsed.

It appeared that she was not an Awakener of the Heaven’s Origin Sector. Like Zhao Kai, she had been remotely controlled by someone in the Heaven’s Origin Sector through the phone and awakened part of the brutal memories of her previous life. She had been turned into a puppet and stationed here as a guardian on purpose.

In the office building of the central business district, many people were workaholics who worked 9 to 10 hours a day. Now that they had encountered such a rarely-seen rainstorm, the underground garage had been flooded, and the public transportation had been halted. Countless office workers were trapped in the building, unable to leave. Right now, the ‘apocalypse’ was engaged in a fierce battle with the ‘Ark’ Awakener. The innocent people were involved, too. Other than the woman in front of them, there were bound to be more victims.

Although the hunter had said that the people in the ‘World of Reincarnation’ all had unlimited lives and that the life and death of a certain life were irrelevant and could be erased easily, Li Yao did not think so.

If possible, he would still try his best to help everyone he could and save everything that was worth saving!

He checked the woman’s breathing. Although it was weak, it was stable. She must’ve lost all her strength. Except for the bruises on her chest and her back, there was nothing wrong with her. Li Yao was relieved and continued walking.

Knowing that the apocalyptic organization might have deployed guards on the escape stairs, Li Yao was even more cautious in his actions. He had to listen to the sounds above him on every floor. If he found weak breathing and heartbeat, he would rather find a different route than to get into a conflict with the puppets controlled by the mobile phone. These people were as innocent as Zhao Kai. Besides, Li Yao was not sure whether or not he would attract the attention of the experts of the apocalyptic organization by slaughtering his way up. The experts did not even need to confront him directly. As long as he could shift Zhang Daniu’s position in time, it would be a headache for him.

Thankfully, there were not many awakened Cultivators in the Heaven’s Origin Sector and the Ark Foundation.

In the morgue, the ‘Grey Mist Lady’ said that the Ark Foundation would send ‘ten Deep Sleepers’ and two ‘Ultimate Sleepers’ to reinforce Jiang Nan City. They were all high-end warriors who had come from other places. Although there was no telling how many primary-level warriors there were in the area, there were unlikely to be more than a hundred of them.

In order to ensure the confidentiality of the traps, the Heaven’s Origin Sector wouldn’t send five hundred people to set up an ambush.

Since the competition between two to three hundred people was focused on each other, it was quite easy for Li Yao, who was an expert at sneaking around, to dodge.

Although he chose to stay out of the conflict and did not witness the fierce confrontation between the two parties, he had passed by quite a few broken and messy floors.

The shocking scene told of the horror a moment ago.

The 17th floor of the Wealth Building seemed to have experienced a terrifying explosion. The cubicles and the walls were all gone. Black scorch marks were everywhere. What was odd was that the seemingly fierce fire did not spread to more than the 18th floor and was confined to the narrow space.

On the nineteenth floor, although the booths and pathways were still there, messy, dense vines were everywhere, as if they had crawled into a primitive forest. One could even hear the sounds of the vines growing and wriggling.

When he reached the 21st floor, something even weirder happened. After Li Yao made sure that there was no one inside and opened the door of the escape channel, he discovered that the entire floor was filled with cold seawater. He could vaguely see terrifying sharks in the seawater, but the seawater was sealed by an invisible barrier. It only surged on the 21st floor and did not leak out at all, as if it were a crazy aquarium.

“Is this the power of the awakened?”

Li Yao was baffled. “The water is bitter and salty. It is indeed seawater and not rainwater or water from the fire tubes. How did they ‘transfer’ so much seawater to this place and seal it?”

There was no time to think.

The energy waves above were getting more and more intense. The sounds of fighting were echoing nonstop, too. It seemed that most of the awakened Cultivators had gathered above the 25th floor for the final attack and defense.

Li Yao finally had the opportunity to awaken the deepest and most familiar memories.

While he was walking up the stairs, he found several emergency boxes and took out the water belts inside.

He pulled out 3-5 fire hose belts in a row. They were probably over a hundred meters long from front to back and weighed over a hundred pounds. In any case, right now, he possessed an inexhaustible amount of monstrous strength. These weights were nothing.

Li Yao kicked open the protective door of an elevator. Inside the elevator was a dark and empty shaft. Carrying the fire hose, Li Yao climbed up along the iron rope of the elevator until he reached the 24th floor and found the ventilation tube nearby.

Li Yao slithered silently in the ventilation tube.

He was like a happy zebra that was happily playing on the savanna of Africa.

Very soon, he sensed two powerful waves of energy. They should be the ‘Gray Mist Lady’ and the ‘Hunter’, the commanders of the battle of Jiangnan City.

Li Yao was of a mind to observe the competition between the two powerful awakeners, but on second thought, since he had just awakened not long ago and could not control his overflowing strength very well, he decided not to rush out and ask for trouble.

Li Yao avoided the two commanders and continued climbing his ventilation tube carefully.

How should he put it? Although the ventilation tube was zigzagging, narrow, and dark, Li Yao felt that he was like a fish in water when he was passing through it. The only drawback was that the design of the ventilation tube was too unprofessional, and it was not guarded at all. There was no difficulty at all when he was sneaking in.

Li Yao had planned to go to the room where Zhang Daniu was locked.

But before he reached the 27th floor, according to the tracker, Zhang Daniu started moving in his direction.

Li Yao was dazed for a moment. Then he heard two bangs coming from the top of the elevator shaft, which indicated that somebody had sabotaged the door of the elevator.

It appeared that great minds think alike. Li Yao had the same thought as the members of the Heaven’s Origin Sector.

Everyone wanted to go up and down through the elevator shaft as quickly as possible. However, Li Yao had invaded the 27th floor through the elevator shaft, and the members of the Heaven’s Origin Sector must’ve been unable to withstand the pressure of the Ark Foundation or prepared to move Zhang Daniu to somewhere else through the elevator shaft just in case.

With such a heavy thunderstorm, the entire building’s water supply, power supply, fire control, and elevator system had all been shut down. However, the elevator shaft itself was impossible to disappear. With the abilities of the Awakened, whether or not there was an elevator didn’t make much of a difference.

Sure enough, Li Yao pricked up his ears and heard Zhang Daniu’s screams coming from above.

“Since you’ve delivered the goods to my doorstep, I won’t hold back.”

Li Yao grinned. He went back the way he came and returned to the elevator shaft through the ventilation tube. Then, he noticed that sparks were popping up in the darkness above his head. It was probably because the members of the Heaven’s Origin Sector were rubbing the steel rope with tools when they were sliding down the elevator.

Including Zhang Daniu, there were four of them.

Two well-trained muscular men were wearing black sportswear, tactical vests, night vision goggles, and gas masks. Their waists were bulging, indicating that they were armed with guns or knives.

The third muscular man, on the other hand, grabbed Zhang Daniu with his legs and fastened two iron rings to the rope. He descended quickly under the protection of his two companions.

Chapter 3212: Escape

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao threw the fire hose that was connected to the ejector at the top of the rope. After a few bangs, it was refracted and bounced on the walls of the narrow elevator shaft before it finally tied up a section of the rope above.

In the meantime, almost ten iron nails darted out like lightning and hit the bottom of the rope precisely. Sparks were spluttering, and half of the rope was cut off.

The four people hanging below the rope immediately started shaking violently. Zhang Daniu even screamed like a pig being butchered.

Taking the opportunity, Li Yao tied the remaining fire hose to his waist and jumped down, landing on the head of the first muscular man like a ferocious vulture.

The muscular man was also an elite expert among the awakened. He did not panic at all when he was ambushed. Instead, he bellowed and punched upward. While his fist was still on the way, he suddenly opened his hands and clenched his fists into claws. His arms and palms seemed to have gone through abnormal mutations. His arms were covered in green scales, and his sleeves were bulging because of the expansion of his muscles. His fingers were like five sharp claws that tore apart his gloves and snatched at Li Yao.

Five iron claws swept out five streaks of silver brightness in the darkness. The sound of rushing wind filled the entire elevator shaft.

Li Yao’s pupils constricted violently. He had analyzed the subsequent routes and changes of the iron claw clearly. He crouched and dodged the slash of the iron claw by a hair’s breadth. When the two of them passed by each other, Li Yao spat out an iron nail from his mouth, which pierced into the eyeball of the Cultivator and blew it up.

The iron-clawed muscular man screamed miserably. Li Yao took the opportunity to grab his neck and pull him down into the depths of the elevator shaft with a dull ‘boom’. There was no telling whether he was dead or alive.

In the meantime, Li Yao pulled out a pistol from his bulging waist.

Weirdly enough, Li Yao had never touched a real gun before. At most, he had played with toy models. But now that the cold metal was in his hands, he seemed to have become a living creature with temperature and intelligence. He grasped the center of gravity and the axis of the pistol in the blink of an eye, and he knew every component of the gun like the back of his hand. Opening the safety, Li Yao shot all nine bullets into the darkness below without aiming at all.

His movements seemed to be random, if not random. He was not aiming at the two awakened Cultivators below him at all, but at the walls of the elevator shaft.

However, when the flying bullets bounced and bounced back from the wall, they formed a fatal trap for the bottommost awakened one and turned into venomous fangs that could barely be blocked or dodged from nine weird angles.

The man must’ve been an expert in guns. The moment Li Yao showed up, he had tied up the rope with his legs and held a pistol in each of his hands, ready for the incoming threats.

His helmet and vest could resist most of the threats from above and from the front, too.

However, he didn’t expect that Li Yao wouldn’t play by the rules at all. After a series of messy bouncing, the bullets actually came from his side and below. Even if he dodged left and right, continuously dodging and blocking the first six bullets, three bullets still hit his feet, thighs, and waist respectively, making him unable to clamp the steel rope. He similarly fell down the elevator shaft, disappearing in the ear-splitting explosions.

Then, there was only the last Awakener, who seemed to be the most awesome one.

In the darkness, the blue eyes of the awakened Cultivator were glittering. While he was mumbling to himself, the sound of wind suddenly echoed inside the narrow shaft of the elevator. The wind suddenly turned sharp, as if it had been compressed into invisible wind blades by a mysterious force.

The mumbling of the Awakened turned into fatal whistles.

Li Yao immediately sensed that invisible wind blades were coming at him from various angles like transparent vipers.

Shua!

In the blink of an eye, his cheek was cut by the wind blade, and hot blood flowed out.

In such a narrow and dark combat environment, invisible wind blades were indeed the best weapons. No wonder the ‘hunter’ had asked the Awakened to escort Zhang Daniu.

Unfortunately, their opponent was Li Yao.

Li Yao grinned. Dark red flames were flowing on the edge of his palm. He suddenly slashed back and did something unexpected.

He cut off the steel rope hanging in midair.

This steel rope supported the entire weight of Li Yao, Zhang Daniu, and the other Awakener. Now that the steel rope had been cut off, the three were naturally controlled by gravity and fell down at the same time.

However, the awakener was caught off guard and had to suppress Zhang Daniu, who was screaming and struggling like a pig being butchered. There was no way that he could control his body precisely. For a moment, both the whistle and the wind blade were half a beat slower.

Li Yao, on the other hand, had been prepared for this. The moment before the rope broke, he had already moved away and stomped on the wall of the elevator shaft. He turned into a roaring cannonball and dashed below the awakened one and Zhang Daniu, narrowly dodging the most brutal wind blades.

Hiu! Hiu!

Li Yao opened his mouth and spat out two iron nails from his tongue, which pierced into the eyes of the awakener.

The awakened one had seen how his first companion was hit. Naturally, he attached great importance to Li Yao’s iron nails and his own eyes. He reacted immediately and tilted his head back. Two iron nails brushed past his eyelids.

But as it turned out, Li Yao was merely feinting. His target had been his throat since the very beginning. He raised his head and exposed his throat, which was exactly what Li Yao had been hoping for. He then slashed the throat of the awakened Cultivator, almost cutting his throat into pieces.

Hiss! Hiss! Hiss! Hiss! Hiss!

The Awakened clutched his throat and hissed like a leaking ball. How could he blow the whistle?

Li Yao guessed that he had to whistle in order to control the wind blades. Since his throat was damaged, he could not make any sound. Therefore, the threat of the wind blades was reduced by 90%. Li Yao followed up with a kick to the chest of the awakened one, sealing his only sound in the depths of his throat and accelerating his fall while he shot out another fire hose and tied up Zhang Daniu’s feet.

BAM!

At this moment, the fire hose on Li Yao’s waist was extended to the end of its length.

There was no knot between the rope and the water belt. The friction of the rope alone was not enough to support the weight of Li Yao and Zhang Daniu. It merely slowed their fall.

But for a monster such as Li Yao, such a cushion was enough for him to control his body and drag Zhang Daniu, who was still screaming and twisting, down. By jumping continuously, he was able to reduce the falling force of the two of them. Just like that, he fell down almost seventy meters and slowly landed below the elevator shaft.

BAM!

Arriving at the first floor, Li Yao kicked open the elevator door from inside and dragged out Zhang Daniu, who had shouted until his throat was hoarse and was struggling to the point of exhaustion.

Huchi! Huchi! Huchi! Huchi!

Zhang Daniu’s face was pale, his body was shivering, and his legs were as soft as springs. He couldn’t stand up no matter how hard he tried. It was obvious that the fierce battle in the elevator shaft had given him quite a scare. He stared at Li Yao for half a minute before his face gradually turned red, and hope was shining in his eyes.

“Li—Li Yao!”

Judging from his face, he was about to hug Li Yao and burst into tears. Zhang Daniu said in a trembling voice, “Thank the heavens, you’re finally here! You don’t even know how those bad guys from the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’ dealt with me—they threatened to cut me into pieces and dissect me. They are not human beings. They are simply a bunch of devils!

“However, I am an iron-willed man. I will not yield just like that! ‘One cannot be corrupted by riches and honor. One cannot be defeated by might.’ This is my motto in life. It is my principle. My principle! You don’t need to worry. No matter how much they tortured me, tortured me, threatened me, and even sent two beautiful female agents to tempt me, I never told them your secret! Because I firmly believe that my good brother Li Yao will definitely risk his life to save me! You have indeed come. Good brother, you are truly loyal!”

“Master Niu…”

Li Yao was silent for a moment. He wiped the blood off his face and said, “Don’t forget that we have a listening device. I heard your conversation with the ‘hunter’.”

“Is that so?”

Zhang Daniu blinked his eyes quickly. With a natural expression, he said frankly, “Then you must’ve discovered how I pretended to play nice with him with my superb acting skills and bought you more time. I even managed to elicit important information from him, didn’t I?”

“Stop it, Teacher Niu. Stop it.”

Li Yao took a long, long breath. “Before I change my mind, please leave me alone and let’s get out of here.”

Li Yao was not sure whether or not there were still traps in the lobby of the first floor. Instead, he led Zhang Daniu to crawl out of the ventilation window of the toilet and arrived at the shabby alley behind the building of wealth.

The terrain here was relatively high. There were still a few cars parked on the side of the road that weren’t submerged in water. Li Yao smashed the window of a large-scale, multipurpose off-road vehicle with a single punch. He forcefully opened the door and stuffed Zhang Daniu inside.

Looking at the rear-view mirror, one would notice that thick, black smoke was popping up from many windows of the building, making it look like a ferocious devil. The building had turned into a demon building.

Countless streams of black smoke snatched at Li Yao and Zhang Daniu as if they were alive.

“We’ve been discovered so quickly?”

Li Yao sniffed. He tore open the panel below the control panel and grabbed a handful of wires.

“I only have one chance. I must—”

He followed his natural instincts and allowed the bioelectricity surging inside his nerves to react naturally with the circuits of the car. Sparks flooded into the control circuits and the engine from the depths of his cells.

After a series of pleasing engine roars, accompanied by violent vibrations, the car started!

Chapter 3213: Abandoning Brightness For Darkness?

Hardly had Li Yao stepped on the gas when he saw through the rear-view mirror that the black fog rushing out of the building of wealth had condensed into an enormous ghost claw that snatched at the multipurpose off-road vehicle. When the black fog covered the top of the off-road vehicle, squeaking noises were echoing on the top of the vehicle, as if it had been scratched by real sharp claws.

Thankfully, the off-road vehicle had already started moving at this moment. The roar of the engine drowned out the shriek of the sharp claws. Li Yao ruthlessly stepped on the accelerator, causing the water around the wheels to splatter as the off-road vehicle rushed out like an arrow released from a bow.

BOOM! CRACK!

It was unknown whether it was intentional or not, but dozens of lightning bolts fell from the sky and hit the SUV from afar. One moment of carelessness, and the SUV and the people inside would be burnt to ashes.

Li Yao’s face was as gloomy as still water. Every pore on his body had been expanded to the limit. He keenly sensed every electric current and ripple between heaven and earth. It was clearly a heavy and clumsy off-road vehicle, but Li Yao drove it with the agility of a racing motorcycle. In between the lightning bolts, he zigzagged left and right and finally accelerated to his maximum speed, leaving the lightning bolts and the black fog far behind. On the other hand, Zhang Daniu had no time to put on his seat belt. He was swinging back and forth in the carriage like the last match in a matchbox. He was about to throw up his bile.

It was raining cats and dogs in front of him. The sky and the earth were covered in darkness. The entire city was drowned in the endless rain. Visibility was extremely low. Li Yao drove the off-road vehicle onto the highway half by memory and half by luck. Right now, there was water everywhere on the ground. Passing through the lower roads and culverts, the engine would die down at any moment. The highway was the only place that was slightly safer.

“Hu—”

After making sure that there were no pursuers behind him, Li Yao took a long breath in relief. Then he glanced at Zhang Daniu out of the corner of his eye. “Are you alright, Master Niu?”

“I—I’m fine. Blurgh!”

Zhang Daniu leaned against the window and retched a few times. Then, he looked at the raging storm and thousands of bolts of lightning. His complexion turned even more ugly as he said in a trembling voice, “This—this damn weather doesn’t look like a normal hurricane or storm no matter how I look at it. It can’t be that the ‘hunter’ was speaking the truth and the world is really starting to ‘collapse’, right? Hey, Student Li Yao, where are we going now?”

“I don’t know.”

Li Yao frowned. Although he had awakened the strength of a monster and the mottled memory pieces, they were still not enough to help an ordinary college student like him find a way out of the ‘Ark’ and the ‘Apocalyptic’ organizations, or even the malicious world. “Master Niu, do you believe what the ‘hunter’ said? That we are all prisoners in a time prison living in an infinitely repetitive ‘reincarnation world’?”

“I don’t know, either!”

Zhang Daniu wiped his face and said pitifully, “Last night, I was still worrying about the rent for the next quarter. I was still feeling sorry that you ate 200-300 dollars worth of casserole bones. In the end, in just a short span of 24 hours, you want me to face some sort of ‘world collapsing, infinite loop’ problem… I really don’t know what to say about such a high-end and elegant problem.”

“However, on second thought, the words of the ‘hunter’ should have a lot of truth to them, because his theory is partly consistent with the Dream Traveler’s and can explain the ‘earth bug’. Besides, we have seen the existence of superpowers with our own eyes. What if the superpowered people did not awaken the memories of their previous lives?

“I think so, too. The words of the ‘hunters’ cannot all be false. Of course, they cannot all be true, either.

Li Yao said, “Master Niu, whether or not you pretended to be nice to the ‘hunter’, I’m going to ask you one more time. What are you thinking? If you have to choose between the ‘apocalypse’ or the ‘arky’, who will you choose? If you have to choose the ‘apocalypse’, I don’t need to flatter myself and save you from the ‘apocalypse’, do I?”

“Well…”

Zhang Daniu thought carefully for a long time and said, “I don’t know, either. I’d better check the status of the ‘Ark Foundation’ first.”

“Oh?”

Li Yao was dazed for a moment. He said, “I thought that you would choose the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’ without any hesitation. After all, what the ‘hunter’ said was very reasonable and tempting. If there is a ‘will of the earth’ and their organization is the messenger, spokesperson, or subordinate of the ‘will of the earth’, they will certainly look more promising than the ‘Ark Foundation’.”

“You are wrong about that.”

A sly smile appeared on Zhang Daniu’s face when he heard Li Yao’s words. He said, “If the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’ is really as awesome as the ‘Hunter’ claimed, with the entire ‘Will of the Earth’ backing them up, how could they still be a hidden underground organization? Even after the shooting at the T3 Game Show, they were still wanted by the police of multiple countries? It doesn’t make any sense at all! If the ‘Will of the Earth’ does exist and can control everything in human society, then the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’ should have replaced the United Nations and the governments of the other superpowers and launched a full-scale arrest warrant for the ‘Ark Foundation’. Is it really necessary to go this far where everyone is sneaking around and not daring to meet anyone?”

Li Yao raised his eyebrow in surprise.

As it turned out, Zhang Daniu had the same thought as him. It was just that he did not dare to say it out loud due to the intimidation of the ‘hunter’.

Although the guy was sordid, he was not stupid.

“Besides, don’t you see that the words of the ‘hunter’ are contradictory?

Zhang Daniu said proudly, “He compared Earth to a ‘reincarnation prison’, and the members of the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’ are both ‘model prisoners’ and ‘prisoners’. Isn’t that right?”

Li Yao nodded. “Yes. That’s what he said.”

“That’s hilarious.

Zhang Daniu said, “I don’t know how much you know about prisons. Although I don’t know the specific organizational structure and management pattern of prisons, I can guarantee one thing—no prison in the world is 100% managed by a prisoner, right? No matter how well-behaved, outstanding, and ‘model’ you are, the prison manager occasionally needs you to maintain the underground order inside the prison and save the manager’s manpower and resources. But no matter what, a prisoner is still a prisoner. It’s impossible for them to be promoted to a real prison guard and manager, right?”

Li Yao thought quickly and immediately understood the simple logic. He nodded and said, “Yes. Maybe in the eyes of the awakeners of the Heaven’s Origin Sector, they are the ‘model prisoners’ and the ‘prisoners’ who are absolutely obedient to the ‘will of the earth’. But a prisoner is still a prisoner. If there is a humanized will of the earth, it is impossible for them to trust any human being, be they ‘apocalypse’ or ‘arks’!”

“Yes. Only in extreme cases can a prisoner become a prison guard.

Zhang Daniu said, “That is because the prison managers are too weak. The order inside and outside the prison has gone out of control. If that is the case, the ‘will of the earth’ is not as invincible as it sounds, is it?”

Li Yao looked at Zhang Daniu seriously. “Fair enough.”

“Of course it does.”

Zhang Daniu said excitedly, “When I was talking to the ‘hunter’ just now, I’d already thought it through. A real man can bow and submit when he wants to. He can even take the insult to his groin. What’s wrong with kneeling down and hugging his thigh? The problem is, even if you want to hug his thigh, you have to choose a thick, long, and real 24-karat golden thigh to hug. For guys who call themselves ‘prisoners’ like the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’, who knows whether or not they are chess pieces in the hands of a higher power? What do you mean by ‘we are born guilty’? They look like zealots. It would be a shame not to be used by others. You and I are smart people. There’s no need to be tied to them so quickly!”

“As for the ‘Ark Foundation’, according to the ‘hunters’, they are just a bunch of prison breakers, but they are acting so arrogantly. Is there something wrong with them, or do they have the capital to be arrogant? If it is the latter, it is not a bad idea for us to join the ‘Ark Foundation’ together. After all, I’ve spent twenty thousand dollars on the resistance. It is not unreasonable to ask the ‘Ark Foundation’ to pay me back a hundred times or even two million, right?

“Of course, it would be best if we don’t go looking for the ‘Ark’. Let’s just let the ‘Ark’ discover us and take us away. This way, if the ‘apocalypse’ gains the upper hand next time and completely destroys the ‘Ark’, we can still say that we were forced by the people of the ‘apocalypse’ to end up like this. Maybe we will have a chance to abandon the darkness and seek refuge in the arms of the ‘apocalypse’ and the ‘will of the earth’. Student Li Yao, think about it, don’t you agree?”

Li Yao stared at Zhang Daniu for a long, long time.

“Master Niu, you’ve changed.”

Li Yao could not help but say, “From last night to this moment, your temperament, way of thinking, and computational ability seem to have undergone a complete transformation. You are completely different from before.”

“Now that you mention it, you do have a point.

Zhang Daniu flipped down the window cover and found a small mirror on the back of the window. He observed his appearance carefully and said, “It’s really strange. Ever since I met you, bubbles seem to be popping up in the depths of my brain. Inside the bubbles are new and strange thoughts one after another. Many super complicated questions can be solved in a flash. What’s going on?”

“Well—”

Just as Li Yao was about to respond, the hair all over his body suddenly stood on end, and his heart seemed to have been ruthlessly pinched. He turned the steering wheel violently as if it was a conditioned reflex, and the off-road vehicle almost turned 180 degrees on the elevated platform.

The complicated viaduct was made of three levels, high and low. While Li Yao was steering the steering wheel hard, another black, multipurpose off-road vehicle roared like an iron beast and flew down from a higher level, trying to crash into Li Yao and Zhang Daniu’s vehicle!

Chapter 3214: Racing Violently

Had Li Yao not braked and made a sharp turn in time, the heavy off-road vehicle would certainly have crashed into the roof of Li Yao’s vehicle. Chances were that the two vehicles would have crashed into burning scrap metal.

But right now, the iron beast that came out of nowhere crashed into the ground right in front of Li Yao and exploded into an exploding fireball.

Li Yao’s eyes seemed to have been ignited by the fireball. He operated his hands and feet as if they were cramping. The engine, the brakes, and the tires screamed one after another. The off-road vehicle started spinning at a high speed like a gyro. It actually knocked away the fireball in front of it and resumed its normal direction and speed.

“Blargh!”

Zhang Daniu’s face turned pale again, as if he was about to vomit.

However, the fire in Li Yao’s eyes did not die down, because in the rear-view mirror, dozens of speeding cars appeared on the highway one after another, which looked like dozens of iron beasts!

Some of the vehicles were coming at them from the back, while some were diving down like the off-road vehicles just now.

The road was slippery in the rain, and the lights were dim. The bottom of the highway could not be seen, and the road conditions were extremely bad. Most of the drivers did not have Li Yao’s driving skills. Often, when they were driving, their tires would slip, and they would lose control of their direction. They would fall into the darkness below the highway, and soon, they would turn into a rising fireball. However, there were still a dozen or so cars that were firmly biting Li Yao. The distance was getting shorter and shorter. Some of them were even trying to crash into Li Yao’s off-road vehicle from the rear and flank.

BAM! BAM!

In the middle of a storm, such a collision was tantamount to suicide. It was a crazy strategy of mutual destruction.

Although Li Yao had awakened his unparalleled driving skills, he was still soaked in cold sweat when he was surrounded by the crazy drivers.

At this moment, a two-seater sports car drove side by side with him. Li Yao looked through the window and saw that the driver was a well-dressed middle-aged man whose hair was not messy at all. He was also wearing gold-rimmed glasses. No matter how one looked at him, he did not look like a fearless death squad member.

However, when he saw the absent-minded look in the other party’s eyes and the Bluetooth earpiece hanging by his ear, Li Yao immediately understood.

It turned out that the pursuers were still puppets that had been manipulated and lost their rationality. That was why they were so crazy and fearless.

Squeak squeak squeak!

The tires of the two sports cars screamed. Despite the fact that they were heavier and smaller than Li Yao, they still crashed into Li Yao’s off-road vehicle. Li Yao had nowhere to run to. The two cars scratched each other hard, raising hideous sparks. The two sports cars were no match for the multipurpose off-road vehicle after all. They were knocked away by Li Yao and fell to the bottom of the guardrail, almost twenty meters away from each other.

But as a result, Li Yao’s speed was inevitably slowed down. The remaining dozen or so vehicles were about to catch up to him.

“Something doesn’t seem right!”

Zhang Daniu shouted, “There’s no end to this section of the highway!”

Li Yao was dazed for a moment. He squinted and observed his surroundings carefully.

Although they were talking about their surroundings, in fact, the lights on the viaduct had long been destroyed by the storm and lightning. Other than the high beams that could illuminate dozens of meters ahead through the rain, they could see nothing else.

He was surrounded by darkness in all directions. The viaduct extended vertically in the darkness to the unknown one moment and zigzagged the next. It seemed to have been tied up by countless forks and on-ramp. It was a maze in an infinite loop.

At this moment, a road sign flashed above their heads. A bolt of lightning struck down, allowing them to clearly see the contents of the road sign.

This was the intersection of ‘People’s Road’ and ‘Tide Tide Road’.

The problem was, Renmin Road and Sea Tide Road were not far away from the central business district. At most, it would take 3-5 minutes for them to arrive, but they had driven for at least 20-30 minutes in the torrential rain—the several dozen kilometers should have already been covered.

How did he end up near the central business district?

Li Yao’s lips twitched, and Zhang Daniu swallowed his saliva. The two of them thought of the same thing at the same time.

“Can you drive?”

Li Yao suddenly asked.

“Huh?”

Zhang Daniu was shocked. He stammered, “I—I do have a driver’s license, but I haven’t touched a car in years. I—I don’t have a car myself. I’m from the ‘original clan’…”

“You drive!”

Li Yao did not give any chance to refuse. “If this road is truly an endless loop, after the intersection of the People’s Road and the Sea Tide Road, there should be a straight road several kilometers ahead. You can slow down a bit. It’s very simple!”

“What? I—I don’t know how. I can’t!”

Zhang Daniu was at a loss. “It’s been a long time since I last touched a real steering wheel. I’m not an experienced driver in this regard!”

But Li Yao had already unbuckled his seat belt and dragged Zhang Daniu over.

The multipurpose off-road vehicle immediately started zigzagging on the high platform. Several times, it brushed against the guardrail, causing sparks to fly.

“Ah!”

Zhang Daniu screamed.

“Steady. Relax. Slow down!”

Li Yao gritted his teeth and roared at Zhang Daniu.

“I’m stabilizing myself!”

Zhang Daniu was almost in tears. “This thing is too exciting!”

“Very good. You drove very well. You don’t look like a typical ‘origin’. That’s right. That’s right.”

Li Yao had been making a last-ditch effort, too. He didn’t expect that Zhang Daniu would get into his role within half a minute after he panicked in the beginning. Although he couldn’t be said to be driving very fast, in this godforsaken weather and godforsaken place, being able to maintain the normal speed and stability on the first try was already very good.

But as a result, the car of the pursuers was only twenty to thirty meters away from them. They could simply step on the gas.

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao kicked open the rear window and crawled out like a loach with wings.

His fingers and toes turned into hooks that were as hard as steel and clutched the roof of the tank. No matter how the wind, rain, and thunder raged, they could not get rid of him.

“Slow down. Slower!”

Li Yao rushed into the car and shouted. The distance between the two was getting shorter and shorter.

When the pursuer tried to crash into Li Yao’s off-road vehicle again, Li Yao swung his arm in an arc and launched the iron nail!

“Chi!”

The iron nail hit the front wheel of the car. At such a high speed, although it was not like the movie where one would spin dozens of times in the air and fall to the ground like a giant fireball, it was still enough to make the car lose control and stagger to the side of the road. The front end and the engine crashed into a mess and could not be started again.

Seeing that his plan was working out, Li Yao immediately took action and threw out the iron nails on his waist, aiming at the tires of his opponent.

With his eyesight and hand strength, even if he was affected by the torrential rain and thunder, it would still be a piece of cake for him to shoot down the tires of the pursuers. It was just that he didn’t want to cause any fatal injuries to the puppets inside the car. Therefore, he repeatedly calculated, carefully aimed, and even had Zhang Daniu slow down the speed of the car before slowing down again. Sure enough, the dozen or so cars either crashed into the railings or lost control of the brakes. There was no more tragedy of falling off the high platform and exploding in the air.

Very soon, the vehicles of the pursuers all stopped.

Li Yao wiped off the hot rainwater on his face and thought to himself that he could finally breathe a sigh of relief and think of a way to get rid of the unpredictable ghost trap.

“Li Yao! Li Yao!”

But right then, Zhang Daniu screamed in the car.

Li Yao was greatly alarmed. When he looked back, he vaguely saw two hazy lights shining at them from the darkness ahead.

Soon, the lights outlined an even darker outline in the darkness. It was a twenty-four-wheel container truck!

God knows where the other party had gotten such a behemoth from. Usually, this kind of container truck was not allowed to enter the city. But right now, it was roaring, galloping, and baring its fangs and brandishing its claws on the highway like a furious steel beast, rushing straight towards Li Yao’s off-road vehicle.

No, it was not as simple as a container truck.

Just as Li Yao was leaning on the roof of the off-road vehicle, a man was standing on the head of the container truck. He was a middle-aged man who was wearing a neat black suit and a pair of black sunglasses despite the storm and lightning.

He seemed to have noticed that Li Yao was observing him, too. He bowed slightly, as if he were greeting Li Yao.

“Hunters!”

Zhang Daniu screamed in the driver’s seat, “He is the ‘Hunter’!”

Li Yao’s heart suddenly sank.

The wind and rain were so heavy that he had to lean on the top of the tank and stabilize himself with one hand and two feet. But the ‘hunter’ was standing on the top of the tank casually and easily. No matter how much he struggled, he did not move at all.

His body control ability alone was already better than Li Yao’s at this moment. No wonder he dared to set up traps unhurriedly when he was faced with the ten ‘Deep Perception’ and the two ‘Ultimate Perception’ of the ‘Ark Foundation’.

Then, he looked at the driver of the truck, who was fat and unshaven and did not look like an Awakener. As it turned out, he had headphones on his ears, too. Then, it was quite obvious what was going on. One of the abilities of the ‘hunters’ was to hack into other people’s brains through electric waves and turn them into his puppets. At the same time, part of the memories of the previous life deep inside other people’s brains would be activated, thereby allowing them to acquire terrifying killing skills!

But was it the only ability of a hunter?

While Li Yao was pondering, the two vehicles were getting closer and closer.

Li Yao could clearly see the increasingly mysterious smile on the hunter’s face. He also saw the hunter snap his fingers. The driver in the driver’s seat immediately pulled the steering wheel as if he had received a mysterious command and made a ninety-degree turn.

Such a heavy storm, such a slippery road, and such a heavy container truck were simply unable to make a 90-degree turn in such a hurry. Sure enough, the large truck immediately flipped over, and under the force of inertia, the entire coach came over and almost blocked the entire lane.

The coach rubbed against the ground, producing earsplitting noises and dazzling sparks. It swept toward Li Yao’s SUV like an iron mill that could crush everything.

The hunter, on the other hand, jumped up the moment the truck flipped over as if it were weightless. It was not until this moment that he floated down and stood on the overturned truck, coldly staring at Li Yao, who was right in front of him!

Chapter 3215: Bullet Time

At that moment, Li Yao felt that he had been locked onto by 108,000 eyes at the same time, as if every weakness on his body had been grasped by the enemy.

He finally understood why the awakened one was named ‘Hunter’. It was true that few things in the world could escape his hunting.

Zhang Daniu was already scared out of his wits when he saw the container truck sweeping over. He could not control the direction at all. The off-road vehicle started moving crazily and trembling violently.

The enemy had blocked the entire lane. Li Yao and Zhang Daniu had nowhere to run. There was no time for them to make a 180-degree turn. In a moment of desperation, Li Yao shouted and expanded the veins and muscles all over his body. He ripped off the entire roof of the off-road vehicle and grabbed it like a surfboard.

“Let’s go!”

When the off-road vehicle and the container truck were about to collide, Li Yao grabbed the back of Zhang Daniu’s neck and pulled him out of the vehicle. He stomped on the back of the off-road vehicle and brought Zhang Daniu into the air like a giant bird. He then covered the top of the vehicle with his feet and used his ankle, knee joint, and leg muscles to ease the impact of landing.

Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi!

The roof of the tank rubbed against the ground, producing dazzling sparks. Li Yao felt excruciating pain in his legs. He slid hundreds of meters away and rolled on the ground more than ten times. At this moment, the off-road vehicle collided with the container truck brutally with earsplitting noises. A gargantuan fireball soared into the sky and illuminated the storm into a crimson blade.

The impact of the container truck was slightly diminished, but the hunter took the opportunity to jump off the truck. While he was still in midair, he took out two pistols from his waist. One of them was silver and carried the coldness of death, while the other was as brilliant as burning gold. The bullets that darted out of the muzzle were mixed with the brilliance of silver and gold, too. Dozens of curved blades seemed to be attacking him from various directions and constituted a crisscross cage.

Dodging the bullets of ordinary guns was a piece of cake for Li Yao, who had awakened the power of Cultivation.

However, the moment the hunter’s bullet left the barrel, Li Yao felt as if the entire space had become exceptionally oppressive and thick. It was as if his body and soul had been entangled, enveloped, and delayed by an inconceivable force. He entered a slow-motion state and could only watch helplessly as several dozen rounds of bullets “slowly and unhurriedly” shot towards him. Even if he bit the tip of his tongue in order to forcefully keep his mind clear and move his four limbs and body inch by inch, narrowly avoiding the first dozen or so rounds of bullets, he was still hit by the next few rounds of bullets, causing his skin to split open and blood to splatter everywhere.

Li Yao grunted and fell off the top of the tank, rolling on the highway.

But in the next second, he stood up again and shot into his body. The bullets that shattered his bones and internal organs did not seem to affect his movements at all. He gritted his teeth and fired the last cluster of iron nails.

“Huh?”

Behind the deep black sunglasses of the hunter, there was a hint of surprise in his eyes. It seemed that he did not expect that his opponent would still maintain such a high motor ability and combat ability even though he had been shot multiple times. His original target was only Zhang Daniu, but right now, he was deeply interested in Li Yao. He dodged Li Yao’s storm of iron nails unhurriedly and came close to Li Yao. He carefully observed Li Yao’s bald head and said, “You are… that college student, Li Yao. You are actually not dead? What is this? Is your ability similar to the super powerful cell reproduction and recovery ability of a gecko?”

Gritting his teeth, Li Yao pushed his hands against the ground and attacked the hunter’s lower body like two axes.

The hunter’s expression didn’t change in the least. He didn’t even bother to take off his sunglasses. The feeling of being so thick that it was almost suffocating once again emerged from his body. It pressed down on Li Yao like a mountain or a deep sea, causing Li Yao’s movements to be slowed down by half a beat. In the end, the hunter lightly kicked him flying hundreds of meters away, nearly breaking a street lamp next to the railing.

“Student Li Yao, there are many secrets behind you. What kind of people are you exactly?”

The hunter finally took off his sunglasses. He then took out a silk handkerchief from the pocket of his suit and wiped his eyes slowly. His eyes, which had been hidden behind the sunglasses all the time, seemed to be burning with cold flames. He walked toward Li Yao step by step and asked, “Who ordered you to approach the author of ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivators’? How did you end up talking about the ‘Ark Foundation’ and the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’? When we return to… the organization, can we talk about the questions?”

Leaning against the cement railing, Li Yao vomited a mouthful of thick, stinky blood. He felt that all the bones in his chest were broken.

Good, very good. This guy’s strength was indeed stronger than he had imagined. However, hehe, it was precisely this kind of heart-wrenching attack that completely opened his nerve endings and gene strands. Li Yao had a faint feeling that as long as he could pass this test, he would definitely become stronger. As long as…

Li Yao groped his body. His face suddenly changed.

“Where are my energy bars? Where are my energy bars and energy gel?”

The hunter was getting closer and closer.

“Uh…”

Li Yao raised his hands and made a gesture of surrender. He said feebly, “Mister… I think there’s been a misunderstanding between us. It’s totally fine if you want to have a nice talk, but can you wait for me to eat something first? How should I put it? I’m suddenly hungry. I’m afraid I have low blood sugar. I can’t think straight at all!”

The hunter smiled and raised his guns at Li Yao again.

Inside the chamber, the ripples of energy emitted by the special bullets targeting the awakened could be clearly seen in the black rain.

Right then, a cluster of rolling grey mist suddenly rose up.

The grey mist seemed to have a life of its own and soon enveloped Li Yao and Zhang Daniu. It even split into dozens of gray tentacles that swept toward the hunter’s hands and the guns.

The hunter finally put on an expression that was different from ‘aloof’. He shot all the bullets at Li Yao in the grey mist.

Li Yao vomited blood and dodged to the side despite his heavy wounds. However, he discovered that the bullets that were shot into the grey mist were corroded, jammed, and deflected by a certain weird force. There was no telling where they went.

The grey mist seemed to have a life of its own. Not only did it possess the power to interfere with bullets, it also seemed to contain the power to calm the soul. Li Yao’s muscles and bones had been broken, his internal bleeding was serious, and there was not a single part of his body that wasn’t in pain. Under the cover of the grey mist, a cool feeling actually surged out from within his body. His hot and restless soul also returned to tranquility as he gradually fell into a peaceful dream.

Before he passed out, he only heard the furious roars of the hunter and Zhang Daniu’s screams.

…

This was the second time that Li Yao had passed out in only half a day.

In the same way, he also had that bizarre, inexplicable dream—not of an interstellar tyrannosaur, but of him living in a bustling, busy metropolis on Earth, lost, resisting, struggling, and even falling.

No, not one big city on Earth, but several different cities and villages. He seemed to have experienced several different lives under different identities. They were all his… previous lives?

However, in every memory piece of his previous life, an enormous silver-white balloon was following him at a distance neither too far nor too close. It was being pulled by a narrow rope from the back of his head. Every time he looked back, he would see his own image twisted like a mirror on the surface of the silver-white balloon.

“Who am I?

“Am I… Li Yao?”

“If I am Li Yao, which Li Yao am I?

“What is my destiny exactly? Is it the ‘Vulture Plan’, or—”

Looking at his twisted self inside the silver-white balloon, he shuddered and woke up.

…

Beep! Beep! Beep!

The pure white room, the smell of disinfectant, and the beeps from the medical equipment next to it were all inexplicably tranquil.

Li Yao observed his surroundings and discovered that his entire body was tied up with IV lines and unknown wires. He was lying on a specially-made hospital bed. The medical equipment around the hospital bed was not as advanced as the “medical pod” in , but it had already far exceeded the emergency equipment that Li Yao had seen in ordinary hospitals.
