He listened carefully. The slight shivering of the ward and the feeble noises coming from below immediately reminded Li Yao that he was not in a fixed building but in a ‘mobile hospital’ modified from a container truck that was running on a highway.

“You’re awake?”

A deep and gentle voice came from the corner. “How do you feel?”

The other party did not conceal his existence. Li Yao managed to raise his head and discovered that the speaker was the “Lady Grey Mist” who had appeared in the morgue.

The awakener seemed to be used to using his own ability as a code name. Recalling the weird grey mist that appeared when the hunter was trying to kill him, Li Yao immediately realized who had saved him.

Besides, he had already fooled the grey mist lady once in the morgue. She must’ve been alarmed. It was unnecessary to pretend that she was still unconscious.

“I feel good.”

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao did not find any shrapnel left inside his body. His joints and internal organs did not seem to have suffered any damage, either, as if his memory of being shot was just an illusion. He sat up and looked at his bulging muscles that were nourished by various drugs and nutrition liquids. “What drugs did you give me?”

“That is a good question.”

The grey mist lady looked at Li Yao. “Other than saline, glucose, and some special high-energy nutrition liquids, we didn’t give you any drugs. But your body has amazing self-recovery ability. All the bullets that the hunter shot into your body were squeezed out. Then, your wounds were healing at a visible speed. What an amazing ability.”

Chapter 3216: Legend Of The Seven Gods

“Is that so?”

Li Yao looked at his hands. He seemed to be able to see the high-speed operation of the mitochondria factories deep inside his cells through his skin and his muscles. They were providing surging energy for his cells and his body, facilitating the proliferation of cells, the expansion of muscles, and the recovery of his body. Of course, they were also providing tremendous damage and damage. He even vaguely remembered that it was a feature of the ‘demons’ or his ‘apocalyptic body’ that was working. But what were the ‘demons’ and the ‘apocalyptic body’? Was it really as the novels described? How weird!

“You don’t seem to be surprised by your super recovery ability?”

The grey mist lady continued, “If my eyes aren’t deceiving me, this should be the second time you’ve come back from the dead in a short span of twelve hours, right? The first time was the ‘magical explosive’ of ‘Deep Perceiver’ White Night, which burnt you from your internal organs to the point that it was almost like you were placed in a super-large microwave oven for an entire ten minutes. Even I didn’t expect you to still be alive. The second time was with the ‘Hunter’. He didn’t use ordinary bullets, but two different types of super bullets. The first type had the characteristics of armor-piercing and explosive. It could even kill an African elephant in one shot. The second type contained a strange component of meteorites that could restrain most of the abilities of the awakened.

“You were shot seven times by the ‘hunter’. Three of the bullets hit the critical parts of your body. You should’ve been dead for sure, but you recovered in only twelve hours. During that time, you also absorbed nutrition liquids that ordinary awakeners wouldn’t be able to digest for an entire month. Your body is like an insatiable black hole. Interesting. Student Li Yao, what are you exactly?”

“If I tell you that I don’t know, will you believe me?”

Li Yao smiled and asked, “What about the author of ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’? How is he?”

“He is fine. He is merely leaving this place through other channels and going to the headquarters of the ‘Ark Foundation’. You are the same. You are safe for now. The transportation route is top secret. The people of the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’ will not be able to catch up to us so easily.

The grey mist lady said, “Zhang Daniu has told us a lot of things. He is even continuing to write. With the help of our hypnotists, his creative efficiency has improved quite a bit compared to the past. If it is confirmed that you are fine, we will provide you with the follow-up content of ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivator’.

“But the first question that needs to be addressed is—who are you exactly? Why did you find the author of ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivator’ and why are you trying to investigate the secrets of the ‘Ark Foundation’ and the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’ with him? What’s the deal with your unparalleled capability, as well as the detectaphones and trackers that you handed over to Zhang Daniu? The detectaphones that you made are very delicate and have a different structure from those of most national spy agencies in this life. How did you make such things?”

“I—I still don’t know.”

Li Yao took a long breath and shook his head gloomily.

Now that Zhang Daniu had been controlled by the members of the ‘Ark Foundation’, he must’ve confessed everything with his personality and integrity. There was no point in keeping it a secret any longer. “You won’t believe it if I tell you, but I was just an ordinary college student. It was only after reading ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’ a week ago that I was somehow awakened to an indescribable power. I—I feel that I am the protagonist of the author’s story. I am the Cultivator Li Yao from the ‘Four Universities of Cultivators’!”

Li Yao was prepared to be mocked by the grey mist lady.

But to his surprise, the grey mist lady’s face remained unchanged. She even nodded in understanding and said, “There are all kinds of amazing things in the world. Incredible things have happened to every one of us who have awakened, mutations that cannot be explained by science. How do you know that I don’t believe you?”

“Huh?”

Li Yao was dazed for a moment. The door of the ward was opened softly, and a middle-aged man in weird clothes with fiery red hair walked in.

“Lady Grey Mist, the pursuers have been taken care of. We should be safe for the next twelve hours.”

The middle-aged man said.

But Li Yao’s eyes were fixed on the middle-aged man, and he couldn’t get rid of them.

It could not be helped. The middle-aged man’s appearance was too… shocking.

It was not a big problem for a middle-aged man to dye his hair red like a young man, but the problem was that the baldness of the middle-aged man was very serious. There was a “Mediterranean Sea” on his head. In order to cover it up, he had also deliberately grown the few remaining strands of hair on the two sides of his head and combed them horizontally in such a clumsy way to cover it up. Even so, he still had to dye his hair red. His determination was firm.

Below the red hair was a big, greasy face with a bulbous nose. The uncle’s face was like a lonely rock in a desert that had been weathered by wind, snow, and time. The interconnecting wrinkles and hollows gave him a medal of vicissitudes.

When one reached middle age, it was inevitable that one would gain weight. If one liked to drink a bit of wine or eat a bit of supper, it wouldn’t be strange for one’s belly to bulge like a pregnant woman’s. However, this uncle was clearly 1.7 meters tall, yet he was carrying a 200 lb weight. Moreover, he was wearing an extremely exaggerated collar and cuffs, as well as an extremely long white shirt that looked like scissors. He was even wearing a black short-sleeved shirt that had been crudely cut. The neckline of the shirt was extremely large, causing the fat on his body to become uneven, revealing his curves. This made everyone’s eyes a bit… sore.

However, that was not the weirdest part.

Below the super long white shirt and the tight black uniform, the middle-aged man was wearing a pair of red jeans and a red belt between his legs!

Li Yao didn’t know how to describe the uncle’s appearance.

He suddenly discovered that the middle-aged man’s thick and short neck, which was almost buried between his chin and the fat on his chest, was actually helping him with a black leather belt. There was no telling whether or not it was a collar.

Li Yao felt that he was suffocating.

In the meantime, he felt that the uncle looked familiar, as if he had seen him somewhere before.

Right, wasn’t it someone from the game, someone from the game?

“Well done, Uncle Hui.”

The grey mist lady nodded her head. She pointed at Li Yao and said, “Right. This young friend Li Yao seems to have just awakened and is full of doubts about the strength in his body. Can I trouble Uncle Hui to explain the process of your awakening to him?”

“Alright, Lady Grey Mist.”

Uncle Hui, a middle-aged man with dyed red hair and red jeans, who was wearing a belt on his neck and knees, said in a southern accent, “However, as I said, please stop calling me ‘Uncle Hui’ and call me ‘Seven Gods’. Ms. Grey Mist, you’ve forgotten.”

“… I’m sorry.”

The grey mist lady’s eyelids were twitching. She said, “Don’t mind the details. Let’s begin!”

“No problem, young man. Fate is indeed a marvelous thing. None of us will know who we are until we are awakened.

Uncle Hui—the ‘Seven Gods’ pointed at his bulbous nose and said in plastic-flavored Mandarin, “Take this uncle for example. I have never achieved much in my life. Originally, I was just an ordinary old man selling pork in a vegetable market in a fourth-tier city in the south. Other people in the vegetable market like to eat, drink, smoke, play, gamble. I don’t like any of these. I only have one hobby since childhood, which is to play video games and games!

“I especially like to play a game called ‘Punching God 97’. It’s been almost twenty years since I was a young man. When I was at my peak, I was invincible. I was quite famous in our city. Unfortunately, times have changed. Young people nowadays like to play online games. The game that I like has been abandoned for a long time. It’s probably the last arcade in the entire country that is about to collapse.

“It doesn’t matter. This is what I like to play. I especially like a game character named ‘Seven Gods’ in ‘Punching God 97’. Every time I choose this character to play, wah, I always feel that I am one with the sword and that I am possessed by my soul. It’s like I have crawled into a game console and become one of the Seven Gods. Every punch hits flesh. It’s so refreshing!”

“That’s it. At the end of last year, the arcade that I’ve been playing with for 20-30 years finally couldn’t hold on any longer and was about to go bankrupt. Although you can play ‘Punching God 97’ on the computers and game consoles nowadays, and my child has already adjusted it for me, it is still different from playing in the arcade. Young man, you haven’t played it before. You don’t understand.

“All in all, it was probably the last time that this uncle went to play. Naturally, I was particularly dedicated and fierce. Over and over, I chose the ‘Seven Gods’ to fight against the heroes of the world. I fought more and more smoothly and skillfully. I even felt that the world around me was shattering and disappearing. Only then did the world of ‘Punching God 97’ become ‘real’. I—I felt that I had crawled into a machine and turned into the real ‘Seven Gods’!

Li Yao was fascinated. He couldn’t help but ask, “Then?”

“Then—”

Uncle Hui said seriously, “I passed out. When I woke up in the hospital, my daughter said that a machine in the arcade had a power leak and knocked me out for three days and three nights!”

“Well…” Li Yao stammered.

Uncle Hui said, “But I’m well aware that it’s not as simple as an electric shock. I’ve awakened the memories of my previous life. Young man, guess what? In my previous life—a long time ago in a certain life—I really did come from the ‘Seven Gods’. This is my destiny. It’s the destiny that I’m really going to face! From this day on, I can’t stand the identity of ‘Porkman’ any longer. I’ve decided to resume the original appearance of the ‘Seven Gods’ and fight to my heart’s content against my destiny and my mortal enemies!”

Chapter 3217: 1999, Battle Of Vulture

Li Yao asked, “Uncle Hui, I don’t know whether or not I should ask… Have you seen a psychiatrist recently?”

Uncle Hui: “Hehe. Young man, are you doubting this uncle’s words? What’s wrong? Do you think this uncle’s age and profession are not enough for this avant-garde, mysterious, and powerful style? Let me tell you, the so-called ‘dreams’ and ‘awakening’ are not just the rights of you young people. Although this uncle has been nothing more than an ordinary old pig all my life, the fire in the bottom of my heart that ‘stepped on the moon and burned everything’ will never be extinguished!”

Shua! Shua!

Uncle Hui wiped his hands on his back. He held a meat cleaver in his right hand and a bone cleaver in his left hand. “Come on, young man. Let me show you the strength of the giant snake. Cry, shout, and die!”

“…Uncle Hui, don’t be rash. I was just asking. In fact, I suspect that I need to see a psychiatrist myself. Also, I’ve played the classic combat game ‘Punching God 97’ with simulators before. I’m not very clear that the ‘Seven Gods’ in the game actually use sabers.

“My martial arts have reached the peak of perfection. I can hurt anyone with flying flowers and picking leaves. What’s the difference between using fists, claws, and sabers? In my previous life, I was a lonely man cursed by the power of serpents. In this life, I relied on these two butcher knives to feed my family. These two butcher knives have been used by me since I was 18 years old. They have long been connected to my mind and blood. They are simply sharper than the legendary artifact ‘Sword of Kusanagi’. I rely on them to feed myself. How can I give them up so easily?”

Uncle Hui said coldly, “Young man, if you don’t believe me, why don’t I show you ‘Hands Off, 221st Style, Eight Children’ with these two sabers?”

Cold sweat appeared on Li Yao’s forehead. “No. No need, Uncle Hui…”

“Don’t call me Uncle Hui. Call me—”

Uncle Hui suddenly burst into fury. “Seven Gods!”

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

An unbelievable scene took place. Uncle Hui’s fat head, big ears, and greasy face suddenly blossomed with a supercilious, arrogant, and dominating aura. Weird purple flames spurted out of every pore on his body and devoured the air inside the ward like thousands of burning vipers, especially the two butcher knives, which were roaring with the enhancement of the purple flames, like the lonely laughter of a crazy man under the moonlight!

“Wow. It really is the ‘Seven Gods’!”

Li Yao was dumbfounded. He observed carefully and found that the two butcher knives had been carved with magnificent words. They should be the names of the knives—the meat cleaver was called ‘Moon Zither’ and the boning cleaver ‘Ghost Burning’. They were both ultimate weapons that could cut through iron as easily as mud!

“How about it, young man?”

Uncle Hui raised his sabers high and shouted, “Now you should believe that you are living in a world where your dreams and hopes are limitless and where everything is possible!”

“I—I do…”

Li Yao stammered and looked at the grey mist lady for help.

“Alright, Uncle Hui. Student Li Yao has just awakened. His soul and memories are not very stable. Don’t scare him.”

The grey mist lady coughed and said, “Thank you for your hard work. Go and take a good rest.”

“Hehe!”

Uncle Hui stopped talking and glared at Li Yao. Then he turned around and left. But when he reached the door, he turned around with a bit of resentment in his eyes. “Ms. Gray Mist, you forgot to call me ‘Seven Gods’ again.”

“Cough cough, cough cough cough cough!”

The grey mist lady coughed violently.

Three to five minutes after Uncle Hui left, the purple flames inside the narrow ward were dispersed by a special ventilation system.

“Sorry to scare you.”

The grey mist lady explained, “Many of the metahumans have mixed up their memories from several lifetimes or even dozens of lifetimes. Therefore, their personalities are more… strange. It’s very easy for them to lose control of their emotions. In addition, everyone is burdened with an incomparably heavy mission. They always have to find an outlet to vent. So, no matter what strange actions they make, you must not take offense.”

“No, no. It’s not easy for Uncle Hui to find the real me at such an old age. Why would I be surprised?”

Scratching his bald head, Li Yao did not know what to say. “I’m just—I’m just a little surprised. The content of ‘Punching God 97’ was actually real?”

“Part of it.”

The grey mist lady said, “Of course, the novels, comic books, movies, and games are not 100% real. But the works created by the ‘observers’ contain mottled pieces of memories of the past. They are like broken mirrors that are still reflecting the brutality and cruelty of the fierce battles before countless reincarnations. Right. Do you know what ‘observers’ are?”

“I know. I heard the entire conversation between Zhang Daniu and the ‘hunter’.”

Now that the detectaphones had been discovered, there was no point in keeping it a secret any longer. Li Yao said, “I know that ‘Father of the Star Ring’ Galiot is an ‘observer’, and Zhang Daniu is very likely to be a very special Observer, too. Their works all reflect the story of a certain reincarnation long ago, or even before it. So, is the creator of ’97 Fist’ the same?”

“You could say that. ‘Punching God 97’ is a large-scale fighting game series. Not just any producer can decide its entire content. There might be one or two observers hidden in the production team who created the character of ‘Seven Gods’ according to the memories from the depths of their soul. They also ‘resonance’ with Uncle Hui and activated Uncle Hui’s bloodline. That’s about it.

“In fact, ‘Punching God 97’ is not the only game. Many other novels, movies, comic books, and games are related to the endless battles between the ‘Prison Breakers’ and the ‘Prisoners’. Therefore, don’t be surprised if you find other characters that seem to have ‘traveled’ from novels, movies, and comic books when you are inside the Ark Foundation.

“Prison Breaker, Prison Keeper?”

Li Yao was dazed for a moment. He seemed to understand what the two names meant. “The Ark Foundation represents the ‘Prison Breakers’, and the Heaven’s Wrath represents the ‘Prisoners’?”

“Yes. In this life, we are known as the ‘Ark Foundation’, and they are known as the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’. But in the previous life, or the previous life, or even the previous life, everyone has different names.

The grey mist lady explained, “But no matter how we change our names, our memories, ideologies, and wills will not change until the day we die. We ‘Prison Breakers’ want to break out of the damn reincarnation prison and find the true meaning and freedom of life. We want to completely control our own souls, and the ‘Prisoners’ are accomplices of their so-called ‘will of the earth’. They believe that we are trapped in the time prison of infinite reincarnation not to be punished or tortured, but to be redeemed and tested. As long as we reflect on our sins, sharpen our souls, and modify ourselves, we will be ‘released’ one day.

“The predestined war between the Hell Breakers and the Prison Guardians lasted for too many cycles to count. We had countless soul-stirring, impassioned, and destructive wars. The most brutal war of all took place in 1999 in one of the cycles. It was known as the ‘Battle of the Vulture’. It was the closest we came to success, but… we were still suppressed.

“Even so, we did not yield. Not only did the Hell Breakers not yield, all the earthlings who were suppressed, corrupted, and tied up did not yield, too. Observers were restoring their memories of the great wars in the past. There were also people such as ‘Uncle Hui’ who were awakened in the resonance, passion, and dreams and became the new generation of Hell Breakers affiliated with the Ark Foundation!

“1999, Battle of Vulture…”

Li Yao gradually frowned. He felt that screams were echoing deep inside his brain again. Bubbles that carried countless memory pieces popped up on the surface of the ocean and exploded one after another, bringing him countless mottled and glittering memories. He pressed his temples softly and said, “So, the hunter is telling the truth. The so-called ‘will of the earth’ does exist. We are indeed in the absurd ‘reincarnation’ time and time again. We are pathetic prisoners?”

“They are prisoners, but they are not pathetic.

The grey mist lady said, “There will be a day when we break out of prison with our own strength and regain our freedom. That will be the real rebirth. By then, we will lose a small planet, but we will be able to take back the entire universe!”

“I… still don’t understand.”

Li Yao was dazed for a long time. “Since we are all prisoners locked in the reincarnation prison, and it is the so-called ‘will of the earth’ that is suppressing us, how can we resist? Can’t the ‘will of the earth’ wipe us out directly? Can’t it observe our every move and make us all—uh, heart attack or sudden death, for example?”

“The so-called ‘will of the earth’ is just a theory from the Heaven’s Origin Sector. It is just a name and not a real omniscient god. There is no omniscient god in the universe. As an Easterner, you should know the logic better than me.

The grey mist lady said, “Even in the most heavily guarded prison, it is impossible for the managers to see every movement of every prisoner clearly. Cold undercurrents are still flowing under the strictest iron fist.

“Besides, the so-called relationship between the will of the earth and us is more like the relationship between a person and the bacteria and cells inside their body than the relationship between a warden and a prisoner. When you are sick, can you ‘see’ a certain bacteria or virus inside your body and kill it precisely?

Li Yao was dazed for a moment. He shook his head thoughtfully.

“It is not impossible to destroy us. What matters is the price.

The grey mist lady continued, “This is exactly what ‘Red Pole’ taught us. If a cancer patient goes through radiation and chemotherapy without caring about the cost, it is, of course, possible to purge most of the cancer cells inside the body. But at the same time, the healthy cells will be weakened and exterminated, which might even accelerate his death. Such a treatment plan is meaningless, is it not?”

Chapter 3218: Forced Restart

“I see. Are we some kind of… cancer cells?”

Li Yao mumbled to himself, “‘Cancer of Earth’? I like the sound of that. After all, cancer cells are very difficult to eliminate. As long as there is even a tiny bit left, it is very easy to spread and lose control.

“It is true that, even if the ‘will of the earth’ does exist, human beings are a part of it, or even an important part of it. That’s why it is named ‘Spirit of All Creatures’!

“We are inextricably linked to the will of the earth,” the grey mist lady said, “so that it cannot simply wipe us out. We can only resort to ‘precise strikes and focus purges’, just like the ‘target drugs’ for cancer. The Heaven’s Origin Sector is one such drug.

“Now that you mention it, I kind of understand.”

Li Yao thought for a moment and said, “But I still don’t understand. If the Heaven’s Origin Sector is targeting the ‘target drugs’ of the Ark Foundation, why don’t they seem to be particularly… awesome? Their operations are suspicious, and they don’t dare to act openly. They don’t seem to have any connection with the governments of other countries, either. They are more like a mysterious terrorist organization that is not as glamorous as the Ark Foundation!”

“The Ark and the Apocalypses, the Hell Breakers and the Prison Guardians, have their own advantages and disadvantages. It is not certain who is stronger.

The grey mist lady smiled and said, “As I said, the Heaven’s Origin Sector is a kind of ‘target drug’ that is designed to kill cancer cells. Therefore, during the ‘treatment’, it is bound by all kinds of rules and cannot interfere with the normal functioning of the world, especially human society. As for why it cannot be interfered with, I don’t know, either. Maybe it is about the price.

“If the Heaven’s Origin Sector and the Ark Foundation fall out with each other and start a ‘superpower war’ in the sky of a metropolis with a population of tens of millions, the politics, economy, and even culture of every nation in the world will be deeply affected. Every aspect of human society will be affected. It might even lead to the complete loss of control and collapse of the human society. Maybe, under such control and collapse, more awakeners will be born. For the Heaven’s Origin Sector, the cancer cells that were supposed to be killed will be stimulated to grow and spread. That would be terrible.

“On the other hand, you must know that the members of the Heaven’s Origin Sector are also Awakeners. They are the prisoners locked in the prison of reincarnation. In the deepest part of their hearts, there are also the factors of disobedience and freedom. If the will of the earth gives them too much strength and makes them awaken deeply, it is very likely that they will see through the lies, realize the truth, and defect to our side.

“So, do you understand now? The Heaven’s Origin Sector is protected, instilled, and commanded by the will of the earth, but it is also monitored and restrained by the will of the earth. They have to use their strength cautiously within certain laws. Naturally, they appear to be cautious and hesitant.

“But we are different.

“Although we are not backed by the will of the earth, we are our own backers. We can awaken the memories before countless reincarnations unscrupulously and wield unimaginable terrifying strength without any hesitation. Therefore, after countless reincarnations, the Hell Breakers are on par with the Prison Guardians, and nobody can destroy the other.

“I see.”

Li Yao more or less understood the origins and contradictions of the Hell Breakers and the Prison Guardians.

Pondering for a moment, he felt that he was more on the side of the Jailbreakers—the Ark Foundation.

The grey mist lady, on the other hand, was more or less open with him. She told him everything she knew without the gloom and coldness of a hunter.

However, there were a few critical questions that Li Yao had to figure out.

“Ms. Gray Mist, if I may ask, what do you want exactly?”

Li Yao pondered for a moment and said, “I mean, I’ve basically figured out what the Ark Foundation is doing right now. You have gathered a batch of awakeners and seem to be searching for and protecting more of them, especially the ‘observers’ among the awakeners. You are trying to piece together the secrets before countless reincarnations, aren’t you?”

“That’s right.”

The grey mist lady nodded and said, “Our current mission is to discover and protect all the ‘observers’. Then, with the works of the ‘observers’, we will awaken more memories, hot blood, and self-awareness that have been buried for a long time, so that more and more people will be awakened to the existence of the true self and the truth of the prison of reincarnation. The mission of the Heaven’s Origin Sector is to wipe out all the ‘observers’ and put an end to anything that might trigger the awakening of human beings.

“Then what?”

Li Yao asked, “Your ultimate purpose is to break out of the reincarnation prison and even destroy Earth, but can you do that with the strength of the awakened Cultivators?”

“Of course, it’s not that I’m underestimating the abilities of the awakened. It’s just that the Earth is too large and too strong after all. Moreover, it involves time, reincarnation, and other bizarre things that can’t be explained by scientific logic. Even if we gather 100,000, 10,000, 10,000, 10,000, 10,000, 10,000, 10,000, 10,000, 10,000 experts like Uncle Hui, who have awakened the ‘Power of Seven Gods’, can destroy the Earth and break through the reincarnation prison?”

“I understand your confusion, but the answer is—yes.”

The grey mist lady smiled casually. “Although it seems that the strength of the awakener is not very impressive compared to the labyrinth of time on Earth, or even in the infinite cycles, Red Pole has told us that, as long as enough awakener is gathered, and enough people burn their blood and stimulate their soul at the same time, a miracle will happen when the strength of their entire life is maximized. It is the key to our escape from the prison of reincarnation!

“A miracle…”

Li Yao frowned. “It sounds like you admire and believe in our leader?”

“Are you trying to say that there are no miracles in the world, that the Red Pole is lying to us, and that he is a con artist?”

The grey mist lady understood what Li Yao was implying, but she was not angry. She smiled and said, “I can understand your suspicion about the Red Pole. After all, it is impossible to be the leader of all the prison breakers without the scent of a ‘trickster’.

“But I can tell you with absolute certainty that Red Pole is not just the leader of this generation. When countless people awaken the memories of their past lives, they will discover at the same time that Red Pole was our leader as early as the previous life, or even in the previous life, all the way back to the past five reincarnations. He used to lead the resistance groups of the Hell Breakers under different names and fought against the will of the earth. Although he failed in every battle, he kept the spark of hope. After the world was restarted, he left valuable information of resistance so that our organization could be rebuilt. His capability grew stronger and stronger after each reincarnation. He looked more and more like the man who could destroy the earth!

“Red Pole, destroy Earth…”

Li Yao sniffed. He was suddenly interested in the leader of the Fangzhou Foundation. He even had a familiar and familiar feeling. What was going on?

“You can imagine how difficult the war must have been for a bunch of seemingly insignificant intelligent carbon-based creatures to fight against the entire Earth.

The grey mist lady said, “If Red Pole had not displayed his unbelievable abilities time and time again to corrupt the reincarnation prison to some extent and preserve the abilities and items of the previous reincarnation to this life, we wouldn’t have been able to persist for so long.

“Besides, what happened just now has proved the Red Pole’s prophecy. Miracles do exist. The will of the earth is already scared!

“What?”

Li Yao said, “I don’t understand.”

“The super-powerful earthquake in the Western Pacific Ocean, the unprecedented tsunami, the super-large typhoons and storms in Jiangnan City, and the thunder that covers the entire world… Do you think these are all normal natural phenomena?”

The grey mist lady said coldly, “An accident of natural disasters might be a natural phenomenon. But the simultaneous arrival of so many natural disasters can only mean one thing. The efforts of the Ark Foundation have paid off. The will of the earth is scared. It cannot allow us to gather more awakeners to create a ‘miracle’. It is preparing to destroy the civilization of mankind in this world in advance and restart the earth in the simplest and crudest way.”

“Ah…”

Li Yao did not know what to say. His head was a mess.

“According to the memory pieces of my more than ten lives, the will of the earth rarely restarts the earth in such a simple and crude way. If it does exist, it prefers to let the civilization of mankind develop naturally and eventually destroy itself.

The grey mist lady pointed at her brain and said, “It is the same every time. From the middle of the twentieth century to the middle of the twenty-first century, the ancient Eastern Empire was revived, and although the iron-blood superpowers in the west were showing signs of decline, they did not lose the ability to take their chances. Besides, they would rather die than allow themselves to fall. In the end, the tiny Earth could not accommodate the ambitions of the two superpowers. The thermonuclear war was declared in the name of holiness, glory, and glory. Human beings would end their own history and civilization without the help of the will of the planet.

“Maybe, for the will of the earth, this is the most resource-saving, cost-free, and natural restarting method. In the past ten reincarnations, at least eight of them have been restarted in this way.

“But this time, the timeline has only just reached 2018. The conflict between the two superpowers has not reached the climax yet. Although the trade frictions have already taken place, it still seems that there is peaceful coexistence, new technologies, and opportunities for new breakthroughs. In short, human beings have not lost their rationality and hope. They have not reached the tipping point of the ‘Third World War’.

“Therefore, the will of the earth cannot rely on human beings to destroy itself. It can only interfere with the laws of nature in the most clumsy, brutal, resource-consuming, and costly way. It will create natural disasters out of thin air and set off ‘apocalypse’ to destroy us. It won’t do that unless it has to.

Chapter 3219: Last War Of The Universe!

“I think I understand.”

Li Yao pondered for a moment and said, “It’s like a very precise but very ancient computer. If you need to, you can reboot it in a conventional way. The advantage is that the components of the computer are not damaged, and most of the data can be retained. The disadvantage is that the speed is too slow. Sometimes, there are faults, and the interface cannot respond. Then, there’s nothing you can do.

“Of course, you can also plug in the power supply and restart it by force. The advantage is that it is fast and can be restarted successfully no matter what. The disadvantage is that it may cause some damage to the machine and lose a lot of data that is not backed up.

“Therefore, generally speaking, the ‘will of the earth’ has chosen the regular, step-by-step approach. But this time, it must’ve run into some kind of problem and decided to restart it by force no matter the cost!

“Yes, your metaphor is very appropriate.”

The grey mist lady smiled and said, “I need to add one more thing. If this precise and ancient super computer is in the middle of an extremely large and complicated task where every computational unit is overloading and billions of data are quickly colliding and burning, if we plug in the power source and restart it by force, it might be even more seriously damaged.”

“Super large and complicated missions?”

Li Yao was dazed. “What is that?”

“Wars. Wars that are beyond our estimation and imagination in the universe.

The grey mist lady said, “Red Pole told us that the Earthlings trapped in the prison of reincarnation, or as they call us, the ‘Heroes of Earth’, are not alone and helpless. In the endless, brilliant universe beyond the prison of reincarnation, many comrades and comrades who share the same ideology as us are waging the last war against the ‘will of the earth’. The war consumed 99% of the will of the earth and gave us the best opportunity after the ‘Battle of the Vulture’. If we can wake up countless heroes and cooperate with them, we might be able to destroy the damn prison and get true freedom and rebirth!”

“Unimaginable wars in the universe?”

Li Yao was more shocked than ever.

Although his words were somewhat absurd and groundless, Li Yao somehow believed them and even pictured the magnificent scenes of the sea of stars.

He seemed to see that all the stars in the universe were releasing the fuel that would take billions of years to run out in one second. The raging flames illuminated the dark, boundless universe into a splendid temple and a battlefield where the heroes were enjoying themselves!

“Student Li Yao, what are your plans next?”

The grey mist lady looked at the ever-changing expression on Li Yao’s face. Her smile remained unchanged, but she extended her hand to him. “After everything you’ve experienced, you wouldn’t say that you want to go back to being an ordinary college student or office worker, would you?”

Li Yao grinned. He had to admit that everything that the Grey Mist Lady and the previous hunters had said had fascinated him. In his brain that was almost boiling, his destiny, which was tied up by flames and thorns, was beginning to reveal itself. He could not turn back and endure the mediocre and repetitive life anymore.

“I don’t know who I was in my previous life, but I have a feeling that my past must have something to do with ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivator’. If possible, I would like to stay with Master Niu and read the latest chapters of ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivator’. I have a feeling that the chapters will make me stronger and bring back more of my past.

Li Yao pondered for a moment and said, “Also, I’m very curious about ‘Red Pole’, your leader. What kind of person is he exactly? How does he know so many things? About Earth, the universe, and even the great war in the sea of stars? Can I see him?”

“Are you sure?”

The grey mist lady stared at Li Yao for a long time and said, “You should know that, to a certain extent, the Ark Foundation is indeed a ‘terrorist organization’ that targets the will of the planet. As the leader of such an organization, our whereabouts are of paramount importance. We can even determine the survival of the organization and the fate of the heroes of the planet. It’s fine if you want to meet the Red Pole, but you must pass a very rigorous test to make sure that you are not an agent sent by the Heaven’s Origin Sector or the will of the planet—the ‘target drug’. Once you see the Red Pole, you will never be able to turn back. We will not give you the opportunity to leak the secrets of the organization.”

Li Yao sensed the killing intent from the grey mist lady.

It did make sense. The Heaven’s Origin Sector was indeed a brutal organization. The Hell Breakers, the foundation of the Ark that could fight against them for countless reincarnations, were apparently not saints, either.

It was especially so when they were well aware that everyone would be reincarnated in the next reincarnation after they were killed. They would have no scruples when they fought.

Li Yao could understand their wariness. After all, their enemy was the ‘will of the earth’. As long as they could lock onto their target, there would be a million ways to kill them. If Li Yao were ‘Red Pole’, he would be even more vigilant than himself.

“Is Red Pole really willing to meet me?”

Li Yao was even somewhat surprised. After all, the guy was the leader of the resistance organization of the Reincarnation Prison, and he was just an unknown Cultivator. The target of the Red Pole Planet was Zhang Daniu. The guy might not take him seriously.

“Yes. We came to Jiangnan City on a mission to bring back the author of 40,000 Years of Cultivation. We didn’t expect to run into you.”

The grey mist lady smiled and said, “We have sent back your files and combat videos, including Zhang Daniu’s description of you when he was hypnotized. The Red Pole Star is very interested in you and wants us to send you to the ‘Ark’ to meet him as soon as possible. Of course, it is to ensure your safety. Is there a problem?”

“…No.”

Li Yao hesitated for a moment, but he soon got rid of the irrelevant thoughts in his head.

At this point, he had no other options.

As wonderful as the real world seemed to be, the feeling of being imprisoned and bound was definitely not pleasant.

No matter how big the prison was, it was still a prison. No matter how many times life was repeated, it was meaningless.

He had to rush out and see the real world, the real universe!

Right then, Li Yao felt that the wheels below him had stopped. Uncle Hui knocked on the door and said, “Ms. Gray Mist, we’re here.”

“Please, Student Li Yao.”

The grey mist lady said courteously, “Judging from the indicators of your body, you should be recovering well. If there are no problems, we will start the first test right now and make sure that you are the one that Red Pole wants.”

Li Yao nodded and stepped on the solid ground.

“Lady Gray Mist…”

Before the door was opened, he asked one last question hesitantly, “There’s one thing that I’m not sure about. Are we… real?”

“Huh?”

The grey mist lady raised her eyebrow. Colorful brilliance beamed out of her grey eyes. “Tell me your question.”

“I mean…”

Li Yao gestured and tried to organize his thoughts. “We mentioned ‘supercomputers’ just now, and we also mentioned ‘rebooting’. The features of the reincarnation prison itself are hard to ignore. It is hard not to wonder whether or not we are living in a virtual world built by computers. We are just irrelevant data, just like what is described in the movie. What is it called again? Right. ‘Twenty-Second Century Killer Network’. That movie can’t be a reflection of reality, can it? So, are we real or are we fake?”

“You should ask yourself that question.

The grey mist lady said, “You don’t even know whether you are telling the truth or not, and you want other people to tell you or even decide for you?”

Li Yao was dazed, not knowing what to say. He felt that he was suddenly enlightened.

“If I tell you that it is not, that we are not living in a virtual world built by computers—what is the significance of such an answer? It is impossible for you to verify it. How do you know whether or not I am right?

The grey mist lady continued, “On the other hand, if I tell you that, yes, we are indeed living in a virtual world built by computers, just like ‘Twenty-Second Century Murderer’s Nexus’, how do you know that I’m not lying? You know that a brilliant hypnotist or even an illusionist can make a real person who is not determined believe that they are living in a virtual world and that they are virtual or even false.

Li Yao opened his mouth but failed to come up with anything.

“Even if the Earth is real, and the cells and genes that make up our body of flesh and blood are real, how do you know that the cells and genes are not the ‘computers’ that a higher-dimensional life form uses? Maybe, what is ‘real’ for the three-dimensional carbon-based life forms is ‘virtual’ for the higher-dimensional life forms, which are data that can be erased and modified at will. Who knows?

The grey mist lady shrugged and said, “Do you think that you are a piece of information carved in flesh, blood, and genes, which makes you more noble, and that the information carved in silicon chips is more lowly?”

“Of course not.”

Li Yao frowned and sorted through his thoughts. “You have a point. Truth and falsehood are defined by myself. I exist. In whatever form, carrier, and method, I am here! I just—I just don’t like the existence of a higher dimension that can erase and modify me at will. Damn things like that happen.”

“Oh.”

“Then let’s fight,” the grey mist lady said. “Just like us, we will fight with the Red Pole until the end. Whatever it is, it will either be destroyed or free!”

Li Yao was dazed again. “That simple?”

“What else could it be?”

The grey mist lady smiled. “It’s as simple as that.”

“True.”

Li Yao tilted his head and thought for a long time. Then he smiled, as if a heavy burden had been lifted off his shoulders. “I was wrong. There is nothing in the world. If the mediocre people bother themselves, they will find their own way out. I only need to follow the steps of my heart and march forward to the bloody end!”

He opened the door and returned to the world that was on the verge of collapse.

Chapter 3220: Teacher Zhang'S Unexpected Calamity

It was a big, unfamiliar city.

Although the rows upon rows of high-rise buildings and the brightly lit city skyline were almost the same as those of Jiangnan City and were similarly stormy with lightning and thunder, as if the entire world had been swallowed by flood and thunder, Li Yao could still tell from the hot and humid climate as well as the tropical plants on the roadside that this place was even closer to the south than Jiangnan City. While he was in a coma, they had fled at least a thousand kilometers away.

As it turned out, the grey mist lady told Li Yao that this was the capital city of the southern hills, as well as the transportation hub of the five or so provinces nearby. It was very convenient to travel across the country or even out of the country by various means of transportation. More importantly, the geologic structure of the area was very stable. No large-scale natural disasters had ever taken place in history. Earthquakes, storms, floods, hail, and tsunamis were rarely seen.

According to the Red Pole Star, if the ‘will of the earth’ was willing to pay a high price, it was theoretically possible to wipe out such a large and stable city that was deep inland. However, it was against the natural disaster that was absolutely impossible. It would consume too much of its strength and might lead to its failure in the ‘war of the universe’. Therefore, they were safe for now.

As he expected, although it was raining heavily and some of the low-lying streets were flooded, the normal functioning mechanism of the city was not affected. It was not completely paralyzed yet.

Li Yao’s destination was a secret office of the Ark Foundation in the downtown area of the city.

Although it was called a ‘secret office’, on the surface, it had a public cover. It was a rather high-class psychological consulting club called ‘Mind Space’.

The grey mist lady told Li Yao that the Heaven’s Origin Sector might know that the ‘mind space’ was a branch of the Ark Foundation. But as she said, the downtown area was their best cover. If the Heaven’s Origin Sector attacked the place without caring about anything else and revealed the existence of the metahumans and their superpowers to the world, it would only stimulate the ignorant human society. The mutation of cancer cells might occur, which was definitely not what the Will of Earth wanted.

Besides, most of the doctors and workers in the ‘mind space’ were ordinary people who knew nothing about the truth. Similar peripheral organizations were all over the world. Even if one of them was destroyed, the main body of the Fangzhou Foundation in the deep sea would not be affected much.

“The Heaven’s Origin Sector suffered heavy casualties in the ambush, too. It will take the hunters at least twenty-four hours to gather enough hands to launch a second attack.

The grey mist lady said to Li Yao, “Let’s rest here for a night and wait for the arrival of the Hell Breakers, who are going to be awakened in several provinces nearby. We will set off tomorrow morning and march southwards across the border. Of course, there is also a batch of advanced equipment here that can examine you.”

“How are you going to test it?”

Li Yao was immediately alarmed.

Although he was biased toward the Ark Foundation and was familiar with Red Pole, it did not mean that he liked to be ‘tested’ when the secrets deep inside his soul were revealed.

“Rest assured.”

The grey mist lady replied, “It won’t be too difficult. We have a very special patient here. I hope you can treat him and relieve his worries. That’s all.”

“Huh?”

Li Yao was dazed. “Wait. What do you mean? Isn’t it a test? Why is it treatment now? Besides, I’m not a psychiatrist. How can I treat your patients? What’s going on?”

“Don’t ask me. I don’t know, either. It was Red Pole’s instruction.

“Red Pole Star said, ‘If you are really you, you must know how to treat the patient.'”

Li Yao was even more confused, but the grey mist lady was equally confused.

Everyone entered the building and took the elevator to the ‘mindspace’ on the thirteenth floor.

This place was not a professional mental treatment facility. It was only set up to relieve the stress of modern people and vent their negative emotions. It looked more like an elegantly decorated house. On the two sides of a long corridor that was filled with bright green lights were five or so ” decompression chambers” in different styles. At this moment, most of the rooms were empty. Only in the innermost room was a middle-aged man with dark eyebrows, swollen eyes, and restless restlessness. He was both anxious and excited.

If not for his absent-mindedness and blurred eyes, this middle-aged man could be said to have a dignified appearance. His every movement was filled with a solemn and scholarly temperament. According to Li Yao’s guess, he should be engaged in an educational profession and was quite successful.

As expected, according to the information that the grey mist lady handed over to him, this middle-aged patient named ‘Zhang Jiashu’ was the head instructor of the No. 12 High School of the city. Before he became the head instructor, he had been the head teacher of the school for many years. His work was serious and responsible, and he treated the students strictly yet with care. The student’s enrollment rate was very high, and he had received the title of city-level outstanding educator multiple times. He could be considered to be a well-acknowledged good teacher. He was upright, resolute, and had principles. His family was also very happy and happy. He was the object of envy for his colleagues and leaders. Logically speaking, there shouldn’t be any psychological problems.

Of course, mental illnesses were unreasonable in the first place.

“What’s wrong with this Teacher Zhang?”

Li Yao looked far ahead through the door and noticed that Zhang Jiashu, the head instructor, was craning his neck and observing the ventilation tube above the ” decompression chamber” with all his concentration. His eyeballs were rolling up and down. He did not have the demeanor of a teacher at all. On the contrary, he looked like a thief.

This kind of very… wretched temperament was very familiar, as if he had seen it somewhere before.

“Teacher Zhang has awakened. It’s just that his awakening is very strange. It’s completely different from the regular awakened ones we’ve discovered. It’s more like… he’s been corrupted by something from the outside and awakened memories and souls that don’t belong to him.”

The grey mist lady handed over a medical record and said, “As you can see, Teacher Zhang was originally a very decent, kind, and traditional person. He had a happy family and a successful career. He could be considered a winner in this life. Generally speaking, such people rarely awaken.

“However, according to Teacher Zhang’s narration, one day, when he was patrolling the classroom, he confiscated one of a student’s cell phone. At that time, the student was secretly using the cell phone to read a novel in the classroom. Naturally, this kind of thing was absolutely forbidden. There was nothing to say even if his cell phone was confiscated. In any case, it would be returned to the student at the end of the semester.

“However, after Teacher Zhang confiscated the student’s cell phone, for some reason, he took a glance and was immediately attracted by the novel inside the cell phone. In his own words, it was like he had entered a devil’s den. He used approximately a week’s time to finish reading the several million words of the novel.

“Naturally, the novel that the student was reading in secret was not a world-famous novel, but a web novel. Teacher Zhang himself graduated from the Chinese Department and taught Chinese literature. Usually, he would rarely read these things… In his original words, these were things that were ‘roughly made, made to fool gods and ghosts’. But this time, I don’t know why, but this novel seemed to possess a terrifying magical power, causing him to forget to sleep and eat day and night. He didn’t even have the mood to do his job.

“Of course, this is not enough for Teacher Zhang to seek help from psychiatrists and psychologists.

“The few days after reading this online novel, Teacher Zhang had a small fever and had nightmares for two to three days. Then, he had many very strange habits.”

Li Yao was fascinated. He couldn’t help but ask, “What habits?”

“Drilling sewers and ventilation tubes.

The grey mist lady said, “According to Teacher Zhang, after this fever, he seems to have the problem of sleepwalking. In the middle of the night, he would often sneak out of the house, open the manhole cover in the neighborhood, and crawl into the sewer to run and explore. It was as if he was like a fish in water in the narrow, dark, and suffocating space. He felt extremely comfortable.

“Later, not just at night, even when he was fully conscious during the day, he could not help but wonder about the ventilation system of every building and how he could crawl in without alarming anybody.

“Teacher Zhang told us that he had really crawled into the ventilation ducts of several buildings in an attempt to avoid everyone’s eyes and ears from invading the most secure space. Every time he retreated, he would break out in cold sweat. With his identity as a teacher, if he was caught in the ventilation ducts, he wouldn’t be able to explain himself even if he had a mouth all over his body. It was really possible that his reputation would be ruined. But he just couldn’t hold back the impulse that came from the depths of his soul.”

“Well…” Li Yao stammered.

“Also, temperament.

The grey mist lady continued, “Teacher Zhang was originally very popular with the students in the school. Although he held the position of ‘Director of Teaching’, a position that had to be strictly enforced, he still won the admiration of many students with his integrity and elegance. However, according to his own narration and our secret investigations, there were indeed many students who believed that Teacher Zhang had become… sleazy, treacherous, and cunning recently. He would use many despicable and shameless tricks to deal with the students. He had a clear understanding of the tricks of the ‘bad students’. He was even worse than the ‘bad students’, causing many students to complain incessantly.

“But sometimes, his brain seemed to be insufficient. His IQ suddenly dropped, and he would blurt out some very childish words. He simply changed from a dignified high school head instructor to a brainless middle school student.”

“Is—is there such a thing?”

“Also, those nightmares, although his fever had long gone, were still haunting him, making him unable to sleep well for several days and nights. Often, when he closed and opened his eyes, he would find himself in the ventilation ducts or the sewers. Teacher Zhang had no choice but to see a psychiatrist. The psychiatrist said that he was middle-aged and his wife’s life was not very harmonious. He could not vent his desires, so subconsciously, he found the sewers and ventilation ducts as… substitutes for his wife. You know.”

The grey mist lady said, “Teacher Zhang scoffed at such a theory, but the disease did exist and had to be treated. After searching for a while, he finally found us.”

“Wait—”

Li Yao frowned. “Let me ask you first. What was the name of the online novel that Teacher Zhang read before his illness acted up?”

“You should’ve guessed it.”

The grey mist lady looked at Li Yao. “It’s ‘40,000 Years of Cultivation’ written by Master Lie Niu.”

Chapter 3221: We Are All Adults

Although Li Yao had guessed the answer, he was still greatly surprised when he heard it from the grey mist lady. “How did it happen?”

“That’s a question that should be asked by you.”

The grey mist lady said, “Since you’ve awakened some amazing memories and abilities after reading ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivator’, why can’t other people awaken them? Why do you think we are in such a hurry to find the author of ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivator’? It’s because there are too many examples.”

“What?”

Li Yao keenly grasped the key point in the grey mist lady’s words. “You mean, you mean to say that it’s not just me and this Teacher Zhang Jiashu. It’s very likely that there are other people, other readers, who have been stimulated, moved, and summoned by this work, have awakened the memories and strength from the ‘Four Universities of Cultivation’?”

“Maybe. The intelligence and missions of ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivators’ are all under the direct command of Red Pole. I didn’t know the existence of the author and Zhang Jiashu, the head instructor, until forty-eight hours ago. Maybe more readers have been awakened or even protected and organized by Red Pole. Who knows?”

The grey mist lady said, “I can only say that in a fantasy world created by an observer, whether it is a novel, a comic, a movie, a TV, or a game, more often than not, more than one Awakener is awakened.

“Countless people were moved, resonated, and awakened on a large scale in the same fantasy world. It was not unusual at all. It was like the ‘World of Stars’ that Garriott created. Maybe he could stimulate and wake up millions of ‘heroic spirits’ at the same time. That was why the Heaven’s Origin Sector had to send out gunmen to kill him in the simplest and crudest way.

“Ms. Gray Mist, do you mean that ‘40,000 Years of Cultivators’ boasts such power, too, that its readers might be, uh, the key to the future of Earth and the entire universe in the Red Pole’s prediction?”

Li Yao did not know how he had grasped the telepathic thoughts of Red Pole naturally. It did not make sense. He just could.

“It’s possible, but only if you are a ‘deep reader’. Readers like me who can’t survive the first 300-500 chapters naturally won’t be able to draw any strength from ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’.

The grey mist lady laughed.

Li Yao smiled, too. He thought for a moment and asked, “Then, other than the fact that Mr. Zhang Jiashu likes to crawl through ventilation tubes and sewers, and that his temperament is more… sleazy, does he have any other superpowers? I mean those that are particularly good at fighting, such as blowing up a wall with one punch or jumping seven to eight feet high, or something like that. Are they as invincible as me?”

“No. Not everyone is like you, who can fight against a ‘hunter’ as soon as they are awakened. You’ve simply completed an impossible task. There are no suitable hands or equipment here to run the most precise tests on you. But I estimate that you are far more than a ‘Deep Perception’. You are at least a ‘Ultimate Perception’, if not a ‘Ultimate Perception’, which is the highest level!”

The grey mist lady said, “Most of the awakened ones are the same as Master Zhang Jiashu without any guidance. They are plagued by hallucinations, auditory hallucinations, and dreams that are both real and hallucinations. They may have neurotic and eccentric habits, but their combat ability might not be significantly improved.

“Besides, you should be able to tell that Master Zhang Jiashu is very resistant to this kind of… memory and strength derived from the . He simply does not want to awaken, and he does not even believe that all of this is true. Under these circumstances, it is even more difficult for him to display the corresponding strength.”

“Then what are you going to do?”

Li Yao asked, “Should we bring him back to the ‘Ark’?”

“Of course not. What do you think our organization is? A garbage recycling center where everybody is welcome? To be a real ‘prison breaker’, we need the most firm belief, the most tenacious will, and the most clear-minded thinking. We don’t need someone who is not determined and resists us to sabotage our mission from the inside.

The grey mist lady said, “Originally, the Red Pole Star came up with a very complicated plan that would consume a lot of resources, with a low success rate, to treat Master Zhang Jiashu. They even considered asking him to talk to the author of ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivators’ directly to see if there would be any sparks. But now that you are here, according to the Red Pole Star, you are the best candidate to treat Master Zhang Jiashu, and the treatment will give you a clearer understanding of yourself.”

“I—I don’t know what to do at all!”

Li Yao frowned. “What can I do to wake him up?”

“You will never wake up a person who is pretending to be asleep. We have been wandering in the labyrinth of infinite reincarnation for so long that many people have lost the courage to look for the exit. They even deny the existence of the exit and fear the world outside. Therefore, I will not force you to wake up Master Zhang Jiashu.”

The grey mist lady handed a green coat to Li Yao, which was sort of the uniform of a consultant. “Please. Listen to your heart and let nature take its course.”

“But—”

Li Yao forced himself to put on his coat. “At my age and with such a bald head, I don’t look like a consultant at all!”

“It doesn’t matter. Red Pole has faith in you. As I said before, ‘As long as you are you, everything will be fine’.”

The grey mist lady pushed Li Yao into the room without further ado. “Right. Special barriers have been installed to the top and bottom of the ‘ decompression chamber’. They can create a very marvelous magnetic field that can help those who enter it achieve ‘resonance’ on the brainwaves level. According to Red Pole, such ‘resonance’ can help you treat your wounds and help you find yourself faster. I hope you don’t mind.”

Li Yao had no time to care. The door closed behind him. He and the tortured Teacher Zhang Jiashu stared at each other.

Zhang Jiashu was deeply immersed in the painful and terrifying memories, unable to free himself. He was like a drowning man who had sunk deep into a swamp. His two bloodshot eyes were about to explode. He swept a glance at Li Yao. He didn’t care about Li Yao’s overly young face and bald head. He was once again immersed in his own world.

With just a glance, Li Yao had a very strange feeling, as if he was looking in a mirror.

He and Zhang Jiashu were not related at all, nor did they have any similarities. But the moment their eyes met, he felt that he was looking at himself in a mirror, a more real version of himself. How weird!

“Hello, Teacher Zhang.”

Li Yao sat down in front of Zhang Jiashu and recalled the description of the psychological consultant in the movies. He tried to put on a professional smile and said seriously, “My surname is Li. I’ve been studying in the San Diego Psychiatric Research Center for a while. I just returned to the country this morning. Well, I have a very special study on sewers and ventilation tubes. How are you feeling? Do you still dream of sewers and ventilation tubes?”

Zhang Jiashu’s reaction was a bit slow. When he heard Li Yao mention the word ‘dream’, the muscles all over his body visibly trembled for a moment, as if he was extremely afraid of recalling the dream. Only after a long time did he weakly say, “… No, I don’t feel good at all. On the contrary, I feel even worse. Other than the sewers and ventilation ducts, I also dream of something very strange, very shameful, and terrifying to the point that it can’t be described with words. I’m about to go mad. I have a premonition that if this goes on, I will definitely go mad. Save me, doctor, save me!”

“I’m not a doctor. I’m just a consultant.

Li Yao smiled. “However, why don’t you tell me what you’ve been dreaming about first, Professor Zhang? Maybe I can help you with the special hypnotizing techniques and meditation techniques in San Diego. This, this ‘guidance’.”

Zhang Jiashu wanted to say something but hesitated. It could be seen that intellectual integrity and the reserve of a teacher were still trying to support his normal thinking.

But his fear of the weird dream and the craziness made him let go of his reservations quickly. His lips trembled as he said, “I—I’ve been having a new weird dream for the past two days. It was very, very weird, terrifying, disgusting, and disgusting. I—I—I dreamed that I was watching a movie.

“Well…”

Li Yao was slightly stunned. He controlled his smile precisely. “There’s nothing disgusting about this, much less disgusting, right? We’re all adults here. Let’s watch some adult movies that adults are supposed to watch. Relax your muscles, ease your spleen, eliminate the poison, strengthen your heart and lungs, and maintain the dynamic balance between endocrine and hormones. Not only will it help maintain the harmony and love between husband and wife, it will also help you devote yourself to work and study without any distractions… There’s no problem. It’s very normal. You must not be under so much pressure. This is really very normal, very reasonable, and very reasonable.”

“You don’t understand. You don’t know what I dreamed at all!

Zhang Jiashu covered his face with his hands and almost lost control. He shrugged and said, “I—I dreamed that the movies I watched were all about men!”

“Well…”

Li Yao chose his words carefully. “This is the 21st century. Science is flourishing, and humanity is liberated. Many things have already been cleared of criminalization and non-criticism. They are just personal hobbies and choices. As long as you don’t hurt others, there’s no need to be too conflicted. Modern society is under so much pressure. Everyone has a lot of wild thoughts. There’s no need to be nervous.”

“You still don’t understand. I have no doubts about my sexual orientation. My wife and I have been married for more than twenty years. We are very in love. I only like women!”

Zhang Jiashu raised his voice like a little animal that had just been humiliated. His voice was shaking as he said, “But I just can’t control myself. I feel that an evil brand has been carved into my soul. Every time I enter a dream, some disgusting, disgusting adult movies will be played over and over. Even if they are measured by the standard of non-mainstream sexual orientation, the content is too hardcore. They are all… scenes of a man who is surrounded by purple flames and can’t be more dominating, as if he is the king of the universe who has stomped on the stars and controlled the entire universe, humiliated and tortured by dozens of muscular men. No. I can’t think about it. I feel like vomiting. Help me. Doctor, help me!”

“What?”

Li Yao blurted out, “You dreamed of Wu Yingqi’s porn?”

“What?”

Zhang Jiashu was dazed, too. “How do you know my dream? How do you know what it is… Black Star Emperor Wu Yingqi? Have you seen it before?”

“Well…” Li Yao stammered.

Chapter 3222: First Fusion

Li Yao and Teacher Zhang Jiashu once again stared at each other. The air was filled with a subtle and awkward atmosphere.

That’s right. Li Yao also didn’t know how he had blurted out these words… What the hell is this “Black Star Emperor Wu Yingqi’s Hairpiece”? What the hell is this!?

That’s right. Of course, Li Yao knew of the character ‘Black Star Emperor Wu Yingqi’. He was a character in the and was the founder of the Imperium of True Human Beings. He should have died a thousand years ago in the timeline of the novel. When several Immortal Cultivators of the Kunlun Chapter had just appeared, it had been mentioned that the Immortal Cultivator Su Changfa had even let the protagonist ‘Cultivator Li Yao’ enter an illusion and watched the scenes of his rise and rise.

From the novel’s description, this was a sinister, cunning, and ambitious character. According to the author’s writing style, it was very likely that he would be resurrected later and become the mortal enemy of the protagonist, “Cultivator Li Yao”. Of course, he would be defeated by the protagonist using hot-bloodedness, seed explosion, breakthrough in battle, and so on. These were all conventional methods, so there was no need to elaborate.

However, what the hell was ‘Black Star Emperor Wu Yingqi’s Hairpiece’? How absurd, shameless, and sordid was the plot of a novel?

Li Yao felt that his outlook on life, his outlook on the world, and his values had collapsed for the second time after they were shattered by Zhang Daniu.

And in the powder, a series of unsightly scenes emerged from the depths of his soul. They were all scenes of Black Star Emperor fighting valiantly against a group of heroes and engaging in a war of attrition. It simply broke the bottom line of all ethics and ethics. It was outrageous to the extreme!

“What—what is this? Why is there such a thing hidden in the depths of my soul? Ah! Ah! My eyes! My eyes!”

Li Yao was about to go crazy.

He vaguely recalled that this was the price he had to pay in order to defeat a strong enemy in the past. It was a very serious mental trauma, a sequela that no secret technique could eradicate.

“I… No. It was not me. It was the sleazy Cultivator Li Yao. How did he defeat Black Star Emperor Wu Yingqi? How sordid, how sordid, and how shameless!

Li Yao’s face was pale as he clutched his chest. He felt that he was the same as Teacher Zhang Jiashu. His humanity was about to be twisted, and his morals were about to sink!

“Who are you? Who are you exactly? How do you know what I dreamed? I’ve never told anyone such a shameful dream. Never!”

Zhang Jiashu was in a trance again. He stared at Li Yao, with disgust and fear in his eyes. “You—you hypnotized me? Why do I find you familiar, and why do I hate you so much? Ah! Ahhhhh! You are not a human being! You are not a human being! You are not a human being!”

He seemed to have seen an incomparably strange scene. His entire body shrunk to a corner as he shivered.

“Calm down, Teacher Zhang. Calm down!”

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao tried to control the situation beyond his control and managed a smile. “What did you see exactly? Why did you say that I was not a human being?”

“You are a bacterium. You are… a virus!”

Zhang Jiashu mumbled to himself, “I saw that your body was covered in… bright gold flagella, and you were waving your hands and waving your arms, ready to attack. What are you exactly? These—these are all illusions, right? Help me get rid of these damn illusions!”

“Bacteria, viruses, flagella?”

Li Yao found it harder and harder to understand what Zhang Jiashu was talking about, but in his heart, he didn’t hate Zhang Jiashu’s explanation. He thought for a moment and tried to calm himself down. “Master Zhang, it seems that we’ve both encountered some problems. Why don’t we work together and explore the mysteries behind the problems? Maybe a new, more beautiful, exciting, and promising world will be opened in front of you?”

“Who the hell wants to explore a new world with you? It’s all an illusion. You can’t fool me!”

Zhang Jiashu suddenly burst into fury. He waved his hands crazily in the air, as if he were trying to tear apart an illusion that did not exist. “What are you talking about? What new world? I don’t understand and I don’t want to understand. I just want to live my own life peacefully and peacefully. Don’t bother me. Don’t bother me. Get lost. Get lost now!”

“What if I told you that such a peaceful, happy life was fake and under someone else’s control?

Li Yao said, “Whether you like it or not, the storm has arrived, and the gate to a new world has been opened.

While talking, Li Yao took out a pen from his pocket and put it in his palm.

He did not know why he was doing this. An inexplicable urge was controlling him.

Before this, other than his immense strength and soaring computational ability, he had never awakened the ability to manipulate objects remotely.

But when he saw Zhang Jiashu, especially when he shattered the layers of seals with the movie of Black Star Emperor Wu Yingqi and blew countless interconnecting gaps on his soul that had been hibernating for a long time, through which the power that could shake the sea of stars surged out and circulated around his body, he suddenly felt that he was omnipotent and could do anything.

Li Yao focused his attention on his palm. From the back of his glabella, where his pineal gland was located, bright golden threads slithered out and wrapped around the pen.

The meditation was as natural as a heartbeat and as easy as breathing.

Pulled by the spiritual threads, the pen floated silently and stopped three inches away from his palm.

“Look.”

Li Yao smiled at Zhang Jiashu.

Zhang Jiashu was dumbfounded and became even more furious. He suddenly pounced over and slapped away the pen on Li Yao’s palm. He shouted hysterically, “Illusion, magic tricks, magic tricks! What—what are you trying to do? Are you a counselor or not? I just want to solve the problem. Don’t show me these weird things. I want to go back to my old life. I want to change back to myself. I am Zhang Jiashu, the dean of No. 12 High School!”

Li Yao stared at the pen on the ground and sighed. He finally understood what the grey mist lady meant.

It was true that he could not wake someone who was pretending to be asleep. Those who had been reincarnated in the labyrinth of time for too long could not tell who they really were.

Was it wrong that Zhang Jiashu was willing to live a peaceful and happy life?

However, it was unnecessary and unqualified for him to have such precious memories and strength.

Hum! Hum! Hum! Hum! Hum!

Li Yao seemed to hear feeble quakes coming from the top and bottom of the ‘ decompression chamber’. The brainwave interference equipment installed in the mezzanine, or ‘magical equipment’, must’ve been activated.

In the meantime, Li Yao could clearly see through the ‘spiritual eye’ in the middle of his eyebrow that there was indeed a tiny, hot, glittering shard sealed inside Zhang Jiashu’s brain.

The fragment sensed Li Yao’s existence, too, and became more and more agitated. It was bouncing inside Zhang Jiashu’s brain, trying to crawl out of his glabella.

“Ah!”

Zhang Jiashu held his head and cried in pain, “What are the pictures, the sounds, and the nonsense?”

Li Yao did not know what it was exactly, either. He felt that it was a small piece of a mirror in which a tiny person was reflecting him.

Just as magnets would attract each other and small magnets would be absorbed by large magnets, Li Yao naturally placed his burning palm on Zhang Jiashu’s forehead.

“Alright. Such strength and the pain and responsibility it brings are indeed too much for you. It was never yours in the first place. Now that you are unwilling to accept it, give it back and let me drive away your pain!”

Li Yao mumbled to himself. His eyes gradually turned transparent, but rainbow colors were blossoming deep inside his eyes.

Looking at his own arm and Zhang Jiashu’s brain with such colorful and splendid eyes, he seemed to be able to see that his countless nerves and veins were all emitting bright gold colors and extending to his palm and his fingertips. Then, he communicated with Zhang Jiashu’s brain and established a stable bridge between his soul and the fingertip buried deep inside Zhang Jiashu’s brain.

The piece that had been bothering Zhang Jiashu for a long time was attracted by the attraction force inside Li Yao’s body. It entered Li Yao’s body smoothly through the bridge of nerves and veins and melted into Li Yao’s brain.

“AHHHHHHHHHHH!”

This time, it was Li Yao’s turn to let out an unbearable scream. In the blink of an eye, an astronomical amount of information exploded in his soul. Countless near-death flashbacks fell down like a waterfall. He clearly saw many, many things. The Federation, the Imperium, the Covenant Alliance, Ding Lingdang, Long Yangjun, Li Jialing, Wu Yingqi, the furiously burning battlefield in the sea of stars, a world made entirely of lightning… Some of it had already been serialized in , and some of it had never been even mentioned!

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Li Yao felt that billions of stars were exploding inside his brain. He and Zhang Jiashu were separated by a weird force and hit the wall hard.

…

When Li Yao woke up from his trance again, he found that he had become the protagonist of the ” decompression room”. He was sitting cross-legged on the sofa in a very comfortable posture. The grey mist lady and a few other awakened Cultivators were looking at him nervously.

Li Yao forced a smile at the grey mist lady and asked, “How is Teacher Zhang?”

“Excellent. The problems that have been bothering me for days are all gone now. I’m only a little weak and nauseated right now. I’ll be fine after I refill my glucose and electrolytes.”

The grey mist lady said, “We are more concerned about you. How do you feel?”

“I…”

Li Yao felt that he had pushed open a door in the depths of his brain. A new world was slowly opening to him. He didn’t know how to describe this state, so he simply opened his palm and stared at it.

Hu!

In a moment, a cluster of red flames dashed out of his palm. The flames surged to the sky and condensed into a tiny butterfly, which flapped its wings and tried to fly away.

Chapter 3223: The True Meaning Of Water

The butterfly was so tiny yet so magnificent. Its wings that were as thin as the wings of a cicada seemed to be able to raise a storm that could sweep the entire planet.

Even Li Yao did not expect that such a thing would fly out of his palm. He stared at the dancing butterflies in a daze and smiled knowingly.

It had been a long time since he last saw such brilliant flames. The flames that once savaged the sea of stars and illuminated the entire universe were now burning and releasing on Earth, illuminating the sky above the six billion souls, weren’t they?

“This is—”

The grey mist lady and the few other awakeners were deeply shocked by Li Yao’s performance.

Activating the mitochondria and releasing the strength deep inside the cells was a very common ability.

However, the precise control over the fire, which was shaped into the shape of a butterfly and dancing in the wind according to its master’s will, was many times better than that of ordinary fireballs.

Besides, most awakened Cultivators had to train themselves hard and awaken deeper memories with the help of hypnotists and other machines in order to master such strength. Li Yao had only been awakened for three to five days and had not gone through any formal training at all!

“The Red Pole Star was right. You are the only one besides him who can treat Master Zhang Jiashu. Are you really the legendary ‘super perceptive Cultivator’? No wonder the Red Pole Star values you so much other than Zhang Daniu.

Li Yao was not lying. The information that flooded into his brain was too complicated and furious. His brain, which had just woken up, could not process such complicated information yet. Although bright spots were appearing in the mist, the road ahead of him was still hazy, and he had not figured out the answer yet.

A deep sorrow and pain suddenly appeared on Li Yao’s face. “I only vaguely remember that I was a man who carried a heavy destiny. With a solemn face, the grey mist lady nodded her head. “Just like ‘Black Star Emperor Wu Yingqi’s Hairpiece’, right? Speaking of which, what exactly is ‘Hairpiece’?”

Although the grey mist lady was fluent in Chinese, it was impossible for her to grasp all the vulgar dialects.

Having awakened more memory fragments, his soul had become even stronger. He could not wait to meet the author of .

Zhang Daniu was one floor below them. He was protected by a few fully-armed, tough-looking Awakened.

Calculating the time, the two of them had not seen each other for more than forty-eight hours, but they felt that a long time had passed. The way they looked at each other was both strange and familiar.

Zhang Daniu seemed to be quite used to the protection of the Ark Foundation. One of his ‘bodyguards’ was a golden-haired beauty who was wearing a tight-fitting black suit and had slender legs like a leopardess.
