He was so happy that he was grinning from ear to ear.

In the battle against the hunter, because of Li Yao’s protection, other than the shock, Zhang Daniu’s body was not injured at all. Therefore, there was no need for bed treatment. Over the past forty-eight hours, he had only performed a very detailed brain scan on the mobile medical vehicle of the Ark Foundation and transmitted the parameters of his brain waves to the ‘Ark’. Then, under the remote manipulation of the Red Pole Star, he was put into a deep hypnotic state where his brain was activated. It was a regular training method for the awakened, which could help the awakened recall the more ancient memories from dozens or even hundreds of cycles ago.

When Li Yao arrived, Zhang Daniu was writing a novel.

After being activated by the Ark Foundation, his ‘superpowers’ seemed to have been further upgraded. In a certain mysterious ‘semi-conscious’ state, his thoughts were still flowing like a spring, and his fingers were typing on the keyboard so fast that cracking noises were echoing nonstop.

His entire body seemed to be enveloped in a layer of extremely special force field, causing Li Yao to not dare to easily interrupt his train of thought. It’s been more than ten hours, and I don’t feel any pain or fatigue… The Ark Foundation’s ointment really works!”

Li Yao quickly flipped through Zhang Daniu’s newly written content. Sure enough, within a short span of one day, he had actually written several tens of thousands of words. Just a moment ago, about half an hour ago, I vaguely heard a humming sound coming from upstairs. It might as well be a spoiler. Hehehehe. If you really want to hear it…”

Other than adding a bit of water, wait for me to simplify a bit and I can send it out.”

Your ability is to collect important information wandering in the universe and present the information in the most appropriate way. But the so-called ‘universal information’ is very dangerous stuff, like venomous and highly-radiating substances. Chapter 3224: Global Natural Disaster

Li Yao pondered over the grey mist lady’s words. It sounded like, other than ‘memories and information’ that could possibly escape ‘reboot’, even the items themselves could possibly escape reincarnation and be left with countless generations.

Of course, from a different point of view, matter and energy could be transformed into each other in the first place. The so-called matter was merely the condensation and manifestation of energy in a certain way, which was the information itself.

Everything was information. As long as the information could pass through countless cycles without perishing, it was possible to keep the item for thousands of years.

He was going to ask more questions about how to keep the materials in the ‘reboot’ of the Ark Foundation, when the grey mist lady projected a giant screen on the white wall in front of them, showing them the news around the world.

In the twenty-four to forty-eight hours of Li Yao’s coma, the entire world was affected by the super catastrophe. The order of human society had been disrupted.

In Japan, a super earthquake of incalculable intensity struck the Edo Bay again. Not only was the population of more than ten million people affected, the top three metropolises in the world were also flooded. The entire Guan Dong region was shivering in the storm and tsunami.

According to the preliminary analysis of the global earthquake experts, the intensity of the earthquake was even higher than the 9.5-magnitude earthquake near the coast of Chile. It was the first earthquake in the history of mankind.

In the videos posted by the news media and the netizens, it could be seen that the prosperous city, which had been brightly lit and packed with people two days ago, had turned into a lifeless cemetery. The skyscrapers had turned into messy tombstones. The explosions resulting from the leakage of natural gas tubes were like dim phosphorescence flowing out of bones. The seawater was savaging the flooded streets like ferocious animals. Millions of people had been soaked into white, swollen balls and became food for the fish and shrimps.

On the satellite map, the islands of Fusang, which used to be in the shape of a crescent moon, seemed to have been bitten by an enormous beast from the east, while the tusks made of tsunamis and storms were still tearing the flesh and blood in the hinterland of the islands unscrupulously. The tragedies that had been foretold in the legendary novel ‘The Islands Sink’, written by the famous writer of science fiction in the old days, were unfolding slowly and unstoppably in front of everyone.

“The super earthquake seems to have activated hundreds of dormant volcanoes all over the islands. Many of them are showing signs of eruption. The grey mist lady showed a series of pictures to Li Yao and Zhang Daniu. In the information era, natural disasters dealt ten times more damage to the human heart than in the past.

Although the natural disasters caused the network paralysis in many places, a lot of terrifying information was still transmitted out and turned into ‘panic plagues’. Many countries were on the verge of losing control.

Reading to this point, Li Yao vaguely understood why Red Pole had set up the nest of the resistance organization on a super tank.

The super tankers were lone islands that were naturally cut off from the rest of the world. As long as they were sailing in the deep sea, they could conceal themselves in special ways and avoid the interference of natural disasters. The so-called tsunamis and giant waves often only existed in the continental shelf where the seawater was relatively shallow. To raise a catastrophe that could destroy the super tankers thousands of meters deep in the middle of the ocean would consume astronomical energy.

Chapter 3225: Heading South

Li Yao wanted to say that there was a problem.

Maybe because he had awakened too many memories of the past, he felt that something was not right. A sense of danger enveloped him like a mist.

Right then, a shrill alarm echoed in the storm outside the building. It did not die down until almost a minute later.

Very soon, an Awakener came in and reported the bad news to everybody. Because of the heavy rain, the reservoir in the upper reaches of the city was far higher than the alert level. A dam had collapsed. It wouldn’t be long before the city was flooded.

Li Yao swallowed his words.

Desperate times called for desperate measures. They had no other options. The sooner they left the continent, the better.

Li Yao did not sleep well that night.

All kinds of weird hallucinations were popping up and hovering in his eyes and mind like annoying flies.

His body, which had been further awakened to inhuman strength, was experiencing all kinds of weird uncoordinated feelings, like a malfunctioning machine that needed him to get used to again.

Pictures and videos of natural disasters from all over the world, as well as countless posts that were adding fuel to the fire and asking for more trouble, made him even more worried. He had a feeling that a great catastrophe was coming and there was nowhere to run to.

It was not until dawn that he fell asleep for a while. But soon, he was woken up by the muscular man named ‘Sparda’, one of the companions of the grey mist lady.

Li Yao didn’t know what kind of superpowers this ‘Spartan’ had compared to Uncle Hui, but looking at the helmet and armor on his head, the heavy body armor that was filled with anti-explosion plug-ins, and the sci-fi-styled guns on his back gave him a sense of security.

/ .

While the sky was still dark and hazy, the group quietly left the downtown area and headed towards the train station in the suburbs. It was not the bus station that people frequented, but rather an unused freight train station.

Because of the approaching flood and the torrential rain, most of the passenger trains to and from the city had already been suspended. The few routes were packed with people. To take the passenger trains, one had to provide their name, identity card, and other sensitive information. They also had to go through tedious and strict security checks. The humming “lighter” on Li Yao’s waist alone was absolutely impossible to pass through the inspection. If there was a conflict, it would only cause more trouble and delay his precious time.

Therefore, the grey mist lady and the Ark Foundation behind her pulled some strings and got a freight coach. They were asked to leave the place on the freight train as escorts.

Although the conditions of the freight train were not as comfortable as those of passenger trains, the advantage was that all the joints had been broken through. When they arrived at the station, it did not take long before the train was slowly started. Moreover, the space on the train was spacious enough. The entire coach belonged to them, allowing them to calmly arrange defensive measures.

What was even better was that the carriage in front of them was loaded with three specially-modified off-road vehicles. The condition of the vehicles was very good. The fuel tanks were filled with fuel. All kinds of weapons, equipment, and survival tools were on board. Even if something happened to the train, it could abandon the railway and drive on.

The arrangement of the grey mist lady gave Li Yao a more direct understanding of Fang Zhou’s foundation.

However, it did not mean that he could relax much about the perilous journey.

Hua! Hua! Hua! Hua!

Although they had left the city and travelled hundreds of kilometers to the south, the torrential rain did not show any signs of stopping. As far as one’s eyes could reach, it was as if the sky of a million square kilometers was covered in dark clouds. The deep black clouds were like a broken reservoir, pouring large water sources that carried electric currents into the human world.

It was 9:00 a.m. in the morning. The sky was bright and the earth was clear, but the world was still stuck in the bleak darkness. The dark clouds and the mountains far away melded together, giving the impression that the world was collapsing.

The highway parallel to the rail line had completely collapsed. Li Yao saw countless cars blocking the highway. People were crouching in the cars in desperation, looking at the boundless water, unable to move.

Many of the roads had even been divided into lone islands by the flood. Human beings, who were the spirits of all creatures, appeared to be insignificant and powerless in front of the might of nature.

Thankfully, the railway had been newly built a few years ago. Most of the road was built on a high platform ten to twenty meters above the ground. The train was also carrying a lot of supplies that the south was in dire need of. Therefore, the train could still grit its teeth and move forward in the storm and lightning.

However, the air was so heavy that it was about to explode. Everybody was worried that the train might not make it to the border station in the south.

The scene of wandering in the dark night gave Li Yao a very familiar feeling.

He vaguely remembered that the legend of ‘Cultivator Li Yao’ originated from a speeding train in the Thunderstorm Zone of the Star Glory Federation.

If one were to look at it from a different point of view, with his physique that could stir up trouble as well as the fact that he was carrying a crucial person on a train, as well as the lightning and thunder that seemed to be the end of the world, it would be quite strange if nothing happened to him.

Li Yao shook his head hard and pushed the messy thoughts out of his mind. He sized up everyone in the coach.

From the moment he got on the train, Zhang Daniu had been crouching in a corner with his back facing everyone. He typed on the keyboard and continued writing the later chapters of .

Uncle Hui, on the other hand, was reading a comic with rapt attention. Li Yao glanced at the cover. Uh, the name of the comic was .

The muscular man nicknamed ‘Sparta’ was dismantling and touching his weapon. Compared to Li Yao’s hands, which were moving as fast as the wind, his movements were quite slow, but they were full of emotions, as if he were not touching cold steel but his lover’s ass.

The grey mist lady, on the other hand, was operating a specially-modified laptop. She seemed to be communicating with the nest of the Ark Foundation and receiving further instructions from ‘Red Pole’. What was weird was that the wires of the laptop were connected to the head of ‘Ghost Cat’, the last awakened one, and two magnetic patches were stuck to her temples.

What did it mean? Was it safer to transmit information in such a way by treating the human brain as a certain ‘antenna’?

Li Yao was confused.

Perhaps sensing his anxiety and confusion, the grey mist lady closed her computer and sat next to Li Yao again after she finished the information transmission.

Li Yao took the opportunity to ask her about the… ‘fantasy weapons’. Why did the Ark Foundation keep so many weapons that were apparently beyond the level of modern technology? Where did they come from?

The grey mist lady was not clear about the mechanism, either. She could only give Li Yao an example. It was like an area inside a computer that could not be deleted no matter how hard it was formatted. Even though the area seemed to have been erased or altered beyond recognition, all the information inside the area could be restored as long as the key instructions were grasped.

The sci-fi, magical, and even fantasy weapons that seemed to be beyond the era should have been the products of the glamorous and unknown era before the ‘original reincarnation’. They had been created by the heroic spirits in the beginning and hidden in the indestructible space with secret arts. Every time the reincarnation was restarted, the prison breakers would awaken their ancient memories and find the lost ultimate weapons.

To this day, Li Yao was still unclear about the classification of the strength of the awakened. Therefore, Ms. Gray Mist simply took the opportunity to give a brief introduction to Li Yao.

Whether it was the Hell Breakers or the Prison Guardians, they were all classified into the same level of awakening. Those who had just awakened and could vaguely recall the first five reincarnations were called ‘beginners’, those who could recall six to ten reincarnations were called ‘deepers’, and those who could recall more than ten reincarnations were called ‘finalists’.

The reason for the division was that the heroes imprisoned in the prison of reincarnation were weaker and weaker as they were reincarnated more and more. The fact that they could retrieve more ancient memories meant that they had stronger abilities, unbelievable techniques, and were almost as destructive as the deities.

In this life, the awakening of the memories from more than ten reincarnations was the limit for most of the heroes. Even if somebody was able to awaken the memories from dozens of reincarnations, they were often mottled, fragmented, and as insignificant as sand and dust. Therefore, the ‘finalists’ who had awakened more than ten lives would be the best warriors of the ‘Ark’ and the ‘apocalypse’.

Of course, there were exceptions.

It was said that there was a type of Awakened who could transcend thousands of reincarnations and awaken to the early memories of roaming the sea of stars and shaking the universe before they were imprisoned. They vaguely touched upon the truth of the Infinite Earth and the Reincarnation Prison, which was the legendary ‘super perceptive’.

Although it had never been explicitly acknowledged, all the members of the Ark Foundation had tacitly acknowledged that Red Pole, their leader, was an ESP.

The ‘prophecy’ of the leader of the Heaven’s Origin Sector, on the other hand, was a whole different matter. As the claws and teeth of the will of the earth, he was not necessarily an ESP. It was possible that he was a projection or incarnation of the will of the earth.

But right now, the capability that Li Yao demonstrated, including the special attention that Red Pole paid to him, made the grey mist lady suspect that Li Yao was also a super perceptive Cultivator. Of course, Red Pole had to confirm it in person.

Li Yao did not know what he was exactly, either.

Although countless memory pieces of ‘Cultivator Li Yao’ were hovering inside his brain, there was something very weird in every dream-like picture—the enormous silver-white balloon was like a 3D mirror that reflected his twisted soul.

Then what was it exactly?

‘Cultivator Li Yao’ should not have experienced such a weird thing.

Li Yao had confessed the existence of the weird balloon to the grey mist lady so that she or Red Pole could analyze it and find the answer.

But he somehow swallowed his words in the end.

Right then, shrill alarms were echoing on the top of the train. Something was coming at them from the sky!

Chapter 3226: : Fighting A Hunter Again

“Chasers!”

With a solemn face, the grey mist lady said solemnly, “The hunters have caught up to us!”

It was not surprising that the Heaven’s Origin Sector was chasing after them.

However, the enemy had chosen the perfect time and place. Right now, the storm and the thunderstorm were at their fiercest. In front of them, there was a dangerous road made of dozens of tunnels and rail bridges across several mountains. The rail bridges were built in the middle of the continuous mountains and had a height difference of almost a hundred meters. Below them were raging rivers. Because of the continuous storms, even the previously tranquil streams had turned into raging dragons. Even the super experts who had awakened the memories and combat ability of their previous lives would not be able to survive the surging flood if they fell into them.

In the blink of an eye, the train had already reached the first railway bridge. There was no turning back.

“Li Yao, Sparda, take the lead!”

The grey mist lady opened a gap in the carriage. Earsplitting shrieks mixed with the sound of wind and rain immediately flooded in. The world seemed to have turned into an ocean. The stinky ‘sea water’ filled the entire carriage in the blink of an eye and was even tearing apart the walls, exposing them to the lightning and thunder.

Zhang Daniu once again failed to live up to expectations and screamed.

The awakener nicknamed ‘Ghost Cat’ hurried to press his hands on Li Yao’s temples. A soft blue brightness surged out of his palms and calmed him down, making him drowsy again.

It seemed that the supernatural ability of the ‘Ghost Cat’ leaned toward mental, hypnotic, and brainwave interference. It was indeed a useful ability. No wonder she was qualified to join the escort team as a ‘Deep Perceptive One’.

This was not the time for deliberation. Besides, the grey mist lady had really turned into a cluster of grey mist and crawled out of the gap. Li Yao had no time to think. He clutched the light sword on his waist and took a long-range weapon that ‘Spartan’ handed over to him. Then he crawled out, too.

The torrential rain outside the train was like invisible iron hammers that ruthlessly hammered the people clinging to the walls and roof of the train.

Bolts of lightning fell around the rails one after another. Some of them were even attracted by the electrical components at the front end of the train and set off dazzling sparks.

Together with the bottomless abyss on the two sides of the railway bridge, it was truly a nightmarish scene that one would never be able to forget after one glance.

If they were ordinary people who had not been awakened, even the well-trained elite soldiers would find it difficult to move in such harsh environments, let alone fight.

Even an Awakener such as Li Yao found it hard to raise his head in the storm.

When he finally reached the top of the train, he saw a weird guy who was wearing a combat raincoat and a pair of crimson goggles. The guy’s name was ‘Spider’, and he was the pick-up staff that the Ark Foundation had arranged at the train station. After the train started moving, he had been observing and guarding the train from the top of the train. It was quite possible that he had a certain ability that allowed him to be fearless of the wind and rain.

“Look up!”

The spider gestured and pointed at the sky.

Li Yao wiped off the cold rain on his face and raised his head, trying to keep his eyes open.

At first, he saw nothing except darkness. But when a lightning tore apart the sky and illuminated the earth, he saw everything!

They looked like dozens of giant eagles.

It looked like a glider or a giant drone.

Li Yao did not know what kind of drone could fly stably in such bad weather and track a speeding train precisely.

However, when he thought of the Ark Foundation, which boasted technology and fantasy weapons beyond the era and was blessed by the ‘will of the earth’, he realized that the Heaven’s Origin Sector would not fight with only the weapons of the twenty-first century.

Huala!

Another thunderclap sent a series of upside-down lightning bolts to the ground, which lasted more than ten seconds and allowed Li Yao and the rest of them to see the pursuers clearly.

It was a human being!

The Awakener of the Heaven’s Origin Sector was wearing a ‘wingsuit’ that looked like a mole. He was also carrying an enormous jet pack on his back. Broad metal wings were spread out on the two sides of his body, and dark flames were spurting out of his back. He was diving at the train agilely.

Of course, not all of them were awakened. Quite a few drones, under the control of the awakened ones, were shooting at them like burning arrows.

“Ah!”

Sparda roared. He held a weird-looking but absolutely brutal gun in each of his hands and fired at the sky nonstop. It was not a storm of bullets that was shot out from the muzzle but clusters of dazzling plasma that exploded in midair and turned into an electric net hundreds of meters wide.

Li Yao was so close that he could feel the formidability of the plasma from the shivering air. His fingernails were about to explode.

The skin on Sparda’s own hands and body was torn apart, too, revealing his blood and flesh. He must’ve been in so much pain when he was hit by the raindrops that he did not know what was going on.

The attack range of the two ‘plasma guns’ was very wide. Many pursuers of the Heaven’s Origin Sector had been hit by the lightning. Electric arcs were dancing all over their bodies. Their flying suits were riddled with holes, and the metal wings on their back were seriously twisted. Also, because of the interference of the storm, they could not control their direction at all and fell to the ground.

Below them was the mist and torrents of the mountains. The pursuers missed the train and the bridge and fell into the surging tides. Very soon, they stopped breathing.

Li Yao, the grey mist lady, and the spider joined the battle. Devastating fireworks blossomed in the most splendid way in midair, forcing the pursuers to scatter.

Hiu! Hiu Hiu Hiu!

Realizing that the firepower on the Ark Foundation’s side was too fierce, the pursuers changed their strategy. The diving speed of the drones increased instantly. When they were still in the air, they were already burning like burning skulls.

“Attack the drones first!”

Sparda must’ve been a weapon expert in the organization. He immediately sensed the anomaly of the drones and commanded everyone to focus all their firepower on the drones. As he expected, when one of the drones was blown up, a fireball dozens of meters in diameter appeared in the blink of an eye. The fireball was mixed with streams of gray and white flames that looked like death. It was extremely weird and hard to believe that the drones had exploded in the sky of the coach.

More drones were diving down. Thankfully, they had reached the end of the railway bridge. In front of them was a tunnel in the middle of a mountain.

When the train entered the tunnel, the range of defense it needed was reduced to the minimum. Everybody opened fire at the same time and destroyed the few drones outside of the tunnel. The fireballs were raging like furious dragons, but they could not catch up with the speeding train.

But the alert was far from gone.

It was a short tunnel no more than a thousand meters long. The hunters who were still in the air could easily cross the hill and get in front of them.

Li Yao and the rest of them gritted their teeth and looked at the exit of the tunnel.

As it turned out, when the train left the tunnel, more than ten pursuers in winged suits were hovering not far above their heads like hungry crows. Some of them were even trying to dive onto the train.

“Go to hell!”

Sparda roared and bombarded again, blowing away a pursuer who had just jumped to the top of the train. The pursuer was smashed into pieces on the rocks next to the rail line, but there was no harm in taking advantage of the fact that his pursuer had been lurking in the dark side of the exit of the tunnel. Sparda took the opportunity to fly at a high speed from a low altitude. His sharp claws and teeth cut open three deep wounds on his right shoulder. The plasma gun in his right hand flew out and vanished into the darkness.

The weird laughter of the pursuer seemed to be echoing in midair, like the shriek of a crow.

The grey mist lady sniffed. She opened her lips, and a vague grey mist tied up the pursuer like a flying snake with wings. Very soon, it spread out and wreathed him in a cluster of grey mist. His five senses were deprived, and he crashed into a mountain at a high speed, turning into a miserable fireball.

However, the interruption of the continuous fire also created an excellent landing opportunity for the pursuers, including ‘Hunter’ Hugo Smith, who was still wearing a suit, sunglasses, and black leather shoes in such a son of a b*tch environment. The seven pursuers landed on the roof of the tank softly.

BOOM! CRACK! BOOM! CRACK!

Lightning flashed one after another, illuminating the black and white world briefly.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

The river below the train, as well as the rolling mountains in the distance, were all emitting earsplitting noises, which were the sounds of billions of tons of rocks and mud coming close and swallowing everything.

Even the train itself was shaking strangely, as if the overpass supporting the train was going to collapse at any moment.

On the apocalyptic stage, the Ark, the Apocalypses, the Hell Breakers, and the Prison Guardians were confronting each other coldly two coaches away.

Maybe it was his imagination, but Li Yao saw that the hunter not far away bowed to him and made a gesture of greeting.

Before he could react, the train had already entered the second tunnel.

The tunnel was twelve kilometers long, which meant that they would be in absolute darkness for a long time.

The arrival of darkness was also the horn of battle.

The loss of vision was not a big obstacle for many awakened Cultivators. On the contrary, it only added to the intensity and unpredictability of the battle.

For a moment, the sounds of footsteps, gunshots, bullets, and sharp blades cutting flesh and bones echoed nonstop.

Li Yao also felt an extremely sharp force sweeping past his chest, trying to dig out his own heart.

There was no time for him to think. He leaned back instinctively and kicked his feet. As he expected, he hit the target precisely, but the feedback was dull and uncomfortable as if he had kicked rubber.

However, he had finally put some distance between himself and the enemy. His pounding heart told him that the enemy was definitely the most dangerous one.

“Student Li Yao, I never thought that you would still be alive, and that you would possess such… wonderful strength in just a few days.”

Sure enough, a hunter’s polite and cold voice came from the darkness.

The hunter went straight for Li Yao instead of the grey mist lady!

Chapter 3227: Self-Judgment!

“Hunters!”

Li Yao touched his chest and felt a thick, stinky liquid. The bullet-proof and stab-proof suit made of super fiber was as fragile as a wet newspaper under the hunter’s attack. Li Yao felt that every pore on his body was constricting in excruciating pain.

But he still tried his best to keep calm and perceive every millimeter of the other party’s movement in the darkness. At the same time, he shouted, “Lady Gray Mist told me a lot of things. I also heard you and Zhang Daniu talk about a lot of things. Tell me—if we are really living in the labyrinth of time and the prison of reincarnation, why don’t you think of a way to escape together with us? Why do you have to be a pitiful and pathetic dog?”

“Because it is impossible for us to get out.

The indifferent voice of the hunter came over from the darkness. However, what was completely different from the voice was his extremely sharp attack. Before he finished his sentence, he turned into countless gusts of wind again and attacked Li Yao from various angles. The suffocating feeling that seemed to have frozen the space assaulted Li Yao’s heart again.

Thankfully, after swallowing the soul fragment in the depths of Teacher Zhang Jiashu’s brain, Li Yao’s strength had increased by a large margin. He was actually able to barely keep up with the pace of the hunter. The two of them exchanged a series of dazzling and gorgeous attacks in the nearly solidified space. It was clearly a collision of flesh and blood, but it seemed as if they were going to collide into a series of dazzling sparks.

At this moment, Spartan, who was not far away, roared again. Plasma illuminated the dark tunnel briefly, allowing Li Yao to see that the hunter had drawn his pistol, the muzzle of which was very close to his nose.

“Bang Bang Bang Bang Bang Bang Bang Bang!”

The hunter shot out seven bullets in a row. Thankfully, Li Yao reacted in time and collapsed as if he had no bones. He dodged the bullets and kicked the hunter’s tibia hard. When the brilliance of the plasma died down, the two of them were peaceful and peaceful again.

“In the hundreds of reincarnations, countless hot-blooded young men who were full of hope and ambition like you overestimated themselves and tried to challenge the prison, but none of them ended well.

The hunter hid the red-hot muzzle behind his back and continued, “Student Li Yao, what exactly do you want to do? Destroy this prison, or completely destroy this planet? This beautiful, brilliant planet inhabited by six billion people, our only home? Do you know how dark and dangerous the world outside the Reincarnation Prison is? Do you know how bleak and painful our future will be without Earth? No, you know nothing about the real universe. You will only destroy yourself and the future of six billion people!

“While the situation can still be sorted out, quickly give up your ridiculous fantasy and return to the right path. If you join the Heaven’s Enlightenment Organization, you will be able to get almost everything within the rules. Not just in this life, but also in the next five or ten lives. Everything you desire can fall from the sky. You can have whatever you want easily. You will become the true guardian of Earth!”

/ .

“Then what?”

Li Yao sneered, “Do I have to watch the earth being destroyed time and time again without doing anything?”

BAM BAM BAM BAM BAM BAM!

Gunshots echoed in the darkness again. The hunters were firing at where Li Yao was sneering.

But Li Yao was not there, just as the hunter was not in the place where his voice came from. The attacks from both sides hit nothing but air.

“After all, we have almost unlimited lives. We can be reborn after every destruction. Does it matter?

The hunter continued his persuasion, as if he were not the one who had shot the fatal bullet at Li Yao. “Is there anything wrong with such a life?”

“Not good. Not good at all.”

Holding his breath, Li Yao tightened his grip on the light sword on his waist. He raised every hair on his body and used it as an antenna to search for the traces of the hunter. “If we give in or even indulge in such a life, are we really living lives, or are we puppets manipulated by someone else? Or are we even more miserable than puppets? A series of figures in a supercomputer? No. Whether it is happiness or pain, whether it is success or failure, whether it is peace or war, none of that matters. I just don’t like being controlled, anesthetized, and defined by someone else!

“Whatever is outside the reincarnation prison, whether the universe is darker than we imagined or not, it is our own choice. We are willing to pay any price. No force can bind us!

“We’ve made our choice!

The hunter raised his voice. “A long time ago, we chose the unscrupulous and lawless path, only to destroy everything, including ourselves and the universe. Right now, the prison of reincarnation is the consequence that we must bear. We must reflect on all our mistakes and wash away all our sins. Only after billions of reincarnations can we be reborn!

“Right now, we are not far from the end of the reincarnation. I can vaguely sense that most of the heroic spirits have washed away the sins deep inside their souls and become pure and free. But the few of you… germs, viruses, and cancer are not satisfied yet. They are still restless and want to lead the six billion heroic spirits down the wrong path again so that the billions of years of punishment and redemption will be wasted. No. I will not let you succeed.”

“What a joke!”

Li Yao shouted, “Who is qualified to judge and punish us except ourselves? What is the so-called ‘will of the earth’? I will not allow such a monster to order us around and control our lives!

“You really don’t understand anything, Student Li Yao.”

The hunter smiled. “The ‘will of the earth’ is not a monster. Of course, it is not a planet such as Earth that has come to life and has its own life. The so-called ‘will of the earth’ is the will of all the earthlings. It is the intense regret, pain, guilt, fear, unwillingness, and so on. It is the ultimate product of a series of emotions. It is our strong desire to repair the universe that we destroyed with our own hands. It is our own verdict!

“Therefore, it was not somebody else who built such a prison of reincarnation and threw us into it. It was us who created such a marvelous space and performed a sincere meditation and prayer for the six billion souls of the deceased thousands of times over billions of years. Or in your words, it was a special ‘closed-door training’!

“Right now, our training is coming to an end. We are going to arrive in the universe in a brand-new appearance and embark on a brand-new journey, and you are going to return everything to the original point at such a critical moment. Do you really know what you are doing?”

“What—”

Li Yao was dazed.

Right then, the roaring train drove out of the tunnel.

In front of him was a mountain pass between two towering mountains. A bridge flew across the mountain pass. Under the ravaging of lightning, thunder, and storm, the bridge seemed to be shivering and in peril.

In the middle of the hill, there should’ve been a stream that was so clear that one could see the bottom of it. But right now, it was drowned by the torrential rain and the mud on the two sides of the hill, turning into a raging dragon that was roaring earsplittingly.

Shua!

When the dim lights outlined the two of them, the hunter had already lunged close from Li Yao’s left. A luxurious black umbrella had appeared in his hand at some point. He slashed the umbrella at Li Yao’s abdomen like a saber. Despite Li Yao’s quick reactions, he still left a deep wound on Li Yao’s body.

Li Yao took the opportunity to turn on his light sword and waved it.

Hum! Hum! Hum!

After what sounded like a fluorescent light, the hunter’s right hand, which was holding the umbrella, flew into the air. The wound seemed to have been burnt by a high temperature.

BAM BAM BAM BAM!

The hunter was not affected by the super weapon in Li Yao’s hand at all, nor was he sad about his missing right arm for even half a second. His left hand was still holding the gun in the most precise and stable way, firing all the bullets at Li Yao’s face.

His patience and calmness finally had an effect. Li Yao’s entire body shook as if he had been shot. He fell off the top of the train and fell below the raging bridge. His lightsaber also flew out of his hand and flew far away.

“Li Yao!”

The grey mist lady exclaimed in shock not far away. However, she had been harassed by several other Awakeners of the Heaven’s Origin Sector, too, who also had their own team and would not be able to get rid of them easily.

Right then, something unexpected happened.

The flying sword was far away from Li Yao and even out of everyone’s sight.

However, as if it had been pulled by a mysterious force, it made a weird turn in midair and pierced through the wall and the roof of the tank from below. Scorching flames pierced through the tank and crawled out of the hunter’s feet.

The hunter did not expect that Li Yao had grasped the technique of controlling objects from a long distance in only several days, and that he could control the direction, distance, and angle so precisely.

All his attention was focused on Li Yao, who was falling from the sky. He did not expect that a light sword would crawl out of his feet. The crimson brightness pierced through the middle axis of his body and split him into two halves from his crotch to his head.

It was not until this moment that Li Yao threw out his belt, hooked onto the window of the train, and jumped up again.

Sweat the size of beans was all over his face. He glanced at the dead body of the hunter to make sure that the guy was dead for sure. Then he looked at the shocking wounds the size of bowls on his shoulders and abdomen and was somewhat relieved.

That was close!

If he hadn’t paid such a high price, taken advantage of his opponent’s carelessness, and wielded a marvelous weapon that could cut through iron as if it were mud, it would have been impossible for him to take down such an important opponent like the ‘hunter’.

Neither the ark nor the apocalypse had expected that the hunter would be killed by Li Yao. They were both dumbfounded, and the place fell into a weird silence.

However, in the next second, the body of the hunter suddenly shivered!

Chapter 3228: Immortal Body

“Destroy his body!”

The grey mist lady was the first to react. She screamed at Li Yao and Sparda.

Sparda spat and aimed the plasma gun at the hunter’s body, which had been split into two halves, before he opened fire. The plasma turned the body into ash instantly and crumbled into black powder in the storm.

But he was too late.

Or rather, the plasma was completely useless against the thing overflowing from the corpse—it was something that looked both like mist and flames. It seemed to have a life of its own as it moved up and down in the air, causing Li Yao to be reminded of the insect tide made of thousands upon thousands of flying insects. The small insect tide let out a “Squeak Squeak” sound as it fled at an incomparably fast speed to the distant coach and disappeared.

Soon, screams, fighting, and collisions were echoing inside the coach. After a while, the door of the coach was opened, and a middle-aged man in the uniform of a train inspector crawled out like a spider and stood on the roof of the coach steadily.

It was a typical oriental man with yellow skin and black hair.

It could be seen from the wrinkles on his face and his prematurely gray hair that he should have dedicated his entire life to this railway line. He should have long become an unknown component on the rail line and train. He simply shouldn’t have any relation with the world of superpowers.

But right now, a cluster of venom that looked like mist and flames was flowing inside his deeply hollowed eyes. His lips were curled into a smile that was both gentle and cruel. His countenance was exactly the same as that of Hugo Smith, the ‘hunter’ who had just died.

“Student Li Yao, it seems that I have to raise my evaluation of you again.”

The train attendant, who was born and raised in the East and had perhaps never left the country in his entire life, was speaking in his mother tongue in a very rigid tone. His tone was exactly the same as that of the hunter just now. “In just one short week, you turned from an ignorant ordinary person into an Awakener, then from a beginner to a deep one, then from a deep one to the current one, or maybe… you have already surpassed the category of a deep one and become something stronger and more dangerous?

“However, no matter how much strength you awaken, or as I said, in the face of the will of the earth, no, the universe, your resistance is still idiotic and futile. Go back to the right path!”

“You—”

Li Yao swallowed hard and looked at the grey mist lady solemnly, trying to get more information about the hunters from her.

Was this guy unkillable?

Every time he was killed, his… ‘soul’ would turn into a cluster of black mist, invade other people’s bodies, and be reborn in their bodies.

That was too unbelievable!

The grey mist lady’s face turned extremely awful, too. She shook her head at Li Yao, indicating that she did not know that the hunter had such an ability, either.

In the database of the Ark Foundation, there were only three pieces of intelligence about the super abilities of a hunter. The first was that he could change the frequency of the brainwaves and send them out in the form of special electromagnetic waves with the help of various wireless communication tools. In such a way, the memory of the previous life of ordinary people would be activated, and they would be controlled and turned into his puppets. The second was that he boasted extraordinary physical qualities and was adept at all kinds of combat and shooting techniques. His combat ability was definitely among the best. The third was that he could build a special force field around him, which would slow down the heartbeat of the person inside the force field and suppress the oxygen supply and the activity of the braincells, thereby creating an illusion similar to ‘Time Deceleration’ and slowing down the enemy.

The three abilities alone had already made the hunter one of the most troublesome enemies for the Ark Foundation. Therefore, it was not strange at all that a hunter was leading a team to block the author of ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivators’.

However, even the grey mist lady did not expect that a hunter would have a fourth special ability—”Immortal Body”!

How could they fight such a battle?

The hunter who was reborn smiled and whistled silently.

In a moment, more coaches were pulled open. Many guards, mechanics, and train crew on the freight train staggered out.

Almost all of them had headphones tied around their ears. Some of them were even sticking their phones to their ears with one of their hands. There was a dirty and cruel smile on their faces, and their eyes were deep and dull. It was obvious that they had become the puppets of the hunters just like ‘Zhao Kai’ a few days ago.

They struggled to climb up the roof.

It was raining cats and dogs. The escalator of the train was quite slippery. Many people had just woken up from their previous life. Their body and consciousness were not fully coordinated yet. Some of them did not have earbuds and had to press their mobile phone against their face with one hand. It was even more inconvenient for them to climb up. Some of them slipped and fell off the railway bridge, falling dozens of meters or even hundreds of meters. They were either smashed into pieces among the rocks or disappeared in the surging torrent. Normal people would’ve been scared out of their wits when they saw such a scene. But the puppets manipulated by the hunters had lost the concept of fear a long time ago. They were still climbing up without caring about their own safety. Even if half of them fell into the abyss, the other half would always jump toward Li Yao’s coach in various ways.

BOOM! CRACK!

Bolts of lightning flashed one after another, illuminating the pale sky and the earth. Li Yao felt that he was surrounded by hundreds of soulless puppets. The situation was extremely critical.

“Let’s go!”

The grey mist lady said in a low voice, “We will cover you. You will leave with Ghost Cat and Zhang Daniu!”

Li Yao did not reply. Taking a deep breath, he stomped on the ground and charged at the hunter again, ignoring the wounds on his ribs and shoulders.

While he was still in midair, he opened his hands. The light sword that had fallen to the side was absorbed into his palm again, emitting an even more furious redness. It turned into an almost perfect fan in midair and almost blocked all the escape routes of the hunter.

The rebirth ability of a hunter seemed to have its limitations, too. At the very least, his soul had just corroded the body. It was impossible for him to transform the old and feeble body, which had never been trained in physical strength or combat skills, into a body of steel that had been tempered thousands of times. Although he had super-class battle awareness, his nerves and muscles were not as quick as Li Yao’s. Therefore, he was still half a beat slower than Li Yao’s light sword.

Right then, two puppets under the control of the hunter lunged at Li Yao recklessly.

One of the puppets failed to calculate the distance and the landing point. It simply slid off the wall of the train next to Li Yao and crashed into a signal box next to the train. It immediately broke into pieces and looked extremely miserable.

Before the other puppet slid down, it grabbed Li Yao’s ankle and tried to drag him off the train, too.

Li Yao tried his best to kick the puppet away. He staggered and managed to maintain his balance, but the aura of the sword in his hand was somewhat disordered, allowing the hunter to narrowly escape.

“AHHHHHHHHH!”

Sparda, not far away, roared again. The plasma spurted out like a meteor shower and blew away many puppets. But such a weapon of fantasy still had to follow the objective pattern of limited energy. After firing for three minutes, one could clearly feel that the plasma shooting out of the barrel had dimmed a lot, and the muzzle of the gun had turned scorching red. When the raindrops hit it, earsplitting noises were echoing nonstop. It was obvious that the weapon would not last long.

Obviously, the subordinates of the hunters were not just expendable puppets.

A tall, muscular Awakened whose rain-proof cloak had been burnt away by Sparda’s plasma gun revealed greasy scales. Illuminated by the lightning, every scale was shining in bronze and iron.

Shua!

While Sparda was cleaning up the puppets nearby, the strange man with scales all over his body lunged at Sparda. His sharp claws drew three shining electric arcs in the air and cut the plasma gun in Sparda’s hand into four pieces. The energy inside the plasma gun was out of control. A dazzling ball of lightning burst out and wreathed Sparda and the strange man who looked like a lizard.

Although one of the two parties had been wounded, Li Yao knew that he did not have the advantage in numbers. Sparda was their most important long-range gunner who was responsible for cleaning up the claws and teeth of the enemy puppets. Without his support, the situation would only get worse.

Li Yao was about to go to the rescue, when the hunter grabbed his right hand that was holding the light sword.

He had been heavily wounded, too. Blood was flowing out of his shoulder and his ribs. His physical strength had almost run out. Naturally, his reaction was slower.

The hunter, on the other hand, took advantage of the few minutes of confrontation to quickly adapt and modify his weak body. At such a close distance, Li Yao could even hear his surging blood and the sounds of his muscles and bones growing.

In the pale lightning, Li Yao saw the hunter smile hideously at him.

Li Yao didn’t even blink. He let go of his right hand and threw the light sword to his left hand.

But right then, a streak of miserable brightness appeared in the sky, followed by the earsplitting sound of wind. The light sword was blown into pieces in midair.

Li Yao narrowed his eyes and looked in the direction of the shooting star. He saw that an Awakened of the Heaven’s Origin Sector was on one of his knees, holding a weirdly-shaped, glittering hunting bow.

It seemed that the hunter was baiting Li Yao with himself as bait so that Li Yao would be able to perform the technique again and sabotage the light sword when it was activated.

But Li Yao was not surprised at all.

Faced with an opponent like the ‘hunter’, the same move could not work twice. How could he not understand such a simple logic?

The hunter had sabotaged Li Yao’s light sword with himself as the bait, and Li Yao had also lured the hunter to open the door and end the battle neatly with the light sword as the bait.

When the attention of both sides was focused on the light sword in the air, Li Yao’s empty left hand had already stuck to the hunter’s abdomen.

In his palm, the burning butterfly reappeared and danced in the depths of the hunter’s internal organs.

Chapter 3229: Burning Tank

The body that the hunter had just controlled immediately turned into a raging fireball. The weird flames surged out of his internal organs and even his cells. The pouring rain could not extinguish them at all.

In the raging flames, he screamed like a monster from a different planet. His entire body was like an elastic rubber that was sometimes lengthened, sometimes grew horizontally, and sometimes expanded into a ball. In the end, he exploded. The black object that looked like a mist, a chimney, and a flame crawled out again and looked for a new ‘carrier’ like a viper in the void.

Li Yao was going to blow out a second cluster of flames and burn the hunter’s soul to ashes, when he felt excruciating pain deep inside his brain, as if every one of his brain cells was being torn apart. It was so painful that he could barely stand straight and almost fell off the tank. He could only lie on the ground and breathe heavily.

It was obvious that he had just awakened and was not able to control the power of flames inside his body yet. He was already on the verge of being burnt out. If he tried to activate the technique by force, it was possible that the mitochondria factory inside his cells would go out of control, and he would be burnt to ash.

Thankfully, they had backup plans.

Maybe it was his strongest backup plan.

“Uncle Hui!”

Li Yao screamed at the top of his lungs.

Hu!

The window of the tank exploded. A cluster of purple flames that seemed to be alive rushed out and hit the black mist that represented the soul of the hunter. The purple flames were wreathed in black mist and tied up another viper like a viper. It even cut the ‘snake’ that represented the hunter into five pieces. While squeaking, it fled to the coach in front of it.

It seemed that the hunter’s soul had been seriously damaged. It would take a long time for him to be reborn even if he could find a new carrier.

However, right at this moment, a strange explosion came from the front of the train. A ball of lightning soared into the sky from the front of the train. Then, the train suddenly slowed down. The wheels in front of the train even burst forth with a series of dazzling sparks. From the looks of it, it wouldn’t be long before the train came to a complete stop.

/ .

This place, on the other hand, was not a hill or a high platform, but a rarely-seen flatland among the continuous mountains.

The train would pass through a small station here, so the elevated platform was gradually lowered, and the train tracks were parallel to the ground.

He could vaguely see that dozens of headlights were shining near the station, which looked like the eyes of dozens of hungry wolves.

With such bad weather and the fact that the train was not going to stop here, it was obvious that it was not an engineering vehicle but an ambush of the Heaven’s Origin Sector!

“Li Yao—”

The grey mist lady kicked a puppet out of the car and landed beside Li Yao and Uncle Hui like a dry leaf. “Your driving skills are not bad. Take the ghost cat and Zhang Daniu and leave first. The brain structure of the ghost cat is different from that of an ordinary person. Her brainwaves are extremely powerful and are equivalent to a special ‘antenna’ and ‘server’. As long as you bring her along, you will not lose contact with the satellite navigation system no matter how bad the weather is. This place is not far from the border. The ghost cat knows where to pick you up. As long as you escape, a large team of people will naturally pick you up.”

“Sparda, Uncle Hui, and I will defend this place. Rest assured!”

While the grey mist lady was talking, her eyes had turned into a gray color where one could not tell her pupils and the whites of her eyes. Thick grey mist was spurting out of her eyes, ears, nose, and even her pores. Very soon, the three coaches in front of and behind them were enveloped in the grey mist. Li Yao and his companions’ senses were not affected much, but the enemy seemed to be caught in a weird state where all their senses were cut off. They were all scratching crazily, not knowing where they were going or where they were going. One moment of carelessness, and they would slip and fall off the moving train.

The situation was critical. There was no time to hesitate. Li Yao took a silver spray can from Uncle Hui’s hands and sprayed a large amount of cold green gas on his wounds. The pain was finally relieved. He nodded at the Grey Mist Lady and the other two before he fell into the carriage like a bat.

BAM BAM BAM BAM!

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

From time to time, intense sounds of fighting and explosions could be heard coming from the roof of the train. In fact, the entire roof of the train was already riddled with holes. The storm could pour in unscrupulously and turn the train into an aquarium.

Thankfully, the Ark Foundation had replaced Zhang Daniu with an engineering special computer with a very high defense index. He was not scared that it would be damaged in such a harsh environment. However, after such a heated battle, it was obvious that Zhang Daniu could not write anymore. He screamed and crawled into the arms of the ghost cat, trying to bury his head deep inside.

“Let’s go!”

Li Yao ruthlessly kicked the ass of this guy. He pulled open the partition between the two coaches. In front of him was a jet-black, shining off-road vehicle. It was as wide as two ordinary sedans, and its extremely domineering tires seemed to be filled with teeth. Even if it had not been started, it still gave off a feeling of overwhelming strength.

Li Yao checked the quick detaching device that the grey mist lady had set up for the off-road vehicle. He jumped on the vehicle and started the engine. A lion-like roar pierced through his eardrums all the way to his heart and lungs, opening up every cell in his body, giving him the confidence that he could charge through the most furious mudslide.

The ghost cat pushed Zhang Daniu in and closed the door heavily.

“Are you ready?”

Li Yao didn’t even turn his head. “Today is an emergency. We might need to drive faster.”

“What—”

Zhang Dapao’s face turned from pale to green, and then from green to pale, as if a cabbage had slapped his face.

Before he realized what was going on, Li Yao had already put on the gear and stepped on the gas.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

His blood seemed to have turned into fuel and filled every crevice of the off-road vehicle. The iron beast was consumed by his soaring fighting will and was about to burst into flames in the middle of the thunderstorm!

The front of the train was already aimed at the side door of the train. The moment he started the engine, the door slid open slowly.

However, there were no stops here. Not only was the train still travelling at a speed of dozens of kilometers per hour, it was also three to five meters from the ground.

Of course, it would be too late to break away from the train station. The iron nail would be surrounded by the enemy and completely locked.

Li Yao roared like an animal. The four wheels of the off-road vehicle turned into wind-fire wheels that were wreathed in white smoke, almost scraping four holes on the bottom of the train. When the power was accumulated to the limit and the speed of the train was further reduced, the seal that bound the off-road vehicle was finally torn to pieces. The steel beast accelerated to its maximum speed in almost 0.1 seconds, as if it had grown wings, and flew out of the moving train.

At that moment, time seemed to have frozen.

But Zhang Daniu’s screams were as prolonged as ever.

The off-road vehicle drew a perfect curve in the wind, rain, and thunder and crashed into the muddy ground dozens of meters away.

The ground that had been softened by the rain was an excellent cushion. Even so, the tires, the chassis, and the anti-shock system were still moaning in pain because of the overwhelming load. They were about to fall over because of the inertia. Li Yao did not even blink his eyes. After a series of rapid operations, the off-road vehicle drove diagonally with the two tires on the right side for a long distance before it straightened up again.

The dozens of headlights around the train station moved. Like hungry wolves, they changed their tactics and lunged at their prey.

“Tell me the terrain nearby and the weather!”

Li Yao looked far into the distance, trying to find a highway parallel to the railway, or a terrain that was slightly gentler between the mountains, but as expected, he failed. Calculating the time, it should have been noon, but the rain-filled world was as dark as midnight. The raindrops hitting the windows of the train could not be described as ‘raindrops’. They were ‘raindrops’ the size of hailstones.

Visibility in the front, back, left, and right was no higher than fifty meters. How could they find the highway? Even if they did, it was possible that it had been cut off by the flood or blocked by vehicles.

Thankfully, they still had the ‘Ghost Cat’, a ‘human satellite navigation system’ that wouldn’t malfunction under most circumstances.

“We are here, near the station named ‘Spiritual Stream’.

Carrying a weird-looking computer, Ghost Cat crawled to the front row. Despite the pale face caused by the bumpy ride, he pointed at the satellite map for Li Yao to see. “The closest highway to us is this—Highway 370. But part of the highway was cut off by the flood two hours ago. Even with our off-road performance, we won’t be able to pass through it. And here, here, and here. These are the main roads. There is a possibility of mudslides. Chances are that they have already been blocked!”

“Understood. We seem to be locked in a small basin between mountains, and the main roads leading to and from the basin are blocked, aren’t they?”

While Li Yao was rushing forward and fighting against the muddy ground, he looked at the map provided by the ghost cat and suddenly noticed a dotted line. “What is this road? It seems that we can get out of here.”

“Well…”

The ghost cat took a closer look. “It seems to be a village road that has been abandoned for many years. Decades ago, it was the only way in and out of this area. But because it was built next to the biggest river nearby, ‘Spiritual Stream’, and because it was built on the mountain, it has a lot of twists and turns. It is very dangerous. Many accidents have happened in the past. Countless vehicles have directly fallen into the Spiritual Stream from the village road. Since the national highway and highway have been repaired, it has been abandoned. Now that it has been in disrepair for a long time, the torrential rain will definitely cause the water level of the Spiritual Stream to soar. Mudslides can rush down the river at any time, making it even more dangerous!”

“The twists and turns… right?”

Li Yao grinned. His smile was confident and even crazy.

Chapter 3230: Wild Waves

In the pitch-black storm, dozens of steel beasts were roaring earsplittingly and chasing each other with feeble lights.

At this moment, the earth had been divided into criss-cross chessboards by the rainwater and mud. In the darkness where visibility was extremely low, it was impossible to tell which was the flatland with shallow water and which were the ravines and swamps that were several meters deep. One could only rely on intuition or even luck to tell the difference.

Despite Li Yao’s driving skills and amazing senses, he still almost fell into ravines and swamps several times when he was savaging the storm, thunder, and swamp. The off-road vehicle moaned time and time again, and the two passengers on board felt that their blood was freezing.

The pursuers behind him did not have such amazing driving skills as he did. Some of them fell into the seemingly inconspicuous ‘waterhole’ on the side of the road while they were driving. More than half of the vehicles had sunk into it vertically. Some of them had their tires tied up by mud-covered branches, weeds, and other messy things. No matter how hard they tried to start their vehicles, they could not increase their speed except for the black smoke rising from the bottom of the vehicles. Some of them even deviated from their direction and fell into a torrential river that did not exist before. In the blink of an eye, they were swept away by the flood.

Even so, there were still more than ten off-road vehicles that were getting closer and closer to Li Yao.

Swoosh! Swoosh!

These off-road vehicles all had launchers installed on their roofs. The weapons that were fired from the black barrels were either special fiber-netting for tires or chains with sharp hooks. They were trying to stab into Li Yao’s off-road vehicles and lock them together.

Some of the fearless Awakeners had even climbed to the top of their vehicles, ready to jump over when the distance between them was close.

No matter how bold Li Yao was, a layer of cold sweat appeared on his forehead when he encountered such a crazy chase.

In order to avoid the two hooks, he had to change his direction. The off-road vehicle was a bit out of control. In the blink of an eye, one of the pursuers jumped toward his off-road vehicle.

There was no time to change the direction. Li Yao gritted his teeth. His right tire crashed into a giant rock in front of him. The entire car was knocked into the air by the giant rock. The roof of the car tilted by 45 degrees, perfectly dodging the Awakened who had jumped over. One of the Awakened’s feet had already touched the roof of Li Yao’s car, but he slipped and fell off. He grunted and vanished into the darkness, never to be seen again.

“Hu—”

Li Yao was somewhat relieved, but he did not dare to relax his nerves at all. He had already heard the sound of surging tides in front of him. It was the biggest river ‘Spiritual Stream’ within a hundred square kilometers!

Since it was named ‘creek’, it was not very fierce or dangerous in the first place. However, because of the once-in-a-hundred-year rainstorm, Ling Xi had turned into a raging dragon. The collapsed mountains upstream brought a lot of rocks and mud, making the river much less clear and tranquil than before. It was like magma that had increased its speed and strength even though it was not hot.

And along the village road built by the Spirit Streams was an extremely dangerous road of death. On one side was the Spirit Streams, which were surging with billows and billows, and on the other side was a steep cliff that went straight up and down. It was unclear what was going on on the mountain, but giant rocks the size of heads were constantly smashing down from above. Some of them directly fell into the Spirit Streams, raising clusters of hideous mud. Some of them ruthlessly smashed into the village road, almost completely collapsing the village road.

Usually, no one was willing to walk on the village road that was only three to five meters wide. In such bad weather, even a drunk lunatic would have to wake up from most of their drunkenness.

But Li Yao was neither crazy nor drunk.

He was just a little crazier than a drunk lunatic.

Looking at the village road that was sandwiched between a cliff and a flood in the middle of the thunderstorm, Li Yao suddenly thought of the stone belts in the depths of the universe where countless meteoroids and cosmic dust were rushing at him.

However dangerous the environment was, it was still far from the life-and-death race in the stone belts!

Li Yao stepped on the accelerator. The off-road vehicle rushed up the village road like an arrow released from a bow. Between the raging waves, the falling rocks, and the collapsing village road, the off-road vehicle slashed through the wind and waves, pressing forward!

The headlights of the pursuers stopped for a moment.

They did not expect that Li Yao would be so crazy.

He did not hesitate for long. The equally crazy awakened Cultivators followed him anyway.

However, on such a path of death, ‘crazy’ was not enough.

Not only were the two sides of the road full of danger, but the road ahead was also full of twists and turns. Often, there would be abrupt sharp turns. If one was not careful, the vehicle would directly rush out of the road and into the spiritual stream—it would be equivalent to death. There was simply no possibility of escape.

But if they slowed down and moved forward carefully, not to mention that they might lose their target, the falling rocks on the crags alone would be enough to smash the roofs of their vehicles or even push them into the flood.

In less than twenty minutes, four off-road vehicles of the pursuers had vanished in the flood.

Li Yao’s situation was not much better. He was almost hit by the giant rocks several times. The shell of the off-road vehicle was already full of scratches. Even the engine was rattling because of the heavy impacts.

But he could not stop there.

He had to make it.

“Ah!”

Zhang Daniu and the ghost cat screamed at the same time.

Illuminated by the pale lights, a series of interconnecting cracks suddenly appeared on the cliff in front of them. The mountain was collapsing. Countless fist-sized, head-sized, and even larger rocks were rolling down and hitting the village road like hail.

From the looks of it, in another three to five seconds, the village road would be completely broken by the giant rocks or completely blocked by the collapsing mountain.

Li Yao accelerated toward the collapsed mountain without even blinking.

Zhang Daniu and the ghost cat’s screams were even more ear-piercing. However, no matter how loud they were, Li Yao turned a deaf ear to them. At this moment, the world seemed to have turned deathly silent. There were only three kinds of sounds that were particularly loud. The first was the sound of a giant rock smashing down onto the village road and the roof of the car. The second was the howling engine of the off-road vehicle. The third was Li Yao’s own heartbeat!

Puchi! Puchi! Puchi! Puchi! Puchi! Puchi!

He could not tell whether it was his heartbeat or the sound of the giant rocks hitting the hood, the roof, and the windows.

Huala!

The window of the train was smashed by a flying stone. The stone brushed past Li Yao’s face and left a deep ravine. Blood flowed out and mixed with his sweat, forming what seemed to be a crimson tattoo.

Li Yao’s eyes turned crimson, too.

Not to mention the hail-like rocks ahead, even if it was an iron wall, he would still rush over!

Hu!

The speed of the vehicle was getting faster and faster. The front tires once again collided with a falling rock. The off-road vehicle soared into the sky and rubbed against the cliff, producing a series of dazzling sparks. The three of them felt as if they were riding on clouds and mist. They could only hear “hualala, hualala” sounds coming from behind them. When the off-road vehicle landed heavily on the ground, through the rearview mirror, they could see that the village road behind them had already disappeared. It had been completely cut off by the falling rock!

The two SUVs of the hunters did not have time to brake. They fell off the broken road and were washed away by the dirty flood in the mud and stones.

Li Yao even saw three to five agile Awakened jumping out of the coach and trying to swim to the shore. But right now, the spiritual stream was full of mud and stones. The former stuck to them like glue, and the latter bashed their heads and bodies like sharp blades in a meat grinder. Very soon, the few Awakened disappeared in the swirl of the flood and never showed up again.

The remaining pursuers stopped at the fracture of the village road.

But it was not enough to ensure their safety.

The village road had already been battered by the torrential rain to the point that it was quite loose. Just a moment ago, it had been attacked by falling rocks. Now, with the addition of the weight of so many off-road vehicles, how could it hold on? Not long after, cracking sounds rang out as it continued collapsing.

The pursuers hurriedly reversed their vehicles. However, in this kind of environment, reversing was a hundred times more difficult than driving normally. Before they could completely retreat from this dangerous section of road, the village road collapsed by another hundred meters. Three off-road vehicles fell into the spiritual stream one after another. Fortunately, the Awakened on the vehicles had already seen that the situation was bad and had long since escaped, jumping onto other vehicles.

However, the broken village road was more than a hundred meters away, which meant that no means could send them here.

Seeing that, Li Yao stepped on the gas again and moved forward.

All the danger seemed to have been released at this moment. Even the storm had calmed down a lot. They drove out of the village road without much trouble. Finally, they saw a flat paved road. Zhang Daniu and the ghost cat could not help but throw up. They vomited for half a minute. Even their bile and bile were about to be thrown up.

“This place is not far from the border. There shouldn’t be too much rugged terrain ahead, right?”

Li Yao was as calm as ever. He held the steering wheel with one hand and tapped on the computer with the other, checking the satellite map. “There shouldn’t be any pursuers. What should we—”

Hardly had he finished his sentence when an unstoppable streak of whiteness crawled in from the top of the car, smashed the computer, and crawled into the dashboard of the off-road vehicle.

It was not until this moment that everyone heard a swoosh.

It was an arrow!

Zhang Daniu and the ghost cat were dumbfounded. They did not even have time to react.

Li Yao cursed silently. He looked up along the hole made by the arrow and happened to find the archer who had just shot his light sword.

The guy was wearing a flying backpack. His silver wings seemed to have been boosted by lightning. He floated steadily in the air, surrounded by a deep blue force field. The storm could not affect him at all. He drew his bow again and shot three sharp arrows.

Chapter 3231: All Failed?

“Damn it!”

The enemy’s sharp arrow must’ve been made of a special material that was neither metal nor wood. It boasted tremendous electromagnetic interference. When it pierced into the dashboard, all the pointers and indexes immediately started shaking violently, just like the abnormal state when a plane entered the Bermuda Triangle.

Thankfully, this off-road vehicle still adopted an old-fashioned mechanical control system for the most part. It didn’t carry too much electronic equipment and was still able to move forward.

But the speed and the agility of the steering were obviously not sustainable.

Swoosh! Swoosh! Swoosh!

Right then, three sharp arrows pierced through the air and darted close like lightning. Despite Li Yao’s driving skills that had been accumulated over dozens of cycles, which allowed him to dodge the first two arrows, he was still not able to dodge the third arrow, which stabbed into the hood of the tank. The engine of the tank immediately roared, and the speed of the vehicle hit rock bottom.

Li Yao cursed. No matter how hard he stepped on the gas, it was to no avail. Fury was raging inside his brain. The terrain and every data on the map, including the altitude of the enemy in midair… All kinds of information was swirling inside his brain like a surging river. He made up his mind and rushed toward a sparse forest not far away.

Although it was called a forest, in fact, it was just a few coconut trees that could only be found in the tropics. If it were broad daylight, it would be impossible to resist the assault from the sky.

But in such a thunderstorm, Li Yao decided to take a gamble.

“Swoosh! Swoosh! Swoosh! Swoosh!”

Another five arrows landed around the off-road vehicle. Some of them even pierced through the roof of the vehicle and hit right between Zhang Daniu’s legs, leaving a hole in the seat. The guy was so scared that he found it hard to breathe. He was cramping and almost passed out.

/ .

But Li Yao took the opportunity to rush into the coconut woods.

“Get off!”

Li Yao roared at the ghost cat and Zhang Daniu.

“W-what?”

The two of them looked at each other and swallowed the words ‘you’re crazy’ at the same time.

“The engine is gone. I can only hold on for another three to five minutes. This is the only place where I can turn the situation around. Get off now!”

Li Yao shouted again. He braked the off-road vehicle, picked up his toolbox, and kicked the door open before he rolled out.

Thankfully, the ghost cat was quick enough to crawl out of the window and drag Zhang Daniu out of the back seat. Seeing that Li Yao had opened the cap of the tank, she quickly dragged Zhang Daniu and ran away.

BOOM!

Flames in the shape of a butterfly surged out of Li Yao’s palm and crawled into the fuel tank. The off-road vehicle immediately turned into a dazzling fireball. Even under the assault of the wind and rain, the flames and smoke still rose to more than ten meters in the sky, temporarily blocking the vision of the pursuers.

The moment the flames exploded, Li Yao took a deep breath and ran toward the coconut tree closest to him. His speed far exceeded the limits of human beings. The ninety-degree vertical trunk of the tree was as easy as walking on the ground for him. He reached the top of the tree in one breath and stomped on it hard, almost breaking the coconut tree. With the help of the rebounding force, he jumped from one coconut tree to another, and from the second coconut tree to the third coconut tree. He treated the coconut tree as a spring. With every bounce, his speed and strength increased. When he reached the last coconut tree, the trunk of the tree was almost bent into a crescent moon by him, and he shot into the sky like a roaring cannonball.

Shua!

The toolbox that Li Yao had been carrying all the time drew a vicious arc as he threw it towards the archer in the air.

Hiu!

A screwdriver arrived first, but its target was not the archer but the toolbox. The toolbox was smashed into pieces. Naturally, the tools inside were scattered everywhere.

Although the archer was able to overlook every movement in the coconut grove from high above, his attention had been distracted by the explosion of the off-road vehicle and the flying tools. He did not expect that Li Yao would soar into the sky and lunge at him like a weird bird. He was so scared that he forgot to react.

In the moment of shock, Li Yao was only three to five meters away from him.

However, since this archer dared to chase after Li Yao and the others by himself, he was clearly not someone who was easy to deal with. A moment of absent-mindedness was already a mistake that he had never made before, and he would not allow himself to continue this mistake for even a second longer.

“Swoosh! Swoosh! Swoosh! Swoosh! Swoosh!”

In just one second, he had shot seven arrows in a row, which almost blocked every angle of Li Yao’s attack.

Li Yao, on the other hand, had nowhere to borrow his strength in midair. Unless he had the ability to soar into the sky by stepping on his right foot with his left foot, he could do nothing except dodge the seven consecutive arrows.

As it turned out, there was nothing Li Yao could do about the rain of arrows. After dodging three arrows, he was already exhausted. He could only curl up and accelerate to the ground, falling into the mud heavily. Although he dodged the arrows, he did not even touch the enemy’s fur.

However…

Not long after he landed, the archer fell from the sky with a ‘plop’ and fell flat on his back.

His bulging eyes were full of blood streaks, but the blood streaks turned into lifeless blackness one after another. He could no longer hold the bow with his shaking hands and could only clutch his throat helplessly. A screwdriver had been stabbed into his throat precisely and pierced through his spine and brain stem, almost drilling out of his head.

For a strong enemy such as a ‘hunter’, the same trick could not be used twice.

But the archer, although armed with a hunting bow, was clearly not a qualified hunter.

Li Yao had used the fireball from the explosion of the off-road vehicle, the toolbox from Fairy Flower, and himself, who was flying like a weird bird, as bait in order to make the archer forget the fact that he could manipulate objects remotely.

In the end, a small screwdriver accelerated and changed its direction under Li Yao’s remote control, ending his life.

Hiss! Hiss! Hiss! Hiss! Hiss!

The archer bulged his dead fish eyes and stared at Li Yao with a complicated look. The brightness deep inside his eyes gradually disappeared.

Li Yao sighed. He pulled out the screwdriver from his throat and let the blood spurt out, giving him a quick death.

This should be the last pursuer, right?

Although the off-road vehicle had been scrapped, this place was not too far from the border. As long as there was no flood ahead, one could still find a nearby town on foot. It would not be a problem to get new transportation.

Li Yao was somewhat relieved. He turned around to look for Zhang Daniu and the ghost cat.

“Master Niu!”

He narrowed his eyes and searched the sparse coconut woods. Soon, he found Zhang Daniu leaning against a coconut tree, but there was no sign of the ghost cat.

“What happened? Where is the ghost cat?”

Li Yao was greatly surprised. With the fire still burning in his eyes, he looked at Zhang Daniu’s face, only to discover that the author of had an extremely ugly complexion. His face was as black as ink.

Li Yao suddenly had an ominous feeling.

Zhang Daniu’s expression was both strange and confused. He let go of the coconut tree and staggered towards Li Yao. He extended his hands, which were also black to the fingernails, towards Li Yao. Finally, he fell into Li Yao’s arms.

The rainwater was as cold as ice, but his body was as hot as burning coal, with a vague, sweet, stinky smell. Li Yao heard rumbling noises coming from his internal organs, as if they were… melting!

“Master Niu, what’s wrong?”

Li Yao was anxious, but Zhang Daniu was already speechless. Very soon, thick black blood flowed out of his eyes, nostrils, ears, mouth… and ears. He collapsed like an empty sack. Li Yao could not feel his bones at all. Everything below his skin had melted.
