Over his shoulder, on his back, where his back was aimed at his heart, there was a dagger with a grinning skull carved on it. It did not look like a human head but more like the head of a cat, which was the self-defense blade of the ghost cat.

BOOM! CRACK!

Thunder and lightning illuminated the bleak land and the burning coconut woods.

Li Yao’s mind was as clear as a mirror. How could he not understand what was going on?

They had all been fooled.

Ghost Cat was not a member of the Ark Foundation but a spy of the Heaven’s Origin Sector.

Maybe the original purpose of the Heaven’s Origin Sector was to capture Zhang Daniu alive. That was why they had sent a hunter team to set up multiple ambushes.

However, as the defense lines were broken through one after another, and even the last archer was killed by Li Yao’s screwdriver, and there was no way that they could bring Zhang Daniu back alive, it was better to leave a dead body than to let Zhang Daniu fall into the hands of the Ark Foundation.

This was the mission of the Ghost Cat.

Li Yao did not know how to react to such a fact. Every muscle on his face was almost out of control.

Zhang Daniu’s heart was no longer beating and he was no longer breathing. He collapsed to the ground. His chest was clearly caved in. Black blood was spreading around him like a deep, black pool.

He died a clean death. Not even the gods could save him. He could only wait for the next ‘reboot’, if there were any.

“Master Niu…”

Li Yao rubbed his face so hard that his eyes were almost blinded, but he still found it hard to accept the fact. He had escorted them all the way to this place and was about to find Red Pole, the leader of the resistance, and solve all the mysteries about him, Zhang Daniu, and Red Pole. But in the end, Zhang Daniu was still—dead?

Are you kidding me?

Li Yao really wanted to roar and shake his nonexistent hair like a lunatic.

But a moment later, something strange about Zhang Daniu’s body caught his attention.

“Wait. What—what is this?”

It was hard to explain what he saw.

As the body gradually stiffened, long, narrow red lines grew out of Zhang Daniu’s blackened skin. The red lines extended and interweaved into dense spider webs. His ‘skin’ peeled off like cracked paint, revealing a new layer of pale skin!

Chapter 3232: Plan B

“What’s going on?”

Li Yao extended his finger and pressed Zhang Daniu’s snow-white skin. He discovered that Zhang Daniu’s ‘body’ was not stiff at all. Instead, it was filled with the elasticity and tenacity of rubber.

Maybe it was his imagination, but Li Yao felt that Zhang Daniu’s body was getting fat at a visible speed, like a balloon that was being inflated.

Very soon, the black skin that had been corroded by the venom peeled off and turned into ink-like liquid. What appeared in front of Li Yao was a white-skinned stranger.

There was not a single hair on his body. His facial features were so shallow that they were barely visible, as if there was a goose egg on his head. Although he was dead, there was a vague smile on his face, as if he was glad that his enemy had been fooled.

This man, this man was not Zhang Daniu!

Another lightning flashed in the sky. Li Yao finally realized what was going on. He was infuriated.

As it turned out, he was the only one who had been fooled from the beginning to the end. In the soul-stirring secret war between the Heaven’s Origin Sector and the Ark Foundation, he was just an ignorant chess piece.

Biting his lips, Li Yao groped the body of the anonymous man and found something hard in his lower abdomen.

Not long after his heart stopped beating, the thing was automatically activated and sent out feeble electric waves.

If Li Yao’s guess was correct, this should be a device similar to a locator.

/ .

As he expected, it didn’t take long before he heard the rotors of a helicopter coming from the horizon. The sound came from the south. It shouldn’t be the pursuers, but the reinforcements. However, by now, Li Yao was no longer able to tell who was his enemy and who was his friend, and which side he should stand on.

…

In the evening, they reached an inconspicuous port in the neighboring country.

It was a typical ‘Banana Republic’, also known as a political chaos. The economy failed, the people’s livelihood declined, and the authorities had no control whatsoever. They could only empty the resources under their territory in exchange for the leftovers of the failed countries.

In the early decades, the country had been dominated by warlords and drug lords. Or rather, in the hot, humid, chaotic land, warlords and drug lords were always the same thing. Even the bloody scouts in the army had to rely on drugs to anesthetize their souls and maintain morale.

Over the past few years, although the authorities had been pushing forward with the help of the outside world, and the country had experienced a brief period of brightness and prosperity, the old forces were too deeply rooted to go back. Earthquakes, floods, storms, and tsunamis had pushed the country back to its original state.

Li Yao looked down at the land from the helicopter. He saw that the roads had been washed away by the flood, and the towns had been drowned by the mud. Countless people were running around like ants on a hot pan, but they could do nothing but watch the natural disasters coming at them. The country did not have the strong infrastructure of the northern neighbors, nor did it have the well-trained government soldiers who were willing to sacrifice themselves for the country. The people who were suffering were as humble as ants and grass.

However, the political ecology that was divided and riddled with holes was the best base for secret organizations such as the Ark Foundation. Superpowers were like fish in water when they were active in the hot and humid forest. They had infiltrated the organizations of some warlords in secret and even fostered their own local forces. The small port in front of them was already under the control of the Ark Foundation.

Since the catastrophe had cut off the connection between the port and the capital city, the authorities and the government army could barely look after themselves. The place had become an independent kingdom temporarily. The members of the Ark Foundation could march out and carry the coldly-shining weapons to their barges and cargo ships without any worries.

Li Yao met the grey mist lady again in the military camp outside the port.

It was said that the place used to belong to a certain magnate drug lord decades ago. The magnate drug lord considered the place to be his last nest. Although it looked plain on the surface, it was a complicated underground palace that looked like an ant nest and a honeycomb.

Naturally, even the most solid underground palace could not resist the infiltration of the super experts. Right now, this place was the biggest secret base of the Ark Foundation in Southeast Asia. No. At this moment, there was no need for ‘secrets’ at all. The Ark Foundation was preparing for the final battle.

After a day of absence, the grey mist lady’s face was much paler than before. There was not the slightest hint of color on her face. Her breathing was feeble, too, as if she had suffered serious internal injuries.

Behind her stood Sparda, who only had one arm left. Even his remaining arm was bandaged in blood, and his face was as pale as paper.

Li Yao had been furious and had made up his mind to curse the moment he saw the grey mist lady. He would even grab her collar and teach her a lesson. Then, he would tell her word by word, “I’m done!”

However, seeing that the grey mist lady and Sparda were both so seriously wounded and did not know where to start, he was dazed for a long time before he asked, “Where are the ‘Seven Gods’?”

“Sacrificed.”

The lady in the grey mist said casually, “We didn’t know that the hunter had an undying body. His capability is already higher than that of the ultimate awakeners. If Uncle Hui hadn’t sacrificed himself, none of us would have been able to run away from the hunter.

“What—”

Li Yao was rendered speechless. The image of the middle-aged man in red jeans with a beer belly appeared in his mind again. He was silent for a long time, before he took a deep breath and gnashed his teeth. “You lied to me. That was not Zhang Daniu!”

“Yes, it is true that we are not escorting the author of ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivator’, but a special awakener named ‘Bizarre Face’ in the foundation.

The grey mist lady said, “Believe it or not, I didn’t know until the last moment, and Uncle Hui didn’t know until the moment of his death.”

“…Why?”

Li Yao asked solemnly.

“Don’t you already know the answer to that question?

The grey mist lady replied casually, “Ghost cat.”

“Undercover?”

Li Yao rolled his eyes. “You knew that the ghost cat was a spy that the ‘apocalypse’ planted in the ‘Ark’.

“At first, we were only suspicious. Besides the ghost cat, we also have a long list of suspects, including you. Don’t be surprised. Isn’t it suspicious that someone like you, who has awakened a powerful ability out of nowhere and is actively trying to get in touch with Zhang Daniu?”

The grey mist lady said, “As I said before, the enemy has the method to precisely strike the core of our organization. We cannot be too cautious if we want to deal with them.

“So—”

Li Yao pondered for a moment and said, “Why don’t you split into two groups, one real and the other false? The elite troops that we gathered along the way and fled south through the train with great fanfare was just a bait to lure the enemy so that the hunters would chase after them and cover the escape of the real Zhang Daniu?”

“Not two ways, but three ways. It was not just to cover Zhang Daniu’s safe retreat, but also to clear the nails that the ‘apocalypse’ nailed into the ‘Ark’.

The grey mist lady said, “Other than the ghost cat, two spies were exposed during the operation and have been taken care of by us. The rest of them proved their loyalty with their actions and were qualified to board the ‘Ark’ to meet the Red Pole. It is a necessary procedure for the ultimate victory. We have no other options whether you forgive us or not.

Li Yao didn’t know what to say when the guy was so straightforward. He thought for a moment and said, “So, Zhang Daniu has been evacuated safely?”

“Yes. He has boarded the starship and will meet the ‘Ark’ soon.”

“Zhang Daniu is indeed the key to the war,” the grey mist lady said. “Both the ‘Ark’ and the ‘apocalypse’ have to get their hands on him or kill him. Even the Red Pole has to reveal its traces and direct the operation in person, which gives the ‘apocalypse’ and even the will of the earth an opportunity.

“I should’ve told you that, for some reason, the ‘will of the earth’ was forced to restart because of a war that took place in the universe. The Red Pole seized the once-in-a-lifetime opportunity and prepared to deal a fatal blow to the will of the earth when it was forced to restart.

“According to the Red Pole Star, at the level of the universal war, the will of the earth has been stalled by the attackers from the outside world. The two parties are in a temporary impasse. Almost all of their strength must be focused on each other. Therefore, it is impossible to obliterate the Ark Foundation and the Red Pole Star themselves on a large scale. It will consume too much energy and lead to the total collapse of the universal war. Therefore, precision strikes and decapitation tactics are the only options.

“The so-called ‘targeted drugs’ must be placed around the cancer cells precisely in order to eliminate the cancer cells without hurting the normal cells. If the cancer cells are never found, it will be useless no matter how effective the targeted drugs are. It is also the reason why the Red Pole Star has to hide itself deep under the ocean and why the Heaven’s Origin Sector and the will of the earth are trying to lock onto him at any cost.

“Red Pole has been hiding behind the scenes for several years. Even we don’t know his real whereabouts. The operation to ‘escort’ the author of ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivator’ is his only chance to expose himself. How can the Heaven’s Origin Sector and the will of the earth let go of such an opportunity? And how can we be so careless as to screen and screen those who might be close to Red Pole?

“Are you clear now?”

Li Yao asked coldly.

“Almost done. Student Li Yao, please follow me.”

The grey mist lady brought Li Yao to a warehouse in the military camp.

An iron chain was dangling from the beam at the center of the warehouse. A woman was tied up by it. It was the ghost cat that had assassinated ‘Zhang Daniu’ when Li Yao was not paying attention.

Li Yao was dazed for a moment. He did not expect that the woman had been captured by the Ark Foundation. It seemed that Red Pole had indeed deployed a dragnet and prepared for all possibilities.

Chapter 3233: His Own Command

The ghost cat used to be a petite, quick-witted girl. But right now, she was like a zealot who had entered a devil’s den. Her upside-down face was filled with confused flames. When she saw Li Yao and the grey mist lady enter, she stared at them and giggled.

“The final trial is coming. No one can escape the judgement of reincarnation. No matter how you struggle, you will only be misleading others. Give up, repent, and be saved!”

She narrowed her eyes, intoxicated by the so-called redemption. She was caught in the endless loop of her own logic and could not free herself.

Beside her stood a hunchbacked, sleazy man. His appearance was plain, but he had a pair of extremely large hands. His fingers were almost twice as long as those of ordinary people, and there was even an extra finger bone, giving the impression that there were two giant spiders on his wrists.

When he placed his hands on the temples of the ghost cat, his palms covered the ghost cat’s head like an iron hat.

The man mumbled in the native language of Nanyang. A series of weird tattoos appeared on his arms, followed by what looked like venomous insects that were dancing weirdly on his arms as if the tattoos were alive.

Along with such a strange scene, Li Yao felt ripples coming from the palm of the ghost cat, which were similar to brainwaves.

In the blink of an eye, the ghost cat’s eyes rolled back as its entire body started trembling violently, causing the iron chains to rattle chaotically.

The man seemed to have read a lot of information from the brain of the ghost cat. He mumbled in satisfaction, sometimes in a trance, and sometimes in a trance. His expression was quite similar to that of the ghost cat earlier.

While he was performing the technique, he was also staring at Li Yao. His eyes were half cold and half scorching, as if they were two daggers that were trying to dig out Li Yao’s deepest secrets.

“They are interrogating the ghost cat.”

Li Yao pondered for a moment and asked, “But why did you do that in front of me? To intimidate me?”

Li Yao rolled his eyes. He suddenly sensed that dozens of strong auras were hiding in the shadows around the warehouse. With the help of the dim lights, he could vaguely see that some fully-armed Awakened were either standing or sitting, touching their daggers, or fiddling with their guns. They were all staring at him in high alert.

Li Yao was dazed for a moment before he burst into fury.

“Wait—”

He asked solemnly, “Are you interrogating her or testing me?”

The weird-looking man with living tattoos on his arms finally finished the spell. He grinned and shook his head at the grey mist lady.

When the fan-like hands left the ghost cat’s head, the female spy’s eyes had already turned dead gray and were drooping down like a dead fish.

“I’m sorry. His name is ‘Brainworm’. He is a mind reader in the organization.”

The grey mist lady explained expressionlessly, “You know that, since she was sent by the Heaven’s Origin Sector to the Ark Foundation as an undercover agent, she must’ve received special training. Normal interrogation methods won’t work on her at all. However, the ‘Brain Insect’ has the ability to absorb brainwaves, which is a perfect counter for the Ghost Cats whose brainwaves are particularly developed.

“If you are really a spy sent by the Heaven’s Origin Sector and your meeting with the ghost cat was just a show, the brainwaves of the ghost cat will definitely have subtle changes when it sees you walking in. The brain bug will definitely notice you.

“But as it turns out, the ghost cat didn’t have any special reactions to you. It’s true that you didn’t know each other before—this is partly to prove your innocence and to build more trust for you.

“My patience has a limit!

Li Yao roared in a low voice, “Professor Zhang Jiashu, the patient in the ‘mindspace’, is the first test. Fighting the hunters in the rushing trains, floods, and mudslides is the second test. Now, he has been suspected by you and sent to the ghost cat for screening. This is the third test. What do you want exactly? Is it not enough?”

“Do you think it’s enough?”

“Think about it in a different way,” the grey mist lady replied. “If you are the leader of a secret organization such as the Ark Foundation, and you run into a mysterious man who has somehow awakened super abilities and might even become a ‘super perceptive Cultivator’ overnight, and who is involved in the center of the swirl by accident, how many tests do you think it will take to prove his innocence and let him enter the most secret nest of the organization, the leader who is of paramount importance for the organization at such a critical moment?”

While she was talking, the grey mist lady gestured to the depths of the warehouse. Immediately, two muscular men pushed an iron chair that looked like a surgical table to Li Yao along two rails on the ground.

“Four times.”

The grey mist lady extended four fingers at Li Yao and said, “No matter how many tests you think it will take to prove the innocence of such a mysterious man, Red Pole believes that it will take four tests for him to be 100% sure of your identity before he lets you into the real ‘Ark’.”

“What’s the meaning of this?”

Li Yao looked at the iron chair with an awful face. “What other tests do you want?”

“We hope to wake you up from your previous life.

In the darkness, a black-haired man wearing a white coat and gold-framed glasses walked over slowly. He slightly bowed to Li Yao and said with a smile, “Allow me to introduce myself. I am ‘Traveler Dream’. Fellow Student Li Yao and Teacher Niu should have seen a lot of my posts on the net. You must be very interested in me, right?”

Li Yao was dazed for a moment. Then he realized that they must’ve talked to Zhang Daniu before they welcomed him. The guy must’ve been spineless and would tell everything without any coercion.

“I am responsible for every word I post on the network. I guarantee that every word is true. Even if it is not true, at least it is my true feelings.

Dream Traveler said, “However, what is not stated in the posts on the net is that I’ve been staying on the ‘Ark’ for the past month and received enlightenment and training from the Red Pole. My capability is much higher than before. Not only can I see my past life, I can also guide more awakeners to explore and discover their past lives.

“Student Li Yao, there’s no need to be so nervous. Don’t treat this as a test. Try to treat it as a special training session. This is a trip to the depths of your soul. Including Red Pole, we are all very curious. You have only awakened part of the hazy memory fragments of your previous life, yet you possess such powerful combat strength. If under my guidance, you can clearly recall your previous life, your previous life, and even the memories of dozens of reincarnations and inherit all your abilities, what kind of terrifying appearance will you reveal?

“This is what I want to do.

“In fact, guiding the awakeners to discover their past lives is a very exhausting task for me. If it were a monster between a ‘finalist’ and a ‘super perceptive’ such as yourself, I would have to take tremendous risks in order to help you find your true self. However, according to Red Pole, you and Master Niu are both indispensable pieces in the jigsaw puzzle. For the sacred mission and true freedom, I… will not regret it even if I die nine times!”

Dream Traveler stopped smiling and stared at Li Yao solemnly.

Li Yao pondered for a long time.

“What if I refuse?

He silently observed the positions of all the awakened ones in the warehouse. He did not even let go of the scent that was as weak as a zombie that was hiding in the deepest part of the darkness. In the blink of an eye, he drafted dozens of plans to break out of the siege in his mind and asked cautiously.

The grey mist lady and Dream Traveler looked at each other and smiled bitterly.

“Rest assured. It’s not as bad as you think. There won’t be any stupid things like ‘silence’.

The grey mist lady said, “The most important reason is that we can’t kill you. Even if you are heavily wounded, we will certainly pay a high price. Maybe two thirds of the thirty or so elite warriors lurking in this place will be killed. The war is drawing near. We can’t afford such losses. This is the result of the Red Pole’s personal evaluation.

“Therefore, even if you refused the last test, there was nothing we could do. There was no need to hurt you at all.

“However, as a result, you will not be able to go to the depths of the ocean with us to look for the ‘Ark’, nor will you be able to meet the real Red Pole. We will part ways here and leave you with sufficient assets, firearms, and local currency. Although the end of the world has come and the currency has become useless paper, I hope you understand that this is our limit.

Li Yao sniffed and said nothing.

It was true that he could have been angry at the other party’s distrust and refused the multiple tests. He believed that both the ‘Ark’ and the ‘apocalypse’ would have to pay a terrible price to keep him.

However, since he had refused the test, the safety of his identity was still questionable. It was reasonable that the other party was not willing to take him to the ocean.

Li Yao hesitated. He did not like the feeling of suspicion and scrutiny.

“I’m not trying to convince you, Student Li Yao.”

Dream Traveler opened his hands, indicating that he meant no harm. “However, before we part, Red Pole has a very strange message for you. ‘No matter how discontented you are about the tests and the suspicions, no matter how wronged you are, and no matter how much you resent him, I hope that you can persist until the end. Because, as long as you awaken your past life’s memories and discover your true identity, you will know that all the tests and suspicions are necessary. You may even be the one to blame for your own arrangements.’

“Well…”

Li Yao was baffled. “‘Blame it on myself’. Did I arrange so many suspicions, checks, and tests? What is going on exactly?”

Although he was baffled, he somehow believed the nonsense in his mind.

Chapter 3234: Who Is It?

“Please?”

Dream Traveler extended another invitation to Li Yao.

“Wait. I have a question.”

Pondering for a moment, Li Yao said, “I want to know whether or not Zhang Daniu accepted your guidance and woke up his previous life with your help. By logic, this guy is quite suspicious, isn’t he?”

“Of course.”

“We are not targeting anybody in particular,” Dream Traveler said. “Even our own people have to undergo rigorous tests before they return to the Ark. Of course, we cannot miss the author of ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivators’. We tested him earlier and traced his memories from his previous five lives under my guidance. There was nothing special about him.

“Oh?”

Li Yao was greatly interested. “What did he do in his previous five lives?”

“Going back in time from this life, I’ve been an online writer for three consecutive lives. You know, writing some weird, sexy, popular books, lying to ignorant teenagers, and fooling them.”

Dream Traveler said, “From the previous life to the fourth life, the development of the reincarnation world was slightly different. There was no such thing as a ‘network novel’. He was a reporter of a tabloid. He wrote some celebrity affairs, made some shocking headlines, went to court with a minor celebrity, and so on.

“In his fifth life, he was still engaged in writing, but he was an author of adult action novels, which seemed to be his specialty. After years of hard work, he finally made a name for himself in the industry, only to be caught in an anti-corruption operation and sentenced to justice. That was basically everything.

“All in all, he is different from the normal ‘Observers’. He is just an ordinary person with no moral bottom line. The original purpose of his creation was purely for the sake of benefits. Or in his own words, ‘money and beautiful women’ did not inject souls into his work. Logically speaking, it is very difficult for such a person to awaken, let alone advance into an Observer. However, a lot of things that he could not write at all happened to appear in . Perhaps this is why Red Pole Star is particularly interested in him.

“In any case, although he was a greedy, lecherous, third-rate writer and tabloid reporter who was willing to write anything for money in his previous five lives, it was true that he had never been related to the Heaven’s Origin Sector at all. He was just one of the most inconspicuous nobodies. When the Earth was restarted, it was just a series of insignificant data. Even if the Heaven’s Origin Sector really wanted to find a spy, they would absolutely not find him.”

“Understood.”

Li Yao nodded. He took a deep breath and sat on the iron chair.

/ .

The grey mist lady and Dream Traveler were both relieved.

Li Yao suddenly asked Traveler Meng, “Do you have a watch? What time is it?”

“Yes.”

Dream Traveler took out a delicate, vintage pocket watch from his pocket. The shell of the watch was carved with eight-tentacled octopuses in the shape of a helix, giving off an oceanic vibe. “It’s 8:05 p.m.”

“A few seconds?”

Li Yao asked again.

“A few seconds?”

Dream Traveler was dazed for a moment.

“Yes, to the second. Tell me.”

Li Yao said coldly.

“Uh, it was 8:05:32.”

Dream Traveler frowned. “What’s wrong?”

“Nothing.”

Li Yao blinked his eyes. There was confusion on his face, as if he didn’t know why he asked for the time so precisely.

But he soon forgot about it and nodded at Traveler Meng. “Since it is a rule of the Ark Foundation, let’s begin!”

“Alright. Please relax and watch the clock. Watch the hour, minute, and second hand.”

Holding the watchchain, Dream Traveler dangled the watch in front of Li Yao and shook it.

The hour hand emitted a gloomy blue glow, the minute hand flickered with a ghostly dark green color, and the second hand flickered with a burning red glow. The small pocket watch emitted clicking mechanical sounds that filled the entire warehouse, suppressing the storm outside and causing the entire world to become exceptionally tranquil.

Staring at the pocket watch, Li Yao’s eyes turned into two immeasurable swirls. In the trance, his senses seemed to be hundreds of times sharper than before. He could clearly hear every breath and heartbeat of Dream Traveler, the grey mist lady, and the Spartan soldiers, as well as the sound of their blood flowing in every vein of their body. He could also see through the containers and see the awakened soldiers who were armed to the teeth and hiding in the darkness. He could even sense the torrential rain outside the military camp and count every drop in the wind.

Hum! Hum! Hum! Hum!

Countless mosquitoes unique to the rainforest were dancing around the dim lights on the beams.

No. Not ‘countless’, but 432 mosquitoes.

The 432 mosquitoes seemed to have turned into a clear mark that was deeply engraved in Li Yao’s heart.

“Very good. I can already vaguely feel your soul. What a beautiful, magnificent, and profound thing…”

Dream Traveler’s eyes were beaming with brilliance behind his gold-rimmed glasses, too. His eyes were like hazy chimneys that were spreading toward Li Yao. “Read a paragraph with me next. Don’t mind it. The paragraph itself is meaningless. It is just meant to resonate with our brainwaves so that I can dig into your memories. If you agree, blink your left eye.”

Li Yao remembered everything around him, including the bouncing pointer, the heartbeat of the awakened Cultivators, the distribution of every raindrop in the wind, and the trajectories of the 432 mosquitoes above his head. Then, he blinked his left eye.

“Blood-black nothingness begins to weave a network.

Dream Traveler said softly.

“Blood-black nothingness begins to weave a network.

Li Yao repeated stiffly.

“The cells are connected, connected, and connected to the main trunk.

Dream Traveler’s voice turned even darker.

“The cells are connected, connected, and connected to the main trunk.

Li Yao’s voice turned low, too. His pupils gradually expanded, and he was lost, as if he had been drowned in endless memories.

“Therefore, against the darkness, a pillar of water with a fountain spurting upwards appeared.

Dream Traveler’s voice was getting softer and softer, turning from a feather to dust. The pocket watch in his hand was shaking slower and slower, too, as if it had been frozen in midair by some weird force. In the end, it was really at a 45-degree angle from the ground and stopped moving!

“Therefore, against the darkness, a spring… that was spurting upward… appeared.

Before Li Yao could finish his poem, he found that the entire world had turned into a fuzzy swirl. Everything around him seemed to be a combination of sand and smoke. The secret port in the depths of the jungle was gone. What replaced it was a city with skyscrapers and glass walls that looked like ten thousand mirrors.

He was dreaming again. He was back in the maze that had been repeated many times over the past week.

However, this time, his mind was particularly clear. He knew that he was dreaming, and he knew that his real self was sitting in the warehouse of the military camp at the port. It was a ‘clear dream’.

“I…”

Li Yao saw himself.

He seemed to have two perspectives at the same time. One was the ‘god’s perspective’ that looked down from the sky at a 45-degree angle, while the other was the ‘first person’s perspective’ that he was in. Through different perspectives, he was quickly recalling his previous life and the soul-stirring and brilliant battles.

“So, I was already a member of the resistance organization in my previous life.

Watching the battles of superpowers that were even more brilliant than those in sci-fi movies, Li Yao suddenly realized what was going on. “No wonder I have a natural sense of familiarity with the ‘Ark Foundation’ even though I dislike the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’. I even feel that the Red Pole, their leader, is very familiar. As it turns out, I was a ‘Prison Breaker’ in my previous life!

And this was not the end.

Li Yao could clearly feel a force pushing him from behind, as if a pair of wings that could allow him to travel through memories had been inserted into his body. Presumably, this was the super ability of Dream Traveler.

The city turned into ruins in the thermonuclear war and was restarted in the ruins. The maze of mirrors made of countless glass walls collapsed one by one, but they were soon reconstructed into a new maze that was even larger and more complicated. Li Yao searched for his past in the dream and saw that he was almost always a member of the resistance groups in more than ten reincarnations. At the very least, he was an Awakener who woke up naturally and discovered the ‘Earth BUG’ before he was brutally wiped out. He even captured a memory piece from a long, long time ago. He recalled that he had been a mechanic and a racer in a certain life and passed through a ‘Earth BUG’ while he was racing.

It had been a long, long time ago. An unimaginably long time ago.

The memories of his previous life strengthened Li Yao’s determination and his combat ability. He could clearly feel that his soul was expanding like a blood-filled organ.

However…

The silver ball had been following him in the dream.

It used to be connected to the back of Li Yao’s head by a silver thread like a balloon, hanging in the air neither too far nor too close. But every time Li Yao recalled his previous life, the distance between Li Yao and it would be shortened. In the end, when the silver thread between Li Yao and itself was less than one meter long, it would hover above Li Yao’s head coldly.

Li Yao’s confused and uneasy face was reflected on the mirror-like surface before it was twisted into a weird and hideous appearance.

Li Yao didn’t remember encountering such a thing in his previous life.

Even ‘Cultivator Li Yao’ in ‘40,000 Years of Cultivation’ had never encountered such a weird existence.

“What are you? What are you exactly?”

The uneasiness in Li Yao’s heart reached its peak. He was so anxious that he could not control his emotions. He punched the silver ball and asked, “Why are you following me?”

BAM!

The mysterious silver ball, on the other hand, seemed to be too weak to withstand a single blow. It was blown into countless pieces by Li Yao’s punch. However, each of the curved pieces reflected a weird and twisted face that was smiling at him weirdly.

“Finally, you’ve come this far. You know who you are and your real destiny, don’t you?

Billions of faces spoke at the same time.

Then, countless faces turned into bloody mouths and swallowed him!

His memory turned into a nightmare, waking him from his nightmare.

He saw that Dream Traveler was vomiting blood and falling backwards. His face was twisted to the point that it was about to be fractured. Dream Traveler pointed at him and screamed, “You—you are not Li Yao!”

Chapter 3235: Glass Ball

He seemed to have woken up from a long sleep that had lasted billions of years. He yawned and smiled at Dream Traveler.

In Traveler Meng’s eyes, the smile was even more hideous than the faces of the ghosts in the deepest part of hell.

“Stop him! Stop him! Stop him!”

The Dream Traveler’s face was pale. His screams drilled into the ears of all the awakeners and scratched their brains brutally.

CRACK! CRACK!

The iron chair he was sitting on had automatic locks that could lock the wrists and ankles of the person sitting on it. However, he had been very cooperative just now, and there were no problems with the first three tests. The grey mist lady did not want to push him too hard, so she did not force him to lock himself.

But right now, it was too late for the spring lock to snap back.

Before the steel locks were fully locked, his wrists and ankles had expanded to twice the size of normal, and the four spring-loaded locks were pushed back. Before anybody realized what was going on, he had arrived in front of Traveler and grasped his neck.

“Shh…”

He said to Dream Traveler with a smile. Then, with a crack, he crushed Dream Traveler’s neck into pieces. His head tilted to one side weirdly.

When he threw Dream Traveler to the ground, the guide of his previous life was already dead.

“Ah!”

The abrupt change took everyone by surprise. Sparda was the most quick-minded of all. He raised the plasma gun out of reflex and pulled the trigger hard.

Without looking back, he extended his right hand and grasped the air.

An unbelievable scene took place. A ball of lightning condensed with destructive force had just whizzed out of the muzzle of Sparda’s gun when it strangely came to a halt in midair. It was as if two forces from two different directions were forcibly tearing it apart, causing it to emit crackling sounds in the solidified air. The deadlock didn’t last long. The ball of plasma unexpectedly returned the way it had come and drilled back into the barrel of the gun. “Boom!” The plasma gun directly exploded. The scattered fragments of the gun pierced deeply into Sparda’s body. Together with the raging electric arcs, the stocky man was directly turned into a broken doll.

“You are not Li Yao. Who are you exactly?”

The grey face of the grey mist lady turned even paler. The wounds all over her body spurted out blood again. She summoned her super power by force. A lot of mist spurted out of her orifices and pores and surrounded him as if it were alive.

His lips curled into a scornful and cruel smile. He clenched his right hand, which had just blown up the plasma, into a fist and punched at the center of the grey mist casually. Suddenly, he heard the sound of air being torn apart through the gaps of his fingers. The grey mist was torn into streams of air and fled in all directions. Even the grey mist lady was blown more than ten meters away and crashed into a container, leaving a human-shaped pit on the ground.

The dozens of awakened Cultivators lurking around the warehouse immediately lunged at him when they saw that he had killed or heavily wounded three core members of the organization.

“Don’t come close!”

The grey mist lady, on the other hand, sensed his horror during the brief exchange. For the first time, indescribable fear appeared in the depths of her eyes as she screamed, “Run! Run now!”

However, she was too late.

No. It should be said that, even if she raised the alarm in the first second, there was no way that she could stop everything and save the life of any Awakened.

“Da da da da da da da da da!”

Dozens of modified heavy firearms bombarded him with a barrage that was like a torrential rain, yet it was unable to cause even a ripple to appear in his swamp-like eyes. The bullets tore his blurred shadow into thousands of threads, while his true body had already turned into an electric arc and flashed behind the numerous awakened ones.

BAM!

An awakened one flew high into the sky. While he was still in the air, his internal organs could not bear the high pressure and exploded, dying a miserable death.

BOOM!

The second awakener was kicked away. At the same time, he activated the four grenades hanging on the enemy’s waist with the ability to manipulate objects remotely so that the Awakener would die without a grave surrounded by four fireworks.

Pa!

The third awakener was hit right on the head. The most solid helmet was as fragile as a matchbox under his palm. The helmet and the head were immediately chopped into pieces.

Hu!

The sacrifice of three awakened Cultivators in a row finally bought enough time for his remaining comrades. The fourth awakened Cultivator closed his hands, from which he was wreathed in burning flames. It was a certain technique that allowed him to control the temperature freely!

But neither the fireballs, the firewall, nor the raging dragons could resist his attacks.

He did not even attack the man directly. He merely snapped his fingers, and the fire inside the man’s body went out of control. The raging flames devoured his cells and his soul instead, resulting in a typical ‘self-combustion’.

It was not a battle but a massacre.

Whether it was the Novice, the Deep Perception, or the Ultimate Perception, whether it was the control over fire, frost, lightning, poison, and other abilities, they were all as vulnerable as three-year-olds in front of him.

He butchered the enemy with ease and turned the warehouse into a blood-stained palace.

His body was wreathed in steaming blood mist, his wet internal organs were stomped on, and a mysterious smile was still on his face. His eyes were like a swamp that could drown all the souls. He was the embodiment of death, the devil king who had walked slowly from the depths of hell!

Right now, other than the heavily wounded Lady Gray Mist and the half-dead Spartan, there was not a single living person in the warehouse—he was definitely not a living person, but a destroyer, a devil, and a death god!

He walked to the front of the grey mist lady and looked down at her in an inhuman way.

BAM!

Not far away, Sparda somehow woke up with his broken body. He raised a pistol casually and shot the last bullet at him.

He waved his hands casually, as if he were chasing away an annoying mosquito. The bullet drew a weird arc in midair and turned 180 degrees, drilling into the center of Sparda’s eyebrow precisely. Then, it drilled a hole the size of a bowl on the back of the head of the loyal resistance, taking away most of the brain tissues.

“As it turns out, cough cough cough cough cough, it really is you. You are the best assassin sent by the Heaven’s Origin Sector to deal with the ‘target drugs’ of the Red Pole.”

The grey mist lady smiled miserably. “Is—is this the power of a ‘super perceptive’? It is indeed powerful. It’s also our fault that we were stupid enough to invite a wolf into our house and bring you to this place.

“However, you were too careless and exposed yourself in advance. So what if your combat ability is strong enough to kill all of us? The Red Pole is hiding in the mist in the depths of the ocean. You won’t be able to find him. Hehe. As long as you can’t see him in person, what can you do even if you are a walking nuclear bomb?

“You are doomed. In the war that has been repeated thousands of times, we—we will be the final winners. No force can stop our freedom and… hope!”

“Oh.”

He listened quietly, nodded, and crushed the heart of the grey mist lady.

He stood in a mountain of corpses and a sea of blood for a long time. The slight frown on his face revealed the irritation in his heart—the irritation of ‘my house is so dirty and needs to be cleaned up for a long time’.

Then, he returned to Dream Traveler’s body. He curled his finger and picked up the pocket watch that was embedded with rubies and blue gems and carved with an octopus pattern.

Fortunately, he was very careful just now and did not damage the precise mechanical structure of the pocket watch. The pointer was still clicking.

He stared at the hovering second hand for a long time. Then he sighed and sent the pocket watch to the sky, where it hovered above his eyes.

He opened his fingers again and aimed his right hand at the pocket watch, twisting his wrist anticlockwise.

CRACK! CRACK! CRACK! CRACK!

The speed at which he twisted his wrist was extremely slow, but the look on his face was extremely prudent. He seemed to be struggling and even in pain, as if an invisible gear was locked in his palm and he was controlling an enormous machine by turning the gear in the opposite direction.

A normal person’s hand would only be able to twist 90 degrees counterclockwise at most. But Li Yao had managed to twist his hand 180 degrees and continued moving forward despite the cracking sounds of his bones.

As the angle of the twist grew larger and larger, the skin of his arm began to crack, and the bones of his arm were completely broken, poking out ghastly white bones. Blood sprayed like a meteor shower on the pocket watch and his face, but his expression didn’t change in the slightest. On the contrary, the look in his eyes grew deeper and darker.

As he twisted the air, the second hand of the pocket watch moved slower and slower. The air was frozen, and visible ripples were spreading out in the space around him, forming a giant counterclockwise swirl.

Finally—

When his palm rotated 360 degrees and his muscles, bones, and veins completely exploded, the second hand, minute hand, and hour hand of the pocket watch all stopped.

What also came to a halt seemed to be… time!

He smiled in satisfaction and turned his broken wrist counterclockwise by 361 degrees.

His blood-stained fingertips seemed to boast terrifying magical power. After the second hand of the pocket watch shivered for a moment, it followed his finger and walked backwards!

It was hard to describe the sound of the second hand going backwards. It was not the clicking sound of the normal rotation, but a sound that seemed to belong to glass bullets that were bouncing up and down until they were gone.

…

“In any case, although he was a greedy, lecherous, third-rate writer and tabloid reporter who was willing to write anything for money in his previous five lives, it was true that he had never been related to the Heaven’s Origin Sector at all. He was just one of the most inconspicuous nobodies. When the Earth was restarted, it was just a series of insignificant data. Even if the Heaven’s Origin Sector really wanted to find a spy, they would absolutely not find him.”

Dream Traveler said to Li Yao.

Li Yao was in a trance. He tilted his head and listened to the voice from afar or deep inside his brain.

He didn’t know whether or not it was his imagination, but he seemed to have heard a sound, how should he put it, a sound of “glass marbles falling on the floor, ‘Dada da da’ bouncing, getting denser and denser, and finally disappearing”.

However, there was only one floor in this warehouse, and the top floor was the canopy. How could there be glass marbles? It was probably the sound of wind and rain outside. He must have heard it wrong.

“Understood.”

Li Yao nodded. He took a deep breath and sat on the iron chair.

The grey mist lady and Dream Traveler were both relieved.

Li Yao suddenly asked Traveler Meng, “Do you have a watch? What time is it?”

“Yes.”

Dream Traveler took out a delicate, vintage pocket watch from his pocket. The shell of the watch was carved with eight-tentacled octopuses in the shape of a helix, giving off an oceanic vibe. “It’s 8:05 p.m.”

“A few seconds?”

Li Yao asked again.

“A few seconds?”

Dream Traveler was dazed for a moment.

“Yes, to the second. Tell me.”

Li Yao said coldly.

“Uh, it was 8:05:32.”

Dream Traveler frowned. “What’s wrong?”

“Nothing.”

Li Yao blinked his eyes. There was confusion on his face, as if he didn’t know why he asked for the time so precisely.

But he soon forgot about it and nodded at Traveler Meng. “Since it is a rule of the Ark Foundation, let’s begin!”

Chapter 3236: Déjà Vu

“Alright. Please relax and watch the clock. Watch the hour, minute, and second hand.”

Holding the watchchain, Dream Traveler dangled the watch in front of Li Yao and shook it.

Li Yao gazed at the pocket watch and entered a mysterious trance. Everything in front of him seemed familiar—the dim light of the pointer, the clicking sounds of the mechanical structure, the howling storm outside, the position of everyone around him, their breathing and heartbeat, and the 432 mosquitoes hovering around the lights.

Yes, every detail was correct.

“Blood-black nothingness begins to weave a network.

Dream Traveler’s voice was very gentle.

The spell put Li Yao in a dream and teleported him to the memory pieces from a long time ago.

The data that had not been completely cleared in the reincarnation cycle was reassembled into illusionary and real worlds. He saw himself rushing in the labyrinths and battlefields of cities, fighting for freedom and hope and even sacrificing his own life time and time again.

“As it turned out, I have always been a ‘Prison Breaker’. The five, no, ten reincarnations in the past have always been the same.

Li Yao suddenly realized something.

However…

He suddenly turned around and gazed at the burning horizon in the distance. He felt that something was missing.

Right, where was the silver ball that appeared in his dream? The thing that was tied to the back of his head like a balloon had vanished.

However, it was an unimportant thing. Li Yao shook his head hard and soon forgot it. Guided by Dream Traveler, he focused his attention on the more ancient past and awakened more memories and abilities.

He did not know how long he had been wandering in the memories of his previous life. Maybe it was not until he and Dream Traveler ran out of their soul power that everything around him gradually turned fuzzy and twisted. He seemed to have been dragged back to a long tunnel and returned to the real world of this life, to a port warehouse somewhere in Southeast Asia.

“Li Yao? Li Yao!”

He heard someone calling him.

/ .

“That’s right.”

His eyes suddenly turned sharp. “There is no doubt that I am Li Yao!”

“Congratulations on passing the final test!”

The room was filled with a relaxed atmosphere. All the awakeners of the Ark Foundation walked out of the shadows with sincere smiles on their faces. Traveler Dream even took the initiative to help Li Yao up. “I didn’t know that you were such a determined ‘Prisoner’. You have even been the core member of the resistance in several reincarnations. You are much stronger than us. No wonder you are already stronger than the ‘Ultimate One’ when you just woke up and can fight evenly with the hunters in this life.

“Welcome to the Ark Foundation. Oh, wait. I should be welcoming you back, ‘Prison Breaker Li Yao’!”

“I…”

Li Yao blinked. The suspicion deep inside his eyes disappeared quickly and was replaced by a vague smile. “You don’t have a fifth test?”

“Now that Dream Traveler has perceived your loyalty after ten reincarnations, there is no need for more tests.

The grey mist lady apologized, “I’m sorry. We shouldn’t have doubted the stance of such a determined prison breaker. But please understand that this is to ensure our absolute safety. After all, the assassins of the Heaven’s Origin Sector must’ve tried everything they could to sneak into our organization. Anyone who is likely to get in touch with Red Pole will have to go through the same rigorous tests.

“Our previous caution was because we had no choice. Right now, we are the closest and most reliable comrades. I hope that we can fight side by side until the end and fight for true freedom and hope!”

The grey mist lady extended her hand to Li Yao.

Li Yao hesitated for a moment, but he strode forward and hugged the stranger.

“Of course, we will succeed!”

He was not a narrow-minded man. In such an apocalyptic environment, he could understand the wariness of his opponent. Now that they had made things clear and won each other’s trust, there was no need to be angry over such a trivial matter.

The grey mist lady did not expect Li Yao to be so frank. She was even happier. Sparda burst into laughter behind her and hugged Li Yao, patting Li Yao’s back hard with his remaining arm. “Welcome back, Prison Breaker Li Yao. I have a feeling that we will be the best brothers!”

“Yes.”

Li Yao looked at Sparda’s beaming face and felt that something was not right. But he could not think of anything right now and could only nod. “We are brothers!”

“My name is ‘Black Panther’. Welcome back, Prison Breaker Li Yao!”

“My name is ‘Purple Electric’. Welcome back, Prison Breaker Li Yao!”

“My name is ‘Deep Flash’. Welcome back, Prison Breaker Li Yao!”

The awakened Cultivators who had been hiding in the shadows went forward and hugged Li Yao, too. They let go of all their wariness and laughed, with the hope of victory shining deep inside their eyes.

“Right. Under the guidance of Dream Traveler, other than restoring more memories, the awakened can often restore more abilities.

The grey mist lady said to Li Yao, “How do you feel right now? Do you remember anything that can be used in a battlefield?”

“Well…”

Pondering for a moment, Li Yao walked to a container not far away and opened his right hand, while he grasped his right wrist with his left hand.

Everyone held their breath, waiting for Li Yao’s performance.

Li Yao felt that his vitality was expanding to an area of three to five square meters. He seemed to have turned into an indomitable giant. Everything within three to five square meters was under his control.

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao scratched the container with his right hand, which was burning because of the rubbing of his blood. The container immediately shrieked in earsplitting metal noises. Then, as if it had been savaged by an invisible 10,000-ton hydraulic press, it was compressed into a cluster of scrap iron no more than one meter in diameter!

The warehouse was silent. Everyone was dumbfounded.

It must be noted that the standard container was more than six meters long and more than two meters in both width and height. Although the shell of the container was not made of super alloys, it was still made of solid metal that was packed with goods.

Yet, Li Yao had compressed it into a one-meter radius with the crushing force. What kind of terrifying strength was that?

For a moment, everybody was looking at Li Yao weirdly. Thankfully, the four tests proved his stance and loyalty and convinced him that he was on their side. Otherwise, they would’ve been in real trouble!

However, on second thought, if he hadn’t been blessed with such a terrifying ability, he wouldn’t have been favored by Red Pole. Red Pole must have known how strong he was before he was fully awakened. He truly deserved to be the leader who had everything under his control!

“Excellent!”

Sparda was overjoyed. He exclaimed, “We are about to wage a final war against the Heaven’s Origin Sector and even the entire Earth, and we’ve received such strong reinforcements as Li Yao. It seems that we will be the winners of the war!”

Hearing that, the other awakened Cultivators all nodded.

They had lost too many times, and the enemy was too strong on paper. They really needed to grasp every straw to prove their stance and courage.

Huchi. Huchi. Huchi…

The ‘void crush’ had drained all of Li Yao’s strength temporarily. Or rather, he seemed to have performed a technique that was hundreds of times more unbelievable than the ‘void crush’ just now, which had drained his brain and body. He was not able to hold on any longer.

“Quickly replenish Li Yao’s energy.”

The grey mist lady said, “I’ll send the test result to the ‘Ark’ right now. The modification of the cargo ship is almost complete. We will be able to set off to the depths of the ocean in three to five hours!”

…

They sailed for three days and three nights on a modified cargo ship named ‘Lucky Star’ and gradually reached the depths of the ocean.

It was not a bad idea to avoid natural disasters on the ocean.

In the boundless ocean, many sea areas were still forbidden areas for human beings. There was no need to worry that they might run into enemies whose stance was unclear. The bottomless seawater could carry too much energy, or rather, change the original appearance of the ocean. An earthshaking storm would consume ten times more energy than on land or in shallow waters.

None of the fatal disasters in the land cities, such as the collapse of buildings caused by earthquakes, or the flooding that brought mud and rocks, could have happened in this place.

Even if a storm did come, the specially-modified cargo ships would be able to survive it with their own weight.

Perhaps because the ‘will of the earth’ had dedicated most of its time and energy to dealing with the human beings on land, the martyrs, Lucky Star had not encountered any apocalyptic storms over the past three days. Although the journey had been bumpy, most of the crew members were Awakeners and were not scared of anything.

With the help of Dream Traveler, Li Yao had snuck into the depths of his memories a few times and awakened even more powerful abilities. At the same time, he also ate food that was dozens of times more than what a normal Awakener would eat.

Everything seemed to be going smoothly, but he felt ill at ease. Something was bothering him and distracting him.

That day, when he had just sat on the iron chair and prepared to take the Dream Traveler’s test, he had heard the sound of glass bullets.

He had never heard such a weird sound before. It was beyond description, but it did not sound like an illusion, either.

No…

On second thought, it was not because he had never heard it before, but because he seemed to have heard a similar sound somewhere. Yes, he must have heard it before. Since childhood, he had heard the sound of glass more than once.

But where was it exactly?

Li Yao suddenly discovered something very strange.

He was sure, sure, sure that he had heard the sound of glass marbles falling from upstairs many times. But when he thought about it carefully, he could not remember when or where he had heard the damn sound of glass marbles!

Chapter 3237: New World

Li Yao wanted to discuss the matter with someone, but for some reason, he was unwilling to talk to the grey mist lady or the Dream Traveler. He thought that it was better to talk to Zhang Daniu.

The guy was quite obedient when he was on the starship. Although the storms and storms every now and then had forced him to vomit his bile, he didn’t complain too much. It was mainly because the grey mist lady had told him everything about the hunting team and the spies, which had scared the coward.

As long as he could save his life, he would be able to endure the pain for now. Besides, the grey mist lady had told Zhang Daniu that there were a lot of his loyal readers in the nest of the Ark Foundation. Naturally, there were a lot of female awakeners who had all kinds of superpowers. He was so happy that his snot was almost popping out. His bones and other organs were suddenly as hard as steel, and he could endure any kind of pain. He could not wait to grow wings and lunge at the ‘Ark’.

Maybe it was because Dream Traveler had guided him to awaken the memories of five reincarnations, or maybe it was because of the useless memories of the illiterate scholars, or maybe it was because so many awakened people were staying together that his brainwaves resonated with each other, but in short, he had been writing ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’ in a crazy state of mind over the past couple of days. It didn’t matter whether or not he was pouring his heart out, but a new chapter of thirty to fifty thousand words would always give Li Yao something to gain and remind him of more things in the ‘Four Universities of Cultivation’.

At this point, Li Yao was even more convinced that he was the ‘Cultivator Li Yao’ of the ‘Four Planets of Cultivation’. Since he had only transmigrated to Earth for a certain reason, the Red Pole must know what had happened.

Zhang Daniu had specifically requested that he provide protection. After all, he was the strongest and there was no problem with his loyalty. The two of them would sleep in the same cabin at night. It would be more convenient for them to discuss anything.

Therefore, when Zhang Daniu finished his five hours of continuous tapping that night, Li Yao finally couldn’t help but raise the question of ‘glass marbles falling’.

“Master Niu, according to the files collected by the Ark Foundation, they have discovered a lot of ‘earth bugs’, which should be due to the errors in logic resulting from multiple reboots and the incomplete coverage of some of the data. But I’ve read them carefully, and there doesn’t seem to be anything about ‘glass bouncing’. Don’t you find it odd?”

Li Yao said, “You know what ‘glass marbles’ sound is, don’t you? It’s something that people all over the world have heard before. It sounds like a child playing with glass marbles upstairs who accidentally fell to the ground. “Dada dada dada” sounds. Every time this kind of sound appears in the dead of night, it is simply not the time when a child is playing. There are even people who are angered by this kind of sound and run upstairs to negotiate. Only then do they know that there are no children in the house upstairs, nor are there any glass marbles… What exactly is going on?”

“Of course I do.”

Zhang Daniu rubbed his swollen fingers and said casually, “I’ve read an exposé on ‘urban legends’ about the phenomenon of ‘glass marbles falling from the floors above’. It’s true that many people blame the phenomenon on the supernatural. The nonsense about ‘kids causing trouble’ is just nonsense that does not make any sense. In fact, it is the sound of the tubes in the floors when they are expanding or shrinking. Don’t worry too much about it.”

“Is that so? But I still feel that something is not right.”

Li Yao thought carefully and said, “The architectural style and materials of every country are different. Their latitudes and climate are different, too. Of course, the distribution and construction methods of the tubes are different, too. But why is the thermal expansion and contraction of all the tubes the same? Besides, if thermal expansion and contraction are as simple as that, can’t an experiment be conducted to prove it? Or can the authorities of the construction industry be asked to explain the phenomenon? Sometimes, people have heard such sounds in houses, tents, and even outdoors. How can you explain that?”

“Well—”

Zhang Daniu scratched his hair. “I don’t know about that. Speaking of which, why do you care so much about the sound of glass? Many people have heard it before. I’ve heard it many times, too. What’s the big deal?”

“Wait. You’ve heard it, too?”

Li Yao’s eyes suddenly turned sharp. “When and where?”

“Who—who knows?”

Zhang Daniu opened his hands and said, “Who would intentionally remember such a thing? If you’ve heard it, then forget it. Anyway, I seem to have only heard it once recently.”

“Recently?”

Li Yao was somewhat excited. “Where? When exactly? Please think about it carefully!”

“This seems to be, um, the day we arrived at the port.”

Zhang Daniu thought for a long time and replied uncertainly, “Around 8 or 9 o’clock at night. I had just been ‘awakened’ by Dream Traveler and was receiving an infusion in a booth next to the warehouse to replenish my energy. When I was in a daze, I seemed to have heard glass bullets falling on the ceiling. ‘Da da da da da. Da da da da da da’. I can’t remember clearly.”

“Eight or nine o’clock at night?”

Li Yao was dazed. On second thought, it was when he was brought to the warehouse by the grey mist lady for the Dream Traveler’s test.

It was also when he heard the sound of glass.

At the same time, the two of them heard the sound of glass bullets falling. Was it a coincidence, or was there a deeper, more terrifying reason?

“What’s there to be confused about? We are about to reach the headquarters of the ‘Ark Foundation’. If you have any questions, why don’t you ask Red Pole?”

Zhang Daniu said unconcernedly, “Ms. Gray Mist and the rest of them have been bragging about the Red Pole as if it were a god-like planet that could foresee the future. He will definitely be able to answer your questions!”

“That’s… true.”

Li Yao nodded and mumbled to himself, “I have to see Red Pole. As long as I see him, everything will be… resolved!”

…

While they were sailing in the ocean, bad news came one after another.

As the natural disasters grew more and more severe, the order of human society gradually collapsed. Many small countries that were in the middle of economic crises could not resist the scourge of earthquakes, tsunamis, storms, and floods at the same time and fell into a humanitarian disaster beyond description.

The loss of a lot of refugees triggered a chain reaction in many other countries. Even the superpowers at the top of the pyramid were barely able to take care of themselves.

On the fifth day after they entered the ocean, an unprecedented earthquake hit the islands of Fusang again and cut them in half. More than 70% of the land was drowned in the cold seawater, and the rest of the land turned into thousands of isolated islands. Tens of millions of refugees were fighting for the limited space. The scene was quite appalling.

In the meantime, the superpowers of the west were faced with their own natural disaster—the violent eruption of the Red Stone Volcano, as expected, covered more than half of North America in ash. Under the shadow, hurricanes and floods were raging. Thieves and lunatics were on a rampage. The glorious nation on the top of the mountain was decaying at a visible speed, revealing its ugliest side.

Right now, not many people were talking about the apocalypse online.

Because everyone was very clear that the end of the world had come. There was no room for luck anymore.

It was in such an environment that Lucky Star reached its destination.

“We’re here!”

It was a gloomy dawn with barely any sunlight. The air was so hot that one could squeeze water out of it. But in the apocalyptic environment, the weather was not half bad. Ms. Gray Mist summoned Li Yao and Zhang Daniu to the deck and said in relief, “I cannot tell you the specific coordinates of this place, but the ‘Ark’ is right ahead.”

Li Yao and Zhang Daniu stood on their tiptoes and looked into the distance. They had thought that they would be able to see an enormous, magnificent super tanker, but as far as their eyes could reach, the surface of the ocean was peaceful and empty. There was nothing at all.

“What’s the meaning of this?”

Li Yao and Zhang Daniu looked at each other in confusion.

Besides, whether or not the grey mist lady kept the coordinates of this place a secret, wouldn’t the ocean be too… shabby?

The sea here was empty, the sky here was completely unobstructed, and there was not the slightest hint of reefs in the surroundings. The fog was rolling, and the environment was sinister. Anything could be seen clearly from the satellites, be it ships or islands.

It had been less than a week since they left the port. Modified cargo ships were designed to resist the wind and waves. Therefore, they were not driving very fast. This place was somewhere in the South Pacific at best. It was definitely not a place that was unattainable.

If the Ark Foundation had established their nest in this place, wouldn’t the organization of the Heaven’s Origin Sector and the ‘will of the earth’ behind it have discovered it in no time? How could they still be indifferent today?

Seeing that the two of them were confused, the grey mist lady smiled and gestured to the cockpit to move forward.

Breaking the waves, Lucky Star moved forward slowly.

An unbelievable scene took place.

There was nothing in front of the head of the starship, but when they touched the air, heavy ripples spread out. They seemed to have passed through an invisible barrier. Li Yao and Zhang Daniu both felt that something soft like jelly was brushing past their skin.

Looking at the front again, the two of them were greatly shocked—the ocean in front of them had changed!

Just a moment ago, the sky was still covered in dark clouds. The sunlight was too lazy to radiate any heat. The scattered raindrops fell upon them, bringing a bone-piercing chill. The seawater was as black as a swamp, with a lot of ugly seaweed floating in it.

However, after sailing forward for only three to five hundred meters, the sky suddenly turned into a cloudless blue. The scorching sun was shining unscrupulously. Even the seawater was shockingly blue. Every wave was colliding like a sapphire, making it impossible to tell the boundary between the sky and the sponge.

Chapter 3238: Ark Island

“Li Yao, look there! Look there!”

While Li Yao was immersed in the blue and resplendent heaven and earth, Zhang Daniu suddenly patted his shoulder and started shouting.

Li Yao narrowed his eyes and looked in the direction he was pointing at. He saw a pure white fish that was as translucent as jade jumping out of the water. It drew a beautiful arc on the surface of the ocean and dived into the water, waving its colorful tail.

“What is this?” Li Yao was dumbfounded.

Although he was not a marine biologist, he was very clear that it was impossible for such a beautiful fish to appear on Earth. This fish was at least five to six meters long, far longer than the average dolphin. It was simply a small-scale whale. But whether it was a dolphin or a whale, they were mainly black and white. There was absolutely no such pure white fish with seven-colored tail fins. This was simply a “spiritual beast” that only existed in the Immortal Realm!

On the surface of the sea where the fish were jumping and swimming, thousands of streaks of gold brightness were glittering. On a closer look, one could see that golden jellyfish were floating in the seawater. Their tentacles were waving and condensing into flames one moment and stars the next. They looked like a bunch of deities wandering in the universe!

“Is this still Earth?”

Li Yao was completely confused.

Not far in front of them, a lush island rose up from the bottom of the ocean like a giant boat that was ready to soar into the sky at any moment.

Near the island, several cone-shaped mountains were floating in midair almost a hundred meters high. Waterfalls were flowing down from the sky, creating glamorous rainbows under the sunlight.

“This is—this is an ‘Ark’!”

Li Yao looked at the scene that defied the common sense of physics and observed the shape of the island carefully. There were no other super tankers nearby. He suddenly realized something and exclaimed.

“That’s right.”

The grey mist lady said with a smile, “Everybody thinks that the ‘Ark’ is a gargantuan super tanker, including the Heaven’s Origin Sector and even the will of the planet. When a super tanker is sailing in the ocean, traces will always be left, including when it is being manufactured in the dock, when it is receiving supplies, and when it is registered to the country of the starship. All the intelligence will betray it.

“Therefore, over the past decades, the Heaven’s Origin Sector has spent a lot of manpower and resources searching for all the super tankers in the world, including the relatively large ten-thousand-ton cargo ships, trying to locate our headquarters, but to no avail.

“But you don’t know that the so-called ‘Ark’ is just a smoke grenade released by Red Pole to interfere with the investigation and lock-down of the Heaven’s Origin Sector and buy us more time for the ultimate battle. The real ‘Ark’ is exactly the island in front of you—”Ark Island!”

“I see!”

Li Yao was suddenly enlightened.

It was true that the super tankers could come and go freely at sea, but everything they needed for daily operations, from manufacturing to purchasing to maintenance, was too complicated and too eye-catching. With the support of the Heaven’s Origin Sector and the will of the earth, it was not difficult to locate and lock onto the thirty-thousand-ton cargo ships and the tankers produced by the major ship manufacturers on Earth.

Once it was locked onto, even the most solid and enormous ‘arky’ would be doomed.

However, an island was different.

An island of such a large scale could even produce its own water, making it completely self-sufficient. The energy, resources, and manpower that it could not provide could be provided by relatively small-scale ships. In this era, navigation technology was so advanced that even a fishing boat weighing a few hundred tons could be installed with a global satellite positioning system, allowing it to enter the depths of the ocean and work for a year and a half. No matter how formidable the Heaven’s Enlightenment Organization was, it was impossible to lock onto all the ocean-going ships in the world.

Li Yao was confused. “What is going on exactly? Are we still on Earth?”

“Of course, we are on Earth.

The grey mist lady was amused. “I merely drove into a space crevice.”

“Space folds?”

Li Yao was dazed. “What is that?”

“As an Easterner, you should be familiar with the concept, right?”

The grey mist lady said, “In the ancient Cultivation classics of the east, there is a saying that goes ‘a natural paradise’ and ‘a winding path leads to a secluded world’. It is said that the ancient people once entered a space crevice by accident and came to a mysterious space where time had been frozen for hundreds of years. After they returned, they wrote an article named ‘The Peach Blossom Spring’. Why don’t you consider the world in front of you a ‘Peach Blossom Spring’ too? Do you understand?”

Of course, Li Yao had read ‘The Peach Blossom Spring’, and he could understand what the grey mist lady meant.

Although the ‘Peach Blossom Spring’ could be interpreted as a remote, remote village that was far from the modern era, the author had gone back to look for it later. He had returned the way he came, but he could not find the location of the Peach Blossom Spring. It seemed to be a feature of a ‘paradise’ or a ‘space wrinkle’.

In the stories of supernatural powers, such mysterious spaces that were invisible to ordinary people had appeared multiple times, both in the ancient tales and in modern fantasy novels.

According to the common understanding of ‘Ark’ and ‘Apocalypse’, it was very likely that ‘Observers’ were the authors of the novels. The absurd stories they wrote more or less reflected part of reality. Therefore, it was reasonable that the ‘space folds’ existed.

“If you still don’t understand, you might as well imagine that there is an enormous ‘server’ behind the earth where we live, and the space folds are a small ‘private server’ that is secretly installed inside the server.

The grey mist lady said, “Maybe because of the logical errors and the overflow of data caused by multiple rebootes, or maybe because of the unswerving battles and breakthroughs of the previous generations of Prisonbreakers, they have finally made this Space Fold Camp what it looks like today. After Red Pole was taken over, it was further expanded and strengthened, forming the ‘Ark Island’ in front of you. This place is both our last secret base and our strongest battle fortress!”

“Server?”

Li Yao frowned. “I still don’t like the theory. It makes me feel that I’m not… real enough.”

“Like I said, there’s no need to dwell on such questions.

The grey mist lady smiled. “I believe in our world, too. The sky above our head and the earth below our feet are real. Our flesh, blood, and soul are real, too. This is definitely not a game in the virtual world.

“However, you have to admit that, if there is indeed a ‘four-dimensional life’ that is higher than us, and the ‘will of the earth’ is such a ‘four-dimensional life’, it is very likely that it will strike us across dimensions. For example, it will lock us in a prison of reincarnation and send us into one reincarnation after another, although we don’t know why it is doing that.

“How… annoying.”

Li Yao’s furrowed brows were still furrowed. “Truth or false, I don’t like the feeling of being manipulated. I don’t even like the feeling of being manipulated. I only like the feeling that somebody is looking down upon all living creatures from above.

“Then, let’s find a way to overthrow it.”

“This is the mission of the Hell Breakers,” the grey mist lady said casually. “Remove everything that is above us and let us—the six billion heroic spirits from Earth—stand at the peak of the three-dimensional, the four-dimensional, and even all the universes. There will be no existence that can interfere with our free will at all, much less restrict or manipulate it!

The grey mist lady’s words raised a sense of pride in Li Yao’s heart. He stopped dwelling on whether it was true or false. Pondering for a moment, he asked, “Is this place safe enough? A lot of traces of our rescue operation have been exposed. Will the Heaven’s Origin Sector follow us and attack us?”

“Rest assured.”

The grey mist lady smiled and said, “The most amazing thing about the folds of space is that it is not fixed at a certain coordinate in the depths of the ocean. Although you can see that this place is peaceful and that the ocean is boundless, in the ocean outside, the ‘wrinkled channel’ is moving irregularly in the vast and boundless Pacific Ocean. It took us several reincarnations to grasp the pattern of its movement. How can the Heaven’s Origin Sector see through the folded channel in just a few days?
