And ten days to half a month is enough for the Red Pole to complete the final preparations. This is also the reason why we have to thoroughly examine and test everyone who has entered the spatial folds. I’m sorry about those few days.”

The grey mist lady was someone who was cold on the outside but warm on the inside. She was still apologizing for the grievances that Li Yao had suffered over the past few days.

“It doesn’t matter. I didn’t know that the headquarters of the Ark Foundation was such a mysterious place, either. Now, I finally understand what Red Pole meant.”

Li Yao smiled. “Indeed. If I were sitting in such a secret base, I would run the most rigorous tests on the people entering and exiting the place. Chances are that I would be a hundred times more careful than him!”

His words made everyone on the deck laugh.

“Anyways, we are finally home!”

Sparda, the muscular man, took a deep breath and grinned. “Everything is settled now that I’m home!”

Although Li Yao had never been to the ‘home’ of the prison breakers, he felt more at ease after hearing the confidence in the grey mist lady’s words.

Right then, Lucky Star was getting closer and closer to the Ark Island. Dozens of large-scale facilities that looked like drilling rigs could be seen on the shallow sea near the island. Above them were towers made of steel that looked like antennas soaring into the sky.

Chapter 3239: 50% Of Li Yao

Of course, these facilities were not oil rigs. The Ark Foundation probably did not need them, nor could they exploit oil to earn a ‘resistance fund’. However, for a moment, Li Yao was not sure what use they had. He could only see that dozens of similar facilities surrounded the entire Ark Island, and most of the facilities did not seem to be completed yet. There were scaffolding all around, on which many workers were working in full swing. The sparks of welding were everywhere, and the sounds of steel were echoing nonstop.

Li Yao narrowed his eyes and observed carefully. He discovered that the vast majority of the workers had peculiar appearances. If they didn’t have wings growing out of their ribs, then they had a stocky build. Some of their limbs were deformed and swollen. A fist the size of a clay pot could be used as a hammer. They could even float in the air without relying on any external force. Presumably, they were all awakened.

The grey mist lady confirmed Li Yao’s speculation. She told Li Yao that more than ten thousand awakened Cultivators had gathered on the entire Ark Island, which was apparently a world of superpowers. Those people boasted dazzling techniques and abilities, and the laws of gravity inside the ‘space folds’ were slightly different from those in the outside world. Together with the Red Pole’s control over the sea near the island, the workers were able to get rid of the restriction of gravity temporarily and improve their efficiency.

However, the grey mist lady was not clear what they were actually making, either. It was a secret that only Red Pole knew. It was said to be the deciding weapon in the ultimate war.

Lucky Star gradually drew close to the dock. After another thorough search and test, it was not until more than half a day later that Li Yao and Zhang Daniu were allowed to go to the command room of Red Pole.

It was a cluster of buildings that looked like fortresses half below the ground. The dome of the buildings was like skulls scattered on the ground that were emitting grey colors. The grey mist lady told Li Yao that the dome of the buildings was covered in a thick lead shell, mainly to prevent the brainwaves of the Red Pole from leaking out and being perceived by the apocalypse and the will of the earth.

Such cautiousness was the reason why Red Pole was not locked onto or destroyed after dozens of cycles.

The underground channel leading to the command room was blocked by an iron wall that was as thick as the door of a vault. Standing in front of the door, Li Yao sensed that almost ten rays of different attributes passed through his body. Then, dull mechanical noises echoed deep inside the door, and a gap was opened slowly.

The grey mist lady gestured for Li Yao and Zhang Daniu to go in alone. This was as far as she could go. After the two of them squeezed in with some difficulty, the door was immediately closed, leaving them in darkness.

For a moment, Li Yao felt his heart tighten. He wondered whether or not everything was a lie. Was the so-called ‘Ark’ and ‘Apocalypse’ the same thing? Were they all traps set up by the will of the earth in order to catch all of them?

However, soon, a series of arrows lit up on the ground in front of them, indicating that they should take a few turns and go deeper and deeper. Finally, they reached a relatively spacious underground space.

The space was enshrouded in a hazy mist of light. The intense brightness stung Li Yao’s pupils, making it difficult for him to open his eyes, even though he had been groping in the darkness. He was dazed for a moment before he realized that it was a screen that had been installed on the walls and the ceiling.

The large and small screens displayed the disasters everywhere in the world, as well as the complicated figures and reports. An astronomical amount of information was rolling together with the mist of light. Many pieces of information were floating in midair, giving the feeling that the place was decades ahead of its time.

Other than the screen, the spacious room was empty except for a ergonomic computer chair that had its back to the two of them. They could not see the person sitting on the chair clearly. They could only see that he was wearing a very funny helmet that seemed to be made of lead, too.

“Slither. Slither.”

Right then, weird noises came from the front of the chair, followed by even weirder noises.

“The ‘Red Pole’, the leader of the Ark Foundation and the most dangerous prison breaker in the past ten reincarnations?”

Li Yao and Zhang Daniu looked at each other in confusion.

Hearing the footsteps of the two, the clicking sounds came to an abrupt end. The computer chair slowly turned around.

Li Yao and Zhang Daniu held their breath and gazed at the leader of the Hell Breakers.

However, the appearance and style of the Red Pole Star surprised them.

The man looked like an Easterner. He had a baby face whose age was hard to guess. His facial features were decent, but there was an air of cunningness lingering around his face. The most eye-catching part about him was the star-shaped birthmark on his left eye. Li Yao had never seen a birthmark with such a clear edge, such a regular shape, and such a bright red color. It was like a red star that had been branded on his face.

Presumably, the nickname ‘Red Pole’ came from this birthmark.

His face gave Li Yao a strangely familiar feeling. He had the same feeling when he met ‘Master Zhang Jiashu’ a few days ago, but the feeling right now was hundreds of times more intense.

However, what surprised Li Yao the most was not his appearance, but his appearance—a T-shirt with coconut trees, beach, blue sky, and white clouds printed on the upper half, a pair of beach shorts made of cotton linen that was both loose and comfortable, a pair of cheap flip-flops clipped to his feet, a cup of Tropical Comprehensive Fruit Juice in his left hand, and a bag of cucumber-flavored potato chips in his right hand.

It was not until this point that Li Yao realized that the slurping sound just now was the sound of him drinking juice through a straw, and the cracking sound was the sound of him eating potato chips!

“Are you serious?”

Li Yao asked himself in his heart, Is this the attitude of the leader of the resistance who has persisted for countless cycles? Is there a mistake somewhere?

However, deep down his heart, he had an urge to drink juice and eat potato chips!

At the same time, Zhang Daniu exclaimed in surprise.

Li Yao came back to himself and immediately realized what Zhang Daniu meant. No wonder the man looked so familiar. Wasn’t he the ‘Pirate Potentate’ Boss Bai in the novel ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’?

No, that’s not right. This flowery T-shirt, beach shorts, flip-flops, and a combination of fruit juice was indeed Boss Bai’s unique classic style in the beginning. But when the “Cultivator Li Yao” in the novel saw it, he was envious and had the idea that a man should be like this. Later, he shamelessly copied this style!

And right now, this figure had appeared on Red Pole’s body, and it was dressed so… just right. It completely matched his temperament. He simply looked like a character from a novel.

So, who was he exactly, and who was he exactly?

Li Yao was completely lost.

Just like that, Li Yao, Red Pole Star, and Zhang Daniu stared at each other for a long time. The more they looked at each other, the more familiar they felt. The more they looked, the more uncomfortable they felt. The more they looked, the weirder they felt.

In the end, the Red Pole Star broke the silence.

“Do you want some juice?”

The leader of the Ark Foundation asked with a smile.

His voice was somewhat hoarse. It sounded completely different from Li Yao’s and Zhang Daniu’s. But for some reason, Li Yao felt that it was strangely familiar—it was as if he was hearing his own voice from a tape recorder.

At this point, no matter how strange things were, there was no need to be shocked. Li Yao and Zhang Daniu looked at each other and nodded.

A small refrigerator was hidden behind a few screens in the corner of the room. Red Pole Star took out three cans of fruit juice and threw two of them to Li Yao. She then opened one of the cans and took a sip, before she exclaimed in satisfaction, “I’ve been cooped up on this island all day and can’t go anywhere. This is all that’s left. Try it. It’s not bad.”

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao opened the bottle and drank half of it.

How should he put it? The sour, sweet, and cold feeling had connected his entire neural network. He seemed to have… never tasted such a feeling for hundreds of years!

“Right? Not bad, right?”

The Red Pole Star looked at them with a smile and opened her hands. “What do you think? Do you have any questions? Let’s hurry up. I promise that I will tell you everything I know.”

“You…”

Li Yao glanced at Zhang Daniu and asked the Red Pole Star, “Who is it exactly? Why do I feel that you are so familiar even though we’ve never met before? You are so familiar that I feel that we are twins or that I am looking at myself in a mirror. Our appearances and voices are so different. This is too weird!”

“Good question.”

Red Extremity tilted his head and thought for a moment. The expression on his face was exactly the same as Li Yao’s. “What if I say that my name is also ‘Li Yao’? What do you think?”

What percentage of ‘Cultivator Li Yao’ do you think Master Zhang Jiashu is?”

Chapter 3240: From Soul Division To Fusion!

Li Yao somewhat understood what Red Pole meant, but he was even more confused.

We are retrieving seeds and viruses that do not belong to him from the depths of his soul and integrating them into our own brains.

As long as we can reassemble the fragments, we will be able to let ‘100%’ of Li Yao’s body be reborn, and he will be even stronger than before. He will be so strong that… he will be in the Body Integration Stage!”

Chapter 3241: One And Only Candidate

“That explains a lot. From ‘distraction’ to ‘fusion’!

Li Yao had read before, so naturally, he knew the classification of the “Four Universities of Cultivation”—beginning from the Divinity Transformation Stage, it was the highest level to break through the limits of human beings, and the Dividing Soul Stage and the Form Synthesis Stage were even more mysterious and incomprehensible. There was no pattern to their training, either. They were like deities and devils.

Li Yao could somewhat understand the formidability of the Dividing Soul Stage. An expert could transform into thousands of clones. Billions of telepathic thoughts equaled to billions of soldiers. One person equaled to the combat ability of an army. Wasn’t it strong enough?

However, Li Yao had never been able to understand how strong a Cultivator in the Form Synthesis Stage was. Now that his soul could be divided into billions of pieces, wouldn’t it be redundant to reunite them?

Now that he heard Red Pole’s explanation, he realized that it did make sense. Countless clones would be cast into the endless reincarnation of the mortal world and experience different waves and emotions in different rivers of life. In the end, they would be reunited and give birth to a new Li Yao in the intense resonation. The strength of billions of people would be concentrated in his body. How strong would he be?

“Just like that, countless soul fragments were dedicated to the brand-new journey of life and the road of resistance. Some of the soul fragments encountered each other by accident and attracted each other. They melded together and formed a relatively large fragment. Gradually, I was able to recall some of my past. I even realized that my ‘previous life’ was a Cultivator in the depths of the endless sea of stars. That’s me.”

“But that was not enough,” the Red Pole Star said. “I was in a muddle-headed, confused state in the previous few reincarnations. Although I knew that I was carrying out an important mission and that I was going to carry out a plan named ‘Vulture’ to break out of the damn cage, I still didn’t know who I was in my previous life, what I had experienced, and how I teleported to Earth from the other side of the sea of stars. The key information was still hidden in the endless darkness. Therefore, I was far less powerful than I am right now.

“I was one of the luckier ones, because I had swallowed enough soul fragments in the previous reincarnations and recovered a lot of information about Li Yao’s personality, memories, and thinking patterns. I was able to pursue my past.

“But most of the broken pieces of the soul are scattered in every corner of the Prison of Reincarnation. Some of them are even hibernating in the depths of somebody’s soul forever. Most of the people who received Li Yao’s soul pieces had their wings broken, their claws removed, and their fighting will diminished like Master Zhang Jiashu. For them, it was not a gift of hope but a great disaster.

“Even if I had all the resources of the Ark Foundation, it would still be impossible for me to locate all the people who had ‘Li Yao’s soul fragments’. Besides, it would cause too much of a stir. The Heaven’s Origin Sector would certainly notice it and intercept it in advance. They might even locate the ‘Ark Island’ through the clue.

Li Yao nodded his head, deep in thought. He glanced at Zhang Daniu from the corner of his eyes and suddenly thought of something. He said excitedly, “Therefore, you will project the information stream of so that all the heroic spirits contaminated by Li Yao’s soul fragments will awaken and come to your side!”

“The way you and I think is exactly the same. I thought so, too. It’s a pity that it was not me but somebody else.

Red Pole Star paused for a moment and pointed at the sky. “Someone from outer space, or rather, a certain force in the universe.”

“What?”

Li Yao and Zhang Daniu were both dazed.

“As I said, before the reincarnation, or more precisely, before I saw the novel ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivator’, although I became the leader of all the Hell Breakers, I didn’t realize that I was ‘Cultivator Li Yao’. Therefore, how could I have guided Master Niu to write ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivator’?”

“But it is not right to say that Master Niu made up an absurd, nonexistent story named ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’ based on his own inspiration.

“Master Niu, you should know who the so-called ‘observers’ are, and you should know that your situation is different from most of the other observers, right? The reason is simple. There are two kinds of observers in total, namely ‘Type 1’ and ‘Type 2’.

‘Type-1 Observer and Type-2 Observer?

Li Yao and Zhang Daniu looked at each other in bewilderment. “What does that mean?”

“Type-1 Observers are the vast majority of Observers that we’ve seen in the past. They boast special abilities that allow them to see the mottled shadows of themselves dozens of times, or even hundreds of thousands of times, before they are reincarnated. Based on the broken, colorful memories, they create fantasy worlds in various forms. They are called ‘fantasies’, but they are actually history and reality that happened a long time ago.

The Red Pole Star explained, “Type-1 Observers are often the witnesses of their creation of a fantasy world. It is something that cannot be erased even after hundreds of thousands of reincarnations. That’s why they can devote a lot of their feelings into their work and make it feel so vivid. Even the crudest words and strokes are deeply moved.

“Master Niu, on the other hand, is the second type, or rather, the unique ‘Type-2 Observer’. You are not recalling your memories actively but reflecting them passively. You are like a mirror facing the sun. The imaginary world you create is almost always a reflection of the information stream from outer space. Do you understand?”

Zhang Daniu was dumbfounded. He shook his head hard. “I don’t understand.”

“Let’s put it this way. Cultivator Li Yao has made a bunch of very good friends in the four universes of cultivation. It can be seen from the previous chapters that, regardless of the stance and beliefs of these friends, after going through so much wind and rain, they have already been deeply impressed by Cultivator Li Yao’s glorious image, his sacred personality, his noble sentiments, his unyielding will, and his blood-stained demeanor. If they knew that Cultivator Li Yao was in prison, do you think they would just stand by and watch?”

The Red Pole Star asked solemnly.

“Uh…”

Li Yao and Zhang Daniu scratched their chins together, somewhat uncertain. “Probably, maybe, no matter what, they wouldn’t just stand by and watch, right?”

“That’s right. If my friends and family knew that Cultivator Li Yao infiltrated Earth in the state of ‘a blank sheet of paper’, they would definitely try to rescue him or even fight side by side with him to destroy the planet.

“And if you want to rescue Li Yao and destroy Earth, the simplest and most effective way is to let Li Yao recover all his memories first, condense a brand-new soul, and rise from the ashes!”

“After all, Li Yao is a ‘humanoid self-made natural disaster generator’ that brings disaster wherever he goes. As long as the real Li Yao is woken up, the earth might be destroyed automatically without doing anything.

“Therefore, I estimate that, on the level of the universe, ‘Li Yao’s Family’, led by Ding Lingdang, must’ve developed a new information warfare weapon that can bypass the defense of the will of the earth and project the information to the planet without anybody knowing.

“However, since they did not know exactly where Li Yao was and what state he was in, it was impossible for them to project the information to the thousands of pieces precisely even if there were thousands of pieces. Therefore, they needed a medium or a carrier that could allow most of the soul pieces to perceive the information.

“That was the purpose of the novel ‘40,000 Years of Cultivation’. They chose Master Niu, the ‘type-2 Observer’, as the medium to illuminate the entire Earth with the light from the depths of the universe!

Red Pole’s words cast Li Yao and Zhang Daniu into a long silence.

“Why—why me?”

Zhang Daniu was stunned for half a minute before he finally came back to himself and stammered.

“Good question. I’ve pondered the answer for a long time, too.

Red Extremity said, “Why you? In terms of fame, you are unknown or even notorious. In terms of character, you will never be able to withstand the interrogation of the Heaven’s Origin Sector for half an hour. It’s very likely that you will kneel and surrender the moment you are captured. As for the thesis pen, there’s no need to mention it. Don’t hit someone in the face, right? There are clearly many more suitable candidates than you. Those who are more famous, more determined, and have more integrity and integrity. Of course, they can also choose better authors. Why you?”

“…No. If you have something to say, just say it. There’s no point in beating around the bush. Who did I offend?”

“No. I was complimenting you. On second thought, there is no better candidate than you!”

The Red Pole Star slapped her thigh and said, “In this soul-stirring information war, the most crucial thing is not to wake up Li Yao, but to avoid the detection and scanning of the will of the earth! Think about it. There will be another day to wake up Li Yao if we don’t succeed today. If we don’t succeed in this reincarnation, there might be a next time. As long as we are not discovered, we will be able to gradually influence him and slow him down.

“But if the will of the earth finds out and the enemy is prepared, it will be very difficult to do the same thing again.

“If Ding Lingdang and the rest of them choose a writer who has been famous for a long time, who has a gorgeous vocabulary, a beautiful writing style, and a skillful technique, and has a large number of loyal readers who painstakingly wait all day and shoot the relevant information about the four cultivation universes into the other party’s brain, it’s true that the other party is likely to create a more perfect work. It’s even possible that the work will become popular all over the world and be known by billions of readers. But this way, everything related to the will enter the vision of the Heaven’s Origin Sector and even the will of the Earth. With the other party’s powerful computational ability, it won’t be difficult for them to deduce the whole story, the location of Li Yao’s soul fragment, and even the precise coordinates of the four cultivation universes!”

Chapter 3242: Super Strategic Deception

“No—”

Zhang Daniu stared at him and asked, “What do you mean? What do you mean?”

“Nothing. I just want to show you how unique and important you are, and how important you are to the entire Earth and even the entire universe!”

Red Pole Star counted his fingers and said, “I didn’t know why the people on the battlefield of the universe chose you as the intermediary, but after I thought about it carefully, I realized that the choice was truly marvelous!

“First of all, as I said just now, the writing style, overall structure control, and characterization ability of this ‘intermediary medium’ must not be too strong, because the stories that took place in the four cultivation universes are too rich, too wonderful, too soul-stirring, and too soul-stirring. If a random writer of a higher level were to write it, it would be very easy for him to write a piece of popular, crazy young people’s work. Then, it would spread to ten people, ten people would spread to a hundred people, and it wouldn’t be long before it would be on the hot list of major search engines. The organization of the apocalypse and the will of the earth would immediately monitor everything and capture or erase the author in advance—what was the point of this? On the contrary, it would be sending important information to the enemy!

“That’s why the people on the battlefield of the universe chose you after thorough consideration. You are a writer who is capable enough to write such a splendid theme that is not as marvelous, not as well known, and very, uh, low-key!

“I—I still don’t understand.”

Zhang Daniu looked at Red Pole Star and then at Li Yao. “Then, do I have the capability or not?”

“If you have the ability, then you naturally have the ability. After all, not every writer can write such a theme like this!”

Red Extremity said, “Especially your writing style. It’s sloppy, long-winded, and full of nonsense. It’s just a tiny detail, yet you have to explain it time and time again. It’s clearly a theory that has been expounded several times, yet you have to tirelessly explain it over and over again. You clearly know that your writing style is bad, but you like to write a few idioms in one breath and pretend that your vocabulary is very gorgeous… Don’t look at me like that. I’m sincerely praising you. This is a reflection of your strength. This shows that you have the ability to create a large amount of redundant information. This is a very amazing thing. It is precisely by relying on a huge amount of redundant data that you can cover up the really crucial information. It caused the Heaven’s Origin Sector and the will of the earth to return in defeat in front of your novel. They were not able to discover the shining gold hidden in the dog’s… soil immediately!”

“I think this guy is quite annoying.”

Zhang Daniu was silent for a while before he turned to Li Yao and said, “Even more annoying than you.”

“It seems…”

Li Yao scratched his nose. “A little bit.”

“Don’t be like that. I’m speaking from the bottom of my heart. Maybe I should express my sincere gratitude to you on behalf of all the unwilling martyred spirits on the planet, or even all the people in the universe who are fighting hard. Although your work is nothing in the eyes of literature, from the perspective of strategical deception, it is invaluable!

“Maybe a long, long time ago, when the will of the earth was at its peak, it had unimaginable computational ability and data retrieval ability. It could monitor all the information released by the six billion heroic spirits at the same time. But as time went by, especially after the damage caused by the Battle of the Vulture, its computational ability, data retrieval ability, and reality distortion ability all declined significantly. It was impossible for it to monitor and change all the information on Earth at its will, especially now that it was faced with the fierce attacks in the battlefield of the universe. The forces that it could deploy to the interior of the planet were barely enough for it. As a result, it could not monitor every fantasy work and artwork on the planet in real time, nor could it scan every heroic spirit’s mental state and emotions every second. It could only monitor the people suspected to be ‘observers’ and ‘prisoners’ selectively!

“Do you understand, Master Niu? You have built a solid ‘Wall of Strategic Deception’ with the first 400-500 chapters of ‘40,000 Years of Cultivation’!

“A long time ago, when you first started writing this novel, the Heaven’s Origin Sector and the will of the earth must’ve scanned you. But you successfully fooled them. The redundant information you sent out must’ve made their heads dizzy, making them regard your works as countless boring, monotonous, and monotonous works of the same genre. After reading one chapter, they would know the next hundred chapters of the same genre. There must’ve been some sort of screening system. The logical judgement procedure went through precise calculations and came to the conclusion that it was not worth putting more computational power and monitoring resources on you and your works. They should pay more attention to the works that are truly imaginative and original like ‘Blood and Sand’, ‘Strong Colonization Hunters’, and ‘World of Stars’. This is the opportunity, this is the loophole, and this is the key to this shocking information war!”

“After 400-500 chapters of strategical deception, the people on the battlefield of the universe are finally able to project the truly important information into your brain remotely and release it with your own hands. Even at this moment, your creative style and your own strength are still playing an important role. You have used your barren writing style to cover up your brilliant imagination. You have used your routine to level up, fight monsters, and show off to drown out countless twisted and bizarre schemes. You have used your pale and powerless characters to cover up the brilliant brilliance of the heroes of the sea of stars to the greatest extent. You have turned them from fireflies in the dark night into floating dust under the sun. You have put on a camouflage uniform of a thousand people so that they can ‘sneak into the night’ with the wind and quietly sneak into the brain or even the depths of the target reader’s soul. With your own strength, you have delayed the Heaven’s Wrath and the will of the Earth from discovering the whole thing!

“Let’s take a look at the time when you wrote the novel. It’s been almost two years. You’ve written 2,000 chapters in two years. The overview and the main storyline of the four worlds have been written. The heroes of the sea of stars and their beliefs have surfaced, too. The struggle between the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan, and even the civilizations that are above the Pangu Civilization… Everything has been vaguely reflected. It is safe to say that 80% of the critical information has been exported and disseminated! It was not until this moment that the Heaven’s Origin Sector and the will of the earth realized that something was wrong and attacked you. It was a very serious oversight and even a fatal mistake!

“It’s too late. They discovered it too late. The information has been sent out. The virus has spread out. The cancer cells have expanded to the maximum. All the preparations for the final battle have been completed. Even the will of the earth cannot change the final outcome. In the endless cycles of time, you are the only one who can fool it successfully. Isn’t that something to be proud of?”

“I—”

Zhang Daniu did not know what kind of expression he should put on. His facial muscles wriggled for a while before he gnashed his teeth. “I’m so f*cking proud!”

“As for our target readers, or the people that Ding Lingdang and the rest of them want to wake up in the battlefield of the universe, they are naturally the heroic spirits with ‘Li Yao’s soul fragments’ embedded deep inside their brains, or the people who don’t have soul fragments but still have unquenchable hot blood and dreams deep inside their hearts.

Red Pole Star said, “These people don’t care that your language is boring, that your characters are dry, and that your plots are old-fashioned. They also like to use many idioms and say them eight times in a row on purpose, because they must be able to feel the ‘hot blood’, ‘soul’, and ‘free will’ hidden in the depths of the information streams of Ding Lingdang and the others. They will be deeply moved by these things, resonate with them, and enter a state similar to self-hypnosis. No matter how you write, they will persist in reading and will pursue Li Yao’s story. The story of the endless universe is also… their own story!”

“Yes, that’s me.”

Li Yao was deep in thought. “Although I also think that Teacher Niu’s work, uh, I’m not saying that it’s bad. I’m saying that it’s not good enough to win the literature award, but it is indeed filled with incredible magic that can make people reject it verbally, but their eyes and hands just can’t stop. For some reason, they just keep reading chapter by chapter. It’s like stinky tofu. It smells bad, but it’s delicious!”

“Because you have ‘Soul of Li Yao’ inside your body. You have boiling hot blood, an undying dream, a yearning for freedom, and a will to never yield to any power. You are the heroic spirit that has been searching for. Naturally, you can resonate with this work.”

“It’s like we are watching a movie shot by someone else and telling their story. Naturally, you will be picky about the plot, the camera, the appearance of the person, and so on. But if you are watching the family video shot by your family and recording your own story, the moment of happiness between you and your family, will you still care about the camera, the light, or the plot? No. It is the moment of your life where your laughter and unforgettable experiences are recorded. They are completely yours. You won’t care about the details at all, will you?”

Li Yao suddenly understood and nodded his head.

“Also, if it is a real creator of the arts, the ‘Type-1 Observers’ often have a very stubborn creative mind. They have their own creative methods and things that they want to talk about. Their will is very firm, and their brain is heavily guarded. They will definitely want to tell their own story. It is impossible for them to accept the projection of foreign information.

The Red Pole Star continued, “But Master Niu doesn’t have such a problem at all. In a sense, his brain is very… clean, his soul is very friendly, and his thinking is relatively simple. With the interference of foreign information, he immediately accepted it happily and never doubted why he wrote such a work.

“Besides, you live alone, don’t you, Master Niu? You don’t have many friends or a girlfriend. You don’t have any friends on the Nexus, either. No one will care what kind of weird phenomena you experience when you are writing. Such comprehensive conditions are too suitable for ‘information transmission and strategic deception’ missions!

Chapter 3243: The Way Of Fusion

“No. Well, why does it sound more and more mysterious to me?”

Zhang Daniu was dumbfounded for a long time before he said, “After hundreds of reincarnations, this is all I’ve got?”

Red Extremity said solemnly, “And all over the world, at least in East Asia, where online novels are popular, there are countless people who have embedded ‘Li Yao’s soul fragments’—people who have ‘hot blood’ and ‘souls’—who have been moved by you, resonated with you, and awakened themselves! I just need to track their reading records, sneak into the relevant reader groups and Tieba forums, and use the big data to search for the entries about ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivator’ on social media. It won’t be difficult to lock onto a large number of loyal readers and get in touch with them.

After reading the latest chapter, they can’t wait to update it every day, because everyone has a resonance! And those readers who are less affected by the ‘soul fragments of Li Yao’ are not as persistent and fanatical, and those readers who give up after reading a few hundred chapters are unfortunately not worth my time and effort to find.

“With such a screening mechanism, it was very easy for me to find most of the ‘Li Yao’s soul fragments’ and invite the owners of the soul fragments to the ‘Ark Island’ through various methods. I was able to piece together the scattered jigsaw pieces and restore 30% of ‘Li Yao’s soul’, 50%, 70% or even higher.

“The work was carried out almost a year ago. After all, I am also the possessor of ‘Li Yao’s soul fragment’. I am more keenly aware of your value than the Heaven’s Origin Sector and the will of the earth. I have been protecting you in secret since then. However, since the Heaven’s Origin Sector and the will of the earth haven’t discovered you yet, I don’t need to alarm them and make things worse for myself, do I?

It seems that, however slow-witted the will of the earth is, it has discovered the tricks that Ding Lingdang and the rest of them played on the battlefield of the universe. Therefore, my men showed up from the darkness and brought you back to the Ark Island before the enemy did, completing the final ‘combination’.

From the two battles between you and the hunter, as well as the results of Dream Traveler’s guidance, your soul strength is extremely high. It is very likely that you have more than 10% of ‘Li Yao’s soul fragments’ stored inside your body. You are closer to the true ‘Cultivator Li Yao’ than any of us. If you can fill the most crucial gap, the success rate of our perfect fusion will be even higher!”

“Wait—”

Zhang Daniu suddenly raised his hand and asked suspiciously, “So, you brought us here to meld with us?”

“Of course, only by breaking through the ‘division’ and becoming a ‘combined body’ will we have the opportunity to compete with the will of the earth. We might even be able to pry into the legendary ‘Void Hole’ and understand the mysteries of reality and illusion. Then, we will be able to become a ‘real person’ on the four-dimensional level and crush the will of the earth!

Red Pole replied matter-of-factly, “Is there a problem?”

“In principle, since I’m here for the six billion heroes and even the unyielding Cultivators of the entire universe, I’m not against the integration.

Zhang Daniu said, “However, I have a small question.”

“What is it?”

Red Pole Star said, “Ask away. Ask away.”

“You said just now that, when the real Li Yao returned to Earth from the depths of the sea of stars, his soul was divided into thousands of pieces regardless of whether he was active or passive. He fell to the ground indiscriminately like a meteor shower, didn’t you?

Zhang Daniu said, “According to the theory, the earthlings who absorbed his soul fragments were not necessarily muscular men but young and beautiful girls, were they?”

“Of course, there is.”

The Red Pole Star nodded. “So?”

Zhang Daniu explained, “Don’t get me wrong. I’m not a vulgar, lecherous, and shameless person. Even if a girl is not very beautiful, as long as her heart is beautiful, I can still accept it. I’m not discriminating against you. The main reason is that I feel that it’s a bit… weird… for several hundred tall and sturdy men with ‘Li Yao’s soul fragments’ to be playing together on a nameless island in the depths of the ocean.”

Red Pole made a gesture. Countless complicated diagrams and formulas of facilities appeared on the hundreds of screens around him. “This is a super-large brainwave amplifier. “That’s right.”

Li Yao nodded. “Indeed!”

“That’s right. It’s the same for me and other loyal readers. It seems that the soul fragments buried deep inside our brains need more information stimulation to be fully awakened.

Red Extremity said, “However, Teacher Niu’s input speed is limited after all. No matter how fast your hand speed is, typing 20,000-30,000 words a day is already your limit. Moreover, you can’t be so crazy every day. Otherwise, your ten fingers will be wasted very soon.
