We can’t guarantee that all the unyielding heroic spirits will be able to see it in the first instance. What’s more, there is still a large number of ‘Li Yao’s soul fragments’ that could infect those who don’t understand Chinese, or those like Teacher Zhang Jiashu who are not interested in online novels… For these people, the current method of information dissemination is completely useless.

“Therefore, in order to allow Master Niu to reflect all the information projected from the battlefield in the universe in the shortest amount of time possible, and to allow all the heroic spirits—whether they like webnovels or don’t know Chinese, they can understand, be moved, and resonate with each other—to build this ‘Thunderbolt Invincible Super Brainwave Magnification System’, which is what you can see right now. With this system and the support of thousands of awakened people, Master Niu’s brainwaves can be amplified to the limit, allowing him to complete the entire in the blink of an eye. And with this novel, he can ignite the hot blood, fighting spirit, unyielding will, and belief of all heroic spirits to fight for freedom!”

“I believe that, when the blood of all the heroes is boiling, when their desire for freedom turns into an iron fist that breaks through the sky, and when their souls are calling the same name over and over again in the intense resonation, the real Li Yao will be reborn in the waves of endless collisions of souls!

Li Yao and Zhang Daniu were both stunned.

“How did you know?”

Li Yao thought for a moment and asked, “You’ve never experienced the process from ‘distraction’ to ‘fusion’. How can you be sure?”

“Firstly, over the past ten or so reincarnations, I’ve narrowly escaped the crushing of the will of the earth several times. I’ve even scouted its source in a certain mysterious way and gained an indescribable understanding about the legendary levels such as ‘distraction’, ‘combination’, and ‘tunxu’.

“Secondly, when I read the latest chapter of ‘40,000 Years of Cultivation’, my soul power became so strong that I could vaguely hear the calls from the battlefields of the great universe. The rebels on the battlefields of the great universe sent me critical information through their dreams, including the methods to build the ‘Invincible Thunderbolt Super Brainwave Amplification System’.

“Thirdly, the will of Earth is too strong. Neither the battlefield of the universe nor the prison of reincarnation can break it alone. Although the success rate of the method is still not very high, we have no other options except to try our best, do we not?

“Wait. Let’s put the success rate aside for now. I want to know what will happen to us if we successfully condense the ‘real Li Yao’?”

Zhang Daniu asked, “Will he die?”

“How is that possible?”

The Red Pole Star couldn’t help but laugh. “Happiness, hot blood, spirit, will… These things are different from money. If you give a dollar to someone else, you will lose a dollar. But if you give your indomitable spirit and hope filled with dreams to someone else, your spirit and dream will not diminish in the slightest. In fact, you might even become stronger because you have found a like-minded comrade, right?

“The so-called ‘combining’ does not mean to forcibly dig out a piece of your soul, or even to cause your soul to scatter and die on the spot. If that’s the case, then with the belief of ‘Cultivator Li Yao’, I’m sure he wouldn’t want to be reborn in such a way, would he? The real ‘combining’ is the unity of the people. It is the unity of the people. All the heroic spirits are burning their hot blood, stimulating their dreams, releasing their greatest strength, facing an incomparably terrifying enemy, and creating a capital person on a three-dimensional level!

“Therefore, even if ‘Cultivator Li Yao’ is reborn, I believe that it will not have a negative effect on most of the heroic spirits. Chances are that it will even help you get rid of your habit of crawling into ventilation tubes!”

Chapter 3244: Human Farm

“I see.”

Zhang Daniu rubbed his chin. “It does not sound unacceptable.”

“Then, when will the ‘Invincible Thunderbolt Super Brainwave Magnification System’ be ready?”

Deep in thought, Li Yao asked, “Suppose everything goes well and ‘100% of Li Yao’ is awakened, then what?”

“Three days.”

Red Pole Star extended three fingers and looked at Li Yao deeply. He smiled and said, “In three days, the ‘Invincible Thunderbolt Super Brainwave Magnification System’ will be ready. By then, all the Hell Breakers on the island will be activated at the same time and help ‘100% Li Yao’ to be reborn. I believe that it will be a heavy blow for the will of the planet. At that time, the people on the battlefield of the universe—including Ding Lingdang and the rest of them—will launch the fiercest attacks. We will be sure to win if we work together!

“Is that so?”

Li Yao narrowed his eyes and put on a smile that even he did not notice. He nodded and said, “The final battle is in three days!”

“That’s right.”

Red Pole Star moved her eyes away from his face and looked at Zhang Daniu. “Any questions?”

“In fact, there is a minor problem that may be irrelevant. You may not know the answer.

Zhang Daniu asked, “We’ve been talking about the ‘will of the earth’, but I can’t understand it at all. If such a ‘four-dimensional being’ does exist, and it has captured billions of heroic spirits in the three-dimensional universe and locked us in a reincarnation prison named ‘Earth’, what is the purpose of all this?”

“It is quite clear that the will of the earth has the ability to kill all the martyred spirits. It can even turn all the earthlings into dreamless, brainless idiots, mindless walking dead, or think of thousands of ways to torture us so that we will live in literal hell and suffer all kinds of suffering and torment.

“But it didn’t do that. Instead, it set a period from the 1970s to the 1950s, a period that could still be considered prosperous and glorious. It locked us inside and repeated it time and time again—it was completely unreasonable!

“The hunter told me that it was because we had committed a grave crime and we needed to redeem ourselves and repent in such a way. But I couldn’t tell what ‘redemption and repentance’ meant at all. Although the Earth at this time was not peaceful, and many places were still shrouded in the shadow of natural disasters and man-made disasters, and the major countries were at each other’s throats, the pressure on the people living in the cement forests was not small, either, but everything had to be compared horizontally. Compared to the history of the past thousand years, this was the best time for the civilization of mankind.

“We have TV, we have movies, we have computers, we have the Internet, we have forums, we have novels. Let’s not talk about the developed countries. Even in the developing countries like ours, the vast majority of the citizens have at least solved the problem of food and clothing. They have made significant progress compared to a few decades ago. Do you understand what I’m saying? I’m saying, if the will of the earth really wanted to torture us, why didn’t it arrange for the time of the reincarnation prison to be during the First World War or the Second World War? At that time, our lives would have been the ‘Hell on Earth’. Maybe it was even earlier. The bloody invasion of foreign species. The high-pressure feudal rule. The dark medieval era. Even the primitive era where we ate raw meat and drank blood. Living in that era where we didn’t have enough food to eat, we didn’t have enough medicine, and any wound could have killed us. This is the treatment that a batch of criminals should receive, right?

“Moreover, in the medieval era, where technology and civilization were extremely underdeveloped, even if the prison breakers awakened the memories of their past lives, they did not have a powerful enough method of information output… I mean, let’s say that the will of the earth set the world background in the medieval era. Even if Ding Lingdang and the others sent to my brain, it would still be impossible for me to spread it on a large scale, right? A novel with a few million words, or even ten million words, could be spread by printing with words or even printing with mud? The printer would kill me first! Even if no one killed me, and I had enough resources to complete the information output, the vast majority of people were illiterate and simply could not accept the information. There was simply no way to resist the will of the earth!

“All in all, everything that the will of the earth did was completely unreasonable and even seemed to be asking for trouble. Don’t you think so?

Li Yao was dazed for a moment. He did not expect that Zhang Daniu would be so thorough.

“That’s right. It seems that Master Niu was chosen as a ‘type-2 Observer’ not only because you are naïve and your soul is as pure as a blank sheet of paper!”

“As for the purpose of the will of the earth,” the Red Pole Star said, “I’ve pondered and explored it multiple times in the previous reincarnation cycles. I even tried to pry into its core secrets with my own death in one of the reincarnation cycles. After all, there was indeed something wrong about it. Maybe its biggest weakness was the purpose of its reincarnation prison.

“After several reincarnations, together with the enlightenment I received from the battlefields of the great universe, although I don’t have solid evidence, I have my own speculations.

“Before I tell you what I think, please tell me what you think is most important for a civilization.

Li Yao and Zhang Daniu looked at each other, deep in thought.

“Survival?”

Li Yao said uncertainly.

“Mating!”

Zhang Daniu was very confident.

The Red Pole Star laughed.

“Alright. I can’t say that your answer is wrong. But survival and reproduction are the most fundamental factors. Almost all the low-level creatures, even the bacteria, need to reproduce. But the unintelligent creatures that can only reproduce cannot create a great civilization.

“I think that, for a super civilization that conquers the universe across the sea of stars, the most important thing is ‘ideal’. The idea of conquering the universe must be the first thing that comes to mind. No matter how absurd the idea is, the civilization will never give up. No matter how many difficulties it faces, the civilization will always overcome them. Even if the odds of success are only one billionth, the civilization must devote 100% of its resources and efforts in order to become the ultimate dominator of the universe, right?”

Li Yao and Zhang Daniu nodded. “That’s true.”

“But ‘ideal’ is too big a word. What can create the greatest dream if it is dissected?

“I think ’emotion’ and ‘imagination’.”

“Emotions and imagination?”

Li Yao and Zhang Daniu looked at each other in bewilderment. On second thought, it did make sense.

“Let’s not talk about the ‘swarm civilization’ made of cold, automated machines. I’ve always believed that an automated bee civilization requires a high speed of network connection and information exchange. It is impossible for them to cover an almost infinite distance in the boundless sea of stars. Therefore, the speed and scale of their expansion are absolutely limited. They cannot be the dominators of the sea of stars in the truest sense. Let’s just talk about the carbon-based intelligent life forms that we can understand. If their civilization wants to flourish and reach the top of the universe, they must be supported by strong emotions and a rich imagination. The two, especially the strong imagination, are absolutely indispensable for a great civilization.

The Red Pole Star said solemnly, “A long time ago, scholars considered the ability to make tools to be the difference between human beings and animals. They even believed that only human beings could make and use tools. But soon, we discovered that monkeys could use tools, leeches could use tools, and even gorillas could make tools on their own.

“However, whether it is monkeys, orangutans, or leeches, or dolphin-like creatures that boast extraordinary wisdom, they will never look up at the sky like human beings and fantasize about the secrets behind the brilliant stars. The ability that belongs to the ‘spirits of all things’ is imagination.

“It was imagination that drove our civilization forward. It was imagination that led our civilization on countless different paths in countless reincarnations. It was imagination that helped us conquer mountains, oceans, and even satellites around us. If we can break the seal of the reincarnation prison and conquer the endless universe one day, it will certainly be because of our ambitions, curiosity, and imagination that are even larger than the endless universe!

“Sounds reasonable.”

“But what does it have to do with the purpose of the reincarnation prison?”

“That’s the problem.

The Red Pole Star replied, “I highly doubt that the will of the earth has no emotions or imagination at all.

“Huh?”

Zhang Daniu was dazed for a long time. “I… don’t quite understand what you mean. What is the so-called will of the earth exactly?”

“It doesn’t matter what it is. It doesn’t matter if it is a four-dimensional life form that we can’t understand, it doesn’t matter if it is a monster created by the earthlings a long, long time ago, it doesn’t matter if it is a civilization of a different species or some random weird thing. What matters is that it must have strong emotions and imagination if it wants to continue growing, evolving, and expanding on the level of a civilization in the universe toward a higher peak.

“Unfortunately, it didn’t,” the Red Pole Star said. “Maybe it once did. But on the long road of evolution, it lost all its emotions and imagination and even exchanged them for tremendous strength as a price that it had to pay. Such strength may have helped it defeat invincible enemies, helped it become the most feared devil of destruction in the universe, and even helped it become the master of the universe. But it also drove it further and further away from the original meaning of ‘life’ and ‘civilization’. It lost itself and forgot who it was and what its purpose was.

“Is that so? It sounds a bit ‘mysterious’!”

Zhang Daniu looked at Li Yao.

But Li Yao’s eyes were glittering. He lowered his head and kept quiet.

“Although it is just my theory and there is not much solid evidence, the assumption can be used to explain many questions.

The Red Pole Star smiled and said, “The so-called ‘reincarnation prison’ is not a prison at all but a ‘farm’ where human beings are grown and ’emotion’ and ‘imagination’ are harvested!

Chapter 3245: Golden Age

“Farm?”

Li Yao and Zhang Daniu both understood what the Red Pole Star meant. Sweat was appearing on their foreheads, and their faces were pale. They pointed at their noses and said, “So, we are the ‘rice’ and ‘fruit trees’ in the farm, and our emotions and imagination are the ‘fruit’ that the will of the earth needs?”

“That’s what I’m guessing. Otherwise, why would the will of the earth lock us up in this damn reincarnation prison after countless reincarnations?”

Red Pole Star pointed at his temples and said, “The purpose is to stimulate our souls to a certain extent so that we can produce emotions and imaginations that are absolutely impossible to be secreted.

“The bizarre, bizarre, and even absurd legends, the works that are condemned as nonsense, the fantastic fantasy worlds, and the boldest and most ignorant yearning for the end of the sea of stars—these are the things that constitute the solid core of a great civilization, and the will of the earth does not have such a core. If it had ever been hot-blooded, it would’ve cooled down and consolidated billions of years ago. Even if it once had dreams, it would’ve turned into mottled fragments and perished!

“On the level of the universe, it is truly an invincible super monster with extraordinary computational ability and enormous military strength. It is a tyrannical existence at the level of a devil who can perfectly control everything in the universe and calculate a perfect answer to all problems. But on the level of ‘life’ and ‘civilization’, the so-called ‘perfectness’ simply does not exist. When the moon is full, there will be losses. After the peak is the abyss, and at the end of the ‘perfectness’ is destruction.

“In short, the will of the earth has no emotions or dreams. It doesn’t even have the ability to make mistakes, and the ability to make mistakes is the most important factor in the evolution of life. Only by continuously making mistakes, continuously making mistakes, and making those whimsical and absurd mistakes can we have the opportunity to survive and develop in the ever-changing universe and even burst forth with endless possibilities.

“A civilization that doesn’t have imagination and can’t make mistakes won’t be able to find the slightest possibility. It won’t even be able to find the meaning of its existence. It is destined to collide with its limits not long after it climbs to the peak. Moreover, it absolutely has no way to break its own limits. Therefore, it has no choice but to rely on external forces—a race that has emotions, dreams, and imaginations and will keep making mistakes in search of endless possibilities, and that is us.

“We humans are not as good as we boast ourselves to be. We are not even worthy of the title of ‘Spirits of All Things’. We are truly a type of very bad, carbon-based intelligent life. We possess a variety of emotions, but we are often impulsive because of our emotions. We fall into the dark swamp of humanity and hurt ourselves. We have the ambition to continuously forge ahead, but this ambition often turns into ambition. In order to not be able to support our ambitions, we lose everything in a muddle-headed manner. We are naïve, romantic, and rich in dreams. But this point often causes us to be tormented by our own dissatisfaction. We are insatiable. No matter how many things we obtain, at most three to five seconds, we will be bored. We yearn to find ten times, a hundred times more things to fill the emptiness in our hearts.

“But it was the flaws that shaped us and endowed us with the greatest strength in the sea of stars. When our ancestors looked up at the sky for the first time in a long time ago and exclaimed, our race was destined to embark on a path of invincibility. Only such us were qualified to be the ‘heart’, ‘brain’, or ‘engine’ of the will of the earth!

Li Yao and Zhang Daniu were shocked again. They looked at the Red Pole in a different way.

“So, the entire Reincarnation Prison is the ‘engine’ of the will of the earth?”

Zhang Daniu mumbled.

“Yes. I prefer the word ‘engine’ to ‘brain’ or ‘farm’.

“Because the ‘Engine Hypothesis’ can explain why the will of the planet did not dare to restart the engine instantly without considering the consequences. It will not only cause serious damage to the engine but also interrupt the continuity of the power output and affect its combat ability in the battlefield of the universe.

Li Yao and Zhang Daniu thought for a moment and agreed with him.

Imagine the will of the earth as a tank that was engaged in fierce combat with the enemy. Naturally, its engine could not be restarted easily. If the engine was damaged for three to five minutes, the will of the earth would be riddled with holes.

“Your assumption does make sense, but I still don’t understand.”

Zhang Daniu frowned. “Even if the Reincarnation Prison is truly a farm with an infinite loop and an engine that is always spinning at a high speed, why is it now? From the 1970s to the 1940s?”

“Because it was the golden era of the civilization on Earth. During the decades, human beings reached the peak of the carbon-based intelligent life on single planets.

The Red Pole Star replied, “My guess is that, of course, the will of the earth could set the background time of the reincarnation prison in the Middle Ages or even the Stone Age, but it would be impossible to harvest enough emotions and imagination. From the Stone Age to the Middle Ages, and even to the modern era when the industrial revolution was just taking shape, it was a time of wisdom and ignorance. The people who lived in the ignorant and closed-off environment did not have enough food to eat. Most of their energy was dedicated to the competition of survival. Only a few people had the luxury of emotions and imagination. The backward methods of information transmission made it impossible for their emotions and imagination to resonate with other people on a large scale.

“For a poet who lived in the Middle Ages, even if he wrote an immortal poem about the stars and the universe, it might take thirty to fifty years, or even a century or two, for it to be transmitted to the ears of another poet thousands of miles away. And most people, other than these noble poets, simply wouldn’t be able to understand even one ten-thousandth of their exquisiteness… The efficiency of this kind of information transmission is really too low. How can the emotions and imagination stimulated by it satisfy the needs of the will of the earth?

“But our era, or rather, the era that the will of the earth created for us, is completely different.

“Steam Revolution, Electric Revolution, Information Revolution!

“World War I, World War II, Cold War!

“Turing machine, computer, network, mobile phone, wireless network, all kinds of information terminal!

“Ultimate universal education, universal entertainment based on solid materials, games where millions of people are online at the same time, webnovels with millions of words, and super-high-speed interactions between authors and readers!

“Space technology, cloning technology, nanotechnology, information explosion, technology explosion. Everyone can share their dreams and emotions with other people through the mobile terminal at any time and place, forming a large-scale resonance similar to mass hysteria!

“Don’t you think that this is the best era if the will of the earth wants to reap our emotions and dreams?

Li Yao and Zhang Daniu were dazed for a long time.

Zhang Daniu scratched his hair and said, “So, what you’re saying is that all the information terminals that we use, including the social environment that we are in, were created by the will of the earth in order to harvest us?”

“It is not necessarily intentional. Everything is too real to be faked. I suspect that a long time ago, there was indeed an ‘Original Earth’. Every reincarnation we experienced was based on the real history of the ‘Original Earth’.

“But this period must’ve been carefully selected by the will of the earth,” the Red Pole Star said. “Only such a golden era is most convenient for it to ‘cultivate’ the strongest emotions and the most abundant imagination for the most efficient harvest.

“If that’s the case, there will be another explanation for the obliteration of the Observer. Maybe it is not just because they are scared that the Observer will wake up more heroes, but also part of their harvest. After all, the Observer is often the one with the strongest emotions and the most imagination. Harvesting one Observer is probably more useful than harvesting a hundred ordinary people.

“Which means—”

Zhang Daniu thought of a chilling possibility. “The creation of the Observer, the awakening of the Awakened, and even the resistance of the prison breakers were all part of the plan of the will of the earth?”

“It’s possible. After all, when the awakened one discovers the truth and resists as a prison breaker, endless waves of emotions, will, and determination will be released. Maybe the will of the earth needs such things?”

The Red Pole Star smiled. “However, I have always believed that there is no such thing as perfect control over everything. Maybe the will of the earth in its prime years could manipulate six billion heroic spirits at will, but things are different now. This time, we are different!

“I understand.”

Zhang Daniu thought for a moment and said, “Then why did the will of the earth set the termination date of the reincarnation prison to the 2130s and 240s? The later it went on, the more information exploded and the more dreams people had. Wouldn’t it be able to harvest more emotions and imagination?”

“No. That would be too much.

The Red Pole Star explained, “The so-called ‘reaping emotions and imagination’ is a double-edged sword. When the martyred spirits output the most intense waves in their soul, they will certainly awaken and resist. The will of the earth must control the resistance of the awakened ones to a reasonable limit. If the limit is too high, the situation will be completely out of control.

“Let’s use the ‘engine’ as an example. The will of the earth wants to keep the output power of the engine within a reasonable limit. Of course, it is not good if the output power is too low. But if the output power is too violent for a long time, the engine will overload or even explode. That will be even worse, right?”

Chapter 3246: You And Me

Zhang Daniu still didn’t understand, but Li Yao had already understood what the Red Pole meant.

It was true that too much was as bad as too little. If the six billion earthlings living in the prison of reincarnation—six billion unyielding and defiant heroes—were regarded as ‘energy sources’, they would be extremely unstable and dangerous sources of energy. If one wanted to use such energy as fuel, they would have to be extremely prudent.

From the end of the twentieth century to the middle of the twenty-first century, it was the golden period for the steady and efficient utilization of such energy. Later, as the information explosion and the space technology grew more mature, it was very likely that mankind would develop the universe beyond Earth. At the very least, it was possible to develop the Moon and Mars on a large scale.

Li Yao recalled a joke that claimed that Earth was in fact a virtual world created by God, and that the angels were responsible for maintaining the splendid illusion for God. It was a pity that the angels were not capable enough to build a vast, vivid universe. All they needed to do was to perfect all the details within the observation range of human beings.

Everything beyond the range of observation, such as the glittering stars, was just a simple background of meaningless light spots.

Therefore, as the observation and exploration ability of human beings improved, the angels had to create more scenes and details to fool human beings. When human beings were in the dark medieval era, all the angels had to do was perfect the details of the molecular level on Earth and smear the brilliance of the sun and the moon.

But when human beings developed telescopes and electron microscopes, the angels had to carefully paint the fuzzy celestial bodies and arrange their trajectories for every atom, nucleus, electron, and proton.

Even for the angels, the real-time computation and rendering work was too exhausting and complicated. The angels were complaining about the speed of human beings’ evolution while they calculated and rendered the pictures in a hurry. They even made mistakes every once in a while, leaving a lot of bugs that made many human beings doubt their own world.

Although the joke was just a joke, the problem faced by the will of the earth was the same as that of the angels, wasn’t it? As the output power of the ‘engine’ increased, the ‘engine’ was gradually awakening its own will and was no longer under its control.

Therefore, it had to be stopped before the engine went out of control, or rather, before the middle of the 21st century. After it cooled down for a while, it had to be restarted in the 1970s and 1980s.

Of course, combined with Red Pole and Lady Grey Mist, another possibility could be inferred. When the civilization of mankind developed into the middle of the 21st century, it was destined to collide with the limits of the civilization of a single planet. The contradiction between population and resources could not be more intense. If the civilization could not break out of the mother planet and embark on the journey to the endless sea of stars, it would inevitably be destroyed in the cruelest bombardment and smoke.

It was inevitable for civilization. Even the will of the earth could not be changed after countless reincarnations.

Either way, at least one thing was proved.

Even if the will of the earth was truly a four-dimensional life form that was above the three-dimensional universe, it was impossible for it to completely control everything about the three-dimensional life form—at least, it could not completely control the dream, pride, and hope of the three-dimensional life form.

Life would always find a way out, even if one lived in an indestructible virtual reincarnation.

Mistake, loss of control, creation and destruction. This was the true meaning of life.

While Li Yao was deep in thought, Zhang Daniu suddenly realized what was going on. He clapped his hands and said, “I understand now. That’s why the residents of many major cities seem to be in low spirits right now. They are muddleheaded and have lost their passion and dreams. They are like the walking dead and the salted fish that has been dried in the sun. It is not because they are willing to do so, but because their emotions, dreams, ambitions, and imaginations have all been harvested and exploited!”

“Yes. The real us is far more than what we are right now. We are a species that can create endless possibilities and billions of miracles among the stars!

The Red Pole Star continued, “However, after countless cycles of exploitation and harvesting, people’s dreams withered, their ambitions collapsed, their personalities were obliterated, and their imagination and courage to create miracles gradually withered. They became what you see today, like grilled salted fish in a dark iron house.

“However, we still have a chance. We can still change. We can still regain the glory and dreams of the past. Pride and ambition… as long as we defeat the will of the Earth!”

“I see!”

Zhang Daniu said excitedly, “So you’re saying that the real me before countless reincarnations is probably not the same as the past few reincarnations. I’m not some mediocre, muddle-headed, third-rate writer and tabloid reporter who has been single for decades, right? The real me is definitely a big hero who has ideals, sentiments, integrity, values, is tall, handsome, full of charisma, and has thousands of crazy girls. It’s just that my noble sentiments and charisma have been sucked away by the hateful will of the earth. That’s why I can’t help but become a salted fish—according to your theory, isn’t that right?”

“Theoretically… uh…”

The Red Pole Star hesitated for a moment and nodded. “Theoretically speaking, I haven’t found any evidence to deny the possibility yet.

“What do you mean?”

Zhang Daniu asked, “I don’t read many books, so don’t try to reason with me. Just tell me, is it possible, or is it not?”

“You know that we have been locked in the prison of reincarnation for too many times to count. Let’s say countless times, which means that we have experienced infinite time.

The Red Pole Star continued, “In the infinite time, any miracle can happen. Even if a monkey is typing on the keyboard for no good reason, it is possible that, after countless repetitions, the monkey will type the words, ‘When the sun reaches the end of the mountain, the Yellow River flows into the ocean. If you want to see a thousand miles ahead, you must reach a higher level’.

“If you look at the problem from this point of view, yes, the possibility you are talking about does exist. If even a monkey can type out ‘Deep Sparrow Pavilion’, why can’t you be a great hero who can shake the sea of stars?

“Remember, it is not about who we are in the past or in the present, but tomorrow and the future. Except for our own inferiority, cowardice, and cowardice, there is no power that can stop us from releasing our true self and burning our most brilliant dreams to become super invincible heroes!

“Then what are we waiting for?”

Zhang Daniu waved his fists hard. Hot blood was crawling all over his shining face as he roared, “Let’s blow up the will of the earth as soon as possible and let the real me be completely released! My ten fingers are itching for blood! Roar! Roar! Roar!”

“There’s no hurry. It will take three days for the preparation of the ‘Invincible Thunderbolt Super Brainwave Magnification System’ to be completed. Besides, Master Niu must be exhausted from the long journey over the past few days. Your current state is not suitable to serve as the information node between the battlefield of the universe and the battlefield on Earth.

Red Extremity said, “Please take a good rest in the next three days, Master Niu. I will prepare the most thorough relaxation and recovery service for you. I guarantee that you will be able to face the last stream of information in the most energetic and comfortable state.

“However, I wonder, is Master Niu free today to attend a reader meeting?”

“Reader meeting?”

Zhang Daniu’s eyes bulged out of their sockets. His pupils were glittering, and he stammered, “We—we are all loyal readers of ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’. Who—who are we?”

“There are all kinds of people, young and old, from all walks of life.

Red Pole Star opened his hands and said, “As I said just now, don’t you want to meet the martyrs whose ‘soul fragments’ or who have been influenced by the fragments and survived countless reincarnations are still ‘hot-blooded’, ‘dreams’, and ‘souls’?”

“Of course, of course!”

Zhang Daniu rubbed his fists. “I’m the most approachable person. I love communicating with readers the most!”

“What about you?”

The Red Pole Star asked again, “Mr. Li Yao, would you like to join us?”

“I… Forget it.”

Li Yao frowned, not sure what he was feeling. “I always feel that we are all ‘Li Yao’. It’s weird when we are together. I feel that I’ve fallen into a maze of mirrors and seen ten thousand twisted reflections of myself.

“You’re wrong again, Student Li Yao. As I said just now, the vast majority of the ‘soul fragments’ of Li Yao have been torn to the extent that they are negligible, like bacteria or even viruses. And those heroic spirits are all independent individuals who have already gone through thousands upon thousands of cycles in the prison of reincarnation. Even if the ‘Li Yao virus’ has infiltrated their souls, at most, it will only have a certain degree of influence on them. By activating the ‘hot blood’ and ‘soul’ in their souls, they will not be completely transformed into another Li Yao!”

Red Extremity smiled. “Just like Master Zhang Jiashu whom you saw with your own eyes. Although he was infected by the ‘Li Yao Virus’, his self-awareness was very clear. It was just a minor ailment, wasn’t it?

“Master Niu next to you is another excellent example. Obviously, Master Niu has been infected by the ‘Li Yao Virus’, too. Otherwise, he wouldn’t have been able to become a ‘Type 2 Observer’ or ‘Information Node’. However, the degree of his infection is very low. Most of the characteristics of his personality are still his own, not influenced by the ‘Li Yao Virus’.

“What I meant was that only the larger of the billions of soul fragments, which retained most of the memories and personality characteristics, could create a ‘newcomer’ out of thin air, a heroic spirit that theoretically shouldn’t exist, and only such a new heroic spirit could condense all the power of resonance and become ‘100% Li Yao’.

“Then—”

Li Yao stared at Red Pole. “Who is the ‘bigger shard’?”

“Me.”

Red Pole Star pointed at his chest and then at Li Yao. “And you.”

Chapter 3247: Wasteland Dream

“Me?”

Li Yao felt that things were getting weirder and weirder. He mumbled, “Which of us will become the ‘100% Li Yao’?”

“As for the question… Don’t be hasty. We will know the answer in three days.”

The Red Pole Star smiled and invited him again. “What do you think? Do you want to join the reader meeting with Master Niu?”

“Forget it.”

Pondering for a moment, Li Yao shook his head and said, “I have a headache. I want to rest and recharge myself.”

“That’s right. Let Student Li Yao go and rest early. I alone am enough to deal with those enthusiastic readers!”

Zhang Daniu rubbed his hands and said impatiently.

Since that was what they said, the Red Pole Star did not insist. It escorted Li Yao and Zhang Daniu out of the pathway in person, before the grey mist lady and the Spartan escorted them to the meeting hall and their residence.

For a nameless, desolate island in the depths of the ocean, the living room was not bad. Li Yao locked the door and threw himself into the soft, sponge-like bed. He rose and fell in a trance in front of the springs and goose down.

For some reason, he felt uneasy.

In fact, his meeting with Red Pole had gone very well today. He had retrieved a lot of precious information and had a certain understanding about the mysteries of the Reincarnation Prison and the universe. The plan proposed by Red Pole was simple and crude, but it was not entirely impossible to succeed if he had no other options.

Logically speaking, he shouldn’t be so uneasy.

However…

“If the novel is true, then when has the protagonist of the book, , ever encountered a smooth sailing plan?”

Li Yao mumbled to himself, “Every time the plan seems to go smoothly, there will be an even more shocking turn of events. The seemingly kindest and strongest teammate will turn hostile in the blink of an eye and become the most terrifying behind-the-scenes boss, increasing the difficulty of the mission by ten times in the blink of an eye… Hey, according to this pattern, could it be that the Red Pole is the real mastermind behind the scenes this time? Yes, it is a bit similar. Although this guy claims to be the holder of the ‘biggest soul fragment’ of Li Yao, his evil appearance is clearly not a decent Li Yao!”

“Besides, I seem to have forgotten something.

“It seems—it seems that I did something a day not long ago. Something very… terrible, bloody, and terrifying. What was it exactly? Was it a battle against a hunter? No. It was a hundred times more brutal than that. How could I forget it?

“Da da da da da da!”

Li Yao heard the sound of glass marbles falling from the ceiling.

He suddenly jumped up from the bed. His eyes bulged, his nostrils flared, and his forehead was soaked in cold sweat.

This was underground. Above the ceiling above his head, there should be tens of millions of tons of rocks and concrete. Where were the glass marbles?

“Is there really sound, or am I mentally ill?”

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao walked to the bathroom and filled a basin with cold water. He buried his head deep inside the basin, trying to forget everything that could not be explained.

However, when he finally cooled down and raised his head after three minutes, he was shocked by what he saw in the mirror.

What appeared in the mirror was not his face, not even his head.

It was a silver ball.

A silver ball grew out of his neck and replaced his face and head.

The ball-shaped mirror reflected the weird brightness back and forth with the mirror countless times. It did not have any facial features, but it was wearing a weird and mocking expression, as if a different version of him was laughing at him in the mirror.

Li Yao took two steps back. He almost slipped and fell to the ground.

He blinked his eyes and looked at himself in the mirror. He even extended his trembling hands to touch his face. The silver ball immediately disappeared, and he resumed his original appearance.

However, his blurred face made him even more confused. His head was dizzy, and he was in a trance. He did not know what was going on at all.

An urgent bell suddenly rang in the room outside.

Li Yao was startled. It was not until he calmed himself down that he realized that the phone that Red Pole had sent to them was ringing.

This cell phone could only be used within the range of the Ark Island. It was not so much a cell phone as it was a powerful, stable signal, and a high-level, anti-jamming, high-level, visible walkie-talkie. Even in the underground of the island, it was completely unobstructed. However, only a few people knew this number. Red Pole, Lady Grey Mist, Spartan, and of course, Zhang Daniu… Who would actually call him at this time?

Li Yao picked up his phone and saw that it was Zhang Daniu.

After the call was connected, deafening music and laughter immediately came from the loudspeaker. Zhang Daniu’s greasy, excited face appeared on the screen.

Looking from the gap between his face and the edge of the screen, it seemed to be a private room of a town concert hall, decorated in splendid colors.

There seemed to be a few men and women as well as fair-skinned arms and legs. Some of them were crying like ghosts, some were laughing strangely. It did not look like a readers’ meeting at all.

Zhang Daniu must’ve drunk a lot. He was burping and his eyes were red.

“Too enthusiastic! Oh my, readers are too enthusiastic!”

He spoke incoherently, “Li—Student Li Yao, Teacher Niu can’t hold on any longer. Why don’t you come along? We can discuss together the follow-up development of , as well as the serious project to revitalize our country’s science fiction industry!”

Li Yao frowned and hung up without saying anything.

“What the hell?”

He mumbled to himself, “What the hell is Red Pole doing?”

The question was destined to be unanswered today.

Li Yao felt that his head was aching more and more. When he lay down, his head was full of humming noises.

He fought against the weird noises for a long time. It was not until he fell asleep that he realized what the humming noises were exactly.

It was the screams and struggles of countless innocent souls in the raging flames, or rather, the horn of revenge.

Li Yao had a dream in which he appeared on an exceptionally clear and real Earth. Maybe it was the place where the Red Pole Star claimed that the Earth was the very beginning of billions of reincarnations.

It was not a beautiful paradise with blue skies and white clouds, but a purgatory that had been roasted by flames, filled with smoke and radiation. He was being carried by countless ragged, bizarre-shaped mutants. They congregated into a surging crowd and marched toward a certain direction together.

He didn’t understand the significance of this scene. He could only vaguely feel that as a member of the tide, he was incomparably angry—it was the kind of anger filled with desperation and betrayal.

Together with his ugly and angry companions, he rolled forward and extended his hunched hands toward the horizon, roaring like an animal. Countless other gray, wriggling, ugly people joined them, too, like a raging tide that was sweeping everything.

However, they failed to capture the traitors and the deserters after all. While they were roaring and waving their hands, dozens of gray pillars of smoke appeared on the horizon, in which brilliant flames were burning. The flames carried streaks of silver brightness, making them look like extraordinary immortals. They waved their sleeves and tore apart the sky without even looking at them.

All that was left was a desolate land full of radiation and a sky riddled with holes.

The sun in the horizon was blocked by the radiation caused by the thermonuclear war. The sunlight was refracted by the dust and turned into a colorful mist that looked like wastewater. Li Yao’s dream turned into an abstract painting. The earthlings in the painting were like pitiful and hilarious ghosts. When they realized that they could not catch up with the traitors and the deserters no matter what, everyone seemed to have lost all their strength and fell to the ground, not knowing what to do. Some of them were sobbing, some were cursing loudly, but most of them were simply staring at the sun, the origin of life, the sun that was generous and treated all creatures equally!

The sun soon gave them the answer.

Or rather, the final verdict had been passed upon them.

Under the cover of the dust, they could not see the entire process of the meteoroid hitting the sun, but they could clearly feel that the brightness of the sun had soared to the maximum and was expanding at a visible speed.

The ocean was vaporized, the forest was burning, and the earth was cracking. All the creatures that were born because of the sun over billions of years were enveloped in the flames and the high temperature that were absolutely unbearable for them. They participated in the intense reaction of the sun in person and were carbonized, shattered, and annihilated within one second.

Including all the earthlings who were stranded on the wasteland.

However, Li Yao’s dream did not end with the burning and dispersing of billions of Earthlings.

As his body perished, he entered an even weirder and more trance-like state, as if he had turned into… a drop of seawater in a vast ocean that constituted a tiny bit of the sun’s hydrogen, or a… cell in the body of a super life form beyond description.

All his companions were the same as him. Without the shackles of the ugly shell, they could get to know each other better. With the help of plasma and electromagnetic waves, they were able to build a more delicate and elegant structure.

It was hard to say whether he liked it or loathed it. Although he was not used to his new living state yet, it was a thousand times better than the deformed, ugly corpses that had been betrayed and abandoned on the wasteland.

Life would eventually find its own path.

Thankfully, they had found it.

…

Right then, Li Yao was woken up by earsplitting buzzes.

There was no telling whether it was because the dream was too strange and filled with fear, or because the humming was too ear-piercing, but in the blink of an eye, his heartbeat soared to the limit. He leaned against the head of the bed and retched for a long time, yet he was still drenched in cold sweat. His entire spine was cold, and his entire body was devoid of strength.

However, the incessant humming was telling him one thing, something that was absolutely impossible.

Enemy attack!

Chapter 3248: Earth Shattering

BOOM!

Before Li Yao realized what was going on, earsplitting noises were echoing all around him. It was not the usual bombardment and explosions but a sign that the undersea mountains were being torn apart and magma was about to burst out.

Then, Li Yao saw a weird scene.

The room was dry and stable, but it seemed to be underwater at this moment. Visible ripples were spreading out. When the ripples swept over the bed, the bed, the bathroom, and his body, everything was blurred and twisted, producing endless wrinkles.

The feeling of nausea intensified.

Taking a deep breath to calm himself down, Li Yao staggered to the door and opened it.

The corridor outside was also twisted into the shape of a fried dough twist by the waves. Many awakened ones were running in panic. For a moment, Li Yao suspected that everyone was going to be flattened by the twisted corridor. However, in the next moment, after the waves passed, the space was stable again, and everything was normal again.

“Li Yao! Li Yao!”

Sparda appeared out of nowhere and beckoned Li Yao to go up with him. “Enemy attack! God knows how the Heaven’s Origin Sector got the coordinates of the Ark Island. A lot of ‘Prisoners’ are bombarding the area outside. The defense layer that is maintaining the wrinkles of our space is about to collapse. Within half an hour, the wrinkles will certainly be broken. By then, the wrinkles will be gone, and the Ark Island will be exposed to the outside world. Nobody knows how serious the distortion will be. It is not safe to stay in the depths of the island. Come with me and fight the Prison Guardians of the Heaven’s Origin Sector!”

Li Yao was dragged to the top of the island by Sparda before he figured out what was going on.

What happened here surprised him even more.

The ocean and the sky around the Ark Island were as tranquil as before, as if there were a blue sky and white clouds. However, in the sky dozens of nautical miles away, interweaving gold lines appeared, which divided the sky and the ocean into hexagonal ‘defense modules’. Right now, all the defense modules were shaking violently and emitting dazzling brilliance.

It was like the sky was a translucent and indestructible bowl, and right now, the bowl had reached the limit of fatigue due to the heavy blow from outside forces. It could not hold on any longer and was cracking.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

The quakes of every hexagonal defense module in the sky were accompanied by earsplitting noises. This time, it was going to be a literal earthquake.

Countless Hell Breakers ran to the top of the island like Li Yao and Sparda.

In the meantime, the shape of the Ark Island gradually changed amid the earsplitting noises. Many rocks shed their camouflage and turned into hideous turrets. Weapons that were far more advanced than those in the early 21st century on Earth slid out of the warehouse along the rails. Black barrels and rails that were glittering with electric arcs were aimed at the surface of the ocean. A lot of super personal weapons similar to light swords and plasma guns were distributed to all the Hell Breakers.

The island had really turned into a warship, an extremely powerful super warship.

Even so, faced with the catastrophe of the universe, the Hell Breakers could not contain the panic and fear deep inside their hearts.

“How come?”

They looked at each other in bewilderment. “How did the enemy find this place so quickly?”

After countless reincarnations, they had long understood that death was not the end.

However, before the preparation work was completed, the enemy had destroyed their nest and sabotaged their plan. They were locked in the damn cage forever, controlled, interfered, exploited, and harvested. It was a fate ten thousand times worse than death.

“Maybe our starship is being followed.”

Sparda said solemnly and spat on the ground. “It doesn’t make sense. We check all the cabins and components on board every day, even the screws, to make sure that there are no trackers that can escape our scanning. All the crew members have proved their loyalty in the tests of deep hypnosis or even the memory of their previous lives. There is no way that they can be followed!

“What went wrong exactly? Damn it!”

After cursing for a while, his face suddenly turned pale. He held the reef next to him and vomited.

Many people were the same as him, including Li Yao. Sparda told Li Yao that it was a normal reaction of space quakes. Right now, they were on a short ‘space trip’ where they would return to the normal space from the space folds. The more disgusted they felt, the weaker the structure of the space folds would be, indicating that they were about to collapse.

Even the apocalyptic beasts that Li Yao saw when he arrived on ‘Lucky Star’—the giant white fish that were as translucent as jade with colorful fins, the glamorous jellyfish that looked like dancing fairies, and the hundreds of bizarre apocalyptic beasts—all sensed the space waves and flooded to the surface of the ocean from the bottom of the ocean. Some of them even jumped high into the air and shouted, “Jiang Ang! Jiang Ang! Jiang Ang!”

The earsplitting noises lasted ten minutes. The world in front of everyone’s eyes was sometimes stable and sometimes twisted. Visible ripples even appeared on the boundless ocean. Then, the noises and quakes suddenly stopped.

Although the attack came to an end, nobody was able to swallow their heart back into their stomach, because after a moment of dead silence, they heard a series of squeaking sounds.

It was hard to describe what kind of sound it was. It was like someone was locked in a sealed iron house, only to be compressed and constricted by an even stronger force.

Or rather, the Hell Breakers were like a group of submarine soldiers who had discovered that the submarine they were on had been seriously damaged by the enemy’s mines. Not only was its shell riddled with holes, it had also lost its impetus and buoyancy and was sinking toward the abyss of increasing pressure.

A storm was brewing. Right now, every inch of space was on the verge of destruction.

Finally—

Li Yao was the first to notice that a hexagonal ‘defense module’ had collapsed and disappeared in the sky far away!

If he hadn’t seen it with his own eyes, he would never have believed what he was seeing. A hexagonal hole suddenly appeared in the blue sky, while dark clouds were rolling in the sky outside the hole. Thunder was rumbling, and a storm was pouring in through the hole like a waterfall, forming a black pillar of water!

“Ah!”

All the Hell Breakers exclaimed.

In the blink of an eye, the cracks spread out at a speed visible to the naked eye. More and more hexagonal holes appeared in the blue sky, and the dark clouds, thunder, and storm gnawed away the few blue skies and white clouds like ferocious giant beasts. It did not take long before the entire sky was swallowed by darkness. The lightning and the torrential rain turned into the main color of the heaven and the earth. Enraged faces were even condensed in the dark clouds, overlooking the lone island and the prison breakers on it.

In the middle of the raging storm, a fearless, insane fleet was approaching the Ark Island at a high speed.

It was a fleet of various kinds of ragged ships. There were ten-thousand-ton ships, inconspicuous ocean fishing boats, and even several warships.

Judging from the flags and the carrier numbers, they were mostly from the countries near Southeast Asia. They had been summoned by a certain weird yet powerful force and marched toward the Ark Island without rest. That was why they were able to assault the place in the first place.

Other tankers, fishing boats, and warships were not a big deal. After all, according to the development of the reincarnation, the countries in Southeast Asia and the South Pacific did not have a strong navy. But the iron beast at the end of the line was as magnificent as a mountain and as intimidating as a deity. It was the largest war machine that mankind could build in this era, an aircraft carrier!

No. It was not just an aircraft carrier. Judging from the brilliance of steel amid the raging tides, it was a fully-armed carrier fleet. Maybe it had been wiped out by the apocalypse, no, the will of the earth, during a live-fire drill or a rescue mission!

“The enemy is going to fight to the death!”

Li Yao had no doubt that the will of the earth had the ability to interfere with the brainwaves of human beings, control their thoughts temporarily, and control an aircraft carrier group as well as more ten-thousand-ton ships and warships in a certain evil way.

Again, it was a question of how many resources to invest and the price to pay. If the will of the earth was willing to pay any price, it would naturally be able to achieve everything in front of it, but doing so would certainly be a serious burden or even a strike for it, which was not conducive to the war in the great universe.

Therefore, the will of the earth was very confident that it would destroy the Ark Foundation in the shortest amount of time and at the least cost. The virus that crawled into its ‘heart, brain, and engine’ would be completely wiped out. Then, it would turn around and deal with the enemies in the battlefield of the universe calmly.

Not good.

Li Yao looked around. The ‘paradise’ that had been hiding the Ark Island was gone. The space wrinkles were gone. Right now, the entire Ark Island was exposed to the will of the earth.

This place had become the ‘home field’ for the will of the earth. Naturally, the attackers had the upper hand.

Lightning and thunder, stormy weather, raging tides, and continuous tsunamis were not suitable for the sailing and warring of a fleet. Even a ten-thousand-ton starship would have to retreat in the face of tides more than ten meters high, not to mention cutting through them.

However, every cargo ship or warship on the attacking side seemed to have received the blessing of all the deities and devils in the world. Enshrouded by mysterious force fields, they were moving among the furious tides as if they were on the ground and even accelerated to the maximum speed with the help of the tides!

Chapter 3249: Silver-White Eyes

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Li Yao saw clusters of white smoke rising up from the front of the row of warships in the middle of the waves. The Heaven’s Origin Sector had opened fire!

Although almost all the warships under the enemy’s command were destroyers and frigates of the 1960s and 1970s except for the carrier group at the end of the war, the collective sound of thousands of cannons was still not to be underestimated. It was different from the movies where a single cannon would only leave a mud pit. In the real world, even the coverage of World War II-level artillery could still cut down every hill on the island.

In that moment, time seemed to have frozen. The air seemed to have turned into something thick and heavy. The trajectories of every cannonball were clearly visible in midair.

No. It was not Li Yao’s imagination. Some kind of mysterious force had formed an invisible force field on the periphery of the Ark Island and slowed and fixed all the rattling cannonballs in midair!

The closest cannonball was no more than fifty meters away from the island. Everybody could see the bullet that was spinning like a gyro, but it could not go any further.

Li Yao sensed that dozens of intense life magnetic fields were surging behind him. When he looked back, he saw dozens of Hell Breakers standing in midair with their hands open and chanting solemnly.

Veins were bulging on their foreheads. Blood was flowing out of their nostrils and the corners of their mouths. Some of their hair was turning white at a visible speed. It was obvious that blocking the cannonball had consumed a lot of their physical and mental strength.

“Ha!”

Led by one of the prison breakers, they roared at the same time and pushed the scattered cannonballs back to the warship in their original trajectories.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Before he returned to the warship, the cannonballs had already exploded in midair, blossoming bloody red lotuses in the pouring storm.

The firepower from the shore of the Ark Island was spreading out, too.

It was neither a cannon nor a missile. Instead, milky-white pillars of light, or crackling metal projectiles that were wreathed in electric arcs, were rushing forward at a high speed. As long as they hit an enemy warship, they were often fatal wounds that could pierce through the front and the back like dry weeds. Whether the target was a fishing boat, a cargo ship, or a warship, they would all collapse within one minute and sink into the cold, deep ocean.

It was a pity that the tides on the surface of the ocean seemed to be against the Ark Foundation on purpose, which made it impossible for them to aim precisely. As a result, their turrets were exposed, and they were attacked one after another.

Although there were a lot of Jailbreakers on the Ark Island, not all of them had the ability to manipulate objects remotely and twist the force field. Even the deep-sensors who had awakened the memories of almost ten reincarnations were only able to interfere with the direction of three to five cannonballs. When more cannonballs came close, they would often vomit blood and fall to the ground with pale faces. Even if they could interfere with the landing point of the cannonballs, they would not be able to get out of the range of the Ark Island, not to mention going back the way they came. Very soon, scorching fireballs appeared one after another on the beachhead of the Ark Island, and the firepower of the counterattack became sparse.

Hum! Hum! Hum! Hum!

There were noises of flies crowding on the surface of the ocean. Lying in the middle of the reefs, Li Yao narrowed his eyes and observed carefully, only to discover that countless Prison Guardians in flying suits and jetpacks were lunging at the ark island.

A war between the Awakeners had to be ended by the Awakeners.

Noticing that the enemy was drawing close, the Hell Breakers behind him roared their last, too.

“For mankind, for freedom!”

“I would rather never be reincarnated than live an ignoble life!

“We are not fighting alone. In the battlefield of the universe, the magnificent, brilliant ultimate war is being waged, too. If we persist and fight until the end, there is still hope for victory!

Many Hell Breakers flew into the sky to fight the Prison Guardians of the Heaven’s Origin Sector with their own superpowers or the weapons provided by the Ark Foundation that were beyond the era!

When the two parties engaged in melee combat and collided in midair, the scene was no better than a street brawl. It was even crueler and uglier.

One of the Hell Breakers had just chopped off the head of one of the Prison Guardians with his light sword when he was burnt to a crisp by another one. In the next second, a second Prison Guardian planted a ‘microbiotic bomb’ that looked like poison into his body. His face immediately turned pale, and his stomach expanded at a visible speed. His internal organs were all blown to smithereens.

The Hell Breakers on this side had just released some kind of brainwave resonance technique to form an invisible great array in the air, which cast the enemy into a state of weakness and stagnation. In the blink of an eye, the few Prison Guardians on the other side burnt their blood and sacrificed their lives, turning themselves into living skeletons. In the end, they spat out clusters of black fog that looked like swarms of insects and broke the great array on this side. Then, both sides were exhausted and unable to resist the gravity. They fell from hundreds of meters in the sky and were swallowed by the deep sea.

Even the deep-sea spiritual beasts that seemed to be aloof from worldly affairs, such as the big white fish with colorful fins and the colorful jellyfish, joined the battle under the control of the Hell Breakers. It was a pity that their combat ability was not as good as they appeared to be. Very soon, they were crushed by the iron beasts and turned into dirty traces in the black seawater.

The carrier group was getting closer and closer to the Ark Island.

In fact, modern warfare was all about over-the-horizon strikes. Even Li Yao could see the enemy’s carrier with his naked eye. This place was far beyond the range of the enemy’s air strikes.

However, due to the heavy rain and thunderstorms, it was not convenient to deploy the fighter jets and the bombers, and it was difficult to deal with the super awakeners with the help of the fighter jets. Therefore, the enemy was still pushing forward unhurriedly until they reached the beach of the Ark Island.

“Li Yao! Li Yao!”

He suddenly heard someone calling him from behind. It was Lady Gray Mist!

The grey mist lady’s face was paler than when she met the hunter the other day. She grabbed Li Yao’s wrist and said, “What are you waiting for? Red Pole wants you to go there immediately. The situation has changed. He has to activate the ‘super brainwave magnification system’ in advance. He wants you to form the core part of the puzzle with him!”

Li Yao was dazed for a moment. He looked at the super-large antennas around the island.

The Heaven’s Origin Sector had apparently discovered the weirdness of the antennas, too. Many of the attacks were aimed at the antennas precisely. Although the Hell Breakers were defending them with their lives, two towers of antennas were still hit by the cannonballs. They were melted and tilted in the raging flames, turning into piles of wasted iron and copper.

It did make sense. Although it was in a hurry, there wouldn’t be a second chance if it was not activated now.

“I’m on it!”

There was brightness in Li Yao’s eyes that even he did not notice. He jumped out of the reef and rushed into the underground command room.

Right now, the underground command room was a mess.

Because the space folds had been flattened, the interior of the island seemed to have been seriously twisted. The originally straight pathway turned into a zigzagging maze. Li Yao crouched and crawled through the gaps in the darkness. It was not easy for him to reach the command room that was full of screens yesterday.

Thankfully, the optical circuits and wireless signals here were very stable. Various pictures were still blinking on most of the screens, including the real-time situation of the battlefield in every corner of the Ark Island and the sea nearby, as well as the natural disasters that were taking place all over the world, as well as a series of data and statements.

With a few casual glances, Li Yao discovered that the situation in various parts of the world was quite bad. In just a few days, the natural disasters everywhere, including but not limited to earthquakes, storms, floods, hailstones, and volcanic eruptions, had become several times more serious. Countless populated traditional residential areas of human beings had been razed to the ground by magma, countless economically developed coastal areas had been reduced to water villages, and countless metropolises with tens of millions of people had collapsed like children’s building blocks in an unprecedented super earthquake.

It was… a catastrophe of civilization, an eternal catastrophe!

Watching the magnificent cities collapsing on the screen, the beautiful villages covered in volcanic ash, and the factories and farmlands swallowed by the flood, Li Yao’s heart and fists shrank to the minimum.

“I don’t have time.”

The Red Pole Star seemed to have suffered a heavy blow. His voice was ten years older than before. He was operating the control panel crazily, as if he were preparing for the super brainwave magnification system. Hearing Li Yao’s footsteps, he said to Li Yao without looking back, “We don’t have a second choice even if we can only activate 30% of the functions. You have to help me!”

“Understood.”

Li Yao asked, “How can I help?”

But he was quite confused. Where was Zhang Daniu? Was he too drunk to get up?

This guy was really a troublemaker!

He glanced back and forth between the hundreds of screens. Suddenly, his eyes froze.

He saw the fleet of the Heaven’s Origin Sector on dozens of screens.

Right then, above almost all the ships, including the carrier groups, a giant silver balloon rose up and floated into the sky despite the wind, rain, thunder, and fire.

Every balloon should be more than five meters in diameter. They were the weather balloons for high-altitude exploration or the air defense balloons for intercepting enemy planes. Therefore, they were very solid. Even though lightning was raging around them, it could not stop them from flying higher and higher.

Hundreds of silver-white balloons were floating between the dark clouds and the thunder, staring at Li Yao through the screen like hundreds of silver eyes.

Li Yao’s breath suddenly came to a halt, and his heartbeat became extremely weak. His fists, which had been clenched in fury for the destruction of the Earth, gradually loosened. His ten fingers twitched unconsciously, but his pupils enlarged slightly and lost their previous brilliance. Two silver spots the size of needles appeared in the depths of his eyes that were as deep as black holes.

Chapter 3250: The Lord'S Projection

Li Yao’s face suddenly turned extremely mysterious.

The silver brightness in the depths of his eyes seemed to be alive and extended zigzagging silver threads to his eyeballs, eyes, and even his entire face. The silver threads cut his face into almost a hundred pieces, each of which was shivering uncontrollably and forming an indescribable expression.

It was as if he had been controlled by a certain force and was caught in a bottomless dream.

Or maybe, he woke up from a long dream and did not reveal his true face until this moment.

The entire process lasted about half a second.

Within half a second, every cell in his body, and even every wisp of his soul in the depths of his brain, had been baptized by the silver storm and changed beyond recognition.

Half a second later, ‘Li Yao’ smiled and waved his hands casually.

BAM!

The thick door, which was the only entrance between the command room and the outside world and was almost the size of a bank vault, was closed for no reason. The gears and bearings were spinning weirdly and locked with earsplitting noises.

“You—”

The Red Pole Star seemed to have realized that something was wrong. When it turned around, it was faced with Li Yao’s attack.

When the silver threads in the depths of his eyes flowed to his right arm, ‘Li Yao’s’ entire right arm was wreathed in silver brightness, as if it had changed from an atom into liquid metal that could change at any time. It condensed into an indestructible blade and stabbed at the spine of the Red Pole.

Red Pole Star was greatly shocked. He did not see Li Yao casting any spells, but a series of broken drops of blood suddenly appeared in front of him. The drops of blood exploded and formed a thick mist of blood. When the blood touched ‘Li Yao’s’ sharp right arm, it was like ten thousand crimson bugs that were trying to crawl into ‘Li Yao’s’ arm, only to be vaporized by the burning silver brightness.

However, it also gave Red Pole a moment of respite. He leaned back in embarrassment and slid almost five meters away from the ground before he finally escaped Li Yao’s first attack.

However, before he could catch his breath, ‘Li Yao’ launched a second wave of attacks.

‘Li Yao’s’ right arm turned into a silver whip. Countless streaks of brightness surrounded the small command room and cut the space inside into pieces no larger than fists, leaving no room for Red Pole to dodge at all.

The Red Pole Star’s face was pale, and the weird red star that covered his eyes was so intense that it seemed to be burning. The silver brightness was cutting tiny wounds on his body that looked like the lips of a baby, and the blood spurting out of the wounds turned into his weapons. Some of them turned into bloody mist, some into fury that looked like red lotuses, some into bullets that were as fast as lightning, and some even unfolded into a certain translucent defense module. However, no matter how hard he tried to jump up and down in the narrow space, he could not get rid of ‘Li Yao’s’ silver storm. The bloody mist was swallowed, the red lotuses were extinguished, the bullets were shattered, and the defense module was riddled with holes and shattered by the dazzling attacks.

In a moment, Li Yao’s right arm split into thousands of silver threads that covered most of the space inside the command room like a spider web. The Red Pole Star was forced into a dead end and had to take the risk. Its body suddenly expanded, and scorching and crimson air currents spurted out of its every pore. It condensed into a bloody demon-like human being in midair and lunged at ‘Li Yao’.

However, his real body was crawling on the ground on all fours, trying to bypass ‘Li Yao’ and run away through the door.

The smile on ‘Li Yao’s’ face grew wider and wider. The five fingers on his right hand turned into five streaks of silver brightness and pierced into the bloody mist demon, swallowing all the redness and redness. His left hand, which had been on his back all the time, suddenly extended and pierced into Red Pole’s chest, piercing through his back and pinning the leader of the prison breakers to the wall.

The short and intense battle came to an abrupt end.

The Red Pole Star’s eyes were wide open. They were covered in the surging blood mist, as if it could not believe what had happened to ‘Li Yao’ at all.

CRACK! CRACK! CRACK!

A weird noise came from his throat. Tiny pink bubbles popped up from his nostrils and the corners of his mouth, indicating that his lungs and even his heart had been seriously damaged.

He struggled on the wall and tried to slap Li Yao’s arm, only to no avail.

‘Li Yao’ stared at Red Pole emotionlessly. More silver threads that looked like spider webs were released from his right hand and pierced into every super computer in the command room. Some of the silver threads were even slithering on the walls and the ceiling toward Red Pole like vipers.

The Red Pole Star’s eyes were almost tearing apart. He twisted his head and struggled, but he could not get rid of Li Yao’s control at all. Not only had Li Yao’s left arm pierced through his chest, the silver threads spreading out of his arm had also invaded his internal organs through his veins and nerves. The two of them were tangled together and could not be separated at all.

Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi!

A few silver threads that looked like worms crawled into his brain through the corners of his eyes, ears, nostrils, and mouth.

“Ah!”

‘Li Yao’ immediately sensed the painful and regretful roars from the depths of Red Pole’s soul. “Who are you? You are not Li Yao. What are you exactly?”

“As for me…”

‘Li Yao’ tilted his head and thought for a long time. When the interconnecting silver threads spread out and finally turned into silver tides that covered all the screens and supercomputers and even surged toward Red Pole, he smiled again and sent a complicated message to the depths of Red Pole’s soul. “Of course, I am Li Yao, but I am far more than just Li Yao. You’ve been wandering in my mind for so long and experienced so many cycles, escapes, and infiltration. Do you still not know what kind of existence is standing in front of you?”

“You—you!”

Red Pole Star was dumbfounded. Desperation and disbelief were rippling deep inside his soul. “You are… the will of the earth!”

“For an existence such as myself, names mean nothing. I have countless faces and names in countless small universes and worlds. The ‘will of the earth’ is just one of my countless faces.

“Li Yao” calmly said, “You can think of me as a ghost left over from the destruction of a certain ancient Earth a long time ago. You can also think of me as a product of billions of intelligent life-forms releasing powerful brainwaves under endless pain and anger, resonating with the stellar storm. However, that was just my initial form. In the millions of years that followed, I travelled through the entire universe that could be reached at the speed of light. I encountered countless bizarre scenes and bizarre civilizations. I experienced countless evolutions that were simply unimaginable for you. I grasped the supreme power of this universe. I even understood the laws of the three-dimensional universe. To some extent, I surpassed the illusion of the three-dimensional universe and continued marching towards the truth of the four-dimensional universe… You are very lucky. Right now, I have yet to evolve into a true four-dimensional life-form. I am currently in the process of a single-celled life-form to a multi-celled life-form. A fish crawls onto the land. So, when I project 1/10,000th of my strength and will onto Earth, I can transform into Li Yao, and I can still barely communicate with you. If I take a step further on the road of breaking through the universe beyond reincarnation, you and I will simply be unable to communicate, and we will be unable to understand each other.”

“As it turns out, cough cough cough cough cough. As it turns out, you’ve already found the biggest and most important soul fragment of ‘Li Yao’ and corrupted and controlled him in the previous or previous reincarnation.”

The Red Pole Star smiled bitterly. “I understand now. You’ve seen through our plan a long time ago. We’ve been working hard for ‘Li Yao’s rebirth’. You even knew about Zhang Daniu and ‘40,000 Years of Cultivation’ a long time ago. But you kept it a secret and regarded Zhang Daniu and ‘40,000 Years of Cultivation’ as a fatal trap that we dug for ourselves!

“In order to avoid being discovered by us, you don’t need to do anything at all. All you need to do is attach your projection to the largest and most important ‘Li Yao’s soul fragment’. Set certain triggering conditions and let nature take its course. Let the ignorant ‘College Student Li Yao’ come into contact with ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’ by himself. Let him awaken on his own. Let him experience a series of soul-stirring and absurd adventures. In the end, let him come to the Ark Island together with Zhang Daniu. Let him come to me and become the ‘target drug’ for precise strikes at the most crucial moment!

“The Heaven’s Origin Sector didn’t capture Zhang Daniu because they were slow to react. It was because you arranged for them to be half a beat slower. From the beginning to the end, you didn’t want the hunters to capture Zhang Daniu. Instead, you wanted the hunters to be defeated by ‘College Student Li Yao’. You wanted ‘College Student Li Yao’ to escort Zhang Daniu safely to the Ark Island and also enter my line of sight so that I would discover his uniqueness!

“Hehe. It’s—it’s truly a wonderful tactic. Yes, even if I can set up the most rigorous screening and examination so that all the people entering and exiting the Ark Island will completely expose the secrets in their hearts, if even they don’t know the first thing about the ‘mission’, how could they reveal any flaws? Even ‘College Student Li Yao’ doesn’t know that he is the deadliest ‘target drug’. His performance is naturally perfect.

“I just—I just don’t understand. When did you lock onto and corrupt the largest piece of ‘Li Yao’s soul fragment’? Traveler Meng clearly guided ‘College Student Li Yao’ to go back in time to countless reincarnations. If you had infiltrated it during that time, you would have definitely left some traces. Why didn’t you? It’s impossible!”
